- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `run_request.go`**

```go
// Triggered runs: the entry point schedules and webhook triggers enqueue
// through (spec 2026-09-18 §2 step 4, §3). Same validation, zone split and
// host-key checks as a manual run — enqueueRunScope is shared — plus the
// trigger kind and reference and the sealed per-run overrides.
package jobs

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// Trigger kinds beyond manual and system.
const (
	TriggerSchedule = "schedule"
	TriggerWebhook  = "webhook"
)

// RunOverrides is the plaintext override a run carries — sealed with
// JobAAD in the job's payload, merged over the template at claim time.
type RunOverrides struct {
	Limit     *string        `json:"limit,omitempty"`
	ExtraVars map[string]any `json:"extra_vars,omitempty"`
}

func (o *RunOverrides) empty() bool {
	return o == nil || (o.Limit == nil && len(o.ExtraVars) == 0)
}

// RunRequest says who or what starts a run and with which overrides.
type RunRequest struct {
	Trigger    string
	TriggerRef *uuid.UUID
	Actor      *uuid.UUID
	Overrides  *RunOverrides
}

func (r RunRequest) validate() error {
	switch r.Trigger {
	case TriggerManual, TriggerSchedule, TriggerWebhook, TriggerSystem:
		return nil
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown trigger %q", ErrInvalidInput, r.Trigger)
}

// EnqueueRunWith is EnqueueRun with an explicit trigger and overrides.
func (s *Service) EnqueueRunWith(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, req RunRequest) ([]Job, error) {
	if err := req.validate(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	tmpl, err := s.templates.TemplateLookup(ctx, orgID, templateID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	scope, err := parseScope(tmpl.Scope)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return s.enqueueRunScope(ctx, orgID, tmpl.ID, scope, req)
}

// EnqueueSyncWith is EnqueueSync with an explicit trigger (a repo_sync
// webhook). A sync takes no overrides.
func (s *Service) EnqueueSyncWith(ctx context.Context, orgID, repoID uuid.UUID, req RunRequest) (Job, error) {
	if err := req.validate(); err != nil {
		return Job{}, err
	}
	if !req.Overrides.empty() {
		return Job{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: a repository sync takes no overrides", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	return s.enqueueSync(ctx, orgID, repoID, req)
}

// HasActiveRun reports whether a job of the template is not terminal yet.
func (s *Service) HasActiveRun(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID) (bool, error) {
	return s.store.HasActiveTemplateJob(ctx, orgID, templateID)
}

// HasActiveSync reports whether a sync of the repository is not terminal.
func (s *Service) HasActiveSync(ctx context.Context, orgID, repoID uuid.UUID) (bool, error) {
	return s.store.HasActiveRepoSync(ctx, orgID, repoID)
}

// runPayload seals the overrides for one job; nil when there are none.
func (s *Service) runPayload(j Job, ov *RunOverrides) (json.RawMessage, error) {
	if ov.empty() {
		return nil, nil
	}
	raw, err := json.Marshal(ov)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("encode run overrides: %w", err)
	}
	return s.sealPayload(j, raw)
}
```

In `service.go`:
- Change `enqueueRunScope(ctx, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, scope render.Scope, actor *uuid.UUID)` to take `req RunRequest` in place of `actor`. In the job literal use `Trigger: req.Trigger, TriggerRef: req.TriggerRef, ActorID: req.Actor`. Replace `s.store.CreateJob(ctx, j, nil)` with:

```go
		payload, err := s.runPayload(j, req.Overrides)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := s.store.CreateJob(ctx, j, payload); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
```

- `EnqueueRun` returns `s.EnqueueRunWith(ctx, orgID, templateID, RunRequest{Trigger: TriggerManual, Actor: actor})`, and `RetryFailed` passes `RunRequest{Trigger: TriggerManual, Actor: actor}`.

- Add `TriggerRef *uuid.UUID` to `Job` (after `Trigger`), with the doc comment "TriggerRef is the schedule or webhook trigger that started the job; nil for manual and system jobs." Extend `Store` with the two methods.

`store_sql.go`: `createJobParams` sets `TriggerRef: db.NullUUID(j.TriggerRef)` and maps an empty `j.Trigger` to `TriggerManual`. Several existing integration tests insert jobs without a trigger; the insert always names the column, so the DB default never applied and the new CHECK would refuse `''`. `toJob` sets `TriggerRef: db.UUIDPtr(r.TriggerRef)`, and `toJobDetail`, which builds a `gen.Job` from the `GetJobRow`, forwards `TriggerRef: r.TriggerRef`. - [ ] **Step 7: Implement `run_overrides.go` and wire the claim**

```go
// Claim-time half of run overrides: the sealed payload of a run job is
// opened and merged over the template's limit and extra vars.
package jobs

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"maps"
)

// runOverrides opens a run job's sealed overrides; nil when it has none.
func (q *SQLQueue) runOverrides(job Job) (*RunOverrides, error) {
	if len(job.Payload) == 0 {
		return nil, nil
	}
	plain, err := q.openKindPayload(job)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if plain == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}
	var ov RunOverrides
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(plain), &ov); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode run overrides: %w", err)
	}
	return &ov, nil
}

// mergeExtraVars overlays the override's top-level vars on the template's.
func mergeExtraVars(base json.RawMessage, ov *RunOverrides) (json.RawMessage, error) {
	if ov == nil || len(ov.ExtraVars) == 0 {
		return base, nil
	}
	merged := map[string]any{}
	if len(base) > 0 {
		if err := json.Unmarshal(base, &merged); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: template extra vars are not a JSON object", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
	}
	maps.Copy(merged, ov.ExtraVars)
	return json.Marshal(merged)
}
```

In `queue_sql.go`'s `buildPayload`, inside `if job.Kind == KindRun { … }` right after the template lookup, replace the three statements that set `limit`, parse `extraVars` and call `redact.addExtraVarSecrets(tmpl.ExtraVarsSchema, tmpl.ExtraVars)`:

```go
		ov, err := q.runOverrides(job)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		limit = tmpl.Limit
		if ov != nil && ov.Limit != nil {
			limit = *ov.Limit
		}
		varsRaw, err := mergeExtraVars(tmpl.ExtraVars, ov)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if extraVars, err = stringMap(varsRaw); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("template extra vars: %w", err)
		}
```

Keep the forbidden-name loop as it is (it now checks the merged map) and change the redaction call to `redact.addExtraVarSecrets(tmpl.ExtraVarsSchema, varsRaw)`.

```go
type Mode string        // ModeCron "cron", ModeSimple "simple"
type Overlap string     // OverlapSkip "skip", OverlapQueue "queue"
type Outcome string     // OutcomeTriggered, OutcomeSkippedOverlap "skipped_overlap", OutcomeMissed, OutcomeFailed
type CatchUp struct { Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`; Window string `json:"window"` }
type Guards struct { Overlap Overlap `json:"overlap"`; CatchUp CatchUp `json:"catch_up"` } // v0.2d adds Maintenance
type Schedule struct {
	ID, OrgID, TemplateID uuid.UUID
	Name string; Mode Mode; Cron string; Simple *cronexpr.Simple; Timezone string
	Guards Guards
	Overrides templates.Overrides // storage form: secrets sealed with AAD(org, id)
	Paused bool
	NextRunAt, LastRunAt *time.Time
	CreatedBy *uuid.UUID
	CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time
	Revision int64
}
type Input struct {
	TemplateID uuid.UUID; Name string; Mode Mode; Cron string; Simple *cronexpr.Simple; Timezone string
	Guards Guards; Overrides templates.Overrides
}
type Run struct {
	ID, OrgID, ScheduleID uuid.UUID
	DueAt, DecidedAt time.Time
	Outcome Outcome; Reason string
	JobGroupID *uuid.UUID
}
type PreviewInput struct { Mode Mode; Cron string; Simple *cronexpr.Simple; Timezone string }
type Preview struct { Cron string; Description string; Next []time.Time }

var ErrNotFound, ErrInvalidInput, ErrNameTaken, ErrRevisionMismatch error

func AAD(orgID, id uuid.UUID) []byte // "schedule:<org>:<id>"

type Store interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, s Schedule) (Schedule, error)
	Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error)
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, templateID *uuid.UUID) ([]Schedule, error)
	Update(ctx context.Context, s Schedule, revision int64) (Schedule, error)
	SetPaused(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, paused bool, next *time.Time) (Schedule, error)
	PauseAll(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (int, error)
	Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	RecordRun(ctx context.Context, r Run) error
	Runs(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Run, error)
	LatestRuns(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]Run, error)
}
type TemplateChecker interface {
	ValidateOverrides(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, in templates.Overrides, opt templates.OverrideOptions) (templates.Overrides, error)
	RevealOverrides(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, stored templates.Overrides, aad []byte) (templates.Overrides, error)
}
type RunEnqueuer interface {
	EnqueueRunWith(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) ([]jobs.Job, error)
	HasActiveRun(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID) (bool, error)
}
type Auditor interface { Record(ctx context.Context, e audit.Event) error }

func NewService(store Store, tmpl TemplateChecker, runs RunEnqueuer, auditor Auditor, now func() time.Time, rand io.Reader, log *slog.Logger) *Service
func (s *Service) Create(ctx context.Context, orgID, actor uuid.UUID, in Input) (Schedule, error)
func (s *Service) Update(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, in Input) (Schedule, error)
func (s *Service) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error)
func (s *Service) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, templateID *uuid.UUID) ([]Schedule, map[uuid.UUID]Run, error)
func (s *Service) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
func (s *Service) Pause(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error)
func (s *Service) Resume(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error)
func (s *Service) PauseAll(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (int, error)
func (s *Service) RunNow(ctx context.Context, orgID, id, actor uuid.UUID) ([]jobs.Job, error)
func (s *Service) Runs(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Run, error)
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the migration**

`internal/db/migrations/00025_schedules.sql`:

```sql
-- +goose Up
-- Schedules for job templates (spec 2026-09-18 §2). Several schedules per
-- template; deleting a template (API only for now) deletes its schedules.
-- simple keeps the form input so it round-trips; cron always holds the
-- expression the ticker evaluates (simple mode compiles to it on save).
-- limit / extra_vars are per-run overrides; secret values inside
-- extra_vars are sealed with AAD schedule:<org>:<id>. next_run_at is NULL
-- while paused. revision drives If-Match; the ticker never bumps it.
CREATE TABLE schedules (
  id           uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  template_id  uuid NOT NULL,
  name         text NOT NULL,
  mode         text NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN ('cron', 'simple')),
  cron         text NOT NULL,
  simple       jsonb,
  timezone     text NOT NULL,
  guards       jsonb NOT NULL,
  "limit"      text,
  extra_vars   jsonb,
  paused       boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  next_run_at  timestamptz,
  last_run_at  timestamptz,
  created_by   uuid,
  created_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  revision     bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  UNIQUE (org_id, name),
  UNIQUE (org_id, id),
  CHECK ((mode = 'simple') = (simple IS NOT NULL)),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, template_id) REFERENCES job_templates (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
-- The ticker's due scan (bypass scope, every org) and the per-template list.
CREATE INDEX schedules_due_idx ON schedules (next_run_at) WHERE NOT paused;
CREATE INDEX schedules_template_idx ON schedules (org_id, template_id);

ALTER TABLE schedules ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE schedules FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON schedules
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON schedules
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- One row per decision of the ticker (or "run now"). The newest 500 per
-- schedule are kept (the ticker trims). The outcome CHECK is named so
-- v0.2d can widen it for skipped_maintenance.
CREATE TABLE schedule_runs (
  id           uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id       uuid NOT NULL,
  schedule_id  uuid NOT NULL,
  due_at       timestamptz NOT NULL,
  decided_at   timestamptz NOT NULL,
  outcome      text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT schedule_runs_outcome_check
                 CHECK (outcome IN ('triggered', 'skipped_overlap', 'missed', 'failed')),
  reason       text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  job_group_id uuid,
  UNIQUE (org_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, schedule_id) REFERENCES schedules (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE INDEX schedule_runs_schedule_idx ON schedule_runs (org_id, schedule_id, decided_at DESC);

ALTER TABLE schedule_runs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE schedule_runs FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON schedule_runs
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON schedule_runs
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- +goose Down
DROP TABLE schedule_runs;
DROP TABLE schedules;
```

`internal/db/queries/schedules.sql` (named parameters only; sqlc cannot mix `$n` and `@name`):

```sql
-- name: CreateSchedule :one
INSERT INTO schedules (id, org_id, template_id, name, mode, cron, simple, timezone, guards, "limit", extra_vars, paused, next_run_at, created_by)
VALUES (@id, @org_id, @template_id, @name, @mode, @cron, @simple, @timezone, @guards, sqlc.narg(limit_override), @extra_vars, false, sqlc.narg(next_run_at), sqlc.narg(created_by))
RETURNING *;

-- name: GetSchedule :one
SELECT * FROM schedules WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id;

-- name: ListSchedules :many
SELECT * FROM schedules
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND (sqlc.narg(template_id)::uuid IS NULL OR template_id = sqlc.narg(template_id)::uuid)
ORDER BY name;

-- name: UpdateSchedule :one
-- No row back means a stale revision or an unknown schedule (the store
-- tells the two apart with GetSchedule).
UPDATE schedules SET template_id = @template_id, name = @name, mode = @mode, cron = @cron, simple = @simple,
  timezone = @timezone, guards = @guards, "limit" = sqlc.narg(limit_override), extra_vars = @extra_vars,
  next_run_at = sqlc.narg(next_run_at), revision = revision + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id AND revision = @revision
RETURNING *;

-- name: SetSchedulePaused :one
UPDATE schedules SET paused = @paused, next_run_at = sqlc.narg(next_run_at), revision = revision + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id
RETURNING *;

-- name: PauseAllSchedules :execrows
UPDATE schedules SET paused = true, next_run_at = NULL, revision = revision + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND NOT paused;

-- name: DeleteSchedule :execrows
DELETE FROM schedules WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id;

-- name: InsertScheduleRun :exec
INSERT INTO schedule_runs (id, org_id, schedule_id, due_at, decided_at, outcome, reason, job_group_id)
VALUES (@id, @org_id, @schedule_id, @due_at, @decided_at, @outcome, @reason, sqlc.narg(job_group_id));

-- name: ListScheduleRuns :many
SELECT * FROM schedule_runs WHERE org_id = @org_id AND schedule_id = @schedule_id
ORDER BY decided_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT @lim;

-- name: LatestScheduleRuns :many
SELECT DISTINCT ON (schedule_id) * FROM schedule_runs WHERE org_id = @org_id
ORDER BY schedule_id, decided_at DESC, id DESC;
```

Run: `make sqlc`
Expected: `internal/db/gen/schedules.sql.go` generated. `simple`, `guards` and `extra_vars` are `[]byte`, `Limit` is `*string`, `next_run_at`/`created_by` are `pgtype.Timestamptz`/`pgtype.UUID`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the fakes and failing validation tests**

`internal/schedules/fakes_test.go`:

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"context"
	"sort"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/audit"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

var ctx = context.Background()

type fakeStore struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	byID map[uuid.UUID]schedules.Schedule
	runs []schedules.Run
}

func newFakeStore() *fakeStore { return &fakeStore{byID: map[uuid.UUID]schedules.Schedule{}} }

func (f *fakeStore) Create(_ context.Context, s schedules.Schedule) (schedules.Schedule, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for _, o := range f.byID {
		if o.OrgID == s.OrgID && o.Name == s.Name {
			return schedules.Schedule{}, schedules.ErrNameTaken
		}
	}
	s.Revision = 1
	f.byID[s.ID] = s
	return s, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Get(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (schedules.Schedule, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	s, ok := f.byID[id]
	if !ok || s.OrgID != orgID {
		return schedules.Schedule{}, schedules.ErrNotFound
	}
	return s, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) List(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, templateID *uuid.UUID) ([]schedules.Schedule, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []schedules.Schedule
	for _, s := range f.byID {
		if s.OrgID == orgID && (templateID == nil || s.TemplateID == *templateID) {
			out = append(out, s)
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Name < out[j].Name })
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Update(_ context.Context, s schedules.Schedule, revision int64) (schedules.Schedule, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	cur, ok := f.byID[s.ID]
	if !ok || cur.OrgID != s.OrgID {
		return schedules.Schedule{}, schedules.ErrNotFound
	}
	if cur.Revision != revision {
		return schedules.Schedule{}, schedules.ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	s.Revision = revision + 1
	f.byID[s.ID] = s
	return s, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) SetPaused(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, paused bool, next *time.Time) (schedules.Schedule, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	s, ok := f.byID[id]
	if !ok || s.OrgID != orgID {
		return schedules.Schedule{}, schedules.ErrNotFound
	}
	s.Paused, s.NextRunAt, s.Revision = paused, next, s.Revision+1
	f.byID[id] = s
	return s, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) PauseAll(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (int, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	n := 0
	for id, s := range f.byID {
		if s.OrgID == orgID && !s.Paused {
			s.Paused, s.NextRunAt = true, nil
			f.byID[id] = s
			n++
		}
	}
	return n, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Delete(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	s, ok := f.byID[id]
	if !ok || s.OrgID != orgID {
		return schedules.ErrNotFound
	}
	delete(f.byID, id)
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) RecordRun(_ context.Context, r schedules.Run) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.runs = append(f.runs, r)
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Runs(_ context.Context, _, scheduleID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]schedules.Run, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []schedules.Run
	for i := len(f.runs) - 1; i >= 0 && len(out) < limit; i-- {
		if f.runs[i].ScheduleID == scheduleID {
			out = append(out, f.runs[i])
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) LatestRuns(_ context.Context, _ uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]schedules.Run, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	out := map[uuid.UUID]schedules.Run{}
	for _, r := range f.runs {
		out[r.ScheduleID] = r
	}
	return out, nil
}

// fakeTemplates records the options it was called with; "sealing" wraps a
// value in a marker map so tests can see it happened.
type fakeTemplates struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	lastOpt  templates.OverrideOptions
	validErr error
	reveals  int
}

func (f *fakeTemplates) ValidateOverrides(_ context.Context, _, _ uuid.UUID, in templates.Overrides, opt templates.OverrideOptions) (templates.Overrides, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.lastOpt = opt
	if f.validErr != nil {
		return templates.Overrides{}, f.validErr
	}
	out := templates.Overrides{Limit: in.Limit, ExtraVars: map[string]any{}}
	for k, v := range in.ExtraVars {
		if opt.SealAAD != nil {
			v = map[string]any{"sealed-with": string(opt.SealAAD), "v": v}
		}
		out.ExtraVars[k] = v
	}
	if len(out.ExtraVars) == 0 {
		out.ExtraVars = nil
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeTemplates) RevealOverrides(_ context.Context, _, _ uuid.UUID, stored templates.Overrides, aad []byte) (templates.Overrides, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.reveals++
	out := templates.Overrides{Limit: stored.Limit, ExtraVars: map[string]any{}}
	for k, v := range stored.ExtraVars {
		m := v.(map[string]any)
		if m["sealed-with"] != string(aad) {
			return templates.Overrides{}, errWrongAAD
		}
		out.ExtraVars[k] = m["v"]
	}
	return out, nil
}

type fakeRuns struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	requests []jobs.RunRequest
	active   bool
	err      error
	group    uuid.UUID
}

func (f *fakeRuns) EnqueueRunWith(_ context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) ([]jobs.Job, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if f.err != nil {
		return nil, f.err
	}
	f.requests = append(f.requests, req)
	if f.group == uuid.Nil {
		f.group = uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	}
	return []jobs.Job{{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: orgID, TemplateID: &templateID, RunGroupID: f.group, Trigger: req.Trigger}}, nil
}

func (f *fakeRuns) HasActiveRun(context.Context, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID) (bool, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return f.active, nil
}

type fakeAudit struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	events []audit.Event
}

func (f *fakeAudit) Record(_ context.Context, e audit.Event) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.events = append(f.events, e)
	return nil
}
```

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

var errWrongAAD = errors.New("wrong aad")

func validInput(templateID uuid.UUID) schedules.Input {
	return schedules.Input{TemplateID: templateID, Name: "Nightly OS patching", Mode: schedules.ModeCron, Cron: "0 3 * * 2,4", Timezone: "Europe/Berlin"}
}

func TestCreateValidation(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	cases := []struct {
		name   string
		mutate func(*schedules.Input)
	}{
		{"empty name", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Name = "  " }},
		{"long name", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Name = strings.Repeat("x", 101) }},
		{"control char in name", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Name = "a\x07b" }},
		{"unknown mode", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Mode = "rrule" }},
		{"bad cron", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Cron = "61 * * * *" }},
		{"never fires", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Cron = "0 0 30 2 *" }},
		{"simple without input", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Mode, in.Simple = schedules.ModeSimple, nil }},
		{"bad simple", func(in *schedules.Input) {
			in.Mode, in.Simple = schedules.ModeSimple, &cronexpr.Simple{Kind: "every_minutes", Every: 7}
		}},
		{"empty timezone", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Timezone = "" }},
		{"Local timezone", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Timezone = "Local" }},
		{"unknown timezone", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Timezone = "Mars/Olympus" }},
		{"bad overlap", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Guards.Overlap = "wait" }},
		{"catch-up window too long", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Guards.CatchUp = schedules.CatchUp{Enabled: true, Window: "25h"} }},
		{"catch-up window too short", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Guards.CatchUp = schedules.CatchUp{Enabled: true, Window: "30s"} }},
		{"catch-up window garbage", func(in *schedules.Input) { in.Guards.CatchUp = schedules.CatchUp{Window: "soon"} }},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			in := validInput(w.templateID)
			c.mutate(&in)
			if _, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrInvalidInput) {
				t.Fatalf("Create = %v, want ErrInvalidInput", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCreateNormalizesDefaults(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	got, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.Guards.Overlap != schedules.OverlapSkip || got.Guards.CatchUp.Enabled || got.Guards.CatchUp.Window != "1h" {
		t.Fatalf("guards = %+v", got.Guards)
	}
	// 2026-09-18 is a Friday; the next Tue/Thu 03:00 Berlin is Tue 22 Sep.
	if got.NextRunAt == nil || got.NextRunAt.In(w.berlin).Format("2006-01-02 15:04 MST") != "2026-09-22 03:00 CEST" {
		t.Fatalf("next = %v", got.NextRunAt)
	}
	if got.CreatedBy == nil || *got.CreatedBy != w.actor {
		t.Fatalf("created_by = %v", got.CreatedBy)
	}
}

func TestCreateCompilesSimpleMode(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	in := validInput(w.templateID)
	in.Mode, in.Cron = schedules.ModeSimple, "ignored"
	in.Simple = &cronexpr.Simple{Kind: "every_hours", Every: 6}
	got, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.Cron != "0 */6 * * *" || got.Simple == nil || got.Simple.Every != 6 {
		t.Fatalf("got %+v", got)
	}
}

func TestPreview(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	p, err := w.svc.Preview(schedules.PreviewInput{Mode: schedules.ModeSimple, Simple: &cronexpr.Simple{Kind: "weekly", Days: []string{"tue", "thu"}, Time: "03:00"}, Timezone: "Europe/Berlin"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if p.Cron != "0 3 * * 2,4" || p.Description != "Tue, Thu at 03:00" || len(p.Next) != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("preview = %+v", p)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Preview(schedules.PreviewInput{Mode: schedules.ModeCron, Cron: "nope", Timezone: "UTC"}); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("bad preview = %v", err)
	}
}
```

```go
import (

type world struct {

	tmpl       *fakeTemplates
	runs       *fakeRuns

	org        uuid.UUID

func newWorld(t *testing.T) *world {
	t.Helper()
	berlin, err := time.LoadLocation("Europe/Berlin")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w := &world{
		store: newFakeStore(), tmpl: &fakeTemplates{}, runs: &fakeRuns{}, audit: &fakeAudit{},
		org: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), actor: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), templateID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()),
		now: time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), berlin: berlin,
	}
	w.svc = schedules.NewService(w.store, w.tmpl, w.runs, w.audit, func() time.Time { return w.now }, rand.Reader,
		slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
	return w
}

func TestCreateSealsOverridesWithTheScheduleAAD(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	in := validInput(w.templateID)
	limit := "web*"
	in.Overrides = templates.Overrides{Limit: &limit, ExtraVars: map[string]any{"token": "s3cret"}}
	got, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in)

	if string(w.tmpl.lastOpt.SealAAD) != string(schedules.AAD(w.org, got.ID)) {
		t.Fatalf("seal aad = %q", w.tmpl.lastOpt.SealAAD)
	}
	if string(schedules.AAD(w.org, got.ID)) != "schedule:"+w.org.String()+":"+got.ID.String() {
		t.Fatalf("AAD format = %q", schedules.AAD(w.org, got.ID))
	}
	if *got.Overrides.Limit != "web*" || got.Overrides.ExtraVars["token"].(map[string]any)["v"] != "s3cret" {
		t.Fatalf("stored overrides = %+v", got.Overrides)
	}
}

func TestCreatePassesTemplateValidationErrorsThrough(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	w.tmpl.validErr = templates.ErrNotFound
	if _, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID)); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrInvalidInput) {

func TestUpdateRequiresTheCurrentRevision(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	s, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	in := validInput(w.templateID)
	in.Cron = "*/30 * * * *"
	if _, err := w.svc.Update(ctx, w.org, s.ID, s.Revision+1, in); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale revision: %v", err)
	}
	up, err := w.svc.Update(ctx, w.org, s.ID, s.Revision, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if up.Cron != "*/30 * * * *" || !up.NextRunAt.Equal(time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 30, 0, 0, time.UTC)) {
		t.Fatalf("updated = %+v", up)
	}
	// Keep markers resolve against the stored overrides.
	if w.tmpl.lastOpt.SealAAD == nil {
		t.Fatal("update must seal with the schedule AAD")
	}
}

func TestPauseAndResume(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	s, _ := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
	p, err := w.svc.Pause(ctx, w.org, s.ID)
	if err != nil || !p.Paused || p.NextRunAt != nil {
		t.Fatalf("paused = %+v, %v", p, err)
	}
	// A week later, resume computes from now and never replays the week.
	w.now = w.now.Add(7 * 24 * time.Hour)
	r, err := w.svc.Resume(ctx, w.org, s.ID)
	if err != nil || r.Paused || r.NextRunAt == nil || !r.NextRunAt.After(w.now) {
		t.Fatalf("resumed = %+v, %v", r, err)
	}
	if n, err := w.svc.PauseAll(ctx, w.org); err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("PauseAll = %d, %v", n, err)
	}
}

func TestRunNowEnqueuesWithRevealedOverridesAndRecordsTheRun(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	in := validInput(w.templateID)
	in.Overrides = templates.Overrides{ExtraVars: map[string]any{"token": "s3cret"}}
	s, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	created, err := w.svc.RunNow(ctx, w.org, s.ID, w.actor)
	if err != nil || len(created) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("RunNow = %v, %v", created, err)
	}
	req := w.runs.requests[0]
	if req.Trigger != jobs.TriggerSchedule || *req.TriggerRef != s.ID || *req.Actor != w.actor {
		t.Fatalf("request = %+v", req)
	}
	if req.Overrides == nil || req.Overrides.ExtraVars["token"] != "s3cret" {
		t.Fatalf("overrides = %+v", req.Overrides)
	}
	runs, _ := w.svc.Runs(ctx, w.org, s.ID, 10)
	if len(runs) != 1 || runs[0].Outcome != schedules.OutcomeTriggered || runs[0].Reason != "run now" || *runs[0].JobGroupID != created[0].RunGroupID {
		t.Fatalf("runs = %+v", runs)
	}
	if len(w.audit.events) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("run now is audited by the HTTP route, not the service")
	}
}

func TestRunNowRecordsAFailure(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	s, _ := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
	w.runs.err = jobs.ErrEmptyScope
	if _, err := w.svc.RunNow(ctx, w.org, s.ID, w.actor); !errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrEmptyScope) {
		t.Fatalf("RunNow = %v", err)
	}
	runs, _ := w.svc.Runs(ctx, w.org, s.ID, 10)
	if len(runs) != 1 || runs[0].Outcome != schedules.OutcomeFailed || runs[0].Reason != "the template's scope resolves to no host" {
		t.Fatalf("runs = %+v", runs)
	}
}

func TestListReturnsTheLatestRunPerSchedule(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	s, _ := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
	if _, err := w.svc.RunNow(ctx, w.org, s.ID, w.actor); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	list, latest, err := w.svc.List(ctx, w.org, nil)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 1 || latest[s.ID].Outcome != schedules.OutcomeTriggered {
		t.Fatalf("List = %v, %v, %v", list, latest, err)
	}
	other := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if l, _, _ := w.svc.List(ctx, w.org, &other); len(l) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("filtered list = %v", l)
	}
}

func TestAuditActorKindConstant(t *testing.T) {
	if audit.ActorKindSchedule != "schedule" {
		t.Fatal("ActorKindSchedule must be the literal \"schedule\"")
	}
}
```

`internal/schedules/schedule.go`:

```go
// Package schedules runs job templates on a timetable (spec 2026-09-18
// §2): cron or simple mode in an IANA time zone, overlap and catch-up
// guards, pause, per-schedule overrides, and a ticker that decides every
// due occurrence exactly once across managers (FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED).
package schedules

import (
	"errors"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

// Sentinels the API maps to HTTP.
var (
	ErrNotFound         = errors.New("not found")
	ErrInvalidInput     = errors.New("invalid input")
	ErrNameTaken        = errors.New("name already in use")
	ErrRevisionMismatch = errors.New("revision mismatch")
)

// Mode is how the timetable was entered.
type Mode string

const (
	ModeCron   Mode = "cron"
	ModeSimple Mode = "simple"
)

// Overlap is the guard for a due run while a run of the template is active.
type Overlap string

const (
	OverlapSkip  Overlap = "skip"
	OverlapQueue Overlap = "queue"
)

// CatchUp lets one late run happen after downtime, inside Window.
type CatchUp struct {
	Enabled bool   `json:"enabled"`
	Window  string `json:"window"`
}

// Guards is the schedules.guards jsonb. v0.2d adds a maintenance guard;
// unknown keys never reach the database because the API decodes into
// this struct.
type Guards struct {
	Overlap Overlap `json:"overlap"`
	CatchUp CatchUp `json:"catch_up"`
}

// Outcome is what the ticker (or run now) decided for a due occurrence.
type Outcome string

const (
	OutcomeTriggered      Outcome = "triggered"
	OutcomeSkippedOverlap Outcome = "skipped_overlap"
	OutcomeMissed         Outcome = "missed"
	OutcomeFailed         Outcome = "failed"
)

// Schedule is one stored schedule. Overrides are in their storage form:
// secret values sealed with AAD(OrgID, ID).
type Schedule struct {
	ID, OrgID, TemplateID uuid.UUID
	Name                  string
	Mode                  Mode
	Cron                  string
	Simple                *cronexpr.Simple
	Timezone              string
	Guards                Guards
	Overrides             templates.Overrides
	Paused                bool
	NextRunAt, LastRunAt  *time.Time
	CreatedBy             *uuid.UUID
	CreatedAt, UpdatedAt  time.Time
	Revision              int64
}

// Input is what an operator submits for create and update.
type Input struct {
	TemplateID uuid.UUID
	Name       string
	Mode       Mode
	Cron       string
	Simple     *cronexpr.Simple
	Timezone   string
	Guards     Guards
	Overrides  templates.Overrides
}

// Run is one recorded decision.
type Run struct {
	ID, OrgID, ScheduleID uuid.UUID
	DueAt, DecidedAt      time.Time
	Outcome               Outcome
	Reason                string
	JobGroupID            *uuid.UUID
}

// PreviewInput and Preview back the editor's "next 5 occurrences".
type PreviewInput struct {
	Mode     Mode
	Cron     string
	Simple   *cronexpr.Simple
	Timezone string
}

type Preview struct {
	Cron        string
	Description string
	Next        []time.Time
}

// AAD is the associated data a schedule's secret override values are
// sealed with.
func AAD(orgID, id uuid.UUID) []byte {
	return []byte("schedule:" + orgID.String() + ":" + id.String())
}
```

`internal/schedules/validate.go`:

```go
package schedules

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"
	"unicode"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
)

const (
	maxNameRunes     = 100
	defaultWindow    = "1h"
	minCatchUpWindow = time.Minute
	maxCatchUpWindow = 24 * time.Hour
)

// timetable is a validated expression in its zone.
type timetable struct {
	expr   cronexpr.Expr
	loc    *time.Location
	cron   string
	simple *cronexpr.Simple
}

func compileTimetable(mode Mode, cron string, simple *cronexpr.Simple, tz string, now time.Time) (timetable, error) {
	var tt timetable
	switch mode {
	case ModeCron:
		tt.cron = strings.TrimSpace(cron)
	case ModeSimple:
		if simple == nil {
			return tt, fmt.Errorf("%w: simple mode needs its form input", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		c, err := simple.Compile()
		if err != nil {
			return tt, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrInvalidInput, err)
		}
		s := *simple
		tt.cron, tt.simple = c, &s
	default:
		return tt, fmt.Errorf("%w: mode must be cron or simple", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	expr, err := cronexpr.Parse(tt.cron)
	if err != nil {
		return tt, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrInvalidInput, err)
	}
	if tz == "" || tz == "Local" {
		return tt, fmt.Errorf("%w: timezone must be an IANA name such as Europe/Berlin or UTC", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	loc, err := time.LoadLocation(tz)
	if err != nil {
		return tt, fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown timezone %q", ErrInvalidInput, tz)
	}
	if expr.Next(now, loc).IsZero() {
		return tt, fmt.Errorf("%w: the expression never fires", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	tt.expr, tt.loc = expr, loc
	return tt, nil
}

func normalizeName(name string) (string, error) {
	n := strings.TrimSpace(name)
	if n == "" || utf8.RuneCountInString(n) > maxNameRunes {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: name must be 1-%d characters", ErrInvalidInput, maxNameRunes)
	}
	for _, r := range n {
		if unicode.IsControl(r) {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: name must not contain control characters", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
	}
	return n, nil
}

func normalizeGuards(g Guards) (Guards, error) {
	switch g.Overlap {
	case "":
		g.Overlap = OverlapSkip
	case OverlapSkip, OverlapQueue:
	default:
		return g, fmt.Errorf("%w: guards.overlap must be skip or queue", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if g.CatchUp.Window == "" {
		g.CatchUp.Window = defaultWindow
	}
	d, err := time.ParseDuration(g.CatchUp.Window)
	if err != nil || d < minCatchUpWindow || d > maxCatchUpWindow {
		return g, fmt.Errorf("%w: guards.catch_up.window must be a duration between 1m and 24h", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	return g, nil
}

// window is the parsed catch-up window of normalized guards.
func (c CatchUp) window() time.Duration {
	d, err := time.ParseDuration(c.Window)
	if err != nil {
		return 0
	}
	return d
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `service.go`**

```go
package schedules

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/audit"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

// Store persists schedules and their runs (org-scoped).
type Store interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, s Schedule) (Schedule, error)
	Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error)
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, templateID *uuid.UUID) ([]Schedule, error)
	Update(ctx context.Context, s Schedule, revision int64) (Schedule, error)
	SetPaused(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, paused bool, next *time.Time) (Schedule, error)
	PauseAll(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (int, error)
	Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	RecordRun(ctx context.Context, r Run) error
	Runs(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Run, error)
	LatestRuns(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]Run, error)
}

// TemplateChecker is the slice of templates.Service overrides need.
type TemplateChecker interface {
	ValidateOverrides(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, in templates.Overrides, opt templates.OverrideOptions) (templates.Overrides, error)
	RevealOverrides(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, stored templates.Overrides, aad []byte) (templates.Overrides, error)
}

// RunEnqueuer is the slice of jobs.Service a schedule fires through.
type RunEnqueuer interface {
	EnqueueRunWith(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) ([]jobs.Job, error)
	HasActiveRun(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID) (bool, error)
}

// Auditor records the scheduled runs themselves (actor schedule:<id>).
type Auditor interface {
	Record(ctx context.Context, e audit.Event) error
}

// Service manages schedules and decides due occurrences.
type Service struct {
	store Store
	tmpl  TemplateChecker
	runs  RunEnqueuer
	audit Auditor
	now   func() time.Time
	rand  io.Reader
	log   *slog.Logger
}

// NewService wires the service; now and rand are injected for tests.
func NewService(store Store, tmpl TemplateChecker, runs RunEnqueuer, auditor Auditor, now func() time.Time, rand io.Reader, log *slog.Logger) *Service {
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Service{store: store, tmpl: tmpl, runs: runs, audit: auditor, now: now, rand: rand, log: log}
}

func (s *Service) newID() uuid.UUID {
	id, err := uuid.NewV7FromReader(s.rand)
	if err != nil {
		panic(err) //nolint:forbidigo // entropy failure is unrecoverable, like uuid.Must
	}
	return id
}

// Create validates and stores a new schedule; next_run_at is computed from now.
func (s *Service) Create(ctx context.Context, orgID, actor uuid.UUID, in Input) (Schedule, error) {
	id := s.newID()
	sch, err := s.build(ctx, orgID, id, in, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	sch.CreatedBy = &actor
	return s.store.Create(ctx, sch)
}

// Update replaces a schedule when revision is still current. A paused
// schedule stays paused (next_run_at stays NULL).
func (s *Service) Update(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, in Input) (Schedule, error) {
	cur, err := s.store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	if cur.Revision != revision {
		return Schedule{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	sch, err := s.build(ctx, orgID, id, in, cur.Overrides.ExtraVars)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	sch.Paused, sch.CreatedBy, sch.LastRunAt = cur.Paused, cur.CreatedBy, cur.LastRunAt
	if cur.Paused {
		sch.NextRunAt = nil
	}
	return s.store.Update(ctx, sch, revision)
}

func (s *Service) build(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, in Input, stored map[string]any) (Schedule, error) {
	name, err := normalizeName(in.Name)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	now := s.now()
	tt, err := compileTimetable(in.Mode, in.Cron, in.Simple, in.Timezone, now)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	guards, err := normalizeGuards(in.Guards)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	ov, err := s.tmpl.ValidateOverrides(ctx, orgID, in.TemplateID, in.Overrides, templates.OverrideOptions{SealAAD: AAD(orgID, id), Stored: stored})
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, templateErr(err)
	}
	next := tt.expr.Next(now, tt.loc)
	return Schedule{
		ID: id, OrgID: orgID, TemplateID: in.TemplateID, Name: name, Mode: in.Mode,
		Cron: tt.cron, Simple: tt.simple, Timezone: in.Timezone, Guards: guards,
		Overrides: ov, NextRunAt: &next,
	}, nil
}

// templateErr keeps schema problems (the API turns them into 422 details)
// and folds everything else the template side refuses into ErrInvalidInput.
func templateErr(err error) error {
	var ve *schema.ValidationError
	switch {
	case errors.As(err, &ve):
		return err
	case errors.Is(err, templates.ErrNotFound):
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: the job template does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
	case errors.Is(err, templates.ErrInvalidInput):
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrInvalidInput, err)
	}
	return err
}

func (s *Service) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error) {
	return s.store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
}

// List returns the org's schedules (optionally one template's) and the
// latest recorded run per schedule for the list's "Last" column.
func (s *Service) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, templateID *uuid.UUID) ([]Schedule, map[uuid.UUID]Run, error) {
	list, err := s.store.List(ctx, orgID, templateID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	latest, err := s.store.LatestRuns(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	return list, latest, nil
}

func (s *Service) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.store.Delete(ctx, orgID, id)
}

// Pause stops the schedule; next_run_at is cleared.
func (s *Service) Pause(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error) {
	return s.store.SetPaused(ctx, orgID, id, true, nil)
}

// Resume restarts the schedule from now: a long pause never replays.
func (s *Service) Resume(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error) {
	cur, err := s.store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	tt, err := compileTimetable(ModeCron, cur.Cron, nil, cur.Timezone, s.now())
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	next := tt.expr.Next(s.now(), tt.loc)
	return s.store.SetPaused(ctx, orgID, id, false, &next)
}

// PauseAll pauses every active schedule of the org and returns how many.
func (s *Service) PauseAll(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (int, error) {
	return s.store.PauseAll(ctx, orgID)
}

// RunNow fires the schedule once, outside its timetable, as the operator.
func (s *Service) RunNow(ctx context.Context, orgID, id, actor uuid.UUID) ([]jobs.Job, error) {
	sch, err := s.store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	now := s.now()
	run := Run{ID: s.newID(), OrgID: orgID, ScheduleID: id, DueAt: now, DecidedAt: now}
	created, err := s.enqueue(ctx, sch, &actor)
	if err != nil {
		run.Outcome, run.Reason = OutcomeFailed, failureReason(err)
	} else {
		run.Outcome, run.Reason, run.JobGroupID = OutcomeTriggered, "run now", &created[0].RunGroupID
	}
	if rerr := s.store.RecordRun(ctx, run); rerr != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("record run: %w", rerr)
	}
	return created, err
}

// Runs lists the newest recorded runs of one schedule.
func (s *Service) Runs(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Run, error) {
	if _, err := s.store.Get(ctx, orgID, id); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return s.store.Runs(ctx, orgID, id, limit)
}

// Preview compiles an editor's input and lists the next five occurrences.
func (s *Service) Preview(in PreviewInput) (Preview, error) {
	now := s.now()
	tt, err := compileTimetable(in.Mode, in.Cron, in.Simple, in.Timezone, now)
	if err != nil {
		return Preview{}, err
	}
	return Preview{Cron: tt.cron, Description: describe(tt), Next: tt.expr.NextN(now, tt.loc, 5)}, nil
}

func describe(tt timetable) string {
	if tt.simple != nil {
		return tt.simple.Describe()
	}
	return cronexpr.Describe(tt.cron)
}

// enqueue reveals the schedule's overrides and enqueues one run with
// trigger schedule / trigger_ref schedule id, inside the schedule's org
// scope (the ticker's context carries none).
func (s *Service) enqueue(ctx context.Context, sch Schedule, actor *uuid.UUID) ([]jobs.Job, error) {
	ctx = db.WithOrgScope(ctx, sch.OrgID)
	var ov *jobs.RunOverrides
	if !sch.Overrides.Empty() {
		revealed, err := s.tmpl.RevealOverrides(ctx, sch.OrgID, sch.TemplateID, sch.Overrides, AAD(sch.OrgID, sch.ID))
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reveal overrides: %w", err)
		}
		ov = &jobs.RunOverrides{Limit: revealed.Limit, ExtraVars: revealed.ExtraVars}
	}
	ref := sch.ID
	return s.runs.EnqueueRunWith(ctx, sch.OrgID, sch.TemplateID, jobs.RunRequest{
		Trigger: jobs.TriggerSchedule, TriggerRef: &ref, Actor: actor, Overrides: ov,
	})
}

// failureReason is the generic, value-free reason a failed run records;
// the detail goes to the manager log.
func failureReason(err error) string {
	var ve *schema.ValidationError
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrEmptyScope):
		return "the template's scope resolves to no host"
	case errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrHostKeyChanged):
		return "every targeted host has a changed host key"
	case errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrNotFound), errors.Is(err, templates.ErrNotFound):
		return "the job template no longer exists"
	case errors.As(err, &ve), errors.Is(err, templates.ErrInvalidInput):
		return "the schedule's overrides no longer match the template"
	}
	return "could not enqueue the run (see the manager log)"
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement `store_sql.go`**

```go
package schedules

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// SQLStore is the org-scoped schedule store.
type SQLStore struct{ q *gen.Queries }

// NewSQLStore builds the store on *db.DB so every statement runs in the
// request's org scope.
func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore { return &SQLStore{q: gen.New(d)} }

func (st *SQLStore) Create(ctx context.Context, s Schedule) (Schedule, error) {
	simple, guards, vars, err := encodeJSON(s)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	row, err := st.q.CreateSchedule(ctx, gen.CreateScheduleParams{
		ID: s.ID, OrgID: s.OrgID, TemplateID: s.TemplateID, Name: s.Name, Mode: string(s.Mode), Cron: s.Cron,
		Simple: simple, Timezone: s.Timezone, Guards: guards, LimitOverride: s.Overrides.Limit, ExtraVars: vars,
		NextRunAt: db.NullTime(s.NextRunAt), CreatedBy: db.NullUUID(s.CreatedBy),
	})
	if db.IsUniqueViolation(err) {
		return Schedule{}, ErrNameTaken
	}
	if db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err) {
		return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: the job template does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("create schedule: %w", err)
	}
	return toSchedule(row)
}

func (st *SQLStore) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Schedule, error) {
	row, err := st.q.GetSchedule(ctx, gen.GetScheduleParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Schedule{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("get schedule: %w", err)
	}
	return toSchedule(row)
}

func (st *SQLStore) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, templateID *uuid.UUID) ([]Schedule, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ListSchedules(ctx, gen.ListSchedulesParams{OrgID: orgID, TemplateID: db.NullUUID(templateID)})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list schedules: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Schedule, 0, len(rows))
	for _, r := range rows {
		s, err := toSchedule(r)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out = append(out, s)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) Update(ctx context.Context, s Schedule, revision int64) (Schedule, error) {
	simple, guards, vars, err := encodeJSON(s)
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, err
	}
	row, err := st.q.UpdateSchedule(ctx, gen.UpdateScheduleParams{
		OrgID: s.OrgID, ID: s.ID, Revision: revision, TemplateID: s.TemplateID, Name: s.Name, Mode: string(s.Mode),
		Cron: s.Cron, Simple: simple, Timezone: s.Timezone, Guards: guards, LimitOverride: s.Overrides.Limit,
		ExtraVars: vars, NextRunAt: db.NullTime(s.NextRunAt),
	})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		if _, gerr := st.Get(ctx, s.OrgID, s.ID); gerr != nil {
			return Schedule{}, gerr
		}
		return Schedule{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	if db.IsUniqueViolation(err) {
		return Schedule{}, ErrNameTaken
	}
	if db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err) {
		return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: the job template does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("update schedule: %w", err)
	}
	return toSchedule(row)
}

func (st *SQLStore) SetPaused(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, paused bool, next *time.Time) (Schedule, error) {
	row, err := st.q.SetSchedulePaused(ctx, gen.SetSchedulePausedParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id, Paused: paused, NextRunAt: db.NullTime(next)})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Schedule{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("set schedule paused: %w", err)
	}
	return toSchedule(row)
}

func (st *SQLStore) PauseAll(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (int, error) {
	n, err := st.q.PauseAllSchedules(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("pause all schedules: %w", err)
	}
	return int(n), nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := st.q.DeleteSchedule(ctx, gen.DeleteScheduleParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete schedule: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) RecordRun(ctx context.Context, r Run) error {
	return insertRun(ctx, st.q, r)
}

func (st *SQLStore) Runs(ctx context.Context, orgID, scheduleID uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Run, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ListScheduleRuns(ctx, gen.ListScheduleRunsParams{OrgID: orgID, ScheduleID: scheduleID, Lim: int32(limit)}) //nolint:gosec // limit is clamped by the API (≤ 100)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list schedule runs: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Run, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = toRun(r)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) LatestRuns(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]Run, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.LatestScheduleRuns(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("latest schedule runs: %w", err)
	}
	out := make(map[uuid.UUID]Run, len(rows))
	for _, r := range rows {
		out[r.ScheduleID] = toRun(r)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// insertRun is shared by the org-scoped store and the ticker's bypass tx.
func insertRun(ctx context.Context, q *gen.Queries, r Run) error {
	if err := q.InsertScheduleRun(ctx, gen.InsertScheduleRunParams{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, ScheduleID: r.ScheduleID, DueAt: r.DueAt, DecidedAt: r.DecidedAt,
		Outcome: string(r.Outcome), Reason: r.Reason, JobGroupID: db.NullUUID(r.JobGroupID),
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("record schedule run: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

func encodeJSON(s Schedule) (simple, guards, vars []byte, err error) {
	if s.Simple != nil {
		if simple, err = json.Marshal(s.Simple); err != nil {
			return nil, nil, nil, err
		}
	}
	if guards, err = json.Marshal(s.Guards); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, nil, err
	}
	if len(s.Overrides.ExtraVars) > 0 {
		if vars, err = json.Marshal(s.Overrides.ExtraVars); err != nil {
			return nil, nil, nil, err
		}
	}
	return simple, guards, vars, nil
}

func toSchedule(r gen.Schedule) (Schedule, error) {
	s := Schedule{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, TemplateID: r.TemplateID, Name: r.Name, Mode: Mode(r.Mode), Cron: r.Cron,
		Timezone: r.Timezone, Paused: r.Paused, NextRunAt: db.TimePtr(r.NextRunAt), LastRunAt: db.TimePtr(r.LastRunAt),
		CreatedBy: db.UUIDPtr(r.CreatedBy), CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: r.UpdatedAt, Revision: r.Revision,
	}
	s.Overrides.Limit = r.Limit
	if len(r.Simple) > 0 {
		s.Simple = &cronexpr.Simple{}
		if err := json.Unmarshal(r.Simple, s.Simple); err != nil {
			return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("decode schedule simple: %w", err)
		}
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(r.Guards, &s.Guards); err != nil {
		return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("decode schedule guards: %w", err)
	}
	if len(r.ExtraVars) > 0 {
		if err := json.Unmarshal(r.ExtraVars, &s.Overrides.ExtraVars); err != nil {
			return Schedule{}, fmt.Errorf("decode schedule extra vars: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return s, nil
}

func toRun(r gen.ScheduleRun) Run {
	return Run{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, ScheduleID: r.ScheduleID, DueAt: r.DueAt, DecidedAt: r.DecidedAt,
		Outcome: Outcome(r.Outcome), Reason: r.Reason, JobGroupID: db.UUIDPtr(r.JobGroupID),
	}
}
```

(sqlc names the `sqlc.narg(limit_override)` parameter `LimitOverride`, and the result column of `"limit"` is `Limit`. If the generated names differ, use them. The query text is the contract.)

`internal/schedules/store_sql_test.go` (`seedTemplate` is reused by Task 6):

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

// seedTemplate creates an organization with one editor playbook and one
// job template through the raw pool (fixtures bypass RLS).
func seedTemplate(t *testing.T, d *db.DB, slug string) (org, templateID uuid.UUID) {
	t.Helper()
	q := gen.New(d.Pool())
	o, err := q.CreateOrganization(ctx, gen.CreateOrganizationParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Slug: slug, Name: slug})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	inline := "- hosts: all\n  tasks: []\n"
	pb, err := q.CreatePlaybook(ctx, gen.CreatePlaybookParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: o.ID, Inline: &inline, Source: "editor"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl, err := q.InsertTemplate(ctx, gen.InsertTemplateParams{
		ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: o.ID, Name: "deploy", PlaybookID: pb.ID,
		Scope: []byte(`{}`), ExtraVars: []byte(`{}`),
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return o.ID, tmpl.ID
}

func TestStoreRoundTripAndConstraints(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org, templateID := seedTemplate(t, d, "sched-store")
	st := schedules.NewSQLStore(d)
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org)
	next := time.Date(2026, 9, 22, 1, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	limit := "web*"
	in := schedules.Schedule{
		ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, TemplateID: templateID, Name: "Nightly", Mode: schedules.ModeSimple,
		Cron: "0 3 * * 2,4", Simple: &cronexpr.Simple{Kind: "weekly", Days: []string{"tue", "thu"}, Time: "03:00"},
		Timezone: "Europe/Berlin", Guards: schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapSkip, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}},
		Overrides: templates.Overrides{Limit: &limit, ExtraVars: map[string]any{"a": "b"}}, NextRunAt: &next,
	}
	got, err := st.Create(scoped, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.Revision != 1 || got.Simple.Days[1] != "thu" || *got.Overrides.Limit != "web*" || !got.NextRunAt.Equal(next) {
		t.Fatalf("round trip = %+v", got)
	}
	if _, err := st.Create(scoped, withID(in)); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNameTaken) {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate name: %v", err)
	}
	in2 := withID(in)
	in2.Name = "Other"
	in2.TemplateID = uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if _, err := st.Create(scoped, in2); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown template: %v", err)
	}
	got.Cron = "*/5 * * * *"
	if _, err := st.Update(scoped, got, got.Revision+1); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale: %v", err)
	}
	up, err := st.Update(scoped, got, got.Revision)
	if err != nil || up.Revision != 2 || up.Cron != "*/5 * * * *" {
		t.Fatalf("update = %+v, %v", up, err)
	}
	if n, err := st.PauseAll(scoped, org); err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("PauseAll = %d, %v", n, err)
	}
	group := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if err := st.RecordRun(scoped, schedules.Run{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, ScheduleID: got.ID, DueAt: next, DecidedAt: next, Outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, JobGroupID: &group}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	latest, err := st.LatestRuns(scoped, org)
	if err != nil || *latest[got.ID].JobGroupID != group {
		t.Fatalf("latest = %v, %v", latest, err)
	}
	paused, err := st.SetPaused(scoped, org, got.ID, false, &next)
	if err != nil || paused.Paused || !paused.NextRunAt.Equal(next) {
		t.Fatalf("SetPaused = %+v, %v", paused, err)
	}
	if _, err := st.SetPaused(scoped, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), true, nil); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("SetPaused unknown: %v", err)
	}
	runs, err := st.Runs(scoped, org, got.ID, 10)
	if err != nil || len(runs) != 1 || runs[0].Outcome != schedules.OutcomeTriggered {
		t.Fatalf("Runs = %+v, %v", runs, err)
	}
	if err := st.Delete(scoped, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("Delete unknown: %v", err)
	}
	// Deleting the template cascades to schedules and runs.
	if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM job_templates WHERE id = $1`, templateID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := st.Get(scoped, org, got.ID); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("after template delete: %v", err)
	}
}

func withID(s schedules.Schedule) schedules.Schedule {
	s.ID = uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	return s
}
```

`internal/schedules/lifecycle_test.go` covers the not-found, paused-update, error-mapping and failure-reason paths. A dry run measured 77 % without this file and 86 % with it:

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

func TestUnknownSchedulesAreNotFound(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	id := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if _, err := w.svc.Get(ctx, w.org, id); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("Get: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Update(ctx, w.org, id, 1, validInput(w.templateID)); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("Update: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Resume(ctx, w.org, id); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("Resume: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Runs(ctx, w.org, id, 10); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("Runs: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.RunNow(ctx, w.org, id, w.actor); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("RunNow: %v", err)
	}
	if err := w.svc.Delete(ctx, w.org, id); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Errorf("Delete: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestGetAndDelete(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	s, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, err := w.svc.Get(ctx, w.org, s.ID); err != nil || got.ID != s.ID {
		t.Fatalf("Get = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Get(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), s.ID); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("another org's schedule must be invisible: %v", err)
	}
	if err := w.svc.Delete(ctx, w.org, s.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestUpdateKeepsAPausedSchedulePaused(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	s, _ := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
	p, _ := w.svc.Pause(ctx, w.org, s.ID)
	up, err := w.svc.Update(ctx, w.org, s.ID, p.Revision, validInput(w.templateID))
	if err != nil || !up.Paused || up.NextRunAt != nil {
		t.Fatalf("update of a paused schedule = %+v, %v", up, err)
	}
	bad := validInput(w.templateID)
	bad.Cron = "nope"
	if _, err := w.svc.Update(ctx, w.org, s.ID, up.Revision, bad); !errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("invalid update: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestTemplateErrorsAreMapped(t *testing.T) {
	ve := &schema.ValidationError{Problems: []schema.Problem{{Path: "region", Message: "is required"}}}
	cases := []struct {
		err  error
		want error
	}{
		{ve, ve},
		{fmt.Errorf("%w: limit too long", templates.ErrInvalidInput), schedules.ErrInvalidInput},
		{errors.New("database down"), nil},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		w := newWorld(t)
		w.tmpl.validErr = c.err
		_, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
		if c.want != nil && !errors.Is(err, c.want) {
			t.Errorf("%v → %v, want %v", c.err, err, c.want)
		}
		if c.want == nil && (err == nil || errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrInvalidInput)) {
			t.Errorf("%v → %v, want the error unchanged", c.err, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestRunNowFailureReasons(t *testing.T) {
	cases := map[error]string{
		jobs.ErrHostKeyChanged: "every targeted host has a changed host key",
		jobs.ErrNotFound:       "the job template no longer exists",
		&schema.ValidationError{Problems: []schema.Problem{{Path: "x", Message: "unknown field"}}}: "the schedule's overrides no longer match the template",
		errors.New("boom"): "could not enqueue the run (see the manager log)",
	}
	for cause, want := range cases {
		w := newWorld(t)
		s, _ := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, validInput(w.templateID))
		w.runs.err = cause
		_, _ = w.svc.RunNow(ctx, w.org, s.ID, w.actor)
		runs, _ := w.svc.Runs(ctx, w.org, s.ID, 1)
		if len(runs) != 1 || runs[0].Reason != want {
			t.Errorf("%v → %+v, want reason %q", cause, runs, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestPreviewDescribesCronMode(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	p, err := w.svc.Preview(schedules.PreviewInput{Mode: schedules.ModeCron, Cron: "0 */6 * * *", Timezone: "UTC"})
	if err != nil || p.Description != "every 6 h" {
		t.Fatalf("preview = %+v, %v", p, err)
	}
	p, err = w.svc.Preview(schedules.PreviewInput{Mode: schedules.ModeCron, Cron: "0 9-17 * * 1-5", Timezone: "UTC"})
	if err != nil || p.Description != "" {
		t.Fatalf("preview of a non-simple shape = %+v, %v", p, err)
	}
}
```

### Task 6: The schedule ticker

Spec §2 "Ticker": a manager goroutine picks due schedules with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` (bypass scope, explicit org ids), applies the late, catch-up and overlap rules, enqueues, records exactly one `schedule_runs` row per decision, recomputes `next_run_at` from now and trims to 500 runs.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/schedules/decide.go` (`Service.Decide`), `internal/schedules/ticker.go` (`Ticker`), `internal/schedules/due_sql.go` (`DueSQLStore`)
- Modify: `internal/db/queries/schedules.sql` (three ticker queries), regenerated gen
- Create: `internal/app/options.go` (`Option`, `WithScheduleClock`), `internal/app/schedule_ports.go` (`scheduleTemplates` adapter, not needed if `*templates.Service` satisfies `TemplateChecker` directly; it does, so skip the file)
- Modify: `internal/app/app.go` (build `schedules.Service`, start the ticker, `stopTicker` on every exit path, `Build(ctx, cfg, opts ...Option)`)
- Test: `internal/schedules/decide_test.go`, `ticker_test.go`, `due_sql_test.go` (integration, including two concurrent tickers)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:

```go
// Decision is what the ticker writes back for one due schedule.
type Decision struct {
	Run       Run
	NextRunAt *time.Time // nil stops the schedule (unparsable stored expression)
}
func (s *Service) Decide(ctx context.Context, sch Schedule, now time.Time, grace time.Duration) Decision

type DueStore interface {
	ProcessDue(ctx context.Context, now time.Time, limit int, decide func(ctx context.Context, s Schedule) Decision) (int, error)
}
func NewDueSQLStore(d *db.DB) *DueSQLStore

const DefaultTick = 15 * time.Second
func NewTicker(svc *Service, store DueStore, tick time.Duration, now func() time.Time, sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error, log *slog.Logger) *Ticker
func (t *Ticker) Run(ctx context.Context)
func (t *Ticker) Tick(ctx context.Context) (int, error)
```

- `app.Option func(*buildOptions)` and `app.WithScheduleClock(now func() time.Time) Option`. `app.Build(ctx, cfg, opts ...Option)` keeps every existing call compiling.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the ticker queries**

Append to `internal/db/queries/schedules.sql`:

```sql
-- name: LockDueSchedules :many
-- The ticker's due scan: runs in a raw-pool transaction without
-- pulsix.org_id (bypass policy), across every organization; SKIP LOCKED
-- lets several managers tick without a leader (spec §2).
SELECT * FROM schedules
WHERE NOT paused AND next_run_at IS NOT NULL AND next_run_at <= @now::timestamptz
ORDER BY next_run_at
LIMIT @lim
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;

-- name: AdvanceSchedule :exec
-- Never touches revision or updated_at: an operator's If-Match stays valid.
UPDATE schedules SET last_run_at = @last_run_at, next_run_at = sqlc.narg(next_run_at)
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id;

-- name: TrimScheduleRuns :exec
DELETE FROM schedule_runs AS r
WHERE r.org_id = @org_id AND r.schedule_id = @schedule_id
  AND r.id NOT IN (
    SELECT k.id FROM schedule_runs AS k
    WHERE k.org_id = @org_id AND k.schedule_id = @schedule_id
    ORDER BY k.decided_at DESC, k.id DESC
    LIMIT @keep
  );
```

Run: `make sqlc`.

```go
func TestDecide(t *testing.T) {
	base := time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	skip := schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapSkip, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}}
	queue := schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapQueue, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}}
	catchUp := schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapSkip, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Enabled: true, Window: "2h"}}
	cases := []struct {
		name       string
		spec       string
		dueAt      time.Time
		now        time.Time

		active     bool
		enqueueErr error
		outcome    schedules.Outcome
		reason     string // substring
		next       string
		enqueued   bool
	}{
		{name: "on time", spec: "0 * * * *", dueAt: base, now: base.Add(5 * time.Second), guards: skip,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, next: "2026-09-18T11:00:00Z", enqueued: true},
		{name: "overlap skip", spec: "0 * * * *", dueAt: base, now: base.Add(time.Second), guards: skip, active: true,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeSkippedOverlap, reason: "still active", next: "2026-09-18T11:00:00Z"},
		{name: "overlap queue runs anyway", spec: "0 * * * *", dueAt: base, now: base.Add(time.Second), guards: queue, active: true,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, next: "2026-09-18T11:00:00Z", enqueued: true},
		{name: "late without catch-up is missed", spec: "0 * * * *", dueAt: base, now: base.Add(10 * time.Minute), guards: skip,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeMissed, reason: "1 occurrence(s) missed", next: "2026-09-18T11:00:00Z"},
		{name: "several missed collapse into one row", spec: "0 * * * *", dueAt: base, now: base.Add(5*time.Hour + 10*time.Minute), guards: skip,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeMissed, reason: "6 occurrence(s) missed", next: "2026-09-18T16:00:00Z"},
		{name: "catch-up inside the window runs once", spec: "0 * * * *", dueAt: base, now: base.Add(3*time.Hour + 10*time.Minute), guards: catchUp,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, reason: "catch-up of 4 missed occurrence(s)", next: "2026-09-18T14:00:00Z", enqueued: true},
		{name: "catch-up outside the window is missed", spec: "0 3 * * *", dueAt: base.Add(-7 * time.Hour), now: base, guards: catchUp,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeMissed, next: "2026-09-19T03:00:00Z"},
		{name: "a frequent schedule whose latest occurrence is on time runs normally", spec: "* * * * *", dueAt: base, now: base.Add(10*time.Minute + 5*time.Second), guards: skip,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeTriggered, reason: "10 earlier occurrence(s) missed", next: "2026-09-18T10:11:00Z", enqueued: true},
		{name: "enqueue failure is recorded generically", spec: "0 * * * *", dueAt: base, now: base, guards: skip, enqueueErr: jobs.ErrEmptyScope,
			outcome: schedules.OutcomeFailed, reason: "resolves to no host", next: "2026-09-18T11:00:00Z"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			w := newWorld(t)
			s := due(t, w, c.spec, c.dueAt, c.guards)
			w.runs.active, w.runs.err = c.active, c.enqueueErr
			d := w.svc.Decide(ctx, s, c.now, grace)
			if d.Run.Outcome != c.outcome || !strings.Contains(d.Run.Reason, c.reason) {
				t.Fatalf("run = %+v, want %s containing %q", d.Run, c.outcome, c.reason)
			}
			if d.NextRunAt == nil || d.NextRunAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339) != c.next {
				t.Fatalf("next = %v, want %s", d.NextRunAt, c.next)
			}
			if !d.Run.DueAt.Equal(c.dueAt) || !d.Run.DecidedAt.Equal(c.now) || d.Run.ScheduleID != s.ID || d.Run.OrgID != w.org {
				t.Fatalf("run identity = %+v", d.Run)
			}
			if got := len(w.runs.requests) == 1; got != c.enqueued {
				t.Fatalf("enqueued = %v, want %v", got, c.enqueued)
			}
			if c.enqueued {

				if req.Trigger != jobs.TriggerSchedule || *req.TriggerRef != s.ID || req.Actor != nil {
					t.Fatalf("request = %+v", req)
				}
				if d.Run.JobGroupID == nil {
					t.Fatal("a triggered run records its job group")
				}
			}
		})
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `decide.go`**

```go
package schedules

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/audit"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
)

// maxCountedOccurrences caps the missed-occurrence count of one decision.
const maxCountedOccurrences = 10000

// Decision is what the ticker writes back for one due schedule.
type Decision struct {
	Run       Run
	NextRunAt *time.Time
}

// Decide applies spec §2 steps 2–5 to one due schedule: the late /
// catch-up rule, the overlap guard, the enqueue, and the next occurrence
// computed from now (a long downtime never replays a backlog).
func (s *Service) Decide(ctx context.Context, sch Schedule, now time.Time, grace time.Duration) Decision {
	run := Run{ID: s.newID(), OrgID: sch.OrgID, ScheduleID: sch.ID, DecidedAt: now}
	if sch.NextRunAt != nil {
		run.DueAt = *sch.NextRunAt
	} else {
		run.DueAt = now
	}
	expr, err := cronexpr.Parse(sch.Cron)
	loc, lerr := time.LoadLocation(sch.Timezone)
	if err != nil || lerr != nil {
		s.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "schedule has an unusable timetable; stopping it", "schedule", sch.ID, "org", sch.OrgID)
		run.Outcome, run.Reason = OutcomeFailed, "the schedule's timetable is invalid; edit and save it"
		return Decision{Run: run}
	}
	next := expr.Next(now, loc)
	decision := Decision{Run: run, NextRunAt: &next}

	reason := ""
	if now.Sub(run.DueAt) > grace {
		count, latest := expr.Between(run.DueAt, now, loc, maxCountedOccurrences)
		if count == 0 {
			count, latest = 1, run.DueAt
		}
		switch {
		case now.Sub(latest) <= grace:
			if count > 1 {
				reason = fmt.Sprintf("%d earlier occurrence(s) missed", count-1)
			}
		case sch.Guards.CatchUp.Enabled && now.Sub(latest) <= sch.Guards.CatchUp.window():
			reason = fmt.Sprintf("catch-up of %d missed occurrence(s)", count)
		default:
			decision.Run.Outcome = OutcomeMissed
			decision.Run.Reason = fmt.Sprintf("%d occurrence(s) missed since %s", count, run.DueAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339))
			return decision
		}
	}

	if sch.Guards.Overlap != OverlapQueue {
		active, err := s.runs.HasActiveRun(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, sch.OrgID), sch.OrgID, sch.TemplateID)
		if err != nil {
			s.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "schedule overlap check failed", "schedule", sch.ID, "err", err)
			decision.Run.Outcome, decision.Run.Reason = OutcomeFailed, "could not check for an active run (see the manager log)"
			s.auditRun(ctx, sch, decision.Run)
			return decision
		}
		if active {
			decision.Run.Outcome, decision.Run.Reason = OutcomeSkippedOverlap, "a run of this template is still active"
			return decision
		}
	}

	created, err := s.enqueue(ctx, sch, nil)
	if err != nil {
		s.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "scheduled run could not be enqueued", "schedule", sch.ID, "org", sch.OrgID, "err", err)
		decision.Run.Outcome, decision.Run.Reason = OutcomeFailed, failureReason(err)
	} else {
		decision.Run.Outcome, decision.Run.Reason = OutcomeTriggered, reason
		decision.Run.JobGroupID = &created[0].RunGroupID
	}
	s.auditRun(ctx, sch, decision.Run)
	return decision
}

// auditRun records a triggered or failed scheduled run with actor
// schedule:<id>; the creator and template ride in meta (spec §2 "Actor").
// An audit failure is logged, never fatal: the run already happened.
func (s *Service) auditRun(ctx context.Context, sch Schedule, run Run) {
	meta := map[string]any{
		"schedule_id": sch.ID.String(), "template_id": sch.TemplateID.String(), "outcome": string(run.Outcome),
	}
	if sch.CreatedBy != nil {
		meta["created_by"] = sch.CreatedBy.String()
	}
	if run.JobGroupID != nil {
		meta["job_group_id"] = run.JobGroupID.String()
	}
	orgID, actor := sch.OrgID, sch.ID
	if err := s.audit.Record(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, orgID), audit.Event{
		OrgID: &orgID, ActorKind: audit.ActorKindSchedule, ActorID: &actor,
		Action: "schedule.run", ObjectType: "job_template", ObjectID: sch.TemplateID.String(),
		Summary: fmt.Sprintf("schedule %q %s", sch.Name, run.Outcome), Meta: meta,
	}); err != nil {
		s.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "audit scheduled run", "schedule", sch.ID, "err", err)
	}
}
```

Check the case table against this logic before running it:
- "several missed collapse into one row": due 10:00, now 15:10. The occurrences are 10:00 through 15:00, so count = 6 and latest = 15:00. 15:10 − 15:00 = 10 min > grace, catch-up is disabled, so the result is missed with "6 occurrence(s) missed" and next = 16:00.
- "catch-up inside the window runs once": due 10:00, now 13:10, count = 4, latest = 13:00. 10 min ≤ 2 h, so the run is triggered with reason "catch-up of 4 missed occurrence(s)" and next = 14:00.

`internal/schedules/ticker_test.go`:

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

type fakeDue struct {
	mu        sync.Mutex
	due       []schedules.Schedule
	decisions []schedules.Decision
	nowSeen   []time.Time
	limitSeen int
	err       error
}

func (f *fakeDue) ProcessDue(ctx context.Context, now time.Time, limit int, decide func(context.Context, schedules.Schedule) schedules.Decision) (int, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.nowSeen, f.limitSeen = append(f.nowSeen, now), limit
	if f.err != nil {
		return 0, f.err
	}
	for _, s := range f.due {
		f.decisions = append(f.decisions, decide(ctx, s))
	}
	n := len(f.due)
	f.due = nil
	return n, nil
}

func TestTickerTickUsesTheInjectedClockAndGrace(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	base := time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	s := due(t, w, "0 * * * *", base, schedules.Guards{})
	store := &fakeDue{due: []schedules.Schedule{s}}
	now := base.Add(40 * time.Second)
	tk := schedules.NewTicker(w.svc, store, 15*time.Second, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
	n, err := tk.Tick(ctx)
	if err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Tick = %d, %v", n, err)
	}
	// 40 s late with a 15 s tick exceeds the 30 s grace: missed.
	if store.decisions[0].Run.Outcome != schedules.OutcomeMissed || !store.nowSeen[0].Equal(now) || store.limitSeen != 50 {
		t.Fatalf("decision = %+v, now %v, limit %d", store.decisions[0], store.nowSeen, store.limitSeen)
	}
}

func TestTickerRunStopsOnCancel(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	store := &fakeDue{err: errors.New("db down")}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	ticks := 0
	sleep := func(ctx context.Context, _ time.Duration) error {
		ticks++
		if ticks == 3 {
			cancel()
		}
		return ctx.Err()
	}
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		schedules.NewTicker(w.svc, store, time.Second, time.Now, sleep, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))).Run(ctx)
		close(done)
	}()
	select {
	case <-done:
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("Run did not stop after cancellation")
	}
	if len(store.nowSeen) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("ticks = %d, want 3 (errors keep the loop alive)", len(store.nowSeen))
	}
}
```

`internal/schedules/due_sql_test.go` (integration):

```go
package schedules_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

// TestTwoTickersDecideEachOccurrenceOnce: 20 due schedules, two tickers
// over two stores ticking concurrently — every schedule gets exactly one
// run row and one enqueue; next_run_at moves past now.
func TestTwoTickersDecideEachOccurrenceOnce(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org, templateID := seedTemplate(t, d, "sched-concurrent")
	st := schedules.NewSQLStore(d)
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org)
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	dueAt := now.Add(-5 * time.Second)
	for i := 0; i < 20; i++ {
		s := schedules.Schedule{
			ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, TemplateID: templateID, Name: "s" + uuid.NewString()[:8],
			Mode: schedules.ModeCron, Cron: "0 * * * *", Timezone: "UTC",
			Guards: schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapQueue, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}}, NextRunAt: &dueAt,
		}
		if _, err := st.Create(scoped, s); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	runs := &fakeRuns{}
	svc := schedules.NewService(st, &fakeTemplates{}, runs, &fakeAudit{}, func() time.Time { return now }, rand.Reader, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		tk := schedules.NewTicker(svc, schedules.NewDueSQLStore(d), 15*time.Second, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			for j := 0; j < 3; j++ {
				if _, err := tk.Tick(context.Background()); err != nil {
					t.Error(err)
				}
			}
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	if len(runs.requests) != 20 {
		t.Fatalf("enqueues = %d, want 20", len(runs.requests))
	}
	var count int
	if err := d.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM schedule_runs WHERE org_id = $1`, org).Scan(&count); err != nil || count != 20 {
		t.Fatalf("runs = %d, %v", count, err)
	}
	list, _ := st.List(scoped, org, nil)
	for _, s := range list {
		if !s.NextRunAt.Equal(time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 11, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)) || s.LastRunAt == nil || s.Revision != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("schedule after tick = %+v", s)
		}
	}
}

		Mode: schedules.ModeCron, Cron: "* * * * *", Timezone: "UTC", Guards: schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.OverlapQueue, CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Window: "1h"}}, NextRunAt: &now})

			t.Fatal(err)
```
