```go
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `ticker.go` and `due_sql.go`**

`internal/schedules/ticker.go`:

```go
package schedules

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"time"
)

// DefaultTick is the ticker interval when none is configured; batchSize
// bounds one transaction; keepRuns is the per-schedule run retention.
const (
	DefaultTick = 15 * time.Second
	batchSize   = 50
	keepRuns    = 500
)

// DueStore locks due schedules and writes back one decision each.
type DueStore interface {
	ProcessDue(ctx context.Context, now time.Time, limit int, decide func(ctx context.Context, s Schedule) Decision) (int, error)
}

// Ticker fires due schedules. Several managers may run one each.
type Ticker struct {
	svc   *Service
	store DueStore
	tick  time.Duration
	now   func() time.Time
	sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error
	log   *slog.Logger
}

// NewTicker builds the ticker; nil sleep waits on the wall clock, nil log
// is slog.Default, a zero tick is DefaultTick.
func NewTicker(svc *Service, store DueStore, tick time.Duration, now func() time.Time, sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error, log *slog.Logger) *Ticker {
	if tick <= 0 {
		tick = DefaultTick
	}
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	if sleep == nil {
		sleep = func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error {
			select {
			case <-ctx.Done():
				return ctx.Err()
			case <-time.After(d):
				return nil
			}
		}
	}
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Ticker{svc: svc, store: store, tick: tick, now: now, sleep: sleep, log: log}
}

// Run ticks until ctx is cancelled; a failed tick is logged and retried.
func (t *Ticker) Run(ctx context.Context) {
	for {
		if _, err := t.Tick(ctx); err != nil {
			t.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "schedule tick failed", "err", err)
		}
		if err := t.sleep(ctx, t.tick); err != nil {
			return
		}
	}
}

// Tick decides every due schedule, one locked batch at a time, until a
// batch comes back smaller than batchSize.
func (t *Ticker) Tick(ctx context.Context) (int, error) {
	total := 0
	for {
		now := t.now()
		n, err := t.store.ProcessDue(ctx, now, batchSize, func(ctx context.Context, s Schedule) Decision {
			return t.svc.Decide(ctx, s, now, 2*t.tick)
		})
		total += n
		if err != nil || n < batchSize {
			return total, err
		}
	}
}
```

`internal/schedules/due_sql.go`:

```go
package schedules

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// DueSQLStore is the ticker's store: one raw-pool transaction per batch,
// with no pulsix.org_id bound — the bypass policy applies, so the due scan
// sees every organization, and every other statement names its org_id.
// decide runs while the rows are locked; its enqueue uses the normal
// org-scoped services on their own connections.
type DueSQLStore struct{ d *db.DB }

// NewDueSQLStore builds the store.
func NewDueSQLStore(d *db.DB) *DueSQLStore { return &DueSQLStore{d: d} }

// ProcessDue implements DueStore.
func (st *DueSQLStore) ProcessDue(ctx context.Context, now time.Time, limit int, decide func(ctx context.Context, s Schedule) Decision) (int, error) {
	tx, err := st.d.Pool().Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("begin schedule tick: %w", err)
	}
	defer tx.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx)) //nolint:errcheck // no-op once committed
	q := gen.New(tx)
	rows, err := q.LockDueSchedules(ctx, gen.LockDueSchedulesParams{Now: now, Lim: int32(limit)}) //nolint:gosec // limit is batchSize
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("lock due schedules: %w", err)
	}
	for _, r := range rows {
		sch, err := toSchedule(r)
		if err != nil {
			return 0, err
		}
		d := decide(ctx, sch)
		if err := insertRun(ctx, q, d.Run); err != nil {
			return 0, err
		}
		if err := q.AdvanceSchedule(ctx, gen.AdvanceScheduleParams{
			OrgID: sch.OrgID, ID: sch.ID, LastRunAt: db.NullTime(&d.Run.DecidedAt), NextRunAt: db.NullTime(d.NextRunAt),
		}); err != nil {
			return 0, fmt.Errorf("advance schedule: %w", err)
		}
		if err := q.TrimScheduleRuns(ctx, gen.TrimScheduleRunsParams{OrgID: sch.OrgID, ScheduleID: sch.ID, Keep: keepRuns}); err != nil {
			return 0, fmt.Errorf("trim schedule runs: %w", err)
		}
	}
	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("commit schedule tick: %w", err)
	}
	return len(rows), nil
}
```

(`last_run_at` is nullable, so `AdvanceScheduleParams.LastRunAt` is `pgtype.Timestamptz`. Match the generated types.)

```go
// buildOptions are test seams for Build; production passes none.
type buildOptions struct {
	scheduleNow func() time.Time
}

// Option customizes Build.
type Option func(*buildOptions)

// WithScheduleClock injects the schedule ticker's clock (the milestone
// E2E fires a schedule without waiting for wall-clock time).
func WithScheduleClock(now func() time.Time) Option {
	return func(o *buildOptions) { o.scheduleNow = now }
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5/6 `schedules.Service`, `jobStart(created)` / `jobStartOutput` from `job_handlers.go`, `requireIfMatch`/`revisionETag` from `ifmatch.go`, `requireOrg`, `tenancy.Allows`.
- Produces routes. Each write carries `withAudit(op, "schedule")`, and every route answers 404 for an unknown or foreign id.

| Method | Path | Permission | operationID | Success |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules?template_id=` | templates.read | — | 200 `{schedules: [ScheduleDTO]}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules` | templates.write | createSchedule | 201 `{schedule}` + `ETag` |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/preview` | templates.read | previewSchedule (allowlisted) | 200 `{cron, description, next: [5]}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/pause-all` | templates.write | pauseAllSchedules | 200 `{paused: n}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}` | templates.read | — | 200 `{schedule}` + `ETag` |
| PUT | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}` | templates.write, If-Match required | updateSchedule | 200 + `ETag` |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}` | templates.write | deleteSchedule | 200 `{}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/pause` | templates.write | pauseSchedule | 200 `{schedule}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/resume` | templates.write | resumeSchedule | 200 `{schedule}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/run` | templates.write **and** jobs.run | runSchedule | 202 `{jobs: [JobDTO]}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/runs?limit=` (1–100, default 50) | templates.read | — | 200 `{runs: [ScheduleRunDTO]}` |

- Errors: `ErrInvalidInput` → 400; `*schema.ValidationError` → 422 with details at `body.extra_vars.<path>`; `ErrNameTaken` → 409; `ErrRevisionMismatch` → 412; missing If-Match → 428; `ErrNotFound` → 404. For run now, `jobs.ErrEmptyScope` → 422 and `jobs.ErrHostKeyChanged` → 409, the same wording as the run route.
- `schedules.MaskSealed(vars map[string]any) map[string]any` replaces every sealed envelope at any depth with `schema.Mask()`. Reads never return sealed bytes.

```go
func TestMaskSealed(t *testing.T) {
	in := map[string]any{
		"plain":  "v",
		"token":  schema.Sealed([]byte("ct")),
		"nested": map[string]any{"pw": schema.Sealed([]byte("x")), "n": float64(1)},
		"list":   []any{schema.Sealed([]byte("y")), "z"},
	}
	want := map[string]any{
		"plain":  "v",
		"token":  schema.Mask(),
		"nested": map[string]any{"pw": schema.Mask(), "n": float64(1)},
		"list":   []any{schema.Mask(), "z"},
	}
	if got := schedules.MaskSealed(in); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("MaskSealed = %#v", got)
	}
	if _, sealed := schema.SealedBytes(in["token"]); !sealed {
		t.Fatal("MaskSealed must not modify its input")
	}
	if schedules.MaskSealed(nil) != nil {
		t.Fatal("nil stays nil")

}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `internal/schedules/mask.go`**

```go
package schedules

import "github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"

// MaskSealed returns a copy of vars with every sealed envelope, at any
// depth, replaced by the {"$secret": "set"} marker reads show.
func MaskSealed(vars map[string]any) map[string]any {
	if vars == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out, _ := maskValue(vars).(map[string]any)
	return out
}

func maskValue(v any) any {
	if _, sealed := schema.SealedBytes(v); sealed {
		return schema.Mask()
	}
	switch x := v.(type) {
	case map[string]any:
		out := make(map[string]any, len(x))
		for k, e := range x {
			out[k] = maskValue(e)
		}
		return out
	case []any:
		out := make([]any, len(x))
		for i, e := range x {
			out[i] = maskValue(e)
		}
		return out
	}
	return v
}
```

Run: `go test -race -timeout 900s -run TestMaskSealed ./internal/schedules/...`

```go
func TestScheduleLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "sched-life")
	c := f.operatorC

	resp := postJSON(t, c, f.schedulesURL(), f.scheduleBody("Nightly OS patching"))
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	etag := resp.Header.Get("ETag")
	var created struct{ Schedule scheduleOut `json:"schedule"` }
	decodeInto(t, resp, &created)
	s := created.Schedule
	if s.Cron != "0 3 * * 2,4" || s.Description != "Tue, Thu at 03:00" || s.NextRunAt == nil || etag != `"1"` ||
		s.Guards.Overlap != "skip" || s.Guards.CatchUp.Window != "1h" {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v, etag %q", s, etag)
	}

	// Update needs If-Match: missing is 428, stale is 412.
	up := f.scheduleBody("Nightly OS patching")
	up["mode"], up["cron"] = "cron", "*/30 * * * *"
	delete(up, "simple")
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodPut, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID, up); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionRequired {
		t.Fatalf("missing If-Match: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, c, http.MethodPut, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID, up, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"7"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionFailed {
		t.Fatalf("stale If-Match: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp = requestWithHeaders(t, c, http.MethodPut, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID, up, map[string]string{"If-Match": etag})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || resp.Header.Get("ETag") != `"2"` {
		t.Fatalf("update: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}

	// Pause, resume, run now, runs.
	if resp := postJSON(t, c, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID+"/pause", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := postJSON(t, c, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID+"/resume", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	resp = postJSON(t, c, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID+"/run", nil)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusAccepted {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	var started struct {
		Jobs []struct {
			Trigger    string `json:"trigger"`
			TriggerRef string `json:"trigger_ref"`
		} `json:"jobs"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &started)
	if len(started.Jobs) != 1 || started.Jobs[0].Trigger != "schedule" || started.Jobs[0].TriggerRef != s.ID {
		t.Fatalf("run now = %+v", started)
	}
	var runs struct {
		Runs []struct {
			Outcome string `json:"outcome"`
			Reason  string `json:"reason"`
		} `json:"runs"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID+"/runs", nil), &runs)
	if len(runs.Runs) != 1 || runs.Runs[0].Outcome != "triggered" || runs.Runs[0].Reason != "run now" {
		t.Fatalf("runs = %+v", runs)
	}

	// The list filters by template and carries the latest run.
	var list struct {
		Schedules []struct {
			ID      string `json:"id"`
			LastRun *struct {
				Outcome string `json:"outcome"`
			} `json:"last_run"`
		} `json:"schedules"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, f.schedulesURL()+"?template_id="+f.templateID.String(), nil), &list)
	if len(list.Schedules) != 1 || list.Schedules[0].LastRun == nil || list.Schedules[0].LastRun.Outcome != "triggered" {
		t.Fatalf("list = %+v", list)
	}

	var paused struct{ Paused int `json:"paused"` }
	decodeInto(t, postJSON(t, c, f.schedulesURL()+"/pause-all", nil), &paused)
	if paused.Paused != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("pause-all = %+v", paused)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodDelete, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, f.schedulesURL()+"/"+s.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("after delete: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	for _, action := range []string{"POST createSchedule", "PUT updateSchedule", "POST pauseSchedule", "POST resumeSchedule", "POST runSchedule", "POST pauseAllSchedules", "DELETE deleteSchedule"} {
		assertActivityContains(t, f, action)
	}
}

func TestScheduleValidationAndPermissions(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "sched-perm")
	// Viewer reads, never writes; nobody outside the org sees anything.
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodGet, f.schedulesURL(), nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {

		t.Fatalf("viewer create: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	bad := f.scheduleBody("bad")
	bad["timezone"] = "Mars/Olympus"

		t.Fatalf("bad timezone: %d", resp.StatusCode)

	bad["extra_vars"] = map[string]any{"ansible_ssh_common_args": "-o X"}
	resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.schedulesURL(), bad)
	if body := mustReadBody(t, resp); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity || !strings.Contains(body, "body.extra_vars.ansible_ssh_common_args") {
		t.Fatalf("denylisted override: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
	}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.schedulesURL(), f.scheduleBody("dup")); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.schedulesURL(), f.scheduleBody("dup")); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate name: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}

// assertActivityContains fails when the org's activity feed (read by the
// admin) has no event whose action is action.
func assertActivityContains(t *testing.T, f runFixture, action string) {
	t.Helper()
	body := mustReadBody(t, requestJSON(t, f.adminC, http.MethodGet, f.s.HTTP.URL+"/api/v1/orgs/"+f.orgSlug+"/activity?limit=100", nil))
	if !strings.Contains(body, `"`+action+`"`) {
		t.Fatalf("activity lacks %q", action)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the shared problems helper**

`internal/api/problems.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

// schemaProblems turns schema problems into a 422 whose details are
// located under location (body.extra_vars for overrides), one per problem.
func schemaProblems(ve *schema.ValidationError, location string) error {
	details := make([]error, len(ve.Problems))
	for i, p := range ve.Problems {
		loc := location
		if p.Path != "" {
			loc += "." + p.Path
		}
		details[i] = &huma.ErrorDetail{Location: loc, Message: p.Message}
	}
	return huma.Error422UnprocessableEntity("validation failed", details...)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the DTOs**

`internal/api/schedule_dto.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
)

type simpleDTO struct {
	Kind  string   `json:"kind" enum:"every_minutes,every_hours,daily,weekly,monthly"`
	Every int      `json:"every,omitempty"`
	Time  string   `json:"time,omitempty" doc:"HH:MM in the schedule's time zone"`
	Days  []string `json:"days,omitempty" doc:"weekly: mon..sun"`
	Day   int      `json:"day,omitempty" doc:"monthly: 1-31; months without that day are skipped"`
}

type catchUpDTO struct {
	Enabled bool   `json:"enabled"`
	Window  string `json:"window,omitempty" doc:"Go duration, 1m to 24h (default 1h)"`
}

type guardsDTO struct {
	Overlap string     `json:"overlap,omitempty" enum:"skip,queue" doc:"skip (default) records skipped_overlap while a run of the template is active"`
	CatchUp catchUpDTO `json:"catch_up,omitempty"`
}

type scheduleRunDTO struct {
	ID         string    `json:"id"`
	DueAt      time.Time `json:"due_at"`
	DecidedAt  time.Time `json:"decided_at"`
	Outcome    string    `json:"outcome" enum:"triggered,skipped_overlap,missed,failed"`
	Reason     string    `json:"reason,omitempty"`
	JobGroupID string    `json:"job_group_id,omitempty"`
}

type scheduleDTO struct {
	ID          string          `json:"id"`
	TemplateID  string          `json:"template_id"`
	Name        string          `json:"name"`
	Mode        string          `json:"mode" enum:"cron,simple"`
	Cron        string          `json:"cron"`
	Description string          `json:"description" doc:"Human text for simple shapes; empty otherwise"`
	Simple      *simpleDTO      `json:"simple,omitempty"`
	Timezone    string          `json:"timezone"`
	Guards      guardsDTO       `json:"guards"`
	Limit       *string         `json:"limit,omitempty"`
	ExtraVars   map[string]any  `json:"extra_vars,omitempty" doc:"Overrides; secret values read as {\"$secret\":\"set\"}"`
	Paused      bool            `json:"paused"`
	NextRunAt   *time.Time      `json:"next_run_at,omitempty"`
	LastRunAt   *time.Time      `json:"last_run_at,omitempty"`
	LastRun     *scheduleRunDTO `json:"last_run,omitempty"`
	Revision    int64           `json:"revision"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt   time.Time       `json:"updated_at"`
}

// ScheduleBody is the create/update body (exported so huma flattens it,
// like TemplateBody).
type ScheduleBody struct {
	TemplateID string         `json:"template_id" format:"uuid"`
	Name       string         `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"100"`
	Mode       string         `json:"mode" enum:"cron,simple"`
	Cron       string         `json:"cron,omitempty" maxLength:"200"`
	Simple     *simpleDTO     `json:"simple,omitempty"`
	Timezone   string         `json:"timezone" minLength:"1" maxLength:"64"`
	Guards     *guardsDTO     `json:"guards,omitempty"`
	Limit      *string        `json:"limit,omitempty" maxLength:"500"`
	ExtraVars  map[string]any `json:"extra_vars,omitempty"`
}

func toScheduleDTO(s schedules.Schedule, last *schedules.Run) scheduleDTO {
	dto := scheduleDTO{
		ID: s.ID.String(), TemplateID: s.TemplateID.String(), Name: s.Name, Mode: string(s.Mode),
		Cron: s.Cron, Description: cronexpr.Describe(s.Cron), Timezone: s.Timezone,
		Guards:   guardsDTO{Overlap: string(s.Guards.Overlap), CatchUp: catchUpDTO{Enabled: s.Guards.CatchUp.Enabled, Window: s.Guards.CatchUp.Window}},
		Limit:    s.Overrides.Limit, ExtraVars: schedules.MaskSealed(s.Overrides.ExtraVars),
		Paused:   s.Paused, NextRunAt: s.NextRunAt, LastRunAt: s.LastRunAt,
		Revision: s.Revision, CreatedAt: s.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: s.UpdatedAt,
	}
	if s.Simple != nil {
		dto.Simple = &simpleDTO{Kind: s.Simple.Kind, Every: s.Simple.Every, Time: s.Simple.Time, Days: s.Simple.Days, Day: s.Simple.Day}
		dto.Description = s.Simple.Describe()
	}
	if last != nil {
		r := toScheduleRunDTO(*last)
		dto.LastRun = &r
	}
	return dto
}

func toScheduleRunDTO(r schedules.Run) scheduleRunDTO {
	dto := scheduleRunDTO{ID: r.ID.String(), DueAt: r.DueAt, DecidedAt: r.DecidedAt, Outcome: string(r.Outcome), Reason: r.Reason}
	if r.JobGroupID != nil {
		dto.JobGroupID = r.JobGroupID.String()
	}
	return dto
}

func (b ScheduleBody) simple() *cronexpr.Simple {
	if b.Simple == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return &cronexpr.Simple{Kind: b.Simple.Kind, Every: b.Simple.Every, Time: b.Simple.Time, Days: b.Simple.Days, Day: b.Simple.Day}
}

func (b ScheduleBody) guards() schedules.Guards {
	if b.Guards == nil {
		return schedules.Guards{}
	}
	return schedules.Guards{Overlap: schedules.Overlap(b.Guards.Overlap), CatchUp: schedules.CatchUp{Enabled: b.Guards.CatchUp.Enabled, Window: b.Guards.CatchUp.Window}}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the handlers**

`internal/api/schedule_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"
	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/cronexpr"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/schedules"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type scheduleListInput struct {
	Org        string `path:"org"`
	TemplateID string `query:"template_id" doc:"Only this template's schedules"`
}

type scheduleListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Schedules []scheduleDTO `json:"schedules"`
	}
}

type scheduleIDInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type scheduleCreateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body ScheduleBody
}

type scheduleUpdateInput struct {
	Org     string `path:"org"`
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	IfMatch string `header:"If-Match" doc:"The schedule's ETag; required"`
	Body    ScheduleBody
}

type scheduleOutput struct {
	ETag string `header:"ETag"`
	Body struct {
		Schedule scheduleDTO `json:"schedule"`
	}
}

type schedulePreviewInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body struct {
		Mode     string     `json:"mode" enum:"cron,simple"`
		Cron     string     `json:"cron,omitempty" maxLength:"200"`
		Simple   *simpleDTO `json:"simple,omitempty"`
		Timezone string     `json:"timezone" minLength:"1" maxLength:"64"`
	}
}

type schedulePreviewOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Cron        string      `json:"cron"`
		Description string      `json:"description"`
		Next        []time.Time `json:"next"`
	}
}

type schedulePauseAllOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Paused int `json:"paused"`
	}
}

type scheduleRunsInput struct {
	Org   string `path:"org"`
	ID    string `path:"id"`
	Limit int    `query:"limit" minimum:"1" maximum:"100" default:"50"`
}

type scheduleRunsOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Runs []scheduleRunDTO `json:"runs"`
	}
}

// registerScheduleRoutes wires spec §2's schedules: templates.read to see,
// templates.write to manage, plus jobs.run for "run now".
func registerScheduleRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules", func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleListInput) (*scheduleListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermTemplatesRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		tmplID, err := optionalUUID(in.TemplateID, "template id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, latest, err := deps.Schedules.List(ctx, scope.OrgID, tmplID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &scheduleListOutput{}
		out.Body.Schedules = make([]scheduleDTO, len(list))
		for i, s := range list {
			var last *schedules.Run
			if r, ok := latest[s.ID]; ok {
				last = &r
			}
			out.Body.Schedules[i] = toScheduleDTO(s, last)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules", func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleCreateInput) (*scheduleOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermTemplatesWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		input, err := scheduleInput(in.Body)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		s, err := deps.Schedules.Create(ctx, scope.OrgID, scope.UserID, input)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, s.ID.String(), "created schedule "+s.Name)
		return scheduleOut(s), nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusCreated }, withAudit("createSchedule", "schedule"))

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/preview", func(ctx context.Context, in *schedulePreviewInput) (*schedulePreviewOutput, error) {
		if _, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermTemplatesRead); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		var simple *cronexpr.Simple
		if in.Body.Simple != nil {
			simple = &cronexpr.Simple{Kind: in.Body.Simple.Kind, Every: in.Body.Simple.Every, Time: in.Body.Simple.Time, Days: in.Body.Simple.Days, Day: in.Body.Simple.Day}
		}
		p, err := deps.Schedules.Preview(schedules.PreviewInput{Mode: schedules.Mode(in.Body.Mode), Cron: in.Body.Cron, Simple: simple, Timezone: in.Body.Timezone})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &schedulePreviewOutput{}
		out.Body.Cron, out.Body.Description, out.Body.Next = p.Cron, p.Description, p.Next
		return out, nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.OperationID = "previewSchedule" })

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/pause-all", func(ctx context.Context, in *struct {
		Org string `path:"org"`
	}) (*schedulePauseAllOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermTemplatesWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		n, err := deps.Schedules.PauseAll(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, "", "paused all schedules")
		out := &schedulePauseAllOutput{}
		out.Body.Paused = n
		return out, nil
	}, withAudit("pauseAllSchedules", "schedule"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleIDInput) (*scheduleOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := scheduleScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermTemplatesRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		s, err := deps.Schedules.Get(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		return scheduleOut(s), nil
	})

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleUpdateInput) (*scheduleOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := scheduleScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermTemplatesWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		rev, err := requireIfMatch(in.IfMatch)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		input, err := scheduleInput(in.Body)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		s, err := deps.Schedules.Update(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, rev, input)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, s.ID.String(), "updated schedule "+s.Name)
		return scheduleOut(s), nil
	}, withAudit("updateSchedule", "schedule"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleIDInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := scheduleScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermTemplatesWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Schedules.Delete(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "deleted schedule")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("deleteSchedule", "schedule"))

	for _, action := range []struct {
		path, op, summary string
		pause             bool
	}{
		{"/pause", "pauseSchedule", "paused schedule ", true},
		{"/resume", "resumeSchedule", "resumed schedule ", false},
	} {
		huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}"+action.path, func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleIDInput) (*scheduleOutput, error) {
			scope, id, err := scheduleScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermTemplatesWrite)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			var s schedules.Schedule
			if action.pause {
				s, err = deps.Schedules.Pause(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
			} else {
				s, err = deps.Schedules.Resume(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
			}
			if err != nil {
				return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
			}
			setAuditObject(ctx, s.ID.String(), action.summary+s.Name)
			return scheduleOut(s), nil
		}, withAudit(action.op, "schedule"))
	}

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/run", func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleIDInput) (*jobStartOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := scheduleScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermTemplatesWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if !tenancy.Allows(scope.Role, tenancy.PermJobsRun) {
			return nil, huma.Error403Forbidden("insufficient role")
		}
		created, err := deps.Schedules.RunNow(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, scope.UserID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "ran schedule now")
		return jobStart(created), nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusAccepted }, withAudit("runSchedule", "schedule"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/runs", func(ctx context.Context, in *scheduleRunsInput) (*scheduleRunsOutput, error) {
		scope, id, err := scheduleScope(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, tenancy.PermTemplatesRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		runs, err := deps.Schedules.Runs(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, in.Limit)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSchedulesErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &scheduleRunsOutput{}
		out.Body.Runs = make([]scheduleRunDTO, len(runs))
		for i, r := range runs {
			out.Body.Runs[i] = toScheduleRunDTO(r)
		}
		return out, nil
	})
}

func scheduleScope(ctx context.Context, deps Deps, org, rawID string, perm tenancy.Permission) (Scope, uuid.UUID, error) {
	scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, org, perm)
	if err != nil {
		return Scope{}, uuid.Nil, err
	}
	id, err := parseUUID(rawID, "schedule id")
	return scope, id, err
}

func scheduleInput(b ScheduleBody) (schedules.Input, error) {
	tmplID, err := parseUUID(b.TemplateID, "template id")
	if err != nil {
		return schedules.Input{}, err
	}
	return schedules.Input{
		TemplateID: tmplID, Name: b.Name, Mode: schedules.Mode(b.Mode), Cron: b.Cron, Simple: b.simple(),
		Timezone: b.Timezone, Guards: b.guards(),
		Overrides: templates.Overrides{Limit: b.Limit, ExtraVars: b.ExtraVars},
	}, nil
}

func scheduleOut(s schedules.Schedule) *scheduleOutput {
	out := &scheduleOutput{ETag: revisionETag(s.Revision)}
	out.Body.Schedule = toScheduleDTO(s, nil)
	return out
}

func mapSchedulesErr(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	var ve *schema.ValidationError
	switch {
	case errors.As(err, &ve):
		return schemaProblems(ve, "body.extra_vars")
	case errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrInvalidInput):
		return huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNotFound):
		return huma.Error404NotFound("not found")
	case errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrNameTaken):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("a schedule with this name already exists")
	case errors.Is(err, schedules.ErrRevisionMismatch):
		return huma.Error412PreconditionFailed("this changed since you loaded it; reload and apply your change again")
	case errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrEmptyScope):
		return huma.Error422UnprocessableEntity("the template's scope resolves to no host")
	case errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrHostKeyChanged):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("every targeted host has a changed host key; re-test and re-pin first")
	default:
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "unmapped schedules error", "err", err)
		return huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
	}
}
```

Keep the file under 400 lines. If it grows past that, move the preview, pause-all and runs handlers into `schedule_handlers_extra.go`.

The SPA has no template delete action, so the warning that deleting a template also deletes its schedules and triggers lives in the docs.

**Interfaces:**
- Hooks (query keys `['schedules', org]`, `['schedules', org, id]`, `['schedule-runs', org, id]`):

```ts
export function useSchedules(org: string, templateId?: string)
export function useSchedule(org: string, id: string)
export function useScheduleRuns(org: string, id: string)
export function useSchedulePreview(org: string, body: SchedulePreviewBody | null) // debounced by the caller; enabled when body !== null
export function useCreateSchedule(org: string)

export function useDeleteSchedule(org: string)
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `simple.ts`**

```ts
export type SimpleKind = 'every_minutes' | 'every_hours' | 'daily' | 'weekly' | 'monthly';

export interface SimpleForm {
  kind: SimpleKind;
  every: number;
  time: string;
  days: string[];
  day: number;
}

export const WEEKDAYS = ['mon', 'tue', 'wed', 'thu', 'fri', 'sat', 'sun'] as const;
export const MINUTE_STEPS = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 10, 12, 15, 20, 30];
export const HOUR_STEPS = [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12];

export const DEFAULT_SIMPLE: SimpleForm = { kind: 'daily', every: 15, time: '03:00', days: ['mon'], day: 1 };

/** toSimpleBody is the API's simple object: exactly the fields the kind
 * compiles from (internal/cronexpr Simple.Compile), days in week order. */
export function toSimpleBody(f: SimpleForm) {
  switch (f.kind) {
    case 'every_minutes':
    case 'every_hours':
      return { kind: f.kind, every: f.every };
    case 'daily':
      return { kind: f.kind, time: f.time };
    case 'weekly':
      return { kind: f.kind, days: WEEKDAYS.filter((d) => f.days.includes(d)), time: f.time };
    case 'monthly':
      return { kind: f.kind, day: f.day, time: f.time };
  }
}

export function fromSimpleBody(s: { kind: string; every?: number; time?: string; days?: string[] | null; day?: number } | undefined | null): SimpleForm {
  if (!s) return DEFAULT_SIMPLE;
  return {
    kind: s.kind as SimpleKind,
    every: s.every ?? DEFAULT_SIMPLE.every,
    time: s.time ?? DEFAULT_SIMPLE.time,
    days: s.days ?? DEFAULT_SIMPLE.days,
    day: s.day ?? DEFAULT_SIMPLE.day,
  };
}

/** relative renders a future instant the way the mockup's "Next" column
 * does: minutes under an hour, hours under two days, then days. */
export function relative(at: string | undefined | null, now: Date): string {
  if (!at) return '—';
  const mins = Math.round((new Date(at).getTime() - now.getTime()) / 60000);
  if (mins < 60) return `in ${Math.max(mins, 0)} min`;
  const hours = Math.round(mins / 60);
  if (hours < 48) return `in ${hours} h`;
  return `in ${Math.round(hours / 24)} d`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the hooks**

`web/src/api/hooks/useSchedules.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../client.ts';
import type { ScheduleInput } from '../types.ts';

const ifMatch = (revision: number) => ({ 'If-Match': `"${revision}"` });

export interface SchedulePreviewBody {
  mode: 'cron' | 'simple';
  cron?: string;
  simple?: { kind: 'every_minutes' | 'every_hours' | 'daily' | 'weekly' | 'monthly'; every?: number; time?: string; days?: string[]; day?: number };
  timezone: string;
}

function useInvalidate(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['schedules', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['schedule-runs', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['jobs', org] });
  };
}

export function useSchedules(org: string, templateId?: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['schedules', org, { templateId }],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules', { params: { path: { org }, query: templateId ? { template_id: templateId } : {} } })),
    enabled: !!org,
  });
}

export function useSchedule(org: string, id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['schedules', org, id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!id,
  });
}

export function useScheduleRuns(org: string, id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['schedule-runs', org, id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/runs', { params: { path: { org, id }, query: { limit: 50 } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!id,
  });
}

export function useSchedulePreview(org: string, body: SchedulePreviewBody | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['schedule-preview', org, body],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/preview', { params: { path: { org } }, body: body! })),
    enabled: !!org && body !== null,
    retry: false,
  });
}

export function useCreateSchedule(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: ScheduleInput) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useUpdateSchedule(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ revision, ...body }: ScheduleInput & { revision: number }) =>
      unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id }, header: ifMatch(revision) }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useDeleteSchedule(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useScheduleAction(org: string, action: 'pause' | 'resume') {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) =>
      action === 'pause'
        ? unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/pause', { params: { path: { org, id } } }))
        : unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/resume', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

/** useRunScheduleNow fires the schedule once as the operator (202 with
 * the run's jobs, so the page can open the run view). */
export function useRunScheduleNow(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/{id}/run', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function usePauseAllSchedules(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/schedules/pause-all', { params: { path: { org } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

```ts
import type { Tone } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';

/** OUTCOME_TONE colours a schedule run's outcome with the state colours. */
export const OUTCOME_TONE: Record<string, Tone> = { triggered: 'ok', failed: 'crit', skipped_overlap: 'warn', missed: 'warn' };
```

`web/src/features/schedules/ScheduleRuns.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Pill } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { useScheduleRuns } from '../../api/hooks/useSchedules.ts';
import { OUTCOME_TONE } from './outcome.ts';

/** ScheduleRuns lists a schedule's newest recorded decisions: when, what
 * happened and why (the reason never carries variables or output). */
export function ScheduleRuns({ org, id }: { org: string; id: string }) {
  const runs = useScheduleRuns(org, id);
  const list = runs.data?.runs ?? [];
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
      <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold">Recent runs</h3>
      {list.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No runs yet.</p>}
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        {list.map((r) => (
          <li key={r.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <Pill tone={OUTCOME_TONE[r.outcome] ?? 'unknown'}>{r.outcome}</Pill>
            <span className="text-muted-foreground">{new Date(r.decided_at).toLocaleString()}</span>
            {r.reason && <span className="truncate">{r.reason}</span>}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/schedules/ScheduleEditor.tsx`. The `<section aria-label="Schedule editor">` stays mounted while the schedule loads, so the tests' `within(panel)` keeps pointing at the live element:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { ApiError } from '../../api/client.ts';
import {
  type SchedulePreviewBody,
  useCreateSchedule,
  useDeleteSchedule,
  useRunScheduleNow,
  useSchedule,
  useScheduleAction,
  useSchedulePreview,
  useUpdateSchedule,
} from '../../api/hooks/useSchedules.ts';
import type { Schedule, ScheduleInput, Template } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { HOUR_STEPS, MINUTE_STEPS, WEEKDAYS, fromSimpleBody, toSimpleBody, type SimpleForm, type SimpleKind } from './simple.ts';
import { ScheduleRuns } from './ScheduleRuns.tsx';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';
const WINDOWS = ['15m', '30m', '1h', '2h', '6h', '12h', '24h'];
const KIND_LABEL: Record<SimpleKind, string> = {
  every_minutes: 'Every N minutes',
  every_hours: 'Every N hours',
  daily: 'Daily',
  weekly: 'Weekly',
  monthly: 'Monthly',
};

export interface ScheduleEditorProps {
  org: string;
  /** The schedule id, or "new". */
  id: string;
  templates: Template[];
  defaultTemplate?: string;
  canWrite: boolean;
  canRun: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

/** ScheduleEditor is the Scheduler artboard's detail panel: template,
 * timezone, simple/cron switch with the next five occurrences, guards,
 * overrides, the recent runs and the run now / pause / save actions. */
export function ScheduleEditor(props: ScheduleEditorProps) {
  const isNew = props.id === 'new';
  const detail = useSchedule(props.org, isNew ? '' : props.id);
  const initial = isNew ? undefined : detail.data?.schedule;
  return (
    <section aria-label="Schedule editor" className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4">
      {!isNew && !initial ? <QueryLoading /> : <EditorForm key={initial ? `${initial.id}-${initial.revision}` : 'new'} {...props} initial={initial} />}
    </section>
  );
}

function EditorForm({ org, templates, defaultTemplate, canWrite, canRun, onClose, initial }: ScheduleEditorProps & { initial?: Schedule }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const toast = useToast();
  const create = useCreateSchedule(org);
  const update = useUpdateSchedule(org, initial?.id ?? '');
  const remove = useDeleteSchedule(org);
  const pause = useScheduleAction(org, 'pause');
  const resume = useScheduleAction(org, 'resume');
  const runNow = useRunScheduleNow(org);

  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? '');
  const [templateId, setTemplateId] = useState(initial?.template_id ?? defaultTemplate ?? templates[0]?.id ?? '');
  const [timezone, setTimezone] = useState(initial?.timezone ?? Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'cron' | 'simple'>(initial ? (initial.mode as 'cron' | 'simple') : 'simple');
  const [cron, setCron] = useState(initial?.cron ?? '0 3 * * *');
  const [simple, setSimple] = useState<SimpleForm>(fromSimpleBody(initial?.simple));
  const [overlapSkip, setOverlapSkip] = useState((initial?.guards.overlap ?? 'skip') === 'skip');
  const [catchUp, setCatchUp] = useState(initial?.guards.catch_up?.enabled ?? false);
  const [window, setWindow] = useState(initial?.guards.catch_up?.window || '1h');
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(initial?.limit ?? '');
  const [extraVars, setExtraVars] = useState(initial?.extra_vars ? JSON.stringify(initial.extra_vars, null, 2) : '');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const previewBody = useMemo<SchedulePreviewBody>(
    () => (mode === 'simple' ? { mode, simple: toSimpleBody(simple), timezone } : { mode, cron, timezone }),
    [mode, simple, cron, timezone],
  );
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState<SchedulePreviewBody | null>(previewBody);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(previewBody), 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [previewBody]);
  const preview = useSchedulePreview(org, debounced);

  function setKind(kind: SimpleKind) {
    setSimple({ ...simple, kind, every: kind === 'every_hours' ? 1 : kind === 'every_minutes' ? 15 : simple.every });
  }

  function toggleDay(day: string) {
    setSimple({ ...simple, days: simple.days.includes(day) ? simple.days.filter((d) => d !== day) : [...simple.days, day] });
  }

  function body(): ScheduleInput | null {
    let vars: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
    if (extraVars.trim() !== '') {
      try {
        vars = JSON.parse(extraVars) as Record<string, unknown>;
      } catch {
        setError('Extra vars must be a JSON object.');
        return null;
      }
    }
    return {
      template_id: templateId,
      name,
      mode,
      ...(mode === 'simple' ? { simple: toSimpleBody(simple) } : { cron }),
      timezone,
      guards: { overlap: overlapSkip ? 'skip' : 'queue', catch_up: { enabled: catchUp, window } },
      ...(limit !== '' ? { limit } : {}),
      ...(vars ? { extra_vars: vars } : {}),
    } as ScheduleInput;
  }

  async function save() {
    setError(null);
    const b = body();
    if (!b) return;
    try {
      if (initial) {
        await update.mutateAsync({ ...b, revision: initial.revision });
      } else {
        const res = await create.mutateAsync(b);
        void navigate({ to: '/o/$org/schedules', params: { org }, search: { id: res.schedule.id, template: undefined } });
      }
      toast.show('Schedule saved');
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 412) {
        setError('This schedule changed since you loaded it — reload and apply your change again.');
      } else {
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed');
      }
    }
  }

  async function run() {
    if (!initial) return;
    try {
      const res = await runNow.mutateAsync(initial.id);
      const first = res.jobs?.[0];
      if (first) await navigate({ to: '/o/$org/runs/$id', params: { org, id: first.id } });
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Starting the run failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  async function del() {
    if (!initial || !confirm(`Delete schedule ${initial.name}?`)) return;
    await remove.mutateAsync(initial.id);
    onClose();
  }

  const zoneFmt = (at: string) => new Date(at).toLocaleString(undefined, { timeZone: timezone, dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });
  const previewError = preview.error instanceof Error ? preview.error.message : null;

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold">{initial ? `Schedule · ${initial.name}` : 'New schedule'}</h2>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>
          Close
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Label htmlFor="sched-name">Name</Label>
      <Input id="sched-name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} disabled={!canWrite} />
      <Label htmlFor="sched-template">Job template</Label>
      <select id="sched-template" className={SELECT} value={templateId} onChange={(e) => setTemplateId(e.target.value)} disabled={!canWrite}>
        {templates.map((t) => (
          <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
            {t.name}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <Label htmlFor="sched-tz">Timezone</Label>
      <Input id="sched-tz" className="font-mono" value={timezone} onChange={(e) => setTimezone(e.target.value)} disabled={!canWrite} />
      <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="When" className="flex gap-1">
        {(['simple', 'cron'] as const).map((m) => (
          <button
            key={m}
            type="button"
            role="radio"
            aria-checked={mode === m}
            className={`rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs ${mode === m ? 'border-primary text-primary' : 'border-border text-muted-foreground'}`}
            onClick={() => setMode(m)}
          >
            {m === 'simple' ? 'Simple' : 'Cron'}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {mode === 'simple' ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Label htmlFor="sched-kind">Repeat</Label>
          <select id="sched-kind" className={SELECT} value={simple.kind} onChange={(e) => setKind(e.target.value as SimpleKind)}>
            {(Object.keys(KIND_LABEL) as SimpleKind[]).map((k) => (
              <option key={k} value={k}>
                {KIND_LABEL[k]}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {(simple.kind === 'every_minutes' || simple.kind === 'every_hours') && (
            <>
              <Label htmlFor="sched-every">Every</Label>
              <select id="sched-every" className={SELECT} value={simple.every} onChange={(e) => setSimple({ ...simple, every: Number(e.target.value) })}>
                {(simple.kind === 'every_minutes' ? MINUTE_STEPS : HOUR_STEPS).map((n) => (
                  <option key={n} value={n}>
                    {n}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </>
          )}
          {simple.kind === 'weekly' && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {WEEKDAYS.map((d) => (
                <label key={d} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={simple.days.includes(d)} onChange={() => toggleDay(d)} />
                  {d[0].toUpperCase() + d.slice(1)}
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {simple.kind === 'monthly' && (
            <>
              <Label htmlFor="sched-day">Day of month</Label>
              <Input id="sched-day" type="number" min={1} max={31} value={simple.day} onChange={(e) => setSimple({ ...simple, day: Number(e.target.value) })} />
            </>
          )}
          {(simple.kind === 'daily' || simple.kind === 'weekly' || simple.kind === 'monthly') && (
            <>
              <Label htmlFor="sched-at">At</Label>
              <Input id="sched-at" type="time" value={simple.time} onChange={(e) => setSimple({ ...simple, time: e.target.value })} />
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Label htmlFor="sched-cron">Cron expression</Label>
          <Input id="sched-cron" className="font-mono" value={cron} onChange={(e) => setCron(e.target.value)} disabled={!canWrite} />
        </>
      )}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-md border border-border p-2">
        {previewError ? (
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--px-crit)]">{previewError}</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            {preview.data?.description && <p className="text-xs">{preview.data.description}</p>}
            <ol className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
              {(preview.data?.next ?? []).map((at, i) => (
                <li key={at} aria-label={`occurrence ${i + 1}`}>
                  {zoneFmt(at)}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={overlapSkip} onChange={(e) => setOverlapSkip(e.target.checked)} disabled={!canWrite} />
        Skip when a run of this template is still active
      </label>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={catchUp} onChange={(e) => setCatchUp(e.target.checked)} disabled={!canWrite} />
        Catch up a missed run after downtime
      </label>
      {catchUp ? (
        <>
          <Label htmlFor="sched-window">Catch-up window</Label>
          <select id="sched-window" className={SELECT} value={window} onChange={(e) => setWindow(e.target.value)}>
            {WINDOWS.map((w) => (
              <option key={w} value={w}>
                {w}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">otherwise the next regular run happens</p>
      )}
      <Label htmlFor="sched-limit">Limit</Label>
      <Input id="sched-limit" className="font-mono" value={limit} onChange={(e) => setLimit(e.target.value)} placeholder="the template's limit" disabled={!canWrite} />
      <Label htmlFor="sched-vars">Extra vars</Label>
      <textarea
        id="sched-vars"
        className="min-h-20 rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-2 font-mono text-xs"
        value={extraVars}
        onChange={(e) => setExtraVars(e.target.value)}
        placeholder='{"release": "v1.2"}'
        disabled={!canWrite}
      />
      {error && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--px-crit)]">{error}</p>}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
        {initial && canRun && (
          <Button type="button" onClick={() => void run()}>
            Run now
          </Button>
        )}
        {initial && canWrite && (
          <Button type="button" onClick={() => void (initial.paused ? resume : pause).mutateAsync(initial.id)}>
            {initial.paused ? 'Resume' : 'Pause'}
          </Button>
        )}
        {initial && canWrite && (
          <Button type="button" variant="destructive" onClick={() => void del()}>
            Delete
          </Button>
        )}
        {canWrite && (
          <Button type="button" variant="primary" onClick={() => void save()} disabled={create.isPending || update.isPending}>
            Save
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      {initial && <ScheduleRuns org={org} id={initial.id} />}
    </>
  );
}

```

`web/src/features/schedules/SchedulesPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useNavigate, useParams, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Clock, Pause } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, GridHeader, GridRow } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useOrg } from '../../api/hooks/useOrgs.ts';
import { useTemplates } from '../../api/hooks/useTemplates.ts';
import { usePauseAllSchedules, useSchedules } from '../../api/hooks/useSchedules.ts';
import { may } from '../../lib/roles.ts';
import { ScheduleEditor } from './ScheduleEditor.tsx';
import { OUTCOME_TONE } from './outcome.ts';
import { relative } from './simple.ts';

const COLUMNS = '90px minmax(0, 1.2fr) minmax(0, 1fr) 90px 120px';

/** SchedulesPage is the Scheduler artboard (spec 2026-09-18 §2): every
 * schedule of the organization with status, template, timetable, next run
 * and last result; "Pause all"; "New schedule"; the detail panel for the
 * selected one (?id=), optionally filtered to one template (?template=). */
export function SchedulesPage() {
  const { org } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { org: string };
  const search = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { id?: string; template?: string };
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const toast = useToast();
  const orgInfo = useOrg(org);
  const canWrite = may(orgInfo.data?.org.role, 'templates.write');
  const canRun = may(orgInfo.data?.org.role, 'jobs.run');
  const schedules = useSchedules(org, search.template);
  const templates = useTemplates(org);
  const pauseAll = usePauseAllSchedules(org);

  const guard = useQueryGuard(schedules);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!schedules.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const list = schedules.data.schedules ?? [];
  const templateList = templates.data?.templates ?? [];
  const templateName = (id: string) => templateList.find((t) => t.id === id)?.name ?? id.slice(0, 8);
  const now = new Date();

  const select = (id?: string) => void navigate({ to: '/o/$org/schedules', params: { org }, search: { id, template: search.template } });

  async function onPauseAll() {
    if (!confirm('Pause every schedule of this organization?')) return;
    try {
      const res = await pauseAll.mutateAsync();
      toast.show(`${res.paused} schedule(s) paused`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Pausing failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Schedules</h1>
          <span className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
            {list.length} schedules · {list.filter((s) => !s.paused).length} active
          </span>
        </div>
        {canWrite && (
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button type="button" onClick={() => void onPauseAll()}>
              <Pause size={14} /> Pause all
            </Button>
            <Button type="button" variant="primary" onClick={() => select('new')}>
              <Clock size={14} /> New schedule
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      {search.template && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
          <span>Template: {templateName(search.template)}</span>
          <button type="button" className="text-primary" onClick={() => void navigate({ to: '/o/$org/schedules', params: { org }, search: { id: undefined, template: undefined } })}>
            Show all
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_420px]">
        <Card>
          <GridHeader columns={COLUMNS}>
            <span>Status</span>
            <span>Schedule</span>
            <span>Cron</span>
            <span>Next</span>
            <span>Last</span>
          </GridHeader>
          {list.map((s) => (
            <div key={s.id} data-row className={s.paused ? 'opacity-55' : undefined}>
              <GridRow columns={COLUMNS}>
                <span>{s.paused ? <Pill tone="unknown">paused</Pill> : <Pill tone="ok">active</Pill>}</span>
                <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-col">
                  <button type="button" className="truncate text-left text-[13px] font-medium hover:text-primary" onClick={() => select(s.id)}>
                    {s.name}
                  </button>
                  <span className="truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{templateName(s.template_id)}</span>
                </span>
                <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-col">
                  <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs">{s.cron}</span>
                  {s.description && <span className="truncate text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{s.description}</span>}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{s.paused ? '—' : relative(s.next_run_at, now)}</span>
                <span>{s.last_run ? <Pill tone={OUTCOME_TONE[s.last_run.outcome] ?? 'unknown'}>{s.last_run.outcome}</Pill> : <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">—</span>}</span>
              </GridRow>
            </div>
          ))}
          {list.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No schedules yet.</p>}
        </Card>
        {search.id && (
          <ScheduleEditor
            key={search.id}
            org={org}
            id={search.id}
            templates={templateList}
            defaultTemplate={search.template}
            canWrite={canWrite}
            canRun={canRun}
            onClose={() => select(undefined)}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

```ts
/** TRIGGER_LABEL names what started a job (jobs.trigger). */
export const TRIGGER_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { manual: 'Manual', schedule: 'Schedule', webhook: 'Webhook', system: 'System' };

export function triggerLabel(trigger: string): string {
  return TRIGGER_LABEL[trigger] ?? trigger;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Docs**

Create `~/_dev/noix-docs/src/content/docs/pulsix/user/schedules.md`. Frontmatter: title "Schedules", description "Run job templates on a timetable". Sections:
- Simple and cron mode, with the five simple kinds and the cron syntax (5 fields, `@hourly`…`@monthly`, day-of-month OR day-of-week)
- Time zones and daylight saving (spring-forward runs at the first valid minute; fall-back runs once)
- Guards: overlap skip/queue; catch-up with a window of 1 minute to 24 hours; missed runs
- Overrides: limit and extra vars; secret values are stored encrypted and read back as "set"; retries use the template's own values
- Pause, pause all, run now; the recent-runs list keeps the newest 500 decisions
- Who may do what (viewer reads; operator manages and runs)
- Deleting a job template (through the API) also deletes its schedules, webhook triggers and their history

### Task 9: The webhook triggers domain (`internal/triggers`: tables, mapping, signatures, management)

This task covers spec §3 "Data", "Mapping" and the management half: trigger records with sealed secrets and the presets, the JSONPath subset, the signature and token check, and the store with the shared token bucket and throttled delivery log. The receive pipeline and the HTTP endpoints follow in Task 10.

**Files:**
- Modify: `go.mod`, `go.sum` (`go get github.com/theory/jsonpath@v0.12.1`, the newest release; verify it with `go list -m -versions github.com/theory/jsonpath` and pin a newer one if it exists)
- Create: `internal/db/migrations/00026_job_triggers.sql`, `internal/db/queries/triggers.sql`, regenerated gen
- Create: `internal/triggers/trigger.go` (types, presets, AAD, errors), `mapping.go`, `auth.go`, `validate.go`, `service.go` (management), `store_sql.go`
- Test: `internal/triggers/mapping_test.go`, `auth_test.go`, `service_test.go` (fakes), `store_sql_test.go` (integration incl. bucket and throttle)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (package `triggers`):

```go
type Kind string      // KindTemplateRun "template_run", KindRepoSync "repo_sync"
type Auth string      // AuthHMAC "hmac", AuthToken "token"
type Preset string    // PresetPulsix "pulsix", PresetGitHub "github", PresetGitea "gitea", PresetGitLab "gitlab", PresetBearer "bearer"
type OnActive string  // OnActiveQueue "queue", OnActiveSkip "skip"
type Outcome string   // triggered, skipped_active, rejected_ip, rejected_auth, rate_limited, invalid_payload, disabled, failed; not_found is never stored
type RateLimit struct { Count int `json:"count"`; Per string `json:"per"` }
type Mapping struct { ExtraVars map[string]string `json:"extra_vars,omitempty"`; Limit string `json:"limit,omitempty"` }
type Trigger struct {
	ID, OrgID uuid.UUID; Kind Kind; TemplateID, RepoID *uuid.UUID; Name string; Enabled bool
	Preset Preset; TimestampHeader string; AllowCIDRs []netip.Prefix; Mapping Mapping
	OnActive OnActive; RateLimit RateLimit; CreatedBy *uuid.UUID; CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time; Revision int64
}
func (t Trigger) Auth() Auth
func (t Trigger) SignatureHeader() string
type Input struct {
	Kind Kind; TemplateID, RepoID *uuid.UUID; Name string; Enabled bool; Preset Preset; TimestampHeader string
	AllowCIDRs []string; Mapping Mapping; OnActive OnActive; RateLimit RateLimit
}
type Delivery struct {
	ID, OrgID, TriggerID uuid.UUID; At time.Time; SourceIP string; Outcome Outcome; Reason string; JobGroupID *uuid.UUID
}
type Created struct { Trigger Trigger; Secret string; URL string }
type ListFilter struct { TemplateID, RepoID *uuid.UUID }

var ErrNotFound, ErrInvalidInput, ErrNameTaken, ErrRevisionMismatch error
func AAD(orgID, id uuid.UUID) []byte // "trigger:<org>:<id>"
func Sign(p Preset, secret, timestamp string, body []byte) string // the header value a sender presents

type Store interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, sealedSecret []byte) (Trigger, error) // also fills the bucket
	Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Trigger, error)
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, f ListFilter) ([]Trigger, error)
	Update(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, revision int64) (Trigger, error) // refills the bucket
	SetSecret(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, sealed []byte) (Trigger, error)
	Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	Deliveries(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error)
	Resolve(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID) (Trigger, []byte, error) // bypass lookup by id: trigger + sealed secret
	TakeToken(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, rl RateLimit, now time.Time) (ok bool, retryAfter time.Duration, err error)
	RecordDelivery(ctx context.Context, d Delivery, throttle bool) error
}
type TemplateChecker interface {
	Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (templates.Template, error)
	ValidateOverrides(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, in templates.Overrides, opt templates.OverrideOptions) (templates.Overrides, error)
}
type RepoLookup interface { Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (repos.Repo, error) }
type Sealer interface { SealAAD(plain, aad []byte) ([]byte, error); OpenAAD(sealed, aad []byte) ([]byte, error) }
type Runner interface { // used from Task 10
	EnqueueRunWith(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) ([]jobs.Job, error)
	EnqueueSyncWith(ctx context.Context, orgID, repoID uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) (jobs.Job, error)
	HasActiveRun(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID) (bool, error)
	HasActiveSync(ctx context.Context, orgID, repoID uuid.UUID) (bool, error)
}
type Auditor interface { Record(ctx context.Context, e audit.Event) error }
type Deps struct {
	Store Store; Templates TemplateChecker; Repos RepoLookup; Box Sealer; Runs Runner; Audit Auditor
	PublicURL string; Now func() time.Time; Rand io.Reader; Log *slog.Logger
}
func NewService(d Deps) *Service
func (s *Service) Create(ctx context.Context, orgID, actor uuid.UUID, in Input) (Created, error)
func (s *Service) Update(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, in Input) (Trigger, error) // kind and target are immutable
func (s *Service) RotateSecret(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Created, error)
func (s *Service) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Trigger, error)
func (s *Service) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, f ListFilter) ([]Trigger, error)
func (s *Service) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
func (s *Service) Deliveries(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error)
func (s *Service) URL(t Trigger) string
```

`internal/db/migrations/00026_job_triggers.sql`:

```sql
-- +goose Up
-- Incoming webhook triggers (spec 2026-09-18 §3). The id (UUIDv7) is the
-- URL identifier; the hook endpoints resolve it without an org scope.
-- Exactly one target: a job template (template_run) or a git repository
-- (repo_sync, the 2.8 push webhook). auth and signature_header derive
-- from preset; secret is sealed with AAD trigger:<org>:<id>.
CREATE TABLE job_triggers (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id           uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  kind             text NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('template_run', 'repo_sync')),
  template_id      uuid,
  repo_id          uuid,
  name             text NOT NULL,
  enabled          boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  preset           text NOT NULL CHECK (preset IN ('pulsix', 'github', 'gitea', 'gitlab', 'bearer')),
  auth             text NOT NULL CHECK (auth IN ('hmac', 'token')),
  signature_header text NOT NULL,
  timestamp_header text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  secret           bytea NOT NULL,
  allow_cidrs      cidr[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  mapping          jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  on_active        text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queue' CHECK (on_active IN ('queue', 'skip')),
  rate_limit       jsonb NOT NULL,
  created_by       uuid,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  revision         bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  UNIQUE (org_id, name),
  UNIQUE (org_id, id),
  CHECK ((kind = 'template_run') = (template_id IS NOT NULL AND repo_id IS NULL)),
  CHECK ((kind = 'repo_sync') = (repo_id IS NOT NULL AND template_id IS NULL)),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, template_id) REFERENCES job_templates (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, repo_id) REFERENCES git_repos (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE INDEX job_triggers_template_idx ON job_triggers (org_id, template_id) WHERE template_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX job_triggers_repo_idx ON job_triggers (org_id, repo_id) WHERE repo_id IS NOT NULL;

ALTER TABLE job_triggers ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE job_triggers FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON job_triggers
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON job_triggers
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- One row per delivery; no request body is ever stored. Rejections are
-- throttled to one row per trigger, outcome and minute; the newest 500 per
-- trigger are kept. 'failed' (enqueue refused) extends the spec's list.
CREATE TABLE trigger_deliveries (
  id           uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id       uuid NOT NULL,
  trigger_id   uuid NOT NULL,
  at           timestamptz NOT NULL,
  source_ip    text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  outcome      text NOT NULL CONSTRAINT trigger_deliveries_outcome_check CHECK (outcome IN (
                 'triggered', 'skipped_active', 'rejected_ip', 'rejected_auth',
                 'rate_limited', 'invalid_payload', 'disabled', 'failed')),
  reason       text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  job_group_id uuid,
  UNIQUE (org_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, trigger_id) REFERENCES job_triggers (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE INDEX trigger_deliveries_trigger_idx ON trigger_deliveries (org_id, trigger_id, at DESC);

ALTER TABLE trigger_deliveries ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE trigger_deliveries FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON trigger_deliveries
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON trigger_deliveries
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- The shared token bucket: one conditional UPDATE ... RETURNING takes a
-- token, so several managers share one limit per trigger.
CREATE TABLE trigger_buckets (
  trigger_id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id     uuid NOT NULL,
  tokens     double precision NOT NULL,
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, trigger_id) REFERENCES job_triggers (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

ALTER TABLE trigger_buckets ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE trigger_buckets FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON trigger_buckets
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON trigger_buckets
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- +goose Down
DROP TABLE trigger_buckets;
DROP TABLE trigger_deliveries;
DROP TABLE job_triggers;
```

`internal/db/queries/triggers.sql`. It uses `sqlc.arg()`/`sqlc.narg()` throughout: sqlc 1.31 mis-rewrites `@name::type` inside arithmetic (verified while writing this plan).

```sql
-- name: CreateTrigger :one
INSERT INTO job_triggers (id, org_id, kind, template_id, repo_id, name, enabled, preset, auth, signature_header,
                          timestamp_header, secret, allow_cidrs, mapping, on_active, rate_limit, created_by)
VALUES (sqlc.arg(id), sqlc.arg(org_id), sqlc.arg(kind), sqlc.narg(template_id), sqlc.narg(repo_id), sqlc.arg(name),
        sqlc.arg(enabled), sqlc.arg(preset), sqlc.arg(auth), sqlc.arg(signature_header), sqlc.arg(timestamp_header),
        sqlc.arg(secret), sqlc.arg(allow_cidrs), sqlc.arg(mapping), sqlc.arg(on_active), sqlc.arg(rate_limit), sqlc.narg(created_by))
RETURNING *;

-- name: GetTrigger :one
SELECT * FROM job_triggers WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id);

-- name: ResolveTrigger :one
-- The hook endpoints' lookup: no org in the path, no org scope bound (the
-- bypass policy applies); the unguessable id is the only key.
SELECT * FROM job_triggers WHERE id = sqlc.arg(id);

-- name: ListTriggers :many
SELECT * FROM job_triggers
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id)
  AND (sqlc.narg(template_id)::uuid IS NULL OR template_id = sqlc.narg(template_id)::uuid)
  AND (sqlc.narg(repo_id)::uuid IS NULL OR repo_id = sqlc.narg(repo_id)::uuid)
ORDER BY name;

-- name: UpdateTrigger :one
UPDATE job_triggers SET name = sqlc.arg(name), enabled = sqlc.arg(enabled), preset = sqlc.arg(preset), auth = sqlc.arg(auth),
  signature_header = sqlc.arg(signature_header), timestamp_header = sqlc.arg(timestamp_header),
  allow_cidrs = sqlc.arg(allow_cidrs), mapping = sqlc.arg(mapping), on_active = sqlc.arg(on_active),
  rate_limit = sqlc.arg(rate_limit), revision = revision + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id) AND revision = sqlc.arg(revision)
RETURNING *;

-- name: SetTriggerSecret :one
UPDATE job_triggers SET secret = sqlc.arg(secret), revision = revision + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id)
RETURNING *;

-- name: DeleteTrigger :execrows
DELETE FROM job_triggers WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id);

-- name: FillTriggerBucket :exec
INSERT INTO trigger_buckets (trigger_id, org_id, tokens, updated_at)
VALUES (sqlc.arg(trigger_id), sqlc.arg(org_id), sqlc.arg(tokens), sqlc.arg(at))
ON CONFLICT (trigger_id) DO UPDATE SET tokens = EXCLUDED.tokens, updated_at = EXCLUDED.updated_at;

-- name: TakeTriggerToken :one
-- Refill by elapsed time (capped at capacity), then take one token — only
-- when at least one is there. No row back means rate limited.
UPDATE trigger_buckets
SET tokens = LEAST(sqlc.arg(capacity)::float8,
      tokens + GREATEST(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (sqlc.arg(now)::timestamptz - updated_at))::float8, 0) * sqlc.arg(refill)::float8) - 1,
    updated_at = sqlc.arg(now)::timestamptz
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND trigger_id = sqlc.arg(trigger_id)
  AND LEAST(sqlc.arg(capacity)::float8,
      tokens + GREATEST(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (sqlc.arg(now)::timestamptz - updated_at))::float8, 0) * sqlc.arg(refill)::float8) >= 1
RETURNING tokens;

-- name: GetTriggerBucket :one
SELECT tokens, updated_at FROM trigger_buckets WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND trigger_id = sqlc.arg(trigger_id);

-- name: InsertTriggerDelivery :exec
-- throttle: at most one row per trigger, outcome and minute.
INSERT INTO trigger_deliveries (id, org_id, trigger_id, at, source_ip, outcome, reason, job_group_id)
SELECT sqlc.arg(id)::uuid, sqlc.arg(org_id)::uuid, sqlc.arg(trigger_id)::uuid, sqlc.arg(at)::timestamptz,
       sqlc.arg(source_ip)::text, sqlc.arg(outcome)::text, sqlc.arg(reason)::text, sqlc.narg(job_group_id)::uuid
WHERE NOT sqlc.arg(throttle)::boolean OR NOT EXISTS (
  SELECT 1 FROM trigger_deliveries d
  WHERE d.org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id)::uuid AND d.trigger_id = sqlc.arg(trigger_id)::uuid
    AND d.outcome = sqlc.arg(outcome)::text AND d.at > sqlc.arg(at)::timestamptz - interval '1 minute'
);

-- name: TrimTriggerDeliveries :exec
DELETE FROM trigger_deliveries AS d
WHERE d.org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND d.trigger_id = sqlc.arg(trigger_id)
  AND d.id NOT IN (
    SELECT k.id FROM trigger_deliveries AS k
    WHERE k.org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND k.trigger_id = sqlc.arg(trigger_id)
    ORDER BY k.at DESC, k.id DESC
    LIMIT 500
  );

-- name: ListTriggerDeliveries :many
SELECT * FROM trigger_deliveries WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND trigger_id = sqlc.arg(trigger_id)
ORDER BY at DESC, id DESC LIMIT sqlc.arg(lim);
```

`internal/triggers/mapping_test.go`:

```go
package triggers

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestParsePathSubset(t *testing.T) {
	for _, ok := range []string{"$", "$.ref", "$.commits[*].id", "$.commits[0].id", "$['a b'].c", "$.hosts[-1]", "$[*]"} {
		if _, err := parsePath(ok); err != nil {
			t.Errorf("parsePath(%q): %v", ok, err)
		}
	}
	for _, bad := range []string{"", "ref", "$..id", "$.commits[?@.id=='a']", "$.commits[0:2]", "$['a','b']", "$.a.length()", strings.Repeat("a", 300)} {
		if _, err := parsePath(bad); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("parsePath(%q) accepted", bad)
		}
	}
}

func TestApplyMapping(t *testing.T) {
	c, err := compileMapping(Mapping{
		ExtraVars: map[string]string{"release": "$.ref", "ids": "$.commits[*].id", "missing": "$.nope", "first": "$.commits[0]"},
		Limit:     "$.hosts[*]",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var doc any
	_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(`{"ref":"refs/heads/main","commits":[{"id":"a"},{"id":"b"}],"hosts":["web1","web2"]}`), &doc)
	got, field, err := c.apply(doc)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("apply: %v (%s)", err, field)
	}
	if got.ExtraVars["release"] != "refs/heads/main" || !reflect.DeepEqual(got.ExtraVars["ids"], []any{"a", "b"}) {
		t.Fatalf("vars = %#v", got.ExtraVars)
	}
	if _, set := got.ExtraVars["missing"]; set {
		t.Fatal("a missing path must leave the var unset")
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got.ExtraVars["first"], map[string]any{"id": "a"}) {
		t.Fatalf("first = %#v", got.ExtraVars["first"])
	}
	if got.Limit == nil || *got.Limit != "web1,web2" {
		t.Fatalf("limit = %v", got.Limit)
	}
}

func TestApplyMappingLimitMustBeStrings(t *testing.T) {
	c, _ := compileMapping(Mapping{Limit: "$.n"})
	var doc any
	_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(`{"n":5}`), &doc)
	if _, field, err := c.apply(doc); err == nil || field != "limit" {
		t.Fatalf("apply = %v, field %q", err, field)
	}
	empty, _ := compileMapping(Mapping{})
	if got, _, err := empty.apply(doc); err != nil || !got.Empty() {
		t.Fatalf("empty mapping = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
}
```

`internal/triggers/auth_test.go`:

```go
package triggers

import (
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func headerOf(h http.Header) func(string) string { return h.Get }

func TestVerifyPresets(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	body := []byte(`{"ref":"main"}`)
	secret := "s3cret-webhook"
	for _, p := range []Preset{PresetPulsix, PresetGitHub, PresetGitea, PresetGitLab, PresetBearer} {
		t.Run(string(p), func(t *testing.T) {
			tr := Trigger{Preset: p}
			good := http.Header{}
			good.Set(tr.SignatureHeader(), Sign(p, secret, "", body))
			if !verify(tr, secret, headerOf(good), body, now) {
				t.Fatalf("a correctly signed %s request was refused (%s: %q)", p, tr.SignatureHeader(), good.Get(tr.SignatureHeader()))
			}
			bad := http.Header{}
			bad.Set(tr.SignatureHeader(), Sign(p, "other-secret", "", body))
			if verify(tr, secret, headerOf(bad), body, now) {
				t.Fatal("a wrong secret was accepted")
			}
			if verify(tr, secret, headerOf(http.Header{}), body, now) {
				t.Fatal("a missing header was accepted")
			}
			if tr.Auth() == AuthHMAC && verify(tr, secret, headerOf(good), []byte(`{"ref":"evil"}`), now) {
				t.Fatal("a tampered body was accepted")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestVerifyTimestampWindow(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	body := []byte(`{}`)
	tr := Trigger{Preset: PresetPulsix, TimestampHeader: "X-Pulsix-Timestamp"}
	sign := func(at time.Time) http.Header {
		ts := strconv.FormatInt(at.Unix(), 10)
		h := http.Header{}
		h.Set("X-Pulsix-Timestamp", ts)
		h.Set(tr.SignatureHeader(), Sign(PresetPulsix, "k", ts, body))
		return h
	}
	if !verify(tr, "k", headerOf(sign(now.Add(-4*time.Minute))), body, now) {
		t.Fatal("inside the window must pass")
	}
	for _, at := range []time.Time{now.Add(-6 * time.Minute), now.Add(6 * time.Minute)} {
		if verify(tr, "k", headerOf(sign(at)), body, now) {
			t.Fatalf("timestamp %v outside ±5 min passed", at)
		}
	}
	// The timestamp is covered by the signature: a fresh timestamp on an
	// old signature fails.
	h := sign(now.Add(-10 * time.Minute))
	h.Set("X-Pulsix-Timestamp", strconv.FormatInt(now.Unix(), 10))
	if verify(tr, "k", headerOf(h), body, now) {
		t.Fatal("a replayed signature with a fresh timestamp passed")
	}
	h = sign(now)
	h.Set("X-Pulsix-Timestamp", "yesterday")
	if verify(tr, "k", headerOf(h), body, now) {
		t.Fatal("a malformed timestamp passed")
	}
}
```
