`internal/triggers/trigger.go`:

```go
// Package triggers implements incoming webhook triggers (spec 2026-09-18
// §3): a secret-guarded URL per job template (template_run) or git
// repository (repo_sync) that starts a run or a sync, with an IP
// allowlist, HMAC or token authentication, a replay window, a shared rate
// limit and a JSONPath mapping from the payload onto extra vars and limit.
package triggers

import (
	"errors"
	"net/netip"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

var (
	ErrNotFound         = errors.New("not found")
	ErrInvalidInput     = errors.New("invalid input")
	ErrNameTaken        = errors.New("name already in use")
	ErrRevisionMismatch = errors.New("revision mismatch")
)

type Kind string

const (
	KindTemplateRun Kind = "template_run"
	KindRepoSync    Kind = "repo_sync"
)

type Auth string

const (
	AuthHMAC  Auth = "hmac"
	AuthToken Auth = "token"
)

type Preset string

const (
	PresetPulsix Preset = "pulsix"
	PresetGitHub Preset = "github"
	PresetGitea  Preset = "gitea"
	PresetGitLab Preset = "gitlab"
	PresetBearer Preset = "bearer"
)

// presetSpec is how a preset authenticates: the header the credential
// travels in and the prefix its value carries.
type presetSpec struct {
	auth   Auth
	header string
	prefix string
}

var presets = map[Preset]presetSpec{
	PresetPulsix: {auth: AuthHMAC, header: "X-Pulsix-Signature-256", prefix: "sha256="},
	PresetGitHub: {auth: AuthHMAC, header: "X-Hub-Signature-256", prefix: "sha256="},
	PresetGitea:  {auth: AuthHMAC, header: "X-Gitea-Signature"},
	PresetGitLab: {auth: AuthToken, header: "X-Gitlab-Token"},
	PresetBearer: {auth: AuthToken, header: "Authorization", prefix: "Bearer "},
}

type OnActive string

const (
	OnActiveQueue OnActive = "queue"
	OnActiveSkip  OnActive = "skip"
)

type Outcome string

const (
	OutcomeTriggered      Outcome = "triggered"
	OutcomeSkippedActive  Outcome = "skipped_active"
	OutcomeRejectedIP     Outcome = "rejected_ip"
	OutcomeRejectedAuth   Outcome = "rejected_auth"
	OutcomeRateLimited    Outcome = "rate_limited"
	OutcomeInvalidPayload Outcome = "invalid_payload"
	OutcomeDisabled       Outcome = "disabled"
	OutcomeFailed         Outcome = "failed"
	// OutcomeNotFound answers an unknown id or a kind/endpoint mismatch;
	// it is never stored (there is no trigger to attach it to).
	OutcomeNotFound Outcome = "not_found"
)

type RateLimit struct {
	Count int    `json:"count"`
	Per   string `json:"per"`
}

// Mapping maps payload JSONPaths onto top-level extra vars and the limit.
type Mapping struct {
	ExtraVars map[string]string `json:"extra_vars,omitempty"`
	Limit     string            `json:"limit,omitempty"`
}

func (m Mapping) empty() bool { return len(m.ExtraVars) == 0 && m.Limit == "" }

// Trigger is a stored trigger without its secret.
type Trigger struct {
	ID, OrgID            uuid.UUID
	Kind                 Kind
	TemplateID, RepoID   *uuid.UUID
	Name                 string
	Enabled              bool
	Preset               Preset
	TimestampHeader      string
	AllowCIDRs           []netip.Prefix
	Mapping              Mapping
	OnActive             OnActive
	RateLimit            RateLimit
	CreatedBy            *uuid.UUID
	CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time
	Revision             int64
}

// Auth is the preset's authentication mode.
func (t Trigger) Auth() Auth { return presets[t.Preset].auth }

// SignatureHeader is the header the credential travels in.
func (t Trigger) SignatureHeader() string { return presets[t.Preset].header }

// Input is what an operator submits.
type Input struct {
	Kind            Kind
	TemplateID      *uuid.UUID
	RepoID          *uuid.UUID
	Name            string
	Enabled         bool
	Preset          Preset
	TimestampHeader string
	AllowCIDRs      []string
	Mapping         Mapping
	OnActive        OnActive
	RateLimit       RateLimit
}

// Delivery is one received call; the body is never kept.
type Delivery struct {
	ID, OrgID, TriggerID uuid.UUID
	At                   time.Time
	SourceIP             string
	Outcome              Outcome
	Reason               string
	JobGroupID           *uuid.UUID
}

// Created carries the secret, shown once, and the URL.
type Created struct {
	Trigger Trigger
	Secret  string
	URL     string
}

type ListFilter struct {
	TemplateID *uuid.UUID
	RepoID     *uuid.UUID
}

// AAD is the associated data a trigger's secret is sealed with.
func AAD(orgID, id uuid.UUID) []byte {
	return []byte("trigger:" + orgID.String() + ":" + id.String())
}
```

`internal/triggers/mapping.go`:

```go
package triggers

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"

	"github.com/theory/jsonpath"
	"github.com/theory/jsonpath/spec"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

const (
	maxPathLen      = 256
	maxMappedVars   = 50
	errLimitMessage = "limit must map to strings"
)

// compiledMapping is a validated mapping ready to apply to payloads.
type compiledMapping struct {
	vars  map[string]*jsonpath.Path
	limit *jsonpath.Path
}

func compileMapping(m Mapping) (compiledMapping, error) {
	c := compiledMapping{vars: make(map[string]*jsonpath.Path, len(m.ExtraVars))}
	if len(m.ExtraVars) > maxMappedVars {
		return c, fmt.Errorf("%w: at most %d mapped variables", ErrInvalidInput, maxMappedVars)
	}
	for name, expr := range m.ExtraVars {
		p, err := parsePath(expr)
		if err != nil {
			return c, fmt.Errorf("%w: mapping for %s: %v", ErrInvalidInput, name, err)
		}
		c.vars[name] = p
	}
	if m.Limit != "" {
		p, err := parsePath(m.Limit)
		if err != nil {
			return c, fmt.Errorf("%w: mapping for limit: %v", ErrInvalidInput, err)
		}
		c.limit = p
	}
	return c, nil
}

// parsePath accepts the RFC 9535 subset of spec §3: root, then child
// segments with exactly one name, index or wildcard selector each.
func parsePath(expr string) (*jsonpath.Path, error) {
	if expr == "" || len(expr) > maxPathLen {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("a path is 1-%d characters", maxPathLen)
	}
	p, err := jsonpath.Parse(expr)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, errors.New("not a JSONPath expression (start with $)")
	}
	for _, seg := range p.Query().Segments() {
		if seg.IsDescendant() {
			return nil, errors.New("descendant segments (..) are not supported")
		}
		sels := seg.Selectors()
		if len(sels) != 1 {
			return nil, errors.New("use one name, index or [*] per segment")
		}
		switch sels[0].(type) {
		case spec.Name, spec.Index, spec.WildcardSelector:
		default:
			return nil, errors.New("only names, indexes and [*] are supported (no filters, slices or functions)")
		}
	}
	return p, nil
}

func hasWildcard(p *jsonpath.Path) bool {
	for _, seg := range p.Query().Segments() {
		if _, ok := seg.Selectors()[0].(spec.WildcardSelector); ok {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// apply maps a decoded payload. A missing path leaves its target unset; a
// wildcard path yields a list (joined with "," for limit). On error the
// second result names the offending field — never its value.
func (c compiledMapping) apply(doc any) (templates.Overrides, string, error) {
	var out templates.Overrides
	for name, p := range c.vars {
		nodes := p.Select(doc)
		if len(nodes) == 0 {
			continue
		}
		if out.ExtraVars == nil {
			out.ExtraVars = map[string]any{}
		}
		if hasWildcard(p) {
			out.ExtraVars[name] = []any(nodes)
		} else {
			out.ExtraVars[name] = nodes[0]
		}
	}
	if c.limit != nil {
		nodes := c.limit.Select(doc)
		if len(nodes) > 0 {
			parts := make([]string, 0, len(nodes))
			for _, n := range nodes {
				s, ok := n.(string)
				if !ok {
					return templates.Overrides{}, "limit", errors.New(errLimitMessage)
				}
				parts = append(parts, s)
			}
			limit := strings.Join(parts, ",")
			out.Limit = &limit
		}
	}
	return out, "", nil
}
```

`internal/triggers/auth.go`:

```go
package triggers

import (
	"crypto/hmac"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/subtle"
	"encoding/hex"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// replayWindow bounds the optional timestamp header (spec §3).
const replayWindow = 5 * time.Minute

// mac is HMAC-SHA256 over the raw body, or over "<timestamp>.<body>" when
// the trigger has a timestamp header, so the timestamp cannot be swapped.
func mac(secret, timestamp string, body []byte) []byte {
	m := hmac.New(sha256.New, []byte(secret))
	if timestamp != "" {
		m.Write([]byte(timestamp))
		m.Write([]byte{'.'})
	}
	m.Write(body)
	return m.Sum(nil)
}

// Sign returns the header value a sender presents for preset p: the
// prefixed hex HMAC, or the prefixed token itself.
func Sign(p Preset, secret, timestamp string, body []byte) string {
	spec := presets[p]
	if spec.auth == AuthToken {
		return spec.prefix + secret
	}
	return spec.prefix + hex.EncodeToString(mac(secret, timestamp, body))
}

// verify checks the credential (and the timestamp window) in constant time.
func verify(t Trigger, secret string, header func(string) string, body []byte, now time.Time) bool {
	ts := ""
	if t.TimestampHeader != "" {
		ts = strings.TrimSpace(header(t.TimestampHeader))
		sec, err := strconv.ParseInt(ts, 10, 64)
		if err != nil {
			return false
		}
		if d := now.Sub(time.Unix(sec, 0)); d > replayWindow || d < -replayWindow {
			return false
		}
	}
	spec, ok := presets[t.Preset]
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	presented := strings.TrimSpace(header(spec.header))
	if spec.prefix != "" {
		if len(presented) < len(spec.prefix) || !strings.EqualFold(presented[:len(spec.prefix)], spec.prefix) {
			return false
		}
		presented = presented[len(spec.prefix):]
	}
	switch spec.auth {
	case AuthHMAC:
		got, err := hex.DecodeString(presented)
		if err != nil {
			return false
		}
		return hmac.Equal(got, mac(secret, ts, body))
	case AuthToken:
		return subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(presented), []byte(secret)) == 1
	}
	return false
}
```

```go
func (fakeBox) SealAAD(plain, aad []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	return []byte("sealed:" + string(aad) + ":" + string(plain)), nil
}

func (fakeBox) OpenAAD(sealed, aad []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	rest, ok := strings.CutPrefix(string(sealed), "sealed:"+string(aad)+":")
	if !ok {
		return nil, errors.New("wrong aad")
	}
	return []byte(rest), nil
}

type fakeStore struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	byID    map[uuid.UUID]triggers.Trigger
	secrets map[uuid.UUID][]byte
	fills   int
}

func newFakeStore() *fakeStore {
	return &fakeStore{byID: map[uuid.UUID]triggers.Trigger{}, secrets: map[uuid.UUID][]byte{}}
}

func (f *fakeStore) Create(_ context.Context, t triggers.Trigger, sealed []byte) (triggers.Trigger, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for _, o := range f.byID {
		if o.OrgID == t.OrgID && o.Name == t.Name {
			return triggers.Trigger{}, triggers.ErrNameTaken
		}
	}
	t.Revision = 1
	f.byID[t.ID], f.secrets[t.ID] = t, sealed
	f.fills++
	return t, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Get(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (triggers.Trigger, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	t, ok := f.byID[id]
	if !ok || t.OrgID != orgID {
		return triggers.Trigger{}, triggers.ErrNotFound
	}
	return t, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) List(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, lf triggers.ListFilter) ([]triggers.Trigger, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []triggers.Trigger
	for _, t := range f.byID {
		if t.OrgID == orgID && (lf.TemplateID == nil || (t.TemplateID != nil && *t.TemplateID == *lf.TemplateID)) {
			out = append(out, t)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Update(_ context.Context, t triggers.Trigger, revision int64) (triggers.Trigger, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	cur, ok := f.byID[t.ID]
	if !ok || cur.OrgID != t.OrgID {
		return triggers.Trigger{}, triggers.ErrNotFound
	}
	if cur.Revision != revision {
		return triggers.Trigger{}, triggers.ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	t.Revision = revision + 1
	f.byID[t.ID] = t
	f.fills++
	return t, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) SetSecret(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, sealed []byte) (triggers.Trigger, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()

	t, ok := f.byID[id]
	if !ok || t.OrgID != orgID {
		return triggers.Trigger{}, triggers.ErrNotFound
	}
	f.secrets[id] = sealed
	return t, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Delete(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if t, ok := f.byID[id]; !ok || t.OrgID != orgID {
		return triggers.ErrNotFound
	}
	delete(f.byID, id)
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) RecordDelivery(context.Context, triggers.Delivery, bool) error { return nil }

func newWorld(t *testing.T) *world {
	t.Helper()
	w := &world{store: newFakeStore(), org: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), actor: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), template: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), repo: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}

	w.tmpls = fakeTemplates{byID: map[uuid.UUID]templates.Template{w.template: {ID: w.template, OrgID: w.org, ExtraVarsSchema: sc}}}
	w.repos = fakeRepos{byID: map[uuid.UUID]repos.Repo{w.repo: {ID: w.repo, OrgID: w.org}}}

	})

}

	w := newWorld(t)

	if err != nil {

}

func TestCreateValidation(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	other := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	cases := []struct {
		name   string
		mutate func(*triggers.Input)
	}{
		{"empty name", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.Name = "" }},
		{"unknown preset", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.Preset = "bitbucket" }},
		{"unknown template", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.TemplateID = &other }},
		{"template run with a repo", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.RepoID = &w.repo }},
		{"bad cidr", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.AllowCIDRs = []string{"10.0.0.0/33"} }},
		{"bad timestamp header", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.TimestampHeader = "X Bad" }},
		{"rate limit count", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.RateLimit = triggers.RateLimit{Count: 0, Per: "1m"} }},
		{"rate limit per", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.RateLimit = triggers.RateLimit{Count: 5, Per: "48h"} }},
		{"on_active", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.OnActive = "wait" }},
		{"descendant path", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.Mapping.ExtraVars = map[string]string{"release": "$..ref"} }},
		{"secret target", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.Mapping.ExtraVars = map[string]string{"token": "$.t"} }},
		{"undeclared target", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.Mapping.ExtraVars = map[string]string{"colour": "$.c"} }},
		{"denylisted target", func(in *triggers.Input) { in.Mapping.ExtraVars = map[string]string{"ansible_connection": "$.c"} }},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			in := w.templateInput()
			c.mutate(&in)
			if _, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrInvalidInput) {
				t.Fatalf("Create = %v, want ErrInvalidInput", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestRepoSyncTriggersTakeNoMapping(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	in := triggers.Input{Kind: triggers.KindRepoSync, RepoID: &w.repo, Name: "sync on push", Enabled: true, Preset: triggers.PresetGitea}
	c, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !strings.HasSuffix(c.URL, "/api/v1/hooks/repos/"+c.Trigger.ID.String()) {
		t.Fatalf("url = %s", c.URL)
	}
	in.Name, in.Mapping = "with mapping", triggers.Mapping{Limit: "$.x"}
	if _, err := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, in); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("repo_sync with mapping: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRotateSecretAndUpdate(t *testing.T) {
	w := newWorld(t)
	c, _ := w.svc.Create(ctx, w.org, w.actor, w.templateInput())
	r, err := w.svc.RotateSecret(ctx, w.org, c.Trigger.ID)
	if err != nil || r.Secret == c.Secret || r.Secret == "" {
		t.Fatalf("rotate = %+v, %v", r, err)
	}
	in := w.templateInput()
	in.Enabled, in.AllowCIDRs = false, []string{"140.82.112.0/20"}
	up, err := w.svc.Update(ctx, w.org, c.Trigger.ID, c.Trigger.Revision, in)
	if err != nil || up.Enabled || len(up.AllowCIDRs) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("update = %+v, %v", up, err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Update(ctx, w.org, c.Trigger.ID, c.Trigger.Revision, in); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale update: %v", err)
	}
	in.Kind, in.RepoID, in.TemplateID = triggers.KindRepoSync, &w.repo, nil
	if _, err := w.svc.Update(ctx, w.org, c.Trigger.ID, up.Revision, in); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Fatalf("changing the kind: %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/triggers/validate.go`:

```go
package triggers

import (
	"fmt"
	"net/netip"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"time"
	"unicode"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/ansiblevars"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/clientip"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

const (
	maxNameRunes = 100
	maxCIDRs     = 50
)

var headerNameRE = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}$`)

// DefaultRateLimit is the spec's 10 per minute.
var DefaultRateLimit = RateLimit{Count: 10, Per: "1m"}

func normalizeName(name string) (string, error) {
	n := strings.TrimSpace(name)
	if n == "" || utf8.RuneCountInString(n) > maxNameRunes {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: name must be 1-%d characters", ErrInvalidInput, maxNameRunes)
	}
	for _, r := range n {
		if unicode.IsControl(r) {
			return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: name must not contain control characters", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
	}
	return n, nil
}

func parseCIDRs(raw []string) ([]netip.Prefix, error) {
	if len(raw) > maxCIDRs {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: at most %d allowlist entries", ErrInvalidInput, maxCIDRs)
	}
	out := make([]netip.Prefix, 0, len(raw))
	for _, r := range raw {
		p, err := clientip.ParsePrefixes(r)
		if err != nil || len(p) != 1 {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: allowlist entry %q is not a CIDR or address", ErrInvalidInput, r)
		}
		out = append(out, p[0])
	}
	return out, nil
}

func normalizeRateLimit(rl RateLimit) (RateLimit, error) {
	if rl == (RateLimit{}) {
		return DefaultRateLimit, nil
	}
	per, err := time.ParseDuration(rl.Per)
	if err != nil || per < time.Second || per > 24*time.Hour || rl.Count < 1 || rl.Count > 1000 {
		return rl, fmt.Errorf("%w: rate limit is 1-1000 calls per 1s-24h", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	return rl, nil
}

// capacityAndRefill turns a limit into bucket parameters.
func capacityAndRefill(rl RateLimit) (capacity, perSecond float64) {
	per, err := time.ParseDuration(rl.Per)
	if err != nil || per <= 0 {
		per = time.Minute
	}
	return float64(rl.Count), float64(rl.Count) / per.Seconds()
}

// checkMappingTargets refuses targets a payload may not set: the 2.36
// denylist, secret fields, and — when the template has a schema — names
// it does not declare.
func checkMappingTargets(m Mapping, t templates.Template) error {
	secret := templates.SecretFields(t.ExtraVarsSchema)
	declared := map[string]bool{}
	if t.ExtraVarsSchema != nil {
		for _, f := range t.ExtraVarsSchema.Fields {
			declared[f.Name] = true
		}
	}
	for name := range m.ExtraVars {
		switch {
		case ansiblevars.Forbidden(name):
			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s controls connection security and cannot be mapped", ErrInvalidInput, name)
		case secret[name]:
			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s is a secret field and cannot be set from a payload", ErrInvalidInput, name)
		case t.ExtraVarsSchema != nil && !declared[name]:
			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s is not declared in the template's extra vars schema", ErrInvalidInput, name)
		}
	}
	return nil
}
```

`internal/triggers/service.go`:

```go
package triggers

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/audit"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/clientip"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/repos"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

type Store interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, sealedSecret []byte) (Trigger, error)
	Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Trigger, error)
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, f ListFilter) ([]Trigger, error)
	Update(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, revision int64) (Trigger, error)
	SetSecret(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, sealed []byte) (Trigger, error)
	Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	Deliveries(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error)
	Resolve(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID) (Trigger, []byte, error)
	TakeToken(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, rl RateLimit, now time.Time) (bool, time.Duration, error)
	RecordDelivery(ctx context.Context, d Delivery, throttle bool) error
}

type TemplateChecker interface {
	Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (templates.Template, error)
	ValidateOverrides(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, in templates.Overrides, opt templates.OverrideOptions) (templates.Overrides, error)
}

type RepoLookup interface {
	Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (repos.Repo, error)
}

type Sealer interface {
	SealAAD(plain, aad []byte) ([]byte, error)
	OpenAAD(sealed, aad []byte) ([]byte, error)
}

type Runner interface {
	EnqueueRunWith(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) ([]jobs.Job, error)
	EnqueueSyncWith(ctx context.Context, orgID, repoID uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) (jobs.Job, error)
	HasActiveRun(ctx context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID) (bool, error)
	HasActiveSync(ctx context.Context, orgID, repoID uuid.UUID) (bool, error)
}

type Auditor interface {
	Record(ctx context.Context, e audit.Event) error
}

// Deps wires the service; Now, Rand and Log default when nil.
type Deps struct {
	Store     Store
	Templates TemplateChecker
	Repos     RepoLookup
	Box       Sealer
	Runs      Runner
	Audit     Auditor
	PublicURL string
	// IPLimit bounds deliveries per source address across every trigger
	// (spec §9 "per-IP rate limits"); nil disables it (unit tests).
	IPLimit *clientip.Limiter
	Now     func() time.Time
	Rand    io.Reader
	Log     *slog.Logger
}

type Service struct{ d Deps }

func NewService(d Deps) *Service {
	if d.Now == nil {
		d.Now = time.Now
	}
	if d.Log == nil {
		d.Log = slog.Default()
	}
	d.PublicURL = strings.TrimRight(d.PublicURL, "/")
	return &Service{d: d}
}

func (s *Service) newID() uuid.UUID {
	id, err := uuid.NewV7FromReader(s.d.Rand)
	if err != nil {
		panic(err) //nolint:forbidigo // entropy failure is unrecoverable, like uuid.Must
	}
	return id
}

// newSecret draws 32 random bytes, base64url without padding (43 chars).
func (s *Service) newSecret() (string, error) {
	b := make([]byte, 32)
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(s.d.Rand, b); err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("draw trigger secret: %w", err)
	}
	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b), nil
}

// URL is where a sender posts to.
func (s *Service) URL(t Trigger) string {
	if t.Kind == KindRepoSync {
		return s.d.PublicURL + "/api/v1/hooks/repos/" + t.ID.String()
	}
	return s.d.PublicURL + "/api/v1/hooks/jobs/" + t.ID.String()
}

// Create validates and stores a trigger; the secret is returned once.
func (s *Service) Create(ctx context.Context, orgID, actor uuid.UUID, in Input) (Created, error) {
	t, err := s.build(ctx, orgID, s.newID(), in)
	if err != nil {
		return Created{}, err
	}
	t.CreatedBy = &actor
	secret, sealed, err := s.sealNewSecret(orgID, t.ID)
	if err != nil {
		return Created{}, err
	}
	stored, err := s.d.Store.Create(ctx, t, sealed)
	if err != nil {
		return Created{}, err
	}
	return Created{Trigger: stored, Secret: secret, URL: s.URL(stored)}, nil
}

// Update replaces the editable fields; kind and target are immutable.
func (s *Service) Update(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, in Input) (Trigger, error) {
	cur, err := s.d.Store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, err
	}
	if in.Kind != cur.Kind || !sameID(in.TemplateID, cur.TemplateID) || !sameID(in.RepoID, cur.RepoID) {
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: a trigger's kind and target cannot change; create a new trigger", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	t, err := s.build(ctx, orgID, id, in)
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, err
	}
	t.CreatedBy, t.CreatedAt = cur.CreatedBy, cur.CreatedAt
	return s.d.Store.Update(ctx, t, revision)
}

// RotateSecret replaces the secret; the new one is returned once.
func (s *Service) RotateSecret(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Created, error) {
	secret, sealed, err := s.sealNewSecret(orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Created{}, err
	}
	t, err := s.d.Store.SetSecret(ctx, orgID, id, sealed)
	if err != nil {
		return Created{}, err
	}
	return Created{Trigger: t, Secret: secret, URL: s.URL(t)}, nil
}

func (s *Service) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Trigger, error) {
	return s.d.Store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
}

func (s *Service) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, f ListFilter) ([]Trigger, error) {
	return s.d.Store.List(ctx, orgID, f)
}

func (s *Service) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.d.Store.Delete(ctx, orgID, id)
}

func (s *Service) Deliveries(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error) {
	if _, err := s.d.Store.Get(ctx, orgID, id); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return s.d.Store.Deliveries(ctx, orgID, id, limit)
}

func (s *Service) sealNewSecret(orgID, id uuid.UUID) (string, []byte, error) {
	secret, err := s.newSecret()
	if err != nil {
		return "", nil, err
	}
	sealed, err := s.d.Box.SealAAD([]byte(secret), AAD(orgID, id))
	if err != nil {
		return "", nil, fmt.Errorf("seal trigger secret: %w", err)
	}
	return secret, sealed, nil
}

func (s *Service) build(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, in Input) (Trigger, error) {
	name, err := normalizeName(in.Name)
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, err
	}
	if _, ok := presets[in.Preset]; !ok {
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: preset must be pulsix, github, gitea, gitlab or bearer", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if in.TimestampHeader != "" && !headerNameRE.MatchString(in.TimestampHeader) {
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: timestamp header must be a header name", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	cidrs, err := parseCIDRs(in.AllowCIDRs)
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, err
	}
	rl, err := normalizeRateLimit(in.RateLimit)
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, err
	}
	onActive := in.OnActive
	switch onActive {
	case "":
		onActive = OnActiveQueue
	case OnActiveQueue, OnActiveSkip:
	default:
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: on_active must be queue or skip", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if _, err := compileMapping(in.Mapping); err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, err
	}
	switch in.Kind {
	case KindTemplateRun:
		if in.TemplateID == nil || in.RepoID != nil {
			return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: a template_run trigger names a template and no repository", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		tmpl, err := s.d.Templates.Get(ctx, orgID, *in.TemplateID)
		if errors.Is(err, templates.ErrNotFound) {
			return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: the job template does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return Trigger{}, err
		}
		if err := checkMappingTargets(in.Mapping, tmpl); err != nil {
			return Trigger{}, err
		}
	case KindRepoSync:
		if in.RepoID == nil || in.TemplateID != nil {
			return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: a repo_sync trigger names a repository and no template", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		if !in.Mapping.empty() {
			return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: repo_sync triggers take no mapping", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		if _, err := s.d.Repos.Get(ctx, orgID, *in.RepoID); errors.Is(err, repos.ErrNotFound) {
			return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: the repository does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
		} else if err != nil {
			return Trigger{}, err
		}
	default:
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: kind must be template_run or repo_sync", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	return Trigger{
		ID: id, OrgID: orgID, Kind: in.Kind, TemplateID: in.TemplateID, RepoID: in.RepoID, Name: name,
		Enabled: in.Enabled, Preset: in.Preset, TimestampHeader: in.TimestampHeader, AllowCIDRs: cidrs,
		Mapping: in.Mapping, OnActive: onActive, RateLimit: rl,
	}, nil
}

func sameID(a, b *uuid.UUID) bool {
	return (a == nil && b == nil) || (a != nil && b != nil && *a == *b)
}
```

`internal/triggers/store_sql.go`:

```go
package triggers

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"net/netip"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// SQLStore persists triggers. Resolve runs on whatever scope ctx carries —
// the hook endpoints carry none (bypass policy) and look up by id alone.
type SQLStore struct {
	d *db.DB
	q *gen.Queries
}

func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore { return &SQLStore{d: d, q: gen.New(d)} }

func (st *SQLStore) Create(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, sealed []byte) (Trigger, error) {
	mapping, rl, err := encode(t)
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, err
	}
	var out Trigger
	err = st.d.WithWriteTx(ctx, t.OrgID, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		row, err := st.q.CreateTrigger(ctx, gen.CreateTriggerParams{
			ID: t.ID, OrgID: t.OrgID, Kind: string(t.Kind), TemplateID: db.NullUUID(t.TemplateID), RepoID: db.NullUUID(t.RepoID),
			Name: t.Name, Enabled: t.Enabled, Preset: string(t.Preset), Auth: string(t.Auth()), SignatureHeader: t.SignatureHeader(),
			TimestampHeader: t.TimestampHeader, Secret: sealed, AllowCidrs: cidrsOrEmpty(t.AllowCIDRs), Mapping: mapping,
			OnActive: string(t.OnActive), RateLimit: rl, CreatedBy: db.NullUUID(t.CreatedBy),
		})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if out, err = toTrigger(row); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return st.fill(ctx, t.OrgID, t.ID, t.RateLimit)
	})
	switch {
	case db.IsUniqueViolation(err):
		return Trigger{}, ErrNameTaken
	case db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err):
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: the target does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
	case err != nil:
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("create trigger: %w", err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) fill(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, rl RateLimit) error {
	capacity, _ := capacityAndRefill(rl)
	return st.q.FillTriggerBucket(ctx, gen.FillTriggerBucketParams{TriggerID: id, OrgID: orgID, Tokens: capacity, At: time.Now()})
}

func (st *SQLStore) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Trigger, error) {
	row, err := st.q.GetTrigger(ctx, gen.GetTriggerParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Trigger{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("get trigger: %w", err)
	}
	return toTrigger(row)
}

func (st *SQLStore) Resolve(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID) (Trigger, []byte, error) {
	row, err := st.q.ResolveTrigger(ctx, id)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Trigger{}, nil, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, nil, fmt.Errorf("resolve trigger: %w", err)
	}
	t, err := toTrigger(row)
	return t, row.Secret, err
}

func (st *SQLStore) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, f ListFilter) ([]Trigger, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ListTriggers(ctx, gen.ListTriggersParams{OrgID: orgID, TemplateID: db.NullUUID(f.TemplateID), RepoID: db.NullUUID(f.RepoID)})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list triggers: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Trigger, 0, len(rows))
	for _, r := range rows {
		t, err := toTrigger(r)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out = append(out, t)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) Update(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, revision int64) (Trigger, error) {
	mapping, rl, err := encode(t)
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, err
	}
	var out Trigger
	err = st.d.WithWriteTx(ctx, t.OrgID, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		row, err := st.q.UpdateTrigger(ctx, gen.UpdateTriggerParams{
			OrgID: t.OrgID, ID: t.ID, Revision: revision, Name: t.Name, Enabled: t.Enabled, Preset: string(t.Preset),
			Auth: string(t.Auth()), SignatureHeader: t.SignatureHeader(), TimestampHeader: t.TimestampHeader,
			AllowCidrs: cidrsOrEmpty(t.AllowCIDRs), Mapping: mapping, OnActive: string(t.OnActive), RateLimit: rl,
		})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if out, err = toTrigger(row); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return st.fill(ctx, t.OrgID, t.ID, t.RateLimit)
	})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		if _, gerr := st.Get(ctx, t.OrgID, t.ID); gerr != nil {
			return Trigger{}, gerr
		}
		return Trigger{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	if db.IsUniqueViolation(err) {
		return Trigger{}, ErrNameTaken
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("update trigger: %w", err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) SetSecret(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, sealed []byte) (Trigger, error) {
	row, err := st.q.SetTriggerSecret(ctx, gen.SetTriggerSecretParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id, Secret: sealed})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Trigger{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("set trigger secret: %w", err)
	}
	return toTrigger(row)
}

func (st *SQLStore) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := st.q.DeleteTrigger(ctx, gen.DeleteTriggerParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete trigger: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

func (st *SQLStore) Deliveries(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, limit int) ([]Delivery, error) {
	rows, err := st.q.ListTriggerDeliveries(ctx, gen.ListTriggerDeliveriesParams{OrgID: orgID, TriggerID: id, Lim: int32(limit)}) //nolint:gosec // clamped by the API
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list deliveries: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Delivery, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = Delivery{ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, TriggerID: r.TriggerID, At: r.At, SourceIP: r.SourceIp,
			Outcome: Outcome(r.Outcome), Reason: r.Reason, JobGroupID: db.UUIDPtr(r.JobGroupID)}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// TakeToken takes one token from the shared bucket; on a miss it reads the
// bucket once to compute Retry-After.
func (st *SQLStore) TakeToken(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, rl RateLimit, now time.Time) (bool, time.Duration, error) {
	capacity, refill := capacityAndRefill(rl)
	_, err := st.q.TakeTriggerToken(ctx, gen.TakeTriggerTokenParams{Capacity: capacity, Now: now, Refill: refill, OrgID: orgID, TriggerID: id})
	if err == nil {
		return true, 0, nil
	}
	if !errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return false, 0, fmt.Errorf("take trigger token: %w", err)
	}
	b, err := st.q.GetTriggerBucket(ctx, gen.GetTriggerBucketParams{OrgID: orgID, TriggerID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return false, 0, fmt.Errorf("read trigger bucket: %w", err)
	}
	current := math.Min(capacity, b.Tokens+math.Max(now.Sub(b.UpdatedAt).Seconds(), 0)*refill)
	wait := time.Duration(math.Ceil((1-current)/refill)) * time.Second
	if wait < time.Second {
		wait = time.Second
	}
	return false, wait, nil
}

// RecordDelivery appends a delivery (throttled for rejections) and trims
// the trigger's log to its newest 500 rows.
func (st *SQLStore) RecordDelivery(ctx context.Context, d Delivery, throttle bool) error {
	if err := st.q.InsertTriggerDelivery(ctx, gen.InsertTriggerDeliveryParams{
		ID: d.ID, OrgID: d.OrgID, TriggerID: d.TriggerID, At: d.At, SourceIp: d.SourceIP,
		Outcome: string(d.Outcome), Reason: d.Reason, JobGroupID: db.NullUUID(d.JobGroupID), Throttle: throttle,
	}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("record delivery: %w", err)
	}
	if err := st.q.TrimTriggerDeliveries(ctx, gen.TrimTriggerDeliveriesParams{OrgID: d.OrgID, TriggerID: d.TriggerID}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("trim deliveries: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// cidrsOrEmpty keeps allow_cidrs NOT NULL: a nil slice would bind NULL.
func cidrsOrEmpty(p []netip.Prefix) []netip.Prefix {
	if p == nil {
		return []netip.Prefix{}
	}
	return p
}

func encode(t Trigger) (mapping, rl []byte, err error) {
	if mapping, err = json.Marshal(t.Mapping); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	if rl, err = json.Marshal(t.RateLimit); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	return mapping, rl, nil
}

func toTrigger(r gen.JobTrigger) (Trigger, error) {
	t := Trigger{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, Kind: Kind(r.Kind), TemplateID: db.UUIDPtr(r.TemplateID), RepoID: db.UUIDPtr(r.RepoID),
		Name: r.Name, Enabled: r.Enabled, Preset: Preset(r.Preset), TimestampHeader: r.TimestampHeader,
		AllowCIDRs: r.AllowCidrs, OnActive: OnActive(r.OnActive), CreatedBy: db.UUIDPtr(r.CreatedBy),
		CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: r.UpdatedAt, Revision: r.Revision,
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(r.Mapping, &t.Mapping); err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("decode trigger mapping: %w", err)
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(r.RateLimit, &t.RateLimit); err != nil {
		return Trigger{}, fmt.Errorf("decode trigger rate limit: %w", err)
	}
	return t, nil
}
```

`internal/triggers/store_sql_test.go`:

```go
package triggers_test

import (
	"errors"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/triggers"
)

// seedTemplate creates an org, an editor playbook and a template through
// the raw pool (fixtures bypass RLS).
func seedTemplate(t *testing.T, d *db.DB, slug string) (org, templateID uuid.UUID) {
	t.Helper()
	q := gen.New(d.Pool())
	o, err := q.CreateOrganization(ctx, gen.CreateOrganizationParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Slug: slug, Name: slug})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	inline := "- hosts: all\n  tasks: []\n"
	pb, err := q.CreatePlaybook(ctx, gen.CreatePlaybookParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: o.ID, Inline: &inline, Source: "editor"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tmpl, err := q.InsertTemplate(ctx, gen.InsertTemplateParams{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: o.ID, Name: "deploy", PlaybookID: pb.ID, Scope: []byte(`{}`), ExtraVars: []byte(`{}`)})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return o.ID, tmpl.ID
}

func storedTrigger(org, templateID uuid.UUID, rl triggers.RateLimit) triggers.Trigger {
	return triggers.Trigger{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, Kind: triggers.KindTemplateRun, TemplateID: &templateID,
		Name: "t-" + uuid.NewString()[:8], Enabled: true, Preset: triggers.PresetPulsix, OnActive: triggers.OnActiveQueue, RateLimit: rl}
}

func TestStoreRoundTripAndResolveWithoutScope(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org, tmpl := seedTemplate(t, d, "trig-store")
	st := triggers.NewSQLStore(d)
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org)
	in := storedTrigger(org, tmpl, triggers.DefaultRateLimit)
	created, err := st.Create(scoped, in, []byte("sealed"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if created.Revision != 1 || created.Auth() != triggers.AuthHMAC {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v", created)
	}
	// The hook path: no org scope bound.
	got, secret, err := st.Resolve(ctx, in.ID)
	if err != nil || got.OrgID != org || string(secret) != "sealed" {
		t.Fatalf("Resolve = %+v, %q, %v", got, secret, err)
	}
	if _, _, err := st.Resolve(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown: %v", err)
	}
	dup := storedTrigger(org, tmpl, triggers.DefaultRateLimit)
	dup.Name = in.Name
	if _, err := st.Create(scoped, dup, []byte("x")); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNameTaken) {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate name: %v", err)
	}
	// Deleting the template cascades.
	if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM job_templates WHERE id = $1`, tmpl); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := st.Get(scoped, org, in.ID); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("after template delete: %v", err)
	}
}

// TestRateLimitIsSharedAcrossManagers: two stores ("managers") over one
// database, 20 concurrent takes against a 10/min bucket: exactly 10 pass.
func TestRateLimitIsSharedAcrossManagers(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org, tmpl := seedTemplate(t, d, "trig-bucket")
	rl := triggers.RateLimit{Count: 10, Per: "1m"}
	a, b := triggers.NewSQLStore(d), triggers.NewSQLStore(d)
	in := storedTrigger(org, tmpl, rl)
	if _, err := a.Create(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), in, []byte("s")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	now := time.Now()
	var mu sync.Mutex
	passed, limited := 0, 0
	var retry time.Duration
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < 20; i++ {
		st := a
		if i%2 == 1 {
			st = b
		}
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			ok, wait, err := st.TakeToken(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), org, in.ID, rl, now)
			mu.Lock()
			defer mu.Unlock()
			if err != nil {
				t.Error(err)
				return
			}
			if ok {
				passed++
			} else {
				limited++
				retry = wait
			}
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	if passed != 10 || limited != 10 {
		t.Fatalf("passed %d, limited %d; want 10/10", passed, limited)
	}
	if retry < time.Second || retry > 7*time.Second {
		t.Fatalf("retry after = %v, want about 6s (one token per 6s)", retry)
	}
	// A minute later the bucket is full again.
	if ok, _, _ := a.TakeToken(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), org, in.ID, rl, now.Add(time.Minute)); !ok {
		t.Fatal("the bucket did not refill")
	}
}

func TestDeliveriesAreThrottledAndTrimmed(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org, tmpl := seedTemplate(t, d, "trig-deliveries")
	st := triggers.NewSQLStore(d)
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org)
	in := storedTrigger(org, tmpl, triggers.DefaultRateLimit)
	if _, err := st.Create(scoped, in, []byte("s")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	at := time.Now()
	for i := 0; i < 3; i++ {
		if err := st.RecordDelivery(scoped, triggers.Delivery{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, TriggerID: in.ID, At: at.Add(time.Duration(i) * time.Second), SourceIP: "203.0.113.7", Outcome: triggers.OutcomeRejectedAuth}, true); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	list, err := st.Deliveries(scoped, org, in.ID, 50)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("throttled rejections = %d, %v; want 1 row per outcome and minute", len(list), err)
	}
	for i := 0; i < 505; i++ {
		if err := st.RecordDelivery(scoped, triggers.Delivery{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, TriggerID: in.ID, At: at.Add(time.Duration(i) * time.Millisecond), Outcome: triggers.OutcomeTriggered}, false); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	var count int
	if err := d.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM trigger_deliveries WHERE trigger_id = $1`, in.ID).Scan(&count); err != nil || count != 500 {
		t.Fatalf("rows after trim = %d, %v", count, err)
	}
}
```

### Task 10: Webhook receive pipeline and the hook endpoints

This task covers spec §3 "Endpoint" and §9's per-IP limit. The two public routes run the fixed check order: body ≤ 1 MiB → per-source-address limit (`HookIPLimit` = 300 deliveries per minute and address over all triggers, in process, nothing recorded) → trigger exists and enabled → allowlist → signature/token (+ timestamp window) → rate limit → mapping → validation → `on_active` → enqueue. Unknown, disabled, wrong-IP and wrong-signature calls all answer the same empty 404. Every call that resolves to a trigger is recorded, and every run is audited as `webhook:<id>` with its source IP.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/triggers/receive.go` (`Receive`, `TestCall`, `Request`, `Result`, `MaxBodyBytes`)
- Create: `internal/api/hook_handlers.go`
- Modify: `internal/api/api.go` (`Deps.Triggers *triggers.Service`, validate, `registerHookRoutes(hapi, deps)` on the **root** API next to `registerSetupRoutes`), `internal/api/apitest/apitest.go`, `internal/app/app.go` (build `triggers.NewService(triggers.Deps{Store: triggers.NewSQLStore(appDB), Templates: templatesSvc, Repos: reposSvc, Box: box, Runs: jobsSvc, Audit: auditRec, PublicURL: cfg.PublicURL, IPLimit: clientip.NewLimiter(triggers.HookIPLimit, time.Minute, time.Now), Now: time.Now, Rand: rand.Reader})`), `internal/api/audit_coverage_test.go` (allowlist both hook routes), `internal/api/openapi_doc_test.go` (the two paths), `web/src/api/schema.d.ts`
- Test: `internal/triggers/receive_test.go` (fakes), `internal/triggers/lifecycle_test.go` (fakes + integration), `internal/api/hook_handlers_test.go` (the API matrix)
- Docs (noix-docs): `reference/api.md` gains "Webhook endpoints" (status codes, headers, signature format, timestamp rule)

**Interfaces:**

```go
const MaxBodyBytes = 1 << 20
const HookIPLimit = 300 // deliveries per minute and source address, over all triggers

type Request struct {
	TriggerID string                   // raw path segment
	Kind      Kind                     // which endpoint was called
	Body      []byte                   // raw body, ≤ MaxBodyBytes (the HTTP layer enforces)
	Header    func(name string) string // case-insensitive header lookup
	SourceIP  netip.Addr               // clientip-resolved
	Test      bool                     // in-process test call: allowlist skipped, reason "test call"
}
type Result struct {
	Outcome    Outcome
	RunID      *uuid.UUID    // the first job of the run (triggered)
	Field      string        // invalid_payload: the offending field, never its value
	RetryAfter time.Duration // rate_limited
}
func (s *Service) Receive(ctx context.Context, req Request) (Result, error) // error only for internal failures
func (s *Service) TestCall(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, body []byte) (Result, error)
```

HTTP mapping (`hookResponse`):

| Outcome | Response |
|---|---|
| triggered | 202 `{"run_id": "<job id>"}` |
| skipped_active | 202 `{"status": "skipped"}` |
| not_found, disabled, rejected_ip, rejected_auth | 404, problem without detail (identical bytes) |
| rate_limited | 429, `Retry-After: <seconds>` |
| invalid_payload | 422 `"the payload could not be mapped"`, one detail `{location: <field>, message: "missing or invalid value"}` |
| failed | 409 `"the trigger's target cannot run right now"` |
| body > 1 MiB | 413 (huma, before the handler) |

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing receive unit tests**

`internal/triggers/receive_test.go` reuses the fakes from `service_test.go`. It extends them with a `fakeRuns` and a `fakeAudit`, plus a `recording` store wrapper that captures deliveries and can refuse tokens:

```go
package triggers_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/audit"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/clientip"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/triggers"
)

type fakeRuns struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	requests []jobs.RunRequest
	active   bool
	err      error
}

func (f *fakeRuns) EnqueueRunWith(_ context.Context, orgID, templateID uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) ([]jobs.Job, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if f.err != nil {
		return nil, f.err
	}
	f.requests = append(f.requests, req)
	return []jobs.Job{{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: orgID, TemplateID: &templateID, RunGroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}}, nil
}

func (f *fakeRuns) EnqueueSyncWith(_ context.Context, orgID, _ uuid.UUID, req jobs.RunRequest) (jobs.Job, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.requests = append(f.requests, req)
	return jobs.Job{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: orgID, RunGroupID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}, f.err
}

func (f *fakeRuns) HasActiveRun(context.Context, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID) (bool, error)  { return f.active, nil }
func (f *fakeRuns) HasActiveSync(context.Context, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID) (bool, error) { return f.active, nil }

type fakeAudit struct{ events []audit.Event }

func (f *fakeAudit) Record(_ context.Context, e audit.Event) error {
	f.events = append(f.events, e)
	return nil
}

type recording struct {
	*fakeStore
	deliveries []triggers.Delivery
	throttled  []bool
	noTokens   bool
}

func (r *recording) TakeToken(context.Context, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID, triggers.RateLimit, time.Time) (bool, time.Duration, error) {
	if r.noTokens {
		return false, 6 * time.Second, nil
	}
	return true, 0, nil
}

func (r *recording) RecordDelivery(_ context.Context, d triggers.Delivery, throttle bool) error {
	r.deliveries = append(r.deliveries, d)
	r.throttled = append(r.throttled, throttle)
	return nil
}

type receiveWorld struct {
	*world
	rec   *recording
	runs  *fakeRuns
	audit *fakeAudit
}

func newReceiveWorld(t *testing.T) *receiveWorld {
	t.Helper()
	w := newWorld(t)
	rw := &receiveWorld{world: w, rec: &recording{fakeStore: w.store}, runs: &fakeRuns{}, audit: &fakeAudit{}}
	rw.svc = triggers.NewService(triggers.Deps{
		Store: rw.rec, Templates: w.tmpls, Repos: w.repos, Box: fakeBox{},
		Runs: rw.runs, Audit: rw.audit, PublicURL: "https://pulsix.example.test",
		Now: func() time.Time { return time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC) }, Rand: rand.Reader,
	})
	return rw
}

func (rw *receiveWorld) signed(t *testing.T, c triggers.Created, body string) triggers.Request {
	t.Helper()
	h := http.Header{}
	h.Set(c.Trigger.SignatureHeader(), triggers.Sign(c.Trigger.Preset, c.Secret, "", []byte(body)))
	return triggers.Request{TriggerID: c.Trigger.ID.String(), Kind: c.Trigger.Kind, Body: []byte(body), Header: h.Get, SourceIP: netip.MustParseAddr("140.82.112.5")}
}

func TestReceiveTriggersARunWithMappedOverrides(t *testing.T) {
	rw := newReceiveWorld(t)
	c, err := rw.svc.Create(ctx, rw.org, rw.actor, rw.templateInput())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	res, err := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, rw.signed(t, c, `{"ref":"v1.2.3"}`))
	if err != nil || res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeTriggered || res.RunID == nil {
		t.Fatalf("Receive = %+v, %v", res, err)
	}
	req := rw.runs.requests[0]
	if req.Trigger != jobs.TriggerWebhook || *req.TriggerRef != c.Trigger.ID || req.Overrides.ExtraVars["release"] != "v1.2.3" {
		t.Fatalf("run request = %+v", req)
	}
	d := rw.rec.deliveries[0]
	if d.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeTriggered || d.SourceIP != "140.82.112.5" || d.JobGroupID == nil || rw.rec.throttled[0] {
		t.Fatalf("delivery = %+v", d)
	}
	e := rw.audit.events[0]
	if e.ActorKind != audit.ActorKindWebhook || *e.ActorID != c.Trigger.ID || e.Action != "webhook.run" || e.Meta["source_ip"] != "140.82.112.5" {
		t.Fatalf("audit = %+v", e)
	}
}

func TestReceiveRejections(t *testing.T) {
	rw := newReceiveWorld(t)
	in := rw.templateInput()
	in.AllowCIDRs = []string{"140.82.112.0/20"}
	c, _ := rw.svc.Create(ctx, rw.org, rw.actor, in)
	good := rw.signed(t, c, `{"ref":"x"}`)

	cases := []struct {
		name    string
		req     func() triggers.Request
		outcome triggers.Outcome
		stored  bool
	}{
		{"unknown id", func() triggers.Request { r := good; r.TriggerID = uuid.NewString(); return r }, triggers.OutcomeNotFound, false},
		{"garbage id", func() triggers.Request { r := good; r.TriggerID = "../etc"; return r }, triggers.OutcomeNotFound, false},
		{"wrong endpoint", func() triggers.Request { r := good; r.Kind = triggers.KindRepoSync; return r }, triggers.OutcomeNotFound, false},
		{"outside the allowlist", func() triggers.Request { r := good; r.SourceIP = netip.MustParseAddr("203.0.113.9"); return r }, triggers.OutcomeRejectedIP, true},
		{"bad signature", func() triggers.Request {
			r := good
			h := http.Header{}
			h.Set(c.Trigger.SignatureHeader(), "sha256=00")
			r.Header = h.Get
			return r
		}, triggers.OutcomeRejectedAuth, true},
		{"not json", func() triggers.Request { return rw.signed(t, c, `ref=x`) }, triggers.OutcomeInvalidPayload, true},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			before := len(rw.rec.deliveries)
			res, err := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, tc.req())
			if err != nil || res.Outcome != tc.outcome {
				t.Fatalf("Receive = %+v, %v; want %s", res, err, tc.outcome)
			}
			if stored := len(rw.rec.deliveries) > before; stored != tc.stored {
				t.Fatalf("stored = %v, want %v", stored, tc.stored)
			}
			if tc.stored && !rw.rec.throttled[len(rw.rec.throttled)-1] {
				t.Fatal("rejections are recorded throttled")
			}
		})
	}
	if len(rw.runs.requests) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("no rejection may enqueue")
	}
}

func TestReceiveDisabledRateLimitedSkippedAndFailed(t *testing.T) {
	rw := newReceiveWorld(t)
	in := rw.templateInput()
	in.OnActive = triggers.OnActiveSkip
	c, _ := rw.svc.Create(ctx, rw.org, rw.actor, in)

	rw.runs.active = true
	if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, rw.signed(t, c, `{}`)); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeSkippedActive {
		t.Fatalf("on_active skip = %+v", res)
	}
	rw.runs.active = false
	rw.runs.err = jobs.ErrEmptyScope
	if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, rw.signed(t, c, `{}`)); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeFailed {
		t.Fatalf("enqueue failure = %+v", res)
	}
	if last := rw.rec.deliveries[len(rw.rec.deliveries)-1]; last.Reason != "the template's scope resolves to no host" {
		t.Fatalf("failure reason = %q", last.Reason)
	}
	rw.runs.err = nil
	rw.rec.noTokens = true
	if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, rw.signed(t, c, `{}`)); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeRateLimited || res.RetryAfter != 6*time.Second {
		t.Fatalf("rate limited = %+v", res)
	}
	rw.rec.noTokens = false
	in.Enabled = false
	if _, err := rw.svc.Update(ctx, rw.org, c.Trigger.ID, 1, in); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, rw.signed(t, c, `{}`)); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeDisabled {
		t.Fatalf("disabled = %+v", res)
	}
}

func TestReceiveRepoSyncAndTestCall(t *testing.T) {
	rw := newReceiveWorld(t)
	c, err := rw.svc.Create(ctx, rw.org, rw.actor, triggers.Input{Kind: triggers.KindRepoSync, RepoID: &rw.repo, Name: "sync", Enabled: true, Preset: triggers.PresetGitLab,
		AllowCIDRs: []string{"10.0.0.0/8"}, TimestampHeader: "X-Pulsix-Timestamp"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// The test call signs with the real secret and a fresh timestamp; the
	// allowlist (which would refuse 127.0.0.1) is not evaluated.
	res, err := rw.svc.TestCall(ctx, rw.org, c.Trigger.ID, nil)
	if err != nil || res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeTriggered {
		t.Fatalf("TestCall = %+v, %v", res, err)
	}
	if rw.rec.deliveries[0].Reason != "test call" || rw.audit.events[0].ObjectType != "repo" {
		t.Fatalf("delivery = %+v, audit = %+v", rw.rec.deliveries[0], rw.audit.events[0])
	}
	if _, err := rw.svc.TestCall(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), c.Trigger.ID, nil); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("another org's test call: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestReceiveLimitsDeliveriesPerSourceAddress(t *testing.T) {
	rw := newReceiveWorld(t)
	now := func() time.Time { return time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC) }
	rw.svc = triggers.NewService(triggers.Deps{
		Store: rw.rec, Templates: rw.tmpls, Repos: rw.repos, Box: fakeBox{},
		Runs: rw.runs, Audit: rw.audit, PublicURL: "https://pulsix.example.test",
		IPLimit: clientip.NewLimiter(2, time.Minute, now), Now: now, Rand: rand.Reader,
	})
	c, err := rw.svc.Create(ctx, rw.org, rw.actor, rw.templateInput())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	req := rw.signed(t, c, `{"ref":"v1"}`)
	unknown := triggers.Request{TriggerID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()).String(), Kind: triggers.KindTemplateRun, Header: http.Header{}.Get, SourceIP: req.SourceIP}
	if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, unknown); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("unknown id = %+v", res)
	}
	if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, req); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeTriggered {
		t.Fatalf("second delivery = %+v", res)
	}
	res, err := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, req)
	if err != nil || res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeRateLimited || res.RetryAfter != time.Minute {
		t.Fatalf("third delivery from the same address = %+v, %v", res, err)
	}
	if len(rw.rec.deliveries) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("an address-limited delivery must record nothing: %+v", rw.rec.deliveries)
	}
	// Another source and the in-process test call are not affected.
	other := req
	other.SourceIP = netip.MustParseAddr("192.0.2.9")
	if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, other); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeTriggered {
		t.Fatalf("another address = %+v", res)
	}
	if res, err := rw.svc.TestCall(ctx, rw.org, c.Trigger.ID, nil); err != nil || res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeTriggered {
		t.Fatalf("test call = %+v, %v", res, err)
	}
}
```

```go
package triggers_test

func TestServiceListDeleteAndDeliveries(t *testing.T) {

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if list, err := w.svc.List(ctx, w.org, triggers.ListFilter{TemplateID: &w.template}); err != nil || len(list) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("List = %v, %v", list, err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Deliveries(ctx, w.org, c.Trigger.ID, 50); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Deliveries(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), c.Trigger.ID, 50); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("another org's deliveries: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.RotateSecret(ctx, w.org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("rotate unknown: %v", err)
	}
	if err := w.svc.Delete(ctx, w.org, c.Trigger.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Get(ctx, w.org, c.Trigger.ID); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("after delete: %v", err)

}

func TestReceiveRepoSyncSkipAndFailure(t *testing.T) {
	rw := newReceiveWorld(t)
	c, err := rw.svc.Create(ctx, rw.org, rw.actor, triggers.Input{Kind: triggers.KindRepoSync, RepoID: &rw.repo, Name: "sync", Enabled: true,
		Preset: triggers.PresetBearer, OnActive: triggers.OnActiveSkip})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	rw.runs.active = true
	if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, rw.signed(t, c, `{}`)); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeSkippedActive {
		t.Fatalf("skip = %+v", res)
	}
	rw.runs.active = false
	for cause, reason := range map[error]string{
		jobs.ErrNoZone:         "the organization has no default zone",
		jobs.ErrHostKeyChanged: "every targeted host has a changed host key",
		errors.New("boom"):     "could not enqueue (see the manager log)",
	} {
		rw.runs.err = cause
		if res, _ := rw.svc.Receive(ctx, rw.signed(t, c, `{}`)); res.Outcome != triggers.OutcomeFailed {
			t.Fatalf("%v: %+v", cause, res)
		}
		if last := rw.rec.deliveries[len(rw.rec.deliveries)-1]; last.Reason != reason {
			t.Fatalf("%v: reason %q", cause, last.Reason)
		}
	}
}

func TestStoreUpdateSecretListDelete(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org, tmpl := seedTemplate(t, d, "trig-lifecycle")
	st := triggers.NewSQLStore(d)
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org)
	in := storedTrigger(org, tmpl, triggers.DefaultRateLimit)
	created, err := st.Create(scoped, in, []byte("one"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	created.Enabled = false
	if _, err := st.Update(scoped, created, created.Revision+1); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale update: %v", err)
	}
	up, err := st.Update(scoped, created, created.Revision)
	if err != nil || up.Enabled || up.Revision != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("update = %+v, %v", up, err)
	}
	unknown := created
	unknown.ID = uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if _, err := st.Update(scoped, unknown, 1); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("update unknown: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := st.SetSecret(scoped, org, created.ID, []byte("two")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, secret, _ := st.Resolve(ctx, created.ID); string(secret) != "two" {
		t.Fatalf("secret after rotation = %q", secret)
	}
	if _, err := st.SetSecret(scoped, org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), []byte("x")); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("set secret unknown: %v", err)
	}
	if list, err := st.List(scoped, org, triggers.ListFilter{TemplateID: &tmpl}); err != nil || len(list) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("List = %v, %v", list, err)
	}
	other := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if list, _ := st.List(scoped, org, triggers.ListFilter{RepoID: &other}); len(list) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("repo filter = %v", list)
	}
	if err := st.Delete(scoped, org, created.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := st.Delete(scoped, org, created.ID); !errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("second delete: %v", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `receive.go`**

```go
package triggers

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/audit"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/templates"
)

// MaxBodyBytes is the hook endpoints' body limit (spec §3).
const MaxBodyBytes = 1 << 20

// HookIPLimit is how many deliveries one source address may send per
// minute, summed over every trigger and both hook endpoints. It sits well
// above a busy CI system's rate (GitHub delivers from a shared address
// pool) and exists so a single source cannot drive unbounded lookups.
const HookIPLimit = 300

// Request is one call to a hook endpoint.
type Request struct {
	TriggerID string
	Kind      Kind
	Body      []byte
	Header    func(name string) string
	SourceIP  netip.Addr
	Test      bool
}

// Result is what the endpoint answers; see the HTTP mapping in hook_handlers.go.
type Result struct {
	Outcome    Outcome
	RunID      *uuid.UUID
	Field      string
	RetryAfter time.Duration
}

// Receive runs spec §3's check order. The error is reserved for internal
// failures; every refusal is an Outcome.
func (s *Service) Receive(ctx context.Context, req Request) (Result, error) {
	// The per-address limit comes first: it costs no database round trip
	// and records nothing, so a flood of unknown ids stays cheap.
	if !req.Test && s.d.IPLimit != nil && !s.d.IPLimit.Allow(req.SourceIP) {
		return Result{Outcome: OutcomeRateLimited, RetryAfter: s.d.IPLimit.Window()}, nil
	}
	id, err := uuid.Parse(req.TriggerID)
	if err != nil {
		return Result{Outcome: OutcomeNotFound}, nil
	}
	t, sealed, err := s.d.Store.Resolve(ctx, id)
	if errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
		return Result{Outcome: OutcomeNotFound}, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, err
	}
	if t.Kind != req.Kind {
		return Result{Outcome: OutcomeNotFound}, nil
	}
	ctx = db.WithOrgScope(ctx, t.OrgID)
	now := s.d.Now()
	d := Delivery{ID: s.newID(), OrgID: t.OrgID, TriggerID: t.ID, At: now}
	if req.SourceIP.IsValid() {
		d.SourceIP = req.SourceIP.String()
	}
	if req.Test {
		d.Reason = "test call"
	}
	if !t.Enabled {
		return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeDisabled}, "", true)
	}
	if !req.Test && !allowed(t.AllowCIDRs, req.SourceIP) {
		return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeRejectedIP}, "", true)
	}
	secret, err := s.d.Box.OpenAAD(sealed, AAD(t.OrgID, t.ID))
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("open trigger secret: %w", err)
	}
	if !verify(t, string(secret), req.Header, req.Body, now) {
		return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeRejectedAuth}, "", true)
	}
	ok, wait, err := s.d.Store.TakeToken(ctx, t.OrgID, t.ID, t.RateLimit, now)
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, err
	}
	if !ok {
		return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeRateLimited, RetryAfter: wait}, "", true)
	}
	if t.Kind == KindRepoSync {
		return s.syncRepo(ctx, t, d)
	}
	return s.runTemplate(ctx, t, d, req.Body)
}

// TestCall signs body (default {}) with the trigger's real secret and runs
// it through Receive from 127.0.0.1 with the allowlist skipped.
func (s *Service) TestCall(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, body []byte) (Result, error) {
	t, err := s.d.Store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, err
	}
	_, sealed, err := s.d.Store.Resolve(ctx, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, err
	}
	secret, err := s.d.Box.OpenAAD(sealed, AAD(orgID, id))
	if err != nil {
		return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("open trigger secret: %w", err)
	}
	if len(body) == 0 {
		body = []byte("{}")
	}
	h := http.Header{}
	ts := ""
	if t.TimestampHeader != "" {
		ts = strconv.FormatInt(s.d.Now().Unix(), 10)
		h.Set(t.TimestampHeader, ts)
	}
	h.Set(t.SignatureHeader(), Sign(t.Preset, string(secret), ts, body))
	return s.Receive(ctx, Request{TriggerID: id.String(), Kind: t.Kind, Body: body, Header: h.Get,
		SourceIP: netip.MustParseAddr("127.0.0.1"), Test: true})
}

func (s *Service) runTemplate(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, d Delivery, body []byte) (Result, error) {
	ov, field, err := s.mapPayload(ctx, t, body)
	if err != nil {
		if field == "" {
			return Result{}, err
		}
		return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeInvalidPayload, Field: field}, "invalid value at "+field, true)
	}
	if t.OnActive == OnActiveSkip {
		active, err := s.d.Runs.HasActiveRun(ctx, t.OrgID, *t.TemplateID)
		if err != nil {
			return Result{}, err
		}
		if active {
			return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeSkippedActive}, "a run of the template is still active", false)
		}
	}
	var runOv *jobs.RunOverrides
	if !ov.Empty() {
		runOv = &jobs.RunOverrides{Limit: ov.Limit, ExtraVars: ov.ExtraVars}
	}
	ref := t.ID
	created, err := s.d.Runs.EnqueueRunWith(ctx, t.OrgID, *t.TemplateID, jobs.RunRequest{Trigger: jobs.TriggerWebhook, TriggerRef: &ref, Overrides: runOv})
	if err != nil {
		s.d.Log.ErrorContext(ctx, "webhook run could not be enqueued", "trigger", t.ID, "org", t.OrgID, "err", err)
		return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeFailed}, failureReason(err), false)
	}
	runID := created[0].ID
	d.JobGroupID = &created[0].RunGroupID
	s.auditRun(ctx, t, d, "job_template", t.TemplateID.String())
	return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeTriggered, RunID: &runID}, "", false)
}

func (s *Service) syncRepo(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, d Delivery) (Result, error) {
	if t.OnActive == OnActiveSkip {
		active, err := s.d.Runs.HasActiveSync(ctx, t.OrgID, *t.RepoID)
		if err != nil {
			return Result{}, err
		}
		if active {
			return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeSkippedActive}, "a sync of the repository is still active", false)
		}
	}
	ref := t.ID
	j, err := s.d.Runs.EnqueueSyncWith(ctx, t.OrgID, *t.RepoID, jobs.RunRequest{Trigger: jobs.TriggerWebhook, TriggerRef: &ref})
	if err != nil {
		s.d.Log.ErrorContext(ctx, "webhook sync could not be enqueued", "trigger", t.ID, "org", t.OrgID, "err", err)
		return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeFailed}, failureReason(err), false)
	}
	runID := j.ID
	d.JobGroupID = &j.RunGroupID
	s.auditRun(ctx, t, d, "repo", t.RepoID.String())
	return s.finish(ctx, d, Result{Outcome: OutcomeTriggered, RunID: &runID}, "", false)
}

// mapPayload applies the mapping and validates the result like a manual
// run (secrets refused). A non-empty field marks a payload problem; an
// empty field with an error is an internal failure.
func (s *Service) mapPayload(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, body []byte) (templates.Overrides, string, error) {
	if t.Mapping.empty() {
		return templates.Overrides{}, "", nil
	}
	c, err := compileMapping(t.Mapping)
	if err != nil {
		return templates.Overrides{}, "mapping", err
	}
	var doc any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &doc); err != nil {
		return templates.Overrides{}, "body", errors.New("the body is not JSON")
	}
	ov, field, err := c.apply(doc)
	if err != nil || ov.Empty() {
		return ov, field, err
	}
	checked, err := s.d.Templates.ValidateOverrides(ctx, t.OrgID, *t.TemplateID, ov, templates.OverrideOptions{RefuseSecrets: true})
	var ve *schema.ValidationError
	switch {
	case err == nil:
		return checked, "", nil
	case errors.As(err, &ve) && len(ve.Problems) > 0:
		return templates.Overrides{}, "extra_vars." + ve.Problems[0].Path, err
	case errors.Is(err, templates.ErrInvalidInput):
		return templates.Overrides{}, "limit", err
	}
	return templates.Overrides{}, "", err
}

func (s *Service) finish(ctx context.Context, d Delivery, res Result, reason string, throttle bool) (Result, error) {
	d.Outcome = res.Outcome
	switch {
	case d.Reason == "":
		d.Reason = reason
	case reason != "":
		d.Reason += "; " + reason
	}
	if err := s.d.Store.RecordDelivery(ctx, d, throttle); err != nil {
		return Result{}, err
	}
	return res, nil
}

func (s *Service) auditRun(ctx context.Context, t Trigger, d Delivery, objectType, objectID string) {
	if s.d.Audit == nil {
		return
	}
	orgID, actor := t.OrgID, t.ID
	meta := map[string]any{"trigger_id": t.ID.String(), "source_ip": d.SourceIP}
	if d.JobGroupID != nil {
		meta["job_group_id"] = d.JobGroupID.String()
	}
	if d.Reason == "test call" {
		meta["test_call"] = true
	}
	if err := s.d.Audit.Record(ctx, audit.Event{
		OrgID: &orgID, ActorKind: audit.ActorKindWebhook, ActorID: &actor, Action: "webhook.run",
		ObjectType: objectType, ObjectID: objectID, Summary: fmt.Sprintf("webhook %q started a run", t.Name), Meta: meta,
	}); err != nil {
		s.d.Log.ErrorContext(ctx, "audit webhook run", "trigger", t.ID, "err", err)
	}
}

func allowed(cidrs []netip.Prefix, ip netip.Addr) bool {
	if len(cidrs) == 0 {
		return true
	}
	if !ip.IsValid() {
		return false
	}
	for _, p := range cidrs {
		if p.Contains(ip) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// failureReason is the generic reason a failed delivery records.
func failureReason(err error) string {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrEmptyScope):
		return "the template's scope resolves to no host"
	case errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrHostKeyChanged):
		return "every targeted host has a changed host key"
	case errors.Is(err, jobs.ErrNoZone):
		return "the organization has no default zone"
	}
	return "could not enqueue (see the manager log)"
}
```

`internal/api/hook_handlers_test.go` reuses `newRunFixture`. The fixture's template has no schema and the extra var `deploy_tag`, and httptest's peer is 127.0.0.1.

```go
package api_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/triggers"
)

func (f runFixture) createTrigger(t *testing.T, mutate func(*triggers.Input)) triggers.Created {
	t.Helper()
	in := triggers.Input{Kind: triggers.KindTemplateRun, TemplateID: &f.templateID, Name: "hook-" + uuid.NewString()[:8], Enabled: true,
		Preset: triggers.PresetPulsix, Mapping: triggers.Mapping{ExtraVars: map[string]string{"deploy_tag": "$.ref"}}}
	if mutate != nil {
		mutate(&in)
	}
	c, err := f.s.Deps.Triggers.Create(db.WithOrgScope(context.Background(), f.orgID), f.orgID, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return c
}

// hook posts body to url with the given headers, anonymously.
func hook(t *testing.T, url string, body []byte, headers map[string]string) *http.Response {
	t.Helper()
	req, err := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, url, bytes.NewReader(body))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	for k, v := range headers {
		req.Header.Set(k, v)
	}
	resp, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return resp
}

func signedHeaders(c triggers.Created, body []byte) map[string]string {
	return map[string]string{c.Trigger.SignatureHeader(): triggers.Sign(c.Trigger.Preset, c.Secret, "", body)}
}

func TestHookTriggersARun(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "hook-run")
	c := f.createTrigger(t, nil)
	body := []byte(`{"ref":"v1.2.3"}`)
	resp := hook(t, c.URL, body, signedHeaders(c, body))
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusAccepted {
		t.Fatalf("hook: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	var out struct {
		RunID string `json:"run_id"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &out)
	var job struct {
		Job struct {
			Trigger    string `json:"trigger"`
			TriggerRef string `json:"trigger_ref"`
		} `json:"job"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodGet, f.baseURL()+"/"+out.RunID, nil), &job)
	if job.Job.Trigger != "webhook" || job.Job.TriggerRef != c.Trigger.ID.String() {
		t.Fatalf("job = %+v", job)
	}
	body2 := mustReadBody(t, requestJSON(t, f.adminC, http.MethodGet, f.s.HTTP.URL+"/api/v1/orgs/"+f.orgSlug+"/activity?limit=100", nil))
	if !strings.Contains(body2, `"webhook.run"`) || !strings.Contains(body2, `"webhook"`) {
		t.Fatalf("activity lacks the webhook run: %s", body2)
	}
}

func TestHookUniformNotFound(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "hook-404")
	body := []byte(`{"ref":"x"}`)
	open := f.createTrigger(t, nil)
	disabled := f.createTrigger(t, func(in *triggers.Input) { in.Enabled = false })
	fenced := f.createTrigger(t, func(in *triggers.Input) { in.AllowCIDRs = []string{"10.0.0.0/8"} })
	responses := map[string]*http.Response{
		"unknown id":      hook(t, f.s.HTTP.URL+"/api/v1/hooks/jobs/"+uuid.NewString(), body, signedHeaders(open, body)),
		"malformed id":    hook(t, f.s.HTTP.URL+"/api/v1/hooks/jobs/not-a-uuid", body, signedHeaders(open, body)),
		"wrong endpoint":  hook(t, f.s.HTTP.URL+"/api/v1/hooks/repos/"+open.Trigger.ID.String(), body, signedHeaders(open, body)),
		"disabled":        hook(t, disabled.URL, body, signedHeaders(disabled, body)),
		"wrong source ip": hook(t, fenced.URL, body, signedHeaders(fenced, body)),
		"bad signature":   hook(t, open.URL, body, map[string]string{"X-Pulsix-Signature-256": "sha256=deadbeef"}),
		"no signature":    hook(t, open.URL, body, nil),
	}
	var first string
	for name, resp := range responses {
		b := mustReadBody(t, resp)
		if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s: %d %s", name, resp.StatusCode, b)
		}
		if first == "" {
			first = b
		} else if b != first {
			t.Errorf("%s: body %q differs from %q; 404s must be indistinguishable", name, b, first)
		}
	}
}

func TestHookLimitsAndPayloadErrors(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "hook-limits")
	big := bytes.Repeat([]byte("a"), triggers.MaxBodyBytes+1)
	c := f.createTrigger(t, func(in *triggers.Input) { in.RateLimit = triggers.RateLimit{Count: 1, Per: "1h"} })
	if resp := hook(t, c.URL, big, signedHeaders(c, big)); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge {
		t.Fatalf("oversized body: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	notJSON := []byte(`ref=x`)
	resp := hook(t, c.URL, notJSON, signedHeaders(c, notJSON))
	if b := mustReadBody(t, resp); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity || !strings.Contains(b, `"body"`) || strings.Contains(b, "ref=x") {
		t.Fatalf("not json: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, b)
	}
	// The 422 above consumed the one token of the hour: the next call is 429.
	body := []byte(`{"ref":"x"}`)
	resp = hook(t, c.URL, body, signedHeaders(c, body))
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusTooManyRequests {
		t.Fatalf("rate limit: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if ra, err := strconv.Atoi(resp.Header.Get("Retry-After")); err != nil || ra < 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Retry-After = %q", resp.Header.Get("Retry-After"))
	}
}

func TestHookOnActiveSkipAndTimestampWindow(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "hook-active")
	c := f.createTrigger(t, func(in *triggers.Input) {
		in.OnActive = triggers.OnActiveSkip
		in.TimestampHeader = "X-Pulsix-Timestamp"
	})
	body := []byte(`{"ref":"x"}`)
	signAt := func(at time.Time) map[string]string {
		ts := strconv.FormatInt(at.Unix(), 10)
		return map[string]string{"X-Pulsix-Timestamp": ts, c.Trigger.SignatureHeader(): triggers.Sign(c.Trigger.Preset, c.Secret, ts, body)}
	}
	if resp := hook(t, c.URL, body, signAt(time.Now())); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusAccepted {
		t.Fatalf("first: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	resp := hook(t, c.URL, body, signAt(time.Now()))
	b, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusAccepted || !strings.Contains(string(b), `"skipped"`) {
		t.Fatalf("second while active: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, b)
	}
	if resp := hook(t, c.URL, body, signAt(time.Now().Add(-10*time.Minute))); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("replayed: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}

func TestRepoHookSyncsTheRepository(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "hook-repo")

	if err != nil {

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusAccepted {
		t.Fatalf("repo hook: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := hook(t, c.URL, body, map[string]string{"X-Gitlab-Token": c.Secret + "x"}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("wrong token: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `hook_handlers.go` and wire it**

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"math"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/triggers"
)

// hookInput carries the raw body and every header; Resolve captures the
// headers and the TCP peer, which the signature check and the allowlist
// need (huma has no typed field for a header whose name the trigger picks).
type hookInput struct {
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	RawBody []byte
	header  http.Header
	remote  string
}

func (in *hookInput) Resolve(ctx huma.Context) []error {
	in.header = http.Header{}
	ctx.EachHeader(func(name, value string) { in.header.Add(name, value) })
	in.remote = ctx.RemoteAddr()
	return nil
}

type hookOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		RunID  string `json:"run_id,omitempty" doc:"The started run's first job"`
		Status string `json:"status,omitempty" enum:"skipped" doc:"skipped when on_active is skip and a run is active"`
	}
}

// registerHookRoutes mounts the two public webhook endpoints on the root
// API: no org in the path, no session or bearer requirement — the trigger's
// secret is the credential (spec §3). They are audited by the triggers
// service itself (actor webhook:<id>), hence allowlisted in the audit
// coverage test.
func registerHookRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	for _, h := range []struct {
		path, op, summary string
		kind              triggers.Kind
	}{
		{"/api/v1/hooks/jobs/{id}", "receiveJobHook", "Start a job template run from a webhook", triggers.KindTemplateRun},
		{"/api/v1/hooks/repos/{id}", "receiveRepoHook", "Sync a git repository from a webhook", triggers.KindRepoSync},
	} {
		huma.Register(hapi, huma.Operation{
			OperationID: h.op, Method: http.MethodPost, Path: h.path, Summary: h.summary, Tags: []string{"Webhooks"},
			DefaultStatus: http.StatusAccepted, MaxBodyBytes: triggers.MaxBodyBytes,
		}, func(ctx context.Context, in *hookInput) (*hookOutput, error) {
			res, err := deps.Triggers.Receive(ctx, triggers.Request{
				TriggerID: in.ID, Kind: h.kind, Body: in.RawBody, Header: in.header.Get,
				SourceIP: deps.ClientIP.Resolve(in.remote, in.header.Values("X-Forwarded-For")),
			})
			if err != nil {
				slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "webhook receive failed", "err", err)
				return nil, huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
			}
			return hookResponse(res)
		})
	}
}

// hookResponse maps an outcome to HTTP. Every refusal that could reveal
// whether a trigger exists is the same empty 404.
func hookResponse(res triggers.Result) (*hookOutput, error) {
	out := &hookOutput{}
	switch res.Outcome {
	case triggers.OutcomeTriggered:
		out.Body.RunID = res.RunID.String()
		return out, nil
	case triggers.OutcomeSkippedActive:
		out.Body.Status = "skipped"
		return out, nil
	case triggers.OutcomeRateLimited:
		secs := int(math.Ceil(res.RetryAfter.Seconds()))
		if secs < 1 {
			secs = 1
		}
		return nil, huma.ErrorWithHeaders(huma.Error429TooManyRequests("rate limited"), http.Header{"Retry-After": {strconv.Itoa(secs)}})
	case triggers.OutcomeInvalidPayload:
		return nil, huma.Error422UnprocessableEntity("the payload could not be mapped",
			&huma.ErrorDetail{Location: res.Field, Message: "missing or invalid value"})
	case triggers.OutcomeFailed:
		return nil, huma.Error409Conflict("the trigger's target cannot run right now")
	}
	return nil, huma.Error404NotFound("")
}
```

Wiring:
- `api.Deps` gains `Triggers *triggers.Service` (validated), and `registerHookRoutes(hapi, deps)` is added in `registerRoutes` right after `registerSetupRoutes`.
- `internal/api/api_test.go` (`baseHealthzDeps`, `testDeps`) gains `Triggers: &triggers.Service{}`.
- `apitest.New` builds the service like app.go with `PublicURL: publicURL`. The harness already derives `publicURL` from the unstarted listener before building Deps, so `Created.URL` points at the test server.
- `audit_coverage_test.go`: add `"POST /api/v1/hooks/jobs/{id}": "public webhook endpoint; the triggers service audits each run as webhook:<id> with the source IP"` and the same for `/api/v1/hooks/repos/{id}`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Docs and commit**

In noix-docs `reference/api.md`, add "Webhook endpoints" covering:
- The two URLs and that no login is needed; the trigger's secret authenticates.
- Headers per preset; the signature is hex HMAC-SHA256 of the raw body, or of `<timestamp>.<body>` when a timestamp header is configured (±5 minutes).
- Only JSON bodies are mapped, up to 1 MiB.
- The status codes table above. A 404 does not tell which check failed.
- No token in the query string.

### Task 11: Trigger management API and the Triggers card (templates and repositories)

This task covers spec §3 "Audit and UI": managing triggers over the API and the mockup's Triggers card. The card shows the URL with copy, auth preset, allowlist, mapping, rate limit, `on_active`, rotate secret, test call, the last 50 deliveries and copy-ready snippets. It opens from the job templates page and, for `repo_sync`, from the repositories page, which closes 2.8's push webhook.
