- Create: `internal/api/trigger_handlers.go`, `internal/api/trigger_dto.go`
- Modify: `internal/api/api.go` (`registerTriggerRoutes(orgScoped, deps)`), `internal/api/openapi_doc_test.go`, `web/src/api/schema.d.ts`
- Create: `web/src/api/hooks/useTriggers.ts`, `web/src/features/triggers/{TriggersDialog.tsx,TriggerForm.tsx,Deliveries.tsx,presets.ts,snippets.ts}`
- Modify: `web/src/api/types.ts` (`Trigger = …['TriggerDTO']`, `TriggerInput = …['TriggerBody']`, `TriggerDelivery = …['TriggerDeliveryDTO']`), `web/src/features/templates/TemplatesPage.tsx` and `web/src/features/repos/ReposPage.tsx` (a "Triggers" row action opening the dialog)
- Test: `internal/api/trigger_handlers_test.go`, `web/src/features/triggers/TriggersDialog.test.tsx`, `snippets.test.ts`
- Docs (noix-docs): create `user/webhook-triggers.md`; update the repositories section of `user/playbooks-and-jobs.md` (push webhook)

**Interfaces:**

| Method | Path | Permission | operationID |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers?template_id=` or `?repo_id=` (exactly one) | templates.read / repos.read | — |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers` | templates.write (template_run) / repos.write (repo_sync) | createTrigger → 201 `{trigger, secret, url}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}` | read perm of its kind | — |
| PUT | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}` (If-Match) | write perm of its kind | updateTrigger |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}` | write perm of its kind | deleteTrigger |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/rotate-secret` | write perm of its kind | rotateTriggerSecret → `{trigger, secret, url}` |
| POST | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/test` body `{payload?: object}` | write perm of its kind **and** jobs.run (template_run) | testTrigger → `{outcome, run_id?}` |
| GET | `/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/deliveries?limit=` (1–100, default 50) | read perm of its kind | — |

`TriggerDTO` never carries the secret. Audit object type `trigger`. A trigger id in another org is 404.

```go
package api_test

import (
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func (f runFixture) triggersURL() string { return f.s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/" + f.orgSlug + "/triggers" }

type triggerCreated struct {
	Trigger struct {
		ID              string `json:"id"`
		Preset          string `json:"preset"`
		SignatureHeader string `json:"signature_header"`
		URL             string `json:"url"`
		Revision        int64  `json:"revision"`
	} `json:"trigger"`
	Secret string `json:"secret"`
	URL    string `json:"url"`
}

func TestTriggerLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "trig-life")
	c := f.operatorC
	resp := postJSON(t, c, f.triggersURL(), map[string]any{
		"kind": "template_run", "template_id": f.templateID.String(), "name": "deploy on push", "preset": "github",
		"mapping": map[string]any{"extra_vars": map[string]string{"deploy_tag": "$.ref"}},
	})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	var created triggerCreated
	decodeInto(t, resp, &created)
	if created.Secret == "" || created.Trigger.SignatureHeader != "X-Hub-Signature-256" || created.URL != created.Trigger.URL {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v", created)
	}
	id := created.Trigger.ID
	// The secret is never read back.
	for _, url := range []string{f.triggersURL() + "?template_id=" + f.templateID.String(), f.triggersURL() + "/" + id} {
		if body := mustReadBody(t, requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodGet, url, nil)); strings.Contains(body, created.Secret) || strings.Contains(body, `"secret"`) {
			t.Fatalf("%s leaks the secret: %s", url, body)
		}
	}
	// Test call: signed in-process, recorded as a delivery.
	resp = postJSON(t, c, f.triggersURL()+"/"+id+"/test", map[string]any{"payload": map[string]any{"ref": "v9"}})
	var test struct {
		Outcome string `json:"outcome"`
		RunID   string `json:"run_id"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &test)
	if test.Outcome != "triggered" || test.RunID == "" {
		t.Fatalf("test call = %+v", test)
	}
	var deliveries struct {
		Deliveries []struct {
			Outcome string `json:"outcome"`
			Reason  string `json:"reason"`
		} `json:"deliveries"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, f.triggersURL()+"/"+id+"/deliveries", nil), &deliveries)
	if len(deliveries.Deliveries) != 1 || deliveries.Deliveries[0].Reason != "test call" {
		t.Fatalf("deliveries = %+v", deliveries)
	}
	// Rotation: the old secret stops working at the hook.
	var rotated triggerCreated
	decodeInto(t, postJSON(t, c, f.triggersURL()+"/"+id+"/rotate-secret", nil), &rotated)
	if rotated.Secret == "" || rotated.Secret == created.Secret {
		t.Fatalf("rotated = %+v", rotated)
	}
	// Update needs If-Match.
	up := map[string]any{"kind": "template_run", "template_id": f.templateID.String(), "name": "deploy on push", "preset": "github", "enabled": false}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodPut, f.triggersURL()+"/"+id, up); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionRequired {
		t.Fatalf("missing If-Match: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp = requestWithHeaders(t, c, http.MethodPut, f.triggersURL()+"/"+id, up, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"2"`})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("update: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodDelete, f.triggersURL()+"/"+id, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	for _, action := range []string{"POST createTrigger", "POST testTrigger", "POST rotateTriggerSecret", "PUT updateTrigger", "DELETE deleteTrigger"} {
		assertActivityContains(t, f, action)
	}
}

func TestTriggerPermissionsAndValidation(t *testing.T) {
	f := newRunFixture(t, "trig-perm")
	body := map[string]any{"kind": "template_run", "template_id": f.templateID.String(), "name": "t", "preset": "pulsix"}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.viewerC, f.triggersURL(), body); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("viewer create: %d", resp.StatusCode)

		"mapping": map[string]any{"extra_vars": map[string]string{"x": "$..ref"}}}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.triggersURL(), bad); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {

	}
	var created triggerCreated
	decodeInto(t, postJSON(t, f.operatorC, f.triggersURL(), body), &created)
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.viewerC, f.triggersURL()+"/"+created.Trigger.ID+"/rotate-secret", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("viewer rotate: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.viewerC, http.MethodGet, f.triggersURL()+"/"+created.Trigger.ID+"/deliveries", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("viewer deliveries: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the DTOs and handlers**

`internal/api/trigger_dto.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/triggers"
)

type triggerMappingDTO struct {
	ExtraVars map[string]string `json:"extra_vars,omitempty" doc:"var name → JSONPath ($, .name, ['name'], [index], [*])"`
	Limit     string            `json:"limit,omitempty"`
}

type rateLimitDTO struct {
	Count int    `json:"count" minimum:"1" maximum:"1000"`
	Per   string `json:"per" doc:"Duration such as 1m or 1h (1s-24h)"`
}

type triggerDTO struct {
	ID              string            `json:"id"`
	Kind            string            `json:"kind" enum:"template_run,repo_sync"`
	TemplateID      string            `json:"template_id,omitempty"`
	RepoID          string            `json:"repo_id,omitempty"`
	Name            string            `json:"name"`
	Enabled         bool              `json:"enabled"`
	Preset          string            `json:"preset" enum:"pulsix,github,gitea,gitlab,bearer"`
	Auth            string            `json:"auth" enum:"hmac,token"`
	SignatureHeader string            `json:"signature_header"`
	TimestampHeader string            `json:"timestamp_header,omitempty"`
	AllowCIDRs      []string          `json:"allow_cidrs"`
	Mapping         triggerMappingDTO `json:"mapping"`
	OnActive        string            `json:"on_active" enum:"queue,skip"`
	RateLimit       rateLimitDTO      `json:"rate_limit"`
	URL             string            `json:"url"`
	Revision        int64             `json:"revision"`
	CreatedAt       time.Time         `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt       time.Time         `json:"updated_at"`
}

type triggerDeliveryDTO struct {
	ID         string    `json:"id"`
	At         time.Time `json:"at"`
	SourceIP   string    `json:"source_ip,omitempty"`
	Outcome    string    `json:"outcome" enum:"triggered,skipped_active,rejected_ip,rejected_auth,rate_limited,invalid_payload,disabled,failed"`
	Reason     string    `json:"reason,omitempty"`
	JobGroupID string    `json:"job_group_id,omitempty"`
}

// TriggerBody is the create/update body.
type TriggerBody struct {
	Kind            string             `json:"kind" enum:"template_run,repo_sync"`
	TemplateID      string             `json:"template_id,omitempty"`
	RepoID          string             `json:"repo_id,omitempty"`
	Name            string             `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"100"`
	Enabled         *bool              `json:"enabled,omitempty" doc:"default true"`
	Preset          string             `json:"preset" enum:"pulsix,github,gitea,gitlab,bearer"`
	TimestampHeader string             `json:"timestamp_header,omitempty" maxLength:"64"`
	AllowCIDRs      []string           `json:"allow_cidrs,omitempty" maxItems:"50"`
	Mapping         *triggerMappingDTO `json:"mapping,omitempty"`
	OnActive        string             `json:"on_active,omitempty" enum:"queue,skip"`
	RateLimit       *rateLimitDTO      `json:"rate_limit,omitempty"`
}

func toTriggerDTO(t triggers.Trigger, url string) triggerDTO {
	dto := triggerDTO{
		ID: t.ID.String(), Kind: string(t.Kind), Name: t.Name, Enabled: t.Enabled, Preset: string(t.Preset),
		Auth: string(t.Auth()), SignatureHeader: t.SignatureHeader(), TimestampHeader: t.TimestampHeader,
		AllowCIDRs: make([]string, len(t.AllowCIDRs)), Mapping: triggerMappingDTO{ExtraVars: t.Mapping.ExtraVars, Limit: t.Mapping.Limit},
		OnActive: string(t.OnActive), RateLimit: rateLimitDTO{Count: t.RateLimit.Count, Per: t.RateLimit.Per},
		URL: url, Revision: t.Revision, CreatedAt: t.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: t.UpdatedAt,
	}
	for i, p := range t.AllowCIDRs {
		dto.AllowCIDRs[i] = p.String()
	}
	if t.TemplateID != nil {
		dto.TemplateID = t.TemplateID.String()
	}
	if t.RepoID != nil {
		dto.RepoID = t.RepoID.String()
	}
	return dto
}

func toTriggerDeliveryDTO(d triggers.Delivery) triggerDeliveryDTO {
	dto := triggerDeliveryDTO{ID: d.ID.String(), At: d.At, SourceIP: d.SourceIP, Outcome: string(d.Outcome), Reason: d.Reason}
	if d.JobGroupID != nil {
		dto.JobGroupID = d.JobGroupID.String()
	}
	return dto
}
```

`internal/api/trigger_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/triggers"
)

type triggerListInput struct {
	Org        string `path:"org"`
	TemplateID string `query:"template_id"`
	RepoID     string `query:"repo_id"`
}

type triggerListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Triggers []triggerDTO `json:"triggers"`
	}
}

type triggerIDInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type triggerCreateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body TriggerBody
}

type triggerUpdateInput struct {
	Org     string `path:"org"`
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	IfMatch string `header:"If-Match" doc:"The trigger's ETag; required"`
	Body    TriggerBody
}

type triggerOutput struct {
	ETag string `header:"ETag"`
	Body struct {
		Trigger triggerDTO `json:"trigger"`
	}
}

type triggerSecretOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Trigger triggerDTO `json:"trigger"`
		Secret  string     `json:"secret" doc:"Shown once; store it in the sender"`
		URL     string     `json:"url"`
	}
}

type triggerTestInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	ID   string `path:"id"`
	Body struct {
		Payload map[string]any `json:"payload,omitempty" doc:"JSON body to send; default {}"`
	}
}

type triggerTestOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Outcome string `json:"outcome"`
		RunID   string `json:"run_id,omitempty"`
	}
}

type triggerDeliveriesInput struct {
	Org   string `path:"org"`
	ID    string `path:"id"`
	Limit int    `query:"limit" minimum:"1" maximum:"100" default:"50"`
}

type triggerDeliveriesOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Deliveries []triggerDeliveryDTO `json:"deliveries"`
	}
}

// triggerPerm is the permission a trigger's kind needs.
func triggerPerm(kind triggers.Kind, write bool) tenancy.Permission {
	switch {
	case kind == triggers.KindRepoSync && write:
		return tenancy.PermReposWrite
	case kind == triggers.KindRepoSync:
		return tenancy.PermReposRead
	case write:
		return tenancy.PermTemplatesWrite
	}
	return tenancy.PermTemplatesRead
}

// loadTrigger resolves the org (viewer level), loads the trigger and then
// requires the permission its kind needs.
func loadTrigger(ctx context.Context, deps Deps, org, rawID string, write bool) (Scope, triggers.Trigger, error) {
	scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, org, tenancy.PermTemplatesRead)
	if err != nil {
		return Scope{}, triggers.Trigger{}, err
	}
	id, err := parseUUID(rawID, "trigger id")
	if err != nil {
		return Scope{}, triggers.Trigger{}, err
	}
	t, err := deps.Triggers.Get(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Scope{}, triggers.Trigger{}, mapTriggersErr(ctx, err)
	}
	if !tenancy.Allows(scope.Role, triggerPerm(t.Kind, write)) {
		return Scope{}, triggers.Trigger{}, huma.Error403Forbidden("insufficient role")
	}
	return scope, t, nil
}

func registerTriggerRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers", func(ctx context.Context, in *triggerListInput) (*triggerListOutput, error) {
		var filter triggers.ListFilter
		var perm tenancy.Permission
		switch {
		case in.TemplateID != "" && in.RepoID == "":
			perm = tenancy.PermTemplatesRead
		case in.RepoID != "" && in.TemplateID == "":
			perm = tenancy.PermReposRead
		default:
			return nil, huma.Error400BadRequest("pass exactly one of template_id or repo_id")
		}
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, perm)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if filter.TemplateID, err = optionalUUID(in.TemplateID, "template id"); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if filter.RepoID, err = optionalUUID(in.RepoID, "repo id"); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Triggers.List(ctx, scope.OrgID, filter)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapTriggersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &triggerListOutput{}
		out.Body.Triggers = make([]triggerDTO, len(list))
		for i, t := range list {
			out.Body.Triggers[i] = toTriggerDTO(t, deps.Triggers.URL(t))
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers", func(ctx context.Context, in *triggerCreateInput) (*triggerSecretOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, triggerPerm(triggers.Kind(in.Body.Kind), true))
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		input, err := triggerInput(in.Body)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		c, err := deps.Triggers.Create(ctx, scope.OrgID, scope.UserID, input)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapTriggersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, c.Trigger.ID.String(), "created webhook trigger "+c.Trigger.Name)
		return triggerSecret(c), nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusCreated }, withAudit("createTrigger", "trigger"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *triggerIDInput) (*triggerOutput, error) {
		_, t, err := loadTrigger(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, false)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		return triggerOut(deps, t), nil
	})

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *triggerUpdateInput) (*triggerOutput, error) {
		scope, t, err := loadTrigger(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, true)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		rev, err := requireIfMatch(in.IfMatch)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		input, err := triggerInput(in.Body)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		up, err := deps.Triggers.Update(ctx, scope.OrgID, t.ID, rev, input)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapTriggersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, up.ID.String(), "updated webhook trigger "+up.Name)
		return triggerOut(deps, up), nil
	}, withAudit("updateTrigger", "trigger"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *triggerIDInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, t, err := loadTrigger(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, true)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Triggers.Delete(ctx, scope.OrgID, t.ID); err != nil {
			return nil, mapTriggersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, t.ID.String(), "deleted webhook trigger "+t.Name)
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("deleteTrigger", "trigger"))

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/rotate-secret", func(ctx context.Context, in *triggerIDInput) (*triggerSecretOutput, error) {
		scope, t, err := loadTrigger(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, true)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		c, err := deps.Triggers.RotateSecret(ctx, scope.OrgID, t.ID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapTriggersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, t.ID.String(), "rotated the secret of webhook trigger "+t.Name)
		return triggerSecret(c), nil
	}, withAudit("rotateTriggerSecret", "trigger"))

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/test", func(ctx context.Context, in *triggerTestInput) (*triggerTestOutput, error) {
		scope, t, err := loadTrigger(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, true)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if t.Kind == triggers.KindTemplateRun && !tenancy.Allows(scope.Role, tenancy.PermJobsRun) {
			return nil, huma.Error403Forbidden("insufficient role")
		}
		var body []byte
		if in.Body.Payload != nil {
			if body, err = json.Marshal(in.Body.Payload); err != nil {
				return nil, huma.Error400BadRequest("payload is not JSON")
			}
		}
		res, err := deps.Triggers.TestCall(ctx, scope.OrgID, t.ID, body)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapTriggersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, t.ID.String(), "test call of webhook trigger "+t.Name+": "+string(res.Outcome))
		out := &triggerTestOutput{}
		out.Body.Outcome = string(res.Outcome)
		if res.RunID != nil {
			out.Body.RunID = res.RunID.String()
		}
		return out, nil
	}, withAudit("testTrigger", "trigger"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/deliveries", func(ctx context.Context, in *triggerDeliveriesInput) (*triggerDeliveriesOutput, error) {
		scope, t, err := loadTrigger(ctx, deps, in.Org, in.ID, false)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Triggers.Deliveries(ctx, scope.OrgID, t.ID, in.Limit)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapTriggersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &triggerDeliveriesOutput{}
		out.Body.Deliveries = make([]triggerDeliveryDTO, len(list))
		for i, d := range list {
			out.Body.Deliveries[i] = toTriggerDeliveryDTO(d)
		}
		return out, nil
	})
}

func triggerInput(b TriggerBody) (triggers.Input, error) {
	in := triggers.Input{
		Kind: triggers.Kind(b.Kind), Name: b.Name, Enabled: b.Enabled == nil || *b.Enabled, Preset: triggers.Preset(b.Preset),
		TimestampHeader: b.TimestampHeader, AllowCIDRs: b.AllowCIDRs, OnActive: triggers.OnActive(b.OnActive),
	}
	var err error
	if in.TemplateID, err = optionalUUID(b.TemplateID, "template id"); err != nil {
		return in, err
	}
	if in.RepoID, err = optionalUUID(b.RepoID, "repo id"); err != nil {
		return in, err
	}
	if b.Mapping != nil {
		in.Mapping = triggers.Mapping{ExtraVars: b.Mapping.ExtraVars, Limit: b.Mapping.Limit}
	}
	if b.RateLimit != nil {
		in.RateLimit = triggers.RateLimit{Count: b.RateLimit.Count, Per: b.RateLimit.Per}
	}
	return in, nil
}

func triggerOut(deps Deps, t triggers.Trigger) *triggerOutput {
	out := &triggerOutput{ETag: revisionETag(t.Revision)}
	out.Body.Trigger = toTriggerDTO(t, deps.Triggers.URL(t))
	return out
}

func triggerSecret(c triggers.Created) *triggerSecretOutput {
	out := &triggerSecretOutput{}
	out.Body.Trigger, out.Body.Secret, out.Body.URL = toTriggerDTO(c.Trigger, c.URL), c.Secret, c.URL
	return out
}

func mapTriggersErr(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrInvalidInput):
		return huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNotFound):
		return huma.Error404NotFound("not found")
	case errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrNameTaken):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("a trigger with this name already exists")
	case errors.Is(err, triggers.ErrRevisionMismatch):
		return huma.Error412PreconditionFailed("this changed since you loaded it; reload and apply your change again")
	}
	slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "unmapped triggers error", "err", err)
	return huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
}
```

Register `registerTriggerRoutes(orgScoped, deps)` after the schedule routes.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { snippets } from './snippets.ts';

const base = { url: 'https://pulsix.example.test/api/v1/hooks/jobs/7d2f', signature_header: 'X-Pulsix-Signature-256', timestamp_header: '' };

describe('trigger snippets', () => {
  it('signs the body in the curl snippet for HMAC presets', () => {
    const s = snippets({ ...base, preset: 'pulsix', auth: 'hmac' });
    expect(s.curl).toContain('openssl dgst -sha256 -hmac "$SECRET"');
    expect(s.curl).toContain('X-Pulsix-Signature-256: sha256=$sig');
    expect(s.curl).toContain(base.url);
    expect(s.curl).not.toContain('?token=');
  });

  it('signs "<timestamp>.<body>" when a timestamp header is set', () => {
    const s = snippets({ ...base, preset: 'pulsix', auth: 'hmac', timestamp_header: 'X-Pulsix-Timestamp' });
    expect(s.curl).toContain('ts=$(date +%s)');
    expect(s.curl).toContain(`printf '%s.%s' "$ts" "$body"`);
    expect(s.curl).toContain('X-Pulsix-Timestamp: $ts');
  });

  it('sends the token in a header for token presets and names the provider settings', () => {
    const s = snippets({ ...base, preset: 'gitlab', auth: 'token', signature_header: 'X-Gitlab-Token' });
    expect(s.curl).toContain('X-Gitlab-Token: $SECRET');
    expect(s.gitlab).toContain('Secret token');
    expect(snippets({ ...base, preset: 'github', auth: 'hmac', signature_header: 'X-Hub-Signature-256' }).github).toContain('application/json');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { TriggersDialog } from './TriggersDialog.tsx';

const trigger = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  id: 'tr1', kind: 'template_run', template_id: 't1', name: 'deploy on push', enabled: true, preset: 'github', auth: 'hmac',
  signature_header: 'X-Hub-Signature-256', allow_cidrs: ['140.82.112.0/20'], mapping: { extra_vars: { release: '$.ref' }, limit: '$.hosts[*]' },
  on_active: 'queue', rate_limit: { count: 10, per: '1m' }, url: 'https://pulsix.example.test/api/v1/hooks/jobs/tr1', revision: 1,
  created_at: '', updated_at: '', ...over,
});

function renderDialog(canWrite = true) {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <TriggersDialog org="acme" target={{ kind: 'template_run', id: 't1', name: 'patch-all' }} canWrite={canWrite} canRun onClose={() => {}} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

describe('TriggersDialog', () => {
  it('shows URL, auth, allowlist, mapping and rate limit', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers': { triggers: [trigger()] }, 'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers/tr1/deliveries': { deliveries: [] } });
    renderDialog();
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Triggers · patch-all' });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('https://pulsix.example.test/api/v1/hooks/jobs/tr1')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Auth: GitHub · X-Hub-Signature-256')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('140.82.112.0/20')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('$.ref → release')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('$.hosts[*] → limit')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('10 per 1m · queue if a run is active')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('creates a trigger and shows the secret exactly once', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let posted: unknown;
    const list: unknown[] = [];
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers': () => ({ triggers: list }),
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers/tr1/deliveries': { deliveries: [] },
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers': (req: MockRequest) => {
        posted = req.body;
        list.push(trigger());
        return { trigger: trigger(), secret: 'whsec-shown-once', url: trigger().url };
      },
    });
    renderDialog();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'New trigger' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Name'), 'deploy on push');
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Sender'), 'github');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Allowed sources'), '140.82.112.0/20');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create trigger' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('whsec-shown-once')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(posted).toMatchObject({ kind: 'template_run', template_id: 't1', name: 'deploy on push', preset: 'github', allow_cidrs: ['140.82.112.0/20'] });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'I stored the secret' }));
    expect(screen.queryByText('whsec-shown-once')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('rotates the secret, runs a test call and lists deliveries', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    window.confirm = () => true;
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers': { triggers: [trigger()] },
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers/tr1/deliveries': { deliveries: [{ id: 'd1', at: '2026-09-18T10:00:00Z', source_ip: '140.82.112.5', outcome: 'rejected_auth' }] },
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers/tr1/rotate-secret': { trigger: trigger({ revision: 2 }), secret: 'rotated-secret', url: trigger().url },
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers/tr1/test': { outcome: 'triggered', run_id: 'job-1' },
    });
    renderDialog();
    expect(await screen.findByText('rejected_auth')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Rotate secret' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('rotated-secret')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Test call' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Test call: triggered/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('is read-only without write permission', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers': { triggers: [trigger()] }, 'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/triggers/tr1/deliveries': { deliveries: [] } });
    renderDialog(false);
    await screen.findByText('deploy on push');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'New trigger' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Rotate secret' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`web/src/api/hooks/useTriggers.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../client.ts';
import type { TriggerInput } from '../types.ts';

/** TriggerTarget is what a trigger starts: a template run or a repo sync. */
export interface TriggerTarget {
  kind: 'template_run' | 'repo_sync';
  id: string;
  name: string;
}

const ifMatch = (revision: number) => ({ 'If-Match': `"${revision}"` });

function useInvalidate(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['triggers', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['trigger-deliveries', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['jobs', org] });
  };
}

export function useTriggers(org: string, target: TriggerTarget) {
  const query = target.kind === 'template_run' ? { template_id: target.id } : { repo_id: target.id };
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['triggers', org, target.kind, target.id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers', { params: { path: { org }, query } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!target.id,
  });
}

export function useTriggerDeliveries(org: string, id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['trigger-deliveries', org, id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/deliveries', { params: { path: { org, id }, query: { limit: 50 } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!id,
  });
}

/** Secret-bearing mutations keep nothing in the cache (gcTime 0). */
export function useCreateTrigger(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: TriggerInput) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
    gcTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useUpdateTrigger(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ revision, ...body }: TriggerInput & { revision: number }) =>
      unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id }, header: ifMatch(revision) }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useDeleteTrigger(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useRotateTriggerSecret(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/rotate-secret', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
    gcTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useTestTrigger(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, payload }: { id: string; payload?: Record<string, unknown> }) =>
      unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/triggers/{id}/test', { params: { path: { org, id } }, body: payload ? { payload } : {} })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

`web/src/features/triggers/snippets.ts`:

```ts
export interface SnippetInput {
  url: string;
  preset: string;
  auth: string;
  signature_header: string;
  timestamp_header?: string;
}

export interface Snippets {
  curl: string;
  github: string;
  gitlab: string;
  gitea: string;
}

/** snippets renders copy-ready sender configurations. The secret is never
 * embedded: every snippet reads $SECRET, which the operator sets from the
 * once-shown value. No query-string token (spec §3). */
export function snippets(t: SnippetInput): Snippets {
  const ts = t.timestamp_header ?? '';
  let curl: string;
  if (t.auth === 'hmac') {
    const prefix = t.signature_header === 'X-Gitea-Signature' ? '' : 'sha256=';
    const signed = ts ? `printf '%s.%s' "$ts" "$body"` : `printf '%s' "$body"`;
    curl = [
      `body='{"ref":"refs/heads/main"}'`,
      ...(ts ? ['ts=$(date +%s)'] : []),
      `sig=$(${signed} | openssl dgst -sha256 -hmac "$SECRET" | sed 's/^.* //')`,
      `curl -X POST ${JSON.stringify(t.url)} \\`,
      `  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\`,
      ...(ts ? [`  -H "${ts}: $ts" \\`] : []),
      `  -H "${t.signature_header}: ${prefix}$sig" \\`,
      `  --data "$body"`,
    ].join('\n');
  } else {
    const value = t.signature_header === 'Authorization' ? 'Bearer $SECRET' : '$SECRET';
    curl = [
      `curl -X POST ${JSON.stringify(t.url)} \\`,
      `  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \\`,
      `  -H "${t.signature_header}: ${value}" \\`,
      `  --data '{"ref":"refs/heads/main"}'`,
    ].join('\n');
  }
  return {
    curl,
    github: `Payload URL: ${t.url}\nContent type: application/json\nSecret: <the trigger secret>\nEvents: Just the push event`,
    gitlab: `URL: ${t.url}\nSecret token: <the trigger secret>\nTrigger: Push events`,
    gitea: `Target URL: ${t.url}\nHTTP method: POST\nPOST content type: application/json\nSecret: <the trigger secret>`,
  };
}
```

`web/src/features/triggers/Deliveries.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Pill, type Tone } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { useTriggerDeliveries } from '../../api/hooks/useTriggers.ts';

const TONE: Record<string, Tone> = { triggered: 'ok', skipped_active: 'unknown', rate_limited: 'warn', invalid_payload: 'warn', disabled: 'unknown', failed: 'crit' };

/** Deliveries is a trigger's delivery log: time, source, outcome, reason
 * (rejections at most one row per minute; request bodies are never kept). */
export function Deliveries({ org, id }: { org: string; id: string }) {
  const list = useTriggerDeliveries(org, id).data?.deliveries ?? [];
  if (list.length === 0) return <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No deliveries yet.</p>;
  return (
    <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      {list.map((d) => (
        <li key={d.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
          <Pill tone={TONE[d.outcome] ?? 'crit'}>{d.outcome}</Pill>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">{new Date(d.at).toLocaleString()}</span>
          {d.source_ip && <span className="font-mono">{d.source_ip}</span>}
          {d.reason && <span className="truncate text-muted-foreground">{d.reason}</span>}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/triggers/TriggerForm.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import type { Trigger, TriggerInput } from '../../api/types.ts';
import type { TriggerTarget } from '../../api/hooks/useTriggers.ts';
import { PRESET_LABEL } from './presets.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

interface Row {
  name: string;
  path: string;
}

/** TriggerForm edits one trigger: name, sender preset, replay header,
 * allowed sources, what happens while a run is active, the rate limit and
 * (template runs only) the payload mapping. */
export function TriggerForm({ target, initial, submitLabel, pending, error, onSubmit, onCancel }: {
  target: TriggerTarget;
  initial?: Trigger;
  submitLabel: string;
  pending: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  onSubmit: (body: TriggerInput) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initial?.name ?? '');
  const [preset, setPreset] = useState<TriggerInput['preset']>(initial?.preset ?? 'pulsix');
  const [timestampHeader, setTimestampHeader] = useState(initial?.timestamp_header ?? '');
  const [sources, setSources] = useState((initial?.allow_cidrs ?? []).join('\n'));
  const [onActive, setOnActive] = useState<'queue' | 'skip'>(initial?.on_active ?? 'queue');
  const [count, setCount] = useState(initial?.rate_limit.count ?? 10);
  const [per, setPer] = useState(initial?.rate_limit.per ?? '1m');
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Row[]>(Object.entries(initial?.mapping.extra_vars ?? {}).map(([n, p]) => ({ name: n, path: p })));
  const [limitPath, setLimitPath] = useState(initial?.mapping.limit ?? '');

  function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const allow = sources.split(/[\n,]/).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const vars = Object.fromEntries(rows.filter((r) => r.name && r.path).map((r) => [r.name, r.path]));
    onSubmit({
      kind: target.kind,
      ...(target.kind === 'template_run' ? { template_id: target.id } : { repo_id: target.id }),
      name,
      enabled: initial?.enabled ?? true,
      preset,
      ...(timestampHeader ? { timestamp_header: timestampHeader } : {}),
      allow_cidrs: allow,
      on_active: onActive,
      rate_limit: { count, per },
      ...(target.kind === 'template_run' ? { mapping: { extra_vars: vars, ...(limitPath ? { limit: limitPath } : {}) } } : {}),
    });
  }

  return (
    <form className="flex flex-col gap-2" onSubmit={submit}>
      <Label htmlFor="trigger-name">Name</Label>
      <Input id="trigger-name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
      <Label htmlFor="trigger-preset">Sender</Label>
      <select id="trigger-preset" className={SELECT} value={preset} onChange={(e) => setPreset(e.target.value as TriggerInput['preset'])}>
        {Object.entries(PRESET_LABEL).map(([k, v]) => (
          <option key={k} value={k}>
            {v}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      {(preset === 'pulsix' || preset === 'github' || preset === 'gitea') && (
        <>
          <Label htmlFor="trigger-ts">Replay protection header</Label>
          <Input id="trigger-ts" className="font-mono" value={timestampHeader} onChange={(e) => setTimestampHeader(e.target.value)} placeholder="X-Pulsix-Timestamp (optional)" />
        </>
      )}
      <Label htmlFor="trigger-sources">Allowed sources</Label>
      <Input id="trigger-sources" className="font-mono" value={sources} onChange={(e) => setSources(e.target.value)} placeholder="any source; or CIDRs, comma separated" />
      <Label htmlFor="trigger-active">If a run is active</Label>
      <select id="trigger-active" className={SELECT} value={onActive} onChange={(e) => setOnActive(e.target.value as 'queue' | 'skip')}>
        <option value="queue">queue another run</option>
        <option value="skip">skip</option>
      </select>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="trigger-count">Calls</Label>
          <Input id="trigger-count" type="number" min={1} max={1000} value={count} onChange={(e) => setCount(Number(e.target.value))} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="trigger-per">Per</Label>
          <select id="trigger-per" className={SELECT} value={per} onChange={(e) => setPer(e.target.value)}>
            {['1m', '5m', '1h'].map((p) => (
              <option key={p} value={p}>
                {p}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      {target.kind === 'template_run' && (
        <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <legend className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">Payload mapping</legend>
          {rows.map((r, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex gap-1">
              <Input aria-label={`Variable ${i + 1}`} className="font-mono" value={r.name} onChange={(e) => setRows(rows.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, name: e.target.value } : x)))} placeholder="release" />
              <Input aria-label={`Path ${i + 1}`} className="font-mono" value={r.path} onChange={(e) => setRows(rows.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, path: e.target.value } : x)))} placeholder="$.ref" />
            </div>
          ))}
          <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setRows([...rows, { name: '', path: '' }])}>
            + Map a variable
          </Button>
          <Label htmlFor="trigger-limit">Limit path</Label>
          <Input id="trigger-limit" className="font-mono" value={limitPath} onChange={(e) => setLimitPath(e.target.value)} placeholder="$.hosts[*]" />
        </fieldset>
      )}
      {error && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--px-crit)]">{error}</p>}
      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
        <Button type="button" onClick={onCancel}>
          Cancel
        </Button>
        <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={pending}>
          {submitLabel}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/triggers/TriggersDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Copy } from 'lucide-react';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import type { Trigger, TriggerInput } from '../../api/types.ts';
import {
  type TriggerTarget,
  useCreateTrigger,
  useDeleteTrigger,
  useRotateTriggerSecret,
  useTestTrigger,
  useTriggers,
  useUpdateTrigger,
} from '../../api/hooks/useTriggers.ts';
import { Deliveries } from './Deliveries.tsx';
import { TriggerForm } from './TriggerForm.tsx';
import { PRESET_LABEL } from './presets.ts';
import { snippets } from './snippets.ts';

/** TriggersDialog is the mockup's Triggers card for one job template or
 * git repository: every incoming webhook with URL, sender, allowlist,
 * mapping, rate limit and its delivery log, plus create, edit, rotate,
 * test call and the sender setup snippets. */
export function TriggersDialog({ org, target, canWrite, canRun, onClose }: {
  org: string;
  target: TriggerTarget;
  canWrite: boolean;
  canRun: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const triggers = useTriggers(org, target);
  const create = useCreateTrigger(org);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [secret, setSecret] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const list = triggers.data?.triggers ?? [];

  async function onCreate(body: TriggerInput) {
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await create.mutateAsync(body);
      setSecret(res.secret);
      setCreating(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Creating the trigger failed');
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onClose={onClose} title={`Triggers · ${target.name}`} size="wide">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {target.kind === 'template_run' ? 'Manual runs use the Run button; schedules live on the Schedules page.' : 'Sync the repository on every push.'}
        </p>
        {secret && <SecretBox secret={secret} onDone={() => setSecret(null)} />}
        {list.map((t) => (
          <TriggerCard key={t.id} org={org} target={target} trigger={t} canWrite={canWrite} canRun={canRun} onSecret={setSecret} />
        ))}
        {list.length === 0 && !creating && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No incoming webhooks yet.</p>}
        {creating ? (
          <TriggerForm target={target} submitLabel="Create trigger" pending={create.isPending} error={error} onSubmit={(b) => void onCreate(b)} onCancel={() => setCreating(false)} />
        ) : (
          canWrite && (
            <Button type="button" variant="primary" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>
              New trigger
            </Button>
          )
        )}
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

/** SecretBox shows a trigger secret exactly once. */
function SecretBox({ secret, onDone }: { secret: string; onDone: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-md border border-[var(--px-warn)] p-3">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold">Shown once — store it in the sender now</p>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <code className="flex-1 truncate font-mono text-xs">{secret}</code>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label="Copy secret" onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(secret)}>
          <Copy size={13} />
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Button type="button" onClick={onDone}>
        I stored the secret
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

function TriggerCard({ org, target, trigger: t, canWrite, canRun, onSecret }: {
  org: string;
  target: TriggerTarget;
  trigger: Trigger;
  canWrite: boolean;
  canRun: boolean;
  onSecret: (s: string) => void;
}) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const update = useUpdateTrigger(org, t.id);
  const remove = useDeleteTrigger(org);
  const rotate = useRotateTriggerSecret(org);
  const test = useTestTrigger(org);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'curl' | 'github' | 'gitlab' | 'gitea'>('curl');
  const sn = snippets(t);
  const mapping = Object.entries(t.mapping.extra_vars ?? {});

  async function onRotate() {
    if (!confirm(`Rotate the secret of ${t.name}? Senders using the old one stop working.`)) return;
    try {
      const res = await rotate.mutateAsync(t.id);
      onSecret(res.secret);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Rotating failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  async function onTest() {
    try {
      const res = await test.mutateAsync({ id: t.id });
      toast.show(`Test call: ${res.outcome}`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Test call failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  async function onSave(body: TriggerInput) {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await update.mutateAsync({ ...body, revision: t.revision });
      setEditing(false);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed');
    }
  }

  if (editing) {
    return <TriggerForm target={target} initial={t} submitLabel="Save" pending={update.isPending} error={error} onSubmit={(b) => void onSave(b)} onCancel={() => setEditing(false)} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-md border border-border p-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">{t.name}</span>
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{t.enabled ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold">POST</span>
        <code className="flex-1 truncate font-mono text-xs">{t.url}</code>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label="Copy URL" onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(t.url)}>
          <Copy size={13} />
        </Button>
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs">{`Auth: ${PRESET_LABEL[t.preset] ?? t.preset} · ${t.signature_header}`}</p>
      {t.timestamp_header && <p className="text-xs">{`Replay window (±5 min) via ${t.timestamp_header}`}</p>}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 text-xs">
        <span>Allowed sources:</span>
        {(t.allow_cidrs ?? []).length === 0 ? (
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">any source</span>
        ) : (
          (t.allow_cidrs ?? []).map((c) => (
            <span key={c} className="font-mono">
              {c}
            </span>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
      {(mapping.length > 0 || t.mapping.limit) && (
        <ul className="font-mono text-xs">
          {mapping.map(([name, path]) => (
            <li key={name}>{`${path} → ${name}`}</li>
          ))}
          {t.mapping.limit && <li>{`${t.mapping.limit} → limit`}</li>}
        </ul>
      )}
      <p className="text-xs">{`${t.rate_limit.count} per ${t.rate_limit.per} · ${t.on_active === 'skip' ? 'skip' : 'queue'} if a run is active`}</p>
      {canWrite && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button type="button" onClick={() => void onRotate()}>
            Rotate secret
          </Button>
          {(canRun || t.kind === 'repo_sync') && (
            <Button type="button" onClick={() => void onTest()}>
              Test call
            </Button>
          )}
          <Button type="button" onClick={() => setEditing(true)}>
            Edit
          </Button>
          <Button type="button" variant="destructive" onClick={() => void (confirm(`Delete trigger ${t.name}?`) && remove.mutateAsync(t.id))}>
            Delete
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
      <Deliveries org={org} id={t.id} />
      <details className="text-xs">
        <summary className="cursor-pointer">Sender setup</summary>
        <div role="tablist" className="mt-2 flex gap-3">
          {(['curl', 'github', 'gitlab', 'gitea'] as const).map((k) => (
            <button key={k} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === k} className={tab === k ? 'font-semibold text-primary' : 'text-muted-foreground'} onClick={() => setTab(k)}>
              {k}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <pre className="mt-2 overflow-x-auto rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-2 font-mono text-[11px]">{sn[tab]}</pre>
      </details>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 12: Enrollment and certificate domain (proto, token, CA, registry)

**Interfaces:**
- Proto (additive; `buf breaking` stays green):

```protobuf
  // Enroll exchanges a one-time enrollment token and a certificate
  // request for the worker's first client certificate. It is the only
  // method callable without a client certificate; the worker pins the CA
  // by the fingerprint in its token before sending anything.
  rpc Enroll(EnrollRequest) returns (EnrollResponse);
  // RenewCertificate issues a fresh certificate for the calling worker's
  // key. The old certificate stays valid until it expires.
  rpc RenewCertificate(RenewCertificateRequest) returns (RenewCertificateResponse);

message EnrollRequest {
  string token_id = 1;   // the enrollment id (uuid) from the token
  bytes secret = 2;      // the token's 32-byte secret
  bytes csr = 3;         // PEM "CERTIFICATE REQUEST"
  string version = 4;    // worker build version
}

message EnrollResponse {
  bytes certificate = 1; // PEM leaf
  bytes ca_chain = 2;    // PEM CA certificate
  string worker_id = 3;
}

message RenewCertificateRequest {
  bytes csr = 1;         // PEM "CERTIFICATE REQUEST"
}

message RenewCertificateResponse {
  bytes certificate = 1; // PEM leaf
}
```

- `enrolltoken`: `type Token struct{ ID uuid.UUID; Secret []byte; CAPin [32]byte }`, `func (t Token) String() string` (`pxw1.<b64url id>.<b64url secret>.<b64url sha256(CA DER)>`, unpadded), `func Parse(s string) (Token, error)`, `func Pin(der []byte) [32]byte`, `var ErrMalformed`.
- `ca`: `const LeafValidity = 90 * 24 * time.Hour`, `var ErrBadCSR`, `func (c *CA) SignCSR(csrPEM []byte, orgID, zoneID uuid.UUID, workerName string) ([]byte, *x509.Certificate, error)`, `func (c *CA) CAPEM() []byte`, `func (c *CA) CAPin() [32]byte`, `func ParseLeaf(certPEM []byte) (*x509.Certificate, error)`.
- `workers`:

```go
type Status string // StatusActive "active", StatusRevoked "revoked"
// Worker gains: Status Status; RevokedAt *time.Time
type Certificate struct {
	Serial              *big.Int
	OrgID, WorkerID     uuid.UUID
	NotBefore, NotAfter time.Time
	RevokedAt           *time.Time
}
type Enrollment struct {
	ID, OrgID, ZoneID uuid.UUID; Name string; ExpiresAt time.Time; UsedAt *time.Time; CreatedBy *uuid.UUID; CreatedAt time.Time
}
type EnrollInput struct { TokenID uuid.UUID; Secret []byte; CSRPEM []byte; Version string }
type EnrollResult struct { Worker Worker; CertPEM, CAPEM []byte }
type Signer interface {
	SignCSR(csrPEM []byte, orgID, zoneID uuid.UUID, workerName string) ([]byte, *x509.Certificate, error)
	CAPEM() []byte
	CAPin() [32]byte
}
type ZoneLister interface { ListZones(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]hosts.Zone, error) }
var ErrRevoked, ErrEnrollmentRefused, ErrBadCSR error
const EnrollmentTTL = time.Hour

type Store interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, w Worker, c Certificate) (Worker, error) // worker + first certificate, one tx
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Worker, error)
	GetByName(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, name string) (Worker, error)
	LookupSerial(ctx context.Context, serial *big.Int) (Worker, Certificate, error) // org-unscoped
	Touch(ctx context.Context, workerID uuid.UUID, version string) error           // org-unscoped
	AddCertificate(ctx context.Context, c Certificate) error
	Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	CreateEnrollment(ctx context.Context, e Enrollment, secretHash []byte) (Enrollment, error)
	ListEnrollments(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, now time.Time) ([]Enrollment, error)
	DeleteEnrollment(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	Enroll(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID, fn func(e Enrollment, secretHash []byte) (Worker, Certificate, error)) (Worker, error)
}

func NewService(store Store, opts ...Option) *Service // WithSigner, WithZones, WithClock, WithRand
func (s *Service) Register(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, in RegisterInput) (Worker, error) // + certificate row
func (s *Service) RegisterOrAttach(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, in RegisterInput) (Worker, error)
func (s *Service) GetBySerial(ctx context.Context, serial *big.Int) (Worker, error) // ErrNotFound | ErrRevoked
func (s *Service) Touch(ctx context.Context, workerID uuid.UUID, version string) error
func (s *Service) CreateEnrollment(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, actor *uuid.UUID, zoneID uuid.UUID, name string) (Enrollment, string, error)
func (s *Service) ListEnrollments(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Enrollment, error)
func (s *Service) WithdrawEnrollment(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
func (s *Service) Enroll(ctx context.Context, in EnrollInput) (EnrollResult, error)
func (s *Service) Renew(ctx context.Context, w Worker, csrPEM []byte) ([]byte, error)
```

`RegisterInput` gains `NotBefore, NotAfter time.Time`. When they are zero, `Register` records `now` and `now + ca.LeafValidity`, because the CLI path's certificate was just issued with exactly that window.

```go
func TestEnrollMessagesRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	req := &workerpb.EnrollRequest{TokenId: "0197c0a2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001", Secret: []byte("s3cr3t"), Csr: []byte("csr"), Version: "v0.2c"}
	raw, err := proto.Marshal(req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var gotReq workerpb.EnrollRequest
	if err := proto.Unmarshal(raw, &gotReq); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !proto.Equal(req, &gotReq) {
		t.Errorf("round-tripped enroll request differs:\nwant %+v\ngot  %+v", req, &gotReq)
	}

	resp := &workerpb.RenewCertificateResponse{Certificate: []byte("cert")}
	raw, err = proto.Marshal(resp)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var gotResp workerpb.RenewCertificateResponse
	if err := proto.Unmarshal(raw, &gotResp); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !proto.Equal(resp, &gotResp) {
		t.Errorf("round-tripped renew response differs:\nwant %+v\ngot  %+v", resp, &gotResp)
	}
}
```

```go
package enrolltoken_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"

func TestRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	tok := enrolltoken.Token{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Secret: bytes.Repeat([]byte{7}, 32), CAPin: sha256.Sum256([]byte("ca"))}
	s := tok.String()
	if !strings.HasPrefix(s, "pxw1.") || strings.Count(s, ".") != 3 || strings.ContainsAny(s, "=+/") {
		t.Fatalf("token = %q", s)
	}
	got, err := enrolltoken.Parse("  " + s + "\n")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.ID != tok.ID || !bytes.Equal(got.Secret, tok.Secret) || got.CAPin != tok.CAPin {
		t.Fatalf("parsed = %+v", got)
	}
	if enrolltoken.Pin([]byte("ca")) != tok.CAPin {
		t.Fatal("Pin is SHA-256 of the CA's DER")

func TestParseRejects(t *testing.T) {
	good := enrolltoken.Token{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Secret: bytes.Repeat([]byte{1}, 32), CAPin: sha256.Sum256(nil)}.String()
	parts := strings.Split(good, ".")

		"three parts":   strings.Join(parts[:3], "."),

		"bad base64":    parts[0] + ".!!!." + parts[2] + "." + parts[3],
		"short id":      parts[0] + ".AAAA." + parts[2] + "." + parts[3],
		"short secret":  parts[0] + "." + parts[1] + ".AAAA." + parts[3],
		"short pin":     parts[0] + "." + parts[1] + "." + parts[2] + ".AAAA",
		"padded base64": parts[0] + "." + parts[1] + "=." + parts[2] + "." + parts[3],
	} {
		if _, err := enrolltoken.Parse(in); !errors.Is(err, enrolltoken.ErrMalformed) {

		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `internal/enrolltoken/token.go`**

```go
// Package enrolltoken is the worker enrollment token shared by the manager
// (which prints it) and the worker (which parses it): pxw1.<id>.<secret>.
// <ca-sha256>, every part unpadded base64url. The id names the enrollment
// row, the 32-byte secret proves possession (the manager stores only its
// SHA-256), and the CA fingerprint lets the worker pin the manager's CA
// before its first connection (spec 2026-09-18 §4).
package enrolltoken

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"strings"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

const prefix = "pxw1"

// ErrMalformed is returned for anything that is not a well-formed token.
var ErrMalformed = errors.New("malformed enrollment token")

// Token is a parsed enrollment token.
type Token struct {
	ID     uuid.UUID
	Secret []byte
	CAPin  [32]byte
}

var enc = base64.RawURLEncoding

// String renders the token.
func (t Token) String() string {
	return strings.Join([]string{prefix, enc.EncodeToString(t.ID[:]), enc.EncodeToString(t.Secret), enc.EncodeToString(t.CAPin[:])}, ".")
}

// Parse reads a token, tolerating surrounding whitespace.
func Parse(s string) (Token, error) {
	parts := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(s), ".")
	if len(parts) != 4 || parts[0] != prefix {
		return Token{}, ErrMalformed
	}
	id, err := enc.DecodeString(parts[1])
	if err != nil || len(id) != 16 {
		return Token{}, ErrMalformed
	}
	secret, err := enc.DecodeString(parts[2])
	if err != nil || len(secret) != 32 {
		return Token{}, ErrMalformed
	}
	pin, err := enc.DecodeString(parts[3])
	if err != nil || len(pin) != 32 {
		return Token{}, ErrMalformed
	}
	var t Token
	copy(t.ID[:], id)
	t.Secret = secret
	copy(t.CAPin[:], pin)
	return t, nil
}

// Pin is the fingerprint a token carries: SHA-256 of the CA certificate's DER.
func Pin(der []byte) [32]byte { return sha256.Sum256(der) }
```

Run: `go test -race -timeout 900s -cover ./internal/enrolltoken/...`
Expected: PASS, ≥ 90 %. Add the package to the 90 % list in `scripts/coverage-gate.sh`.

`internal/ca/csr_test.go`:

```go
package ca_test

import (
	"crypto"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/rsa"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/x509"
	"crypto/x509/pkix"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/ca"
)

func csrPEM(t *testing.T, key crypto.Signer, tpl *x509.CertificateRequest) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, tpl, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: der})
}

func TestSignCSRAcceptedKeysAndIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	a := newCA(t)
	org, zone := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	p256, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	p384, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P384(), rand.Reader)
	_, edKey, _ := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	for name, key := range map[string]crypto.Signer{"p256": p256, "p384 (legacy bundles)": p384, "ed25519": edKey} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			// The CSR's own subject and SANs are ignored: the CA names the worker.
			req := csrPEM(t, key, &x509.CertificateRequest{Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: "evil"}, DNSNames: []string{"manager"}})
			certPEM, crt, err := a.SignCSR(req, org, zone, "fra-1")
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if crt.Subject.CommonName != "pulsix-worker fra-1" || len(crt.DNSNames) != 0 {
				t.Fatalf("subject = %q, dns = %v", crt.Subject.CommonName, crt.DNSNames)
			}
			if got := crt.NotAfter.Sub(crt.NotBefore); got != ca.LeafValidity {
				t.Fatalf("validity = %v", got)
			}
			id, err := a.VerifyClient([][]byte{crt.Raw})
			if err != nil || id.ZoneID != zone || id.OrgID == nil || *id.OrgID != org || id.WorkerName != "fra-1" {
				t.Fatalf("identity = %+v, %v", id, err)
			}
			parsed, err := ca.ParseLeaf(certPEM)
			if err != nil || parsed.SerialNumber.Cmp(crt.SerialNumber) != 0 {
				t.Fatalf("ParseLeaf = %v, %v", parsed, err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestSignCSRRejects(t *testing.T) {
	a := newCA(t)
	org, zone := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	rsaKey, _ := rsa.GenerateKey(rand.Reader, 2048)
	p224, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P224(), rand.Reader)
	good, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	tampered := csrPEM(t, good, &x509.CertificateRequest{})
	block, _ := pem.Decode(tampered)
	block.Bytes[len(block.Bytes)-1] ^= 0xff
	cases := map[string][]byte{
		"not pem":        []byte("garbage"),
		"wrong pem type": pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: []byte{1}}),
		"rsa key":        csrPEM(t, rsaKey, &x509.CertificateRequest{}),
		"p224 key":       csrPEM(t, p224, &x509.CertificateRequest{}),
		"bad signature":  pem.EncodeToMemory(block),
	}
	for name, in := range cases {
		if _, _, err := a.SignCSR(in, org, zone, "w"); !errors.Is(err, ca.ErrBadCSR) {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v, want ErrBadCSR", name, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestCAPEMAndPin(t *testing.T) {
	a := newCA(t)
	_, root := a.Pool()
	block, _ := pem.Decode(a.CAPEM())
	if block == nil || string(block.Bytes) != string(root.Raw) {
		t.Fatal("CAPEM is the root certificate")
	}
	if a.CAPin() != sha256.Sum256(root.Raw) {
		t.Fatal("CAPin is SHA-256 of the root DER")
	}
	if _, err := ca.ParseLeaf([]byte("x")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("ParseLeaf accepted garbage")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `csr.go` and the `ca.go` refactor**

In `ca.go`, rename `leafValidity` to `LeafValidity` with the value `90 * 24 * time.Hour`, and document it: "LeafValidity is the lifetime of every worker and server certificate (spec 2026-09-18 §4: 90 days; the worker renews once a third is left)." Extract from `Issue`:

```go
// leafTemplate is the certificate Issue and SignCSR both sign: a worker
// client certificate with the pulsix URI SANs, or (orgID nil, workerName
// empty) the gRPC server certificate.
func (c *CA) leafTemplate(orgID *uuid.UUID, zoneID uuid.UUID, workerName string) (*x509.Certificate, error) {
	leafSerial, err := newSerial(c.rand)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("draw serial: %w", err)
	}
	server := orgID == nil && workerName == ""
	commonName := c.cfg.CommonName
	if !server {
		commonName = workerCNPrefix + workerName
	}
	var uris []*url.URL
	if orgID != nil {
		uris = append(uris, pulsixURI("org", *orgID))
	}
	if !server {
		uris = append(uris, pulsixURI("zone", zoneID))
	}
	now := c.now()
	tpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: leafSerial, Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: commonName},
		NotBefore: now, NotAfter: now.Add(LeafValidity),
		KeyUsage: x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature, BasicConstraintsValid: true, URIs: uris,
	}
	if server {
		tpl.ExtKeyUsage = []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth}
		if c.cfg.ListenHost != "" {
			tpl.DNSNames = []string{c.cfg.ListenHost}
		}
	} else {
		tpl.ExtKeyUsage = []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth}
	}
	return tpl, nil
}
```

`Issue` then becomes: check `c.cert`, generate the P-384 key, `tpl, err := c.leafTemplate(...)`, sign, PEM-encode.

`internal/ca/csr.go`:

```go
package ca

import (
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// ErrBadCSR wraps every refusal of a certificate request.
var ErrBadCSR = errors.New("certificate request rejected")

// SignCSR issues a worker certificate for the key in csrPEM. Only the
// public key is taken from the request: its subject, SANs and extensions
// are ignored, and the CA names the worker (CN, org and zone URI SANs).
// Accepted keys: Ed25519, ECDSA P-256 and ECDSA P-384 (the key type every
// bundle `pulsix-manager worker add` issued so far carries).
func (c *CA) SignCSR(csrPEM []byte, orgID, zoneID uuid.UUID, workerName string) ([]byte, *x509.Certificate, error) {
	if c.cert == nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("sign certificate request: %w", ErrNotReady)
	}
	block, _ := pem.Decode(csrPEM)
	if block == nil || block.Type != "CERTIFICATE REQUEST" {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: expected a PEM CERTIFICATE REQUEST", ErrBadCSR)
	}
	req, err := x509.ParseCertificateRequest(block.Bytes)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrBadCSR, err)
	}
	if err := req.CheckSignature(); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: signature: %v", ErrBadCSR, err)
	}
	switch k := req.PublicKey.(type) {
	case ed25519.PublicKey:
	case *ecdsa.PublicKey:
		if k.Curve != elliptic.P256() && k.Curve != elliptic.P384() {
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: ECDSA keys must use P-256 or P-384", ErrBadCSR)
		}
	default:
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: key must be Ed25519 or ECDSA P-256/P-384", ErrBadCSR)
	}
	tpl, err := c.leafTemplate(&orgID, zoneID, workerName)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(c.rand, tpl, c.cert, req.PublicKey, c.key)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("sign certificate: %w", err)
	}
	crt, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("parse signed certificate: %w", err)
	}
	return pemCert(der), crt, nil
}

// CAPEM is the root certificate as PEM (the chain a worker stores).
func (c *CA) CAPEM() []byte { return pemCert(c.cert.Raw) }

// CAPin is the fingerprint an enrollment token carries: SHA-256 of the
// root certificate's DER.
func (c *CA) CAPin() [32]byte { return sha256.Sum256(c.cert.Raw) }

// ParseLeaf decodes the first PEM certificate of certPEM.
func ParseLeaf(certPEM []byte) (*x509.Certificate, error) {
	block, _ := pem.Decode(certPEM)
	if block == nil || block.Type != "CERTIFICATE" {
		return nil, errors.New("no PEM CERTIFICATE block")
	}
	return x509.ParseCertificate(block.Bytes)
}
```

`internal/db/migrations/00027_worker_identity.sql`:

```sql
-- +goose Up
-- Worker identity (spec 2026-09-18 §4): token enrollment, per-certificate
-- revocation and renewal. A worker may hold several certificates (the
-- renewed one and the old one until it expires); the gRPC interceptor
-- resolves the presented serial here on every RPC. workers.cert_serial
-- stays (NOT NULL, UNIQUE) as the first certificate's serial but is no
-- longer read.
ALTER TABLE workers ADD COLUMN status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active', 'revoked'));
ALTER TABLE workers ADD COLUMN revoked_at timestamptz;

CREATE TABLE worker_certificates (
  serial     text PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id     uuid NOT NULL,
  worker_id  uuid NOT NULL,
  not_before timestamptz NOT NULL,
  not_after  timestamptz NOT NULL,
  revoked_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, worker_id) REFERENCES workers (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE INDEX worker_certificates_worker_idx ON worker_certificates (org_id, worker_id);

ALTER TABLE worker_certificates ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE worker_certificates FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON worker_certificates
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON worker_certificates
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- Every existing worker keeps authenticating: its serial moves into the
-- certificate table. The validity window is an approximation (the 825-day
-- leaves of v0.2b), used for display only — the worker renews from its
-- real certificate.
INSERT INTO worker_certificates (serial, org_id, worker_id, not_before, not_after)
SELECT cert_serial, org_id, id, created_at, created_at + interval '825 days' FROM workers;

-- One-time enrollment tokens: only the SHA-256 of the secret is kept.
CREATE TABLE worker_enrollments (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id      uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  zone_id     uuid NOT NULL,
  name        text NOT NULL,
  secret_hash bytea NOT NULL,
  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,
  used_at     timestamptz,
  worker_id   uuid,
  created_by  uuid,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (org_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, zone_id) REFERENCES zones (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE INDEX worker_enrollments_pending_idx ON worker_enrollments (org_id, expires_at) WHERE used_at IS NULL;

ALTER TABLE worker_enrollments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE worker_enrollments FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON worker_enrollments
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON worker_enrollments
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- +goose Down
DROP TABLE worker_enrollments;
DROP TABLE worker_certificates;
ALTER TABLE workers DROP COLUMN revoked_at;
ALTER TABLE workers DROP COLUMN status;
```

Append to `internal/db/queries/workers.sql`, and **delete** the now-unused `GetWorkerBySerial` and `TouchWorker` queries (queries are code, not schema):

```sql
-- name: LookupWorkerSerial :one
-- The gRPC authentication path, org-unscoped: a presented serial resolves
-- to its certificate and worker; the caller decides revoked vs usable.
SELECT sqlc.embed(w), c.serial AS cert_serial_presented, c.not_before AS cert_not_before,
       c.not_after AS cert_not_after, c.revoked_at AS cert_revoked_at
FROM worker_certificates c
JOIN workers w ON w.org_id = c.org_id AND w.id = c.worker_id
WHERE c.serial = sqlc.arg(serial);

-- name: TouchWorkerByID :exec
-- Org-unscoped heartbeat by worker id (a renewed certificate has a new serial).
UPDATE workers SET last_seen = now(), version = sqlc.arg(version) WHERE id = sqlc.arg(id);

-- name: InsertWorkerCertificate :exec
INSERT INTO worker_certificates (serial, org_id, worker_id, not_before, not_after)
VALUES (sqlc.arg(serial), sqlc.arg(org_id), sqlc.arg(worker_id), sqlc.arg(not_before), sqlc.arg(not_after));

-- name: GetWorkerByName :one
SELECT * FROM workers WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND name = sqlc.arg(name);

-- name: CreateWorkerEnrollment :one
INSERT INTO worker_enrollments (id, org_id, zone_id, name, secret_hash, expires_at, created_by)
VALUES (sqlc.arg(id), sqlc.arg(org_id), sqlc.arg(zone_id), sqlc.arg(name), sqlc.arg(secret_hash), sqlc.arg(expires_at), sqlc.narg(created_by))
RETURNING *;

-- name: ListPendingWorkerEnrollments :many
SELECT * FROM worker_enrollments
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND used_at IS NULL AND expires_at > sqlc.arg(now)
ORDER BY created_at;

-- name: DeleteWorkerEnrollment :execrows
DELETE FROM worker_enrollments WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id) AND used_at IS NULL;

-- name: GetWorkerEnrollmentOrg :one
-- Enroll's first step, org-unscoped: the id is the token's public half.
SELECT org_id FROM worker_enrollments WHERE id = sqlc.arg(id);

-- name: LockWorkerEnrollment :one
SELECT * FROM worker_enrollments WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id) FOR UPDATE;

-- name: MarkWorkerEnrollmentUsed :exec
UPDATE worker_enrollments SET used_at = now(), worker_id = sqlc.narg(worker_id)
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id);
```
