In `TestDeleteMapsNotFound`, `f.GetBySerial(ctx, big.NewInt(7))` becomes `f.LookupSerial(ctx, big.NewInt(7))` (three results). `TestTouchDelegates` touches by id: `svc.Touch(ctx, id, "0.2.0")` with `f.touched[0] == id.String()+":0.2.0"`.

`internal/workers/fakes_test.go`:

```go
package workers_test

import (
	"context"
	"math/big"
	"slices"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/workers"
)

var ctx = context.Background()

// fakeStore is the in-memory workers.Store the service tests run on.
type fakeStore struct {
	mu           sync.Mutex
	rows         []workers.Worker
	certs        map[string]workers.Certificate
	created      workers.Worker
	touched      []string
	enrollments  map[uuid.UUID]workers.Enrollment
	enrollHashes map[uuid.UUID][]byte
}

func (f *fakeStore) init() {
	if f.certs == nil {
		f.certs = map[string]workers.Certificate{}
		f.enrollments = map[uuid.UUID]workers.Enrollment{}
		f.enrollHashes = map[uuid.UUID][]byte{}
	}
}

func (f *fakeStore) Create(_ context.Context, w workers.Worker, c workers.Certificate) (workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.init()
	return f.createLocked(w, c)
}

func (f *fakeStore) createLocked(w workers.Worker, c workers.Certificate) (workers.Worker, error) {
	for _, r := range f.rows {
		if r.OrgID == w.OrgID && r.Name == w.Name {
			return workers.Worker{}, workers.ErrNameTaken
		}
	}
	f.created = w
	f.rows = append(f.rows, w)
	f.certs[c.Serial.String()] = c
	return w, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) List(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []workers.Worker
	for _, r := range f.rows {
		if r.OrgID == orgID {
			out = append(out, r)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) GetByName(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, name string) (workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for _, r := range f.rows {
		if r.OrgID == orgID && r.Name == name {
			return r, nil
		}
	}
	return workers.Worker{}, workers.ErrNotFound
}

func (f *fakeStore) LookupSerial(_ context.Context, serial *big.Int) (workers.Worker, workers.Certificate, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.init()
	c, ok := f.certs[serial.String()]
	if !ok {
		return workers.Worker{}, workers.Certificate{}, workers.ErrNotFound
	}
	for _, r := range f.rows {
		if r.ID == c.WorkerID {
			return r, c, nil
		}
	}
	return workers.Worker{}, workers.Certificate{}, workers.ErrNotFound
}

func (f *fakeStore) Touch(_ context.Context, id uuid.UUID, version string) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.touched = append(f.touched, id.String()+":"+version)
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) AddCertificate(_ context.Context, c workers.Certificate) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.init()
	f.certs[c.Serial.String()] = c
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Delete(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for i, r := range f.rows {
		if r.OrgID == orgID && r.ID == id {
			f.rows = slices.Delete(f.rows, i, i+1)
			for s, c := range f.certs {
				if c.WorkerID == id {
					delete(f.certs, s)
				}
			}
			return nil
		}
	}
	return workers.ErrNotFound
}

func (f *fakeStore) CreateEnrollment(_ context.Context, e workers.Enrollment, hash []byte) (workers.Enrollment, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.init()
	f.enrollments[e.ID], f.enrollHashes[e.ID] = e, hash
	return e, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) ListEnrollments(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, now time.Time) ([]workers.Enrollment, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []workers.Enrollment
	for _, e := range f.enrollments {
		if e.OrgID == orgID && e.UsedAt == nil && now.Before(e.ExpiresAt) {
			out = append(out, e)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) DeleteEnrollment(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	e, ok := f.enrollments[id]
	if !ok || e.OrgID != orgID || e.UsedAt != nil {
		return workers.ErrNotFound
	}
	delete(f.enrollments, id)
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) Enroll(_ context.Context, id uuid.UUID, fn func(workers.Enrollment, []byte) (workers.Worker, workers.Certificate, error)) (workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.init()
	e, ok := f.enrollments[id]
	if !ok {
		return workers.Worker{}, workers.ErrNotFound
	}
	w, c, err := fn(e, f.enrollHashes[id])
	if err != nil {
		return workers.Worker{}, err
	}
	if _, err := f.createLocked(w, c); err != nil {
		return workers.Worker{}, err
	}
	used := time.Now()
	e.UsedAt = &used
	f.enrollments[id] = e
	return w, nil
}

// Test-only state changes.
func (f *fakeStore) revokeCert(serial *big.Int) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	c := f.certs[serial.String()]
	now := time.Now()
	c.RevokedAt = &now
	f.certs[serial.String()] = c
}

func (f *fakeStore) unrevokeCert(serial *big.Int) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	c := f.certs[serial.String()]
	c.RevokedAt = nil
	f.certs[serial.String()] = c
}

func (f *fakeStore) setStatus(id uuid.UUID, s workers.Status) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for i := range f.rows {
		if f.rows[i].ID == id {
			f.rows[i].Status = s
		}
	}
}
```

`internal/workers/enroll_test.go`:

```go
package workers_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"math/big"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/ca"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/crypto"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/enrolltoken"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/hosts"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/workers"
)

// memCAStore keeps the sealed CA in memory.
type memCAStore struct{ saved []byte }

func (m *memCAStore) Load(context.Context) ([]byte, bool, error) { return m.saved, m.saved != nil, nil }
func (m *memCAStore) Save(_ context.Context, b []byte) error    { m.saved = b; return nil }

type fakeZones struct{ zones []hosts.Zone }

func (f fakeZones) ListZones(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]hosts.Zone, error) {
	var out []hosts.Zone
	for _, z := range f.zones {
		if z.OrgID == orgID {
			out = append(out, z)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

type enrollWorld struct {
	svc            *workers.Service
	store          *fakeStore
	ca             *ca.CA
	org, zone      uuid.UUID
	actor          uuid.UUID
	now            time.Time
	existing       workers.Worker
	existingSerial *big.Int
}

func newEnrollWorld(t *testing.T) *enrollWorld {
	t.Helper()
	var key [32]byte
	copy(key[:], "pulsix-workers-test-key-0123456")
	authority, err := ca.New(&memCAStore{}, crypto.NewBox(key), ca.Config{CommonName: "pulsix-manager", ListenHost: "manager"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := authority.Ensure(t.Context()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w := &enrollWorld{store: &fakeStore{}, ca: authority, org: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), zone: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()),
		actor: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), now: time.Date(2026, 9, 18, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC), existingSerial: big.NewInt(777)}
	w.svc = workers.NewService(w.store, workers.WithSigner(authority),
		workers.WithZones(fakeZones{zones: []hosts.Zone{{ID: w.zone, OrgID: w.org, Slug: "default"}}}),
		workers.WithClock(func() time.Time { return w.now }))
	w.existing, err = w.svc.Register(ctx, w.org, workers.RegisterInput{ZoneID: w.zone, Name: "old-1", CertSerial: w.existingSerial})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return w
}

// newCSR is an ECDSA P-256 certificate request as the worker sends it.
func newCSR(t *testing.T) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: der})
}

func TestCreateEnrollmentAndEnroll(t *testing.T) {
	w := newEnrollWorld(t)
	e, token, err := w.svc.CreateEnrollment(ctx, w.org, &w.actor, w.zone, "fra-1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !e.ExpiresAt.Equal(w.now.Add(workers.EnrollmentTTL)) {
		t.Fatalf("expires = %v", e.ExpiresAt)
	}
	tok, err := enrolltoken.Parse(token)
	if err != nil || tok.ID != e.ID || tok.CAPin != w.ca.CAPin() {
		t.Fatalf("token = %+v, %v", tok, err)
	}
	if hash := w.store.enrollHashes[e.ID]; bytes.Equal(hash, tok.Secret) || len(hash) != 32 {
		t.Fatal("only the SHA-256 of the secret may be stored")
	}
	if pending, _ := w.svc.ListEnrollments(ctx, w.org); len(pending) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("pending = %v", pending)
	}
	res, err := w.svc.Enroll(ctx, workers.EnrollInput{TokenID: tok.ID, Secret: tok.Secret, CSRPEM: newCSR(t), Version: "v0.2c"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if res.Worker.Name != "fra-1" || res.Worker.ZoneID != w.zone || res.Worker.Status != workers.StatusActive || len(res.CAPEM) == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("result = %+v", res)
	}
	leaf, err := ca.ParseLeaf(res.CertPEM)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, leaf.SerialNumber); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the enrolled certificate must authenticate: %v", err)
	}
	// Single use.
	if _, err := w.svc.Enroll(ctx, workers.EnrollInput{TokenID: tok.ID, Secret: tok.Secret, CSRPEM: newCSR(t)}); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrEnrollmentRefused) {
		t.Fatalf("second use: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestEnrollRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	w := newEnrollWorld(t)
	_, token, _ := w.svc.CreateEnrollment(ctx, w.org, &w.actor, w.zone, "fra-2")
	tok, _ := enrolltoken.Parse(token)
	wrong := bytes.Repeat([]byte{9}, 32)
	if _, err := w.svc.Enroll(ctx, workers.EnrollInput{TokenID: tok.ID, Secret: wrong, CSRPEM: newCSR(t)}); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrEnrollmentRefused) {
		t.Fatalf("wrong secret: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Enroll(ctx, workers.EnrollInput{TokenID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), Secret: tok.Secret, CSRPEM: newCSR(t)}); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrEnrollmentRefused) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown id: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Enroll(ctx, workers.EnrollInput{TokenID: tok.ID, Secret: tok.Secret, CSRPEM: []byte("x")}); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrBadCSR) {
		t.Fatalf("bad csr: %v", err)
	}
	w.now = w.now.Add(workers.EnrollmentTTL + time.Second)
	if _, err := w.svc.Enroll(ctx, workers.EnrollInput{TokenID: tok.ID, Secret: tok.Secret, CSRPEM: newCSR(t)}); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrEnrollmentRefused) {
		t.Fatalf("expired: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCreateEnrollmentValidationAndWithdraw(t *testing.T) {
	w := newEnrollWorld(t)
	for name, c := range map[string]struct {
		zone uuid.UUID
		name string
		want error
	}{
		"bad name":      {w.zone, "Bad_Name", workers.ErrInvalidInput},
		"foreign zone":  {uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), "x", workers.ErrInvalidInput},
		"existing name": {w.zone, w.existing.Name, workers.ErrNameTaken},
	} {
		if _, _, err := w.svc.CreateEnrollment(ctx, w.org, &w.actor, c.zone, c.name); !errors.Is(err, c.want) {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v, want %v", name, err, c.want)
		}
	}
	e, _, err := w.svc.CreateEnrollment(ctx, w.org, &w.actor, w.zone, "fra-3")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.svc.WithdrawEnrollment(ctx, w.org, e.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := w.svc.WithdrawEnrollment(ctx, w.org, e.ID); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("second withdraw: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestGetBySerialRefusesRevokedCertificatesAndWorkers(t *testing.T) {
	w := newEnrollWorld(t)
	serial := w.existingSerial
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, serial); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	w.store.revokeCert(serial)
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, serial); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrRevoked) {
		t.Fatalf("revoked certificate: %v", err)
	}
	w.store.unrevokeCert(serial)
	w.store.setStatus(w.existing.ID, workers.StatusRevoked)
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, serial); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrRevoked) {
		t.Fatalf("revoked worker: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, big.NewInt(999)); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, nil); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("nil: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRenewAddsACertificate(t *testing.T) {
	w := newEnrollWorld(t)
	certPEM, err := w.svc.Renew(ctx, w.existing, newCSR(t))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	leaf, _ := ca.ParseLeaf(certPEM)
	got, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, leaf.SerialNumber)
	if err != nil || got.ID != w.existing.ID || got.CertSerial.Cmp(leaf.SerialNumber) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("renewed lookup = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, w.existingSerial); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the old certificate stays valid: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.Renew(ctx, w.existing, []byte("x")); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrBadCSR) {
		t.Fatalf("bad csr: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRegisterOrAttachReusesTheRow(t *testing.T) {
	w := newEnrollWorld(t)
	got, err := w.svc.RegisterOrAttach(ctx, w.org, workers.RegisterInput{ZoneID: w.zone, Name: w.existing.Name, CertSerial: big.NewInt(4242)})
	if err != nil || got.ID != w.existing.ID {
		t.Fatalf("attach = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, big.NewInt(4242)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("attached certificate: %v", err)
	}
	fresh, err := w.svc.RegisterOrAttach(ctx, w.org, workers.RegisterInput{ZoneID: w.zone, Name: "embedded", CertSerial: big.NewInt(4244)})
	if err != nil || fresh.ID == w.existing.ID {
		t.Fatalf("register = %+v, %v", fresh, err)
	}
	w.store.setStatus(w.existing.ID, workers.StatusRevoked)
	if _, err := w.svc.RegisterOrAttach(ctx, w.org, workers.RegisterInput{ZoneID: w.zone, Name: w.existing.Name, CertSerial: big.NewInt(4243)}); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrRevoked) {
		t.Fatalf("revoked row: %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/workers/service.go` (complete file):

```go
// Package workers is the zone-worker registry (spec §6, spec 2026-09-18
// §4): which workers an organization runs, in which zone, which client
// certificates each one authenticates with, and the one-time enrollment
// tokens new workers redeem. The gRPC interceptor resolves every presented
// certificate serial here; a revoked certificate or worker is refused.
package workers

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"math/big"
	"regexp"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/ca"
)

// Sentinel errors mapped to HTTP / gRPC status by the transport layers.
var (
	ErrInvalidInput = errors.New("invalid input")
	ErrNotFound     = errors.New("not found")
	// ErrNameTaken means another worker of the same organization already
	// carries the name (mapped to 409 by the transport layer).
	ErrNameTaken = errors.New("name already in use")
	// ErrRevoked refuses a revoked certificate or a revoked worker.
	ErrRevoked = errors.New("worker or certificate revoked")
)

// namePattern lets a name start with a lowercase letter or digit and
// continue with lowercase letters, digits or hyphens, at most 63 characters
// in total — the same shape host names follow, without dots or underscores.
var namePattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,62}$`)

// Status is a worker's lifecycle state; online/offline is derived from
// last_seen, never stored.
type Status string

const (
	StatusActive  Status = "active"
	StatusRevoked Status = "revoked"
)

// Worker is a registered zone worker. CertSerial is the serial the worker
// authenticated with (GetBySerial) or its first certificate's serial.
type Worker struct {
	ID         uuid.UUID
	OrgID      uuid.UUID
	ZoneID     uuid.UUID
	Name       string
	CertSerial *big.Int
	LastSeen   *time.Time
	Version    string
	Status     Status
	RevokedAt  *time.Time
	CreatedAt  time.Time
}

// Certificate is one client certificate a worker holds.
type Certificate struct {
	Serial              *big.Int
	OrgID, WorkerID     uuid.UUID
	NotBefore, NotAfter time.Time
	RevokedAt           *time.Time
}

// RegisterInput is a new worker with its first certificate. CertSerial
// must be positive; a zero NotBefore/NotAfter records now / now +
// ca.LeafValidity (the certificate was just issued with that window).
type RegisterInput struct {
	ZoneID              uuid.UUID
	Name                string
	CertSerial          *big.Int
	Version             string
	NotBefore, NotAfter time.Time
}

// Store persists workers, certificates and enrollments. LookupSerial and
// Touch run outside any org scope (the gRPC interceptor has none).
type Store interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, w Worker, c Certificate) (Worker, error)
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Worker, error)
	GetByName(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, name string) (Worker, error)
	LookupSerial(ctx context.Context, serial *big.Int) (Worker, Certificate, error)
	Touch(ctx context.Context, workerID uuid.UUID, version string) error
	AddCertificate(ctx context.Context, c Certificate) error
	Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	CreateEnrollment(ctx context.Context, e Enrollment, secretHash []byte) (Enrollment, error)
	ListEnrollments(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, now time.Time) ([]Enrollment, error)
	DeleteEnrollment(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	Enroll(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID, fn func(e Enrollment, secretHash []byte) (Worker, Certificate, error)) (Worker, error)
}

// Service implements the worker registry.
type Service struct {
	store  Store
	signer Signer
	zones  ZoneLister
	now    func() time.Time
	rand   io.Reader
}

// NewService wires the store; enrollment and renewal need WithSigner and
// WithZones.
func NewService(store Store, opts ...Option) *Service {
	s := &Service{store: store, now: time.Now, rand: rand.Reader}
	for _, o := range opts {
		o(s)
	}
	return s
}

// Register validates the input and stores a new worker with its first
// certificate.
func (s *Service) Register(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, in RegisterInput) (Worker, error) {
	if !namePattern.MatchString(in.Name) {
		return Worker{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: name must be 1-63 lowercase letters, digits or hyphens, starting with a letter or digit", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if in.ZoneID == uuid.Nil {
		return Worker{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: zone is required", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if in.CertSerial == nil || in.CertSerial.Sign() <= 0 {
		return Worker{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: certificate serial must be a positive integer", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	id, err := uuid.NewV7FromReader(s.rand)
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, err
	}
	w := Worker{ID: id, OrgID: orgID, ZoneID: in.ZoneID, Name: in.Name, CertSerial: in.CertSerial, Version: in.Version, Status: StatusActive}
	w, err = s.store.Create(ctx, w, s.certificateFor(orgID, id, in))
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, fmt.Errorf("register worker: %w", err)
	}
	return w, nil
}

// RegisterOrAttach is Register for a worker whose name may already exist
// (the embedded worker, re-issued on every manager start): an existing
// active row gets the new certificate; a revoked one stays refused.
func (s *Service) RegisterOrAttach(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, in RegisterInput) (Worker, error) {
	cur, err := s.store.GetByName(ctx, orgID, in.Name)
	if errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
		return s.Register(ctx, orgID, in)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, err
	}
	if cur.Status == StatusRevoked {
		return Worker{}, ErrRevoked
	}
	if in.CertSerial == nil || in.CertSerial.Sign() <= 0 {
		return Worker{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: certificate serial must be a positive integer", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if err := s.store.AddCertificate(ctx, s.certificateFor(cur.OrgID, cur.ID, in)); err != nil {
		return Worker{}, fmt.Errorf("attach certificate: %w", err)
	}
	cur.CertSerial = in.CertSerial
	return cur, nil
}

func (s *Service) certificateFor(orgID, workerID uuid.UUID, in RegisterInput) Certificate {
	nb, na := in.NotBefore, in.NotAfter
	if nb.IsZero() {
		nb = s.now()
	}
	if na.IsZero() {
		na = nb.Add(ca.LeafValidity)
	}
	return Certificate{Serial: in.CertSerial, OrgID: orgID, WorkerID: workerID, NotBefore: nb, NotAfter: na}
}

// List returns orgID's workers ordered by name.
func (s *Service) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Worker, error) {
	return s.store.List(ctx, orgID)
}

// GetBySerial is the gRPC authentication path. The returned Worker carries
// the presented serial in CertSerial. Unknown serials are ErrNotFound; a
// revoked certificate or worker is ErrRevoked — both refuse the call.
func (s *Service) GetBySerial(ctx context.Context, serial *big.Int) (Worker, error) {
	if serial == nil {
		return Worker{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	w, c, err := s.store.LookupSerial(ctx, serial)
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, err
	}
	if c.RevokedAt != nil || w.Status == StatusRevoked {
		return Worker{}, ErrRevoked
	}
	w.CertSerial = c.Serial
	return w, nil
}

// Touch records a heartbeat: last_seen becomes now and version the
// worker's self-reported build.
func (s *Service) Touch(ctx context.Context, workerID uuid.UUID, version string) error {
	return s.store.Touch(ctx, workerID, version)
}

// Delete removes a worker row; deleting an absent one is ErrNotFound.
func (s *Service) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.store.Delete(ctx, orgID, id)
}
```

`internal/workers/options.go`:

```go
package workers

import (
	"io"
	"time"
)

// Option configures a Service.
type Option func(*Service)

// WithSigner wires the CA for enrollment and renewal.
func WithSigner(s Signer) Option { return func(svc *Service) { svc.signer = s } }

// WithZones wires the zone lookup enrollment creation validates against.
func WithZones(z ZoneLister) Option { return func(svc *Service) { svc.zones = z } }

// WithClock injects the clock (tests).
func WithClock(now func() time.Time) Option { return func(svc *Service) { svc.now = now } }

// WithRand injects the entropy source for ids and enrollment secrets.
func WithRand(r io.Reader) Option { return func(svc *Service) { svc.rand = r } }
```

`internal/workers/enroll.go`:

```go
package workers

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/subtle"
	"crypto/x509"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/enrolltoken"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/hosts"
)

// EnrollmentTTL is how long a token is valid (spec §4).
const EnrollmentTTL = time.Hour

var (
	// ErrEnrollmentRefused covers unknown, used, expired and wrong-secret
	// tokens alike: the caller cannot tell which.
	ErrEnrollmentRefused = errors.New("enrollment refused")
	ErrBadCSR            = errors.New("certificate request rejected")
)

type Enrollment struct {
	ID, OrgID, ZoneID uuid.UUID
	Name              string
	ExpiresAt         time.Time
	UsedAt            *time.Time
	CreatedBy         *uuid.UUID
	CreatedAt         time.Time
}

type EnrollInput struct {
	TokenID uuid.UUID
	Secret  []byte
	CSRPEM  []byte
	Version string
}

type EnrollResult struct {
	Worker  Worker
	CertPEM []byte
	CAPEM   []byte
}

type Signer interface {
	SignCSR(csrPEM []byte, orgID, zoneID uuid.UUID, workerName string) ([]byte, *x509.Certificate, error)
	CAPEM() []byte
	CAPin() [32]byte
}

type ZoneLister interface {
	ListZones(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]hosts.Zone, error)
}

// CreateEnrollment creates a one-time token for a worker named name in
// zoneID. The token is returned once; only SHA-256(secret) is stored.
func (s *Service) CreateEnrollment(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, actor *uuid.UUID, zoneID uuid.UUID, name string) (Enrollment, string, error) {
	if !namePattern.MatchString(name) {
		return Enrollment{}, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: name must be 1-63 lowercase letters, digits or hyphens, starting with a letter or digit", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	zones, err := s.zones.ListZones(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return Enrollment{}, "", fmt.Errorf("list zones: %w", err)
	}
	found := false
	for _, z := range zones {
		found = found || z.ID == zoneID
	}
	if !found {
		return Enrollment{}, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown zone", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if _, err := s.store.GetByName(ctx, orgID, name); err == nil {
		return Enrollment{}, "", ErrNameTaken
	} else if !errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
		return Enrollment{}, "", err
	}
	secret := make([]byte, 32)
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(s.rand, secret); err != nil {
		return Enrollment{}, "", fmt.Errorf("draw enrollment secret: %w", err)
	}
	hash := sha256.Sum256(secret)
	now := s.now()
	id, err := uuid.NewV7FromReader(s.rand)
	if err != nil {
		return Enrollment{}, "", err
	}
	e, err := s.store.CreateEnrollment(ctx, Enrollment{ID: id, OrgID: orgID, ZoneID: zoneID, Name: name, ExpiresAt: now.Add(EnrollmentTTL), CreatedBy: actor, CreatedAt: now}, hash[:])
	if err != nil {
		return Enrollment{}, "", err
	}
	token := enrolltoken.Token{ID: e.ID, Secret: secret, CAPin: s.signer.CAPin()}.String()
	return e, token, nil
}

func (s *Service) ListEnrollments(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Enrollment, error) {
	return s.store.ListEnrollments(ctx, orgID, s.now())
}

func (s *Service) WithdrawEnrollment(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.store.DeleteEnrollment(ctx, orgID, id)
}

// Enroll redeems a token: unused, unexpired, secret compared in constant
// time; the CSR is signed; worker, certificate and the used mark are
// written in one transaction (the store runs fn while holding the row).
func (s *Service) Enroll(ctx context.Context, in EnrollInput) (EnrollResult, error) {
	var certPEM []byte
	w, err := s.store.Enroll(ctx, in.TokenID, func(e Enrollment, secretHash []byte) (Worker, Certificate, error) {
		sum := sha256.Sum256(in.Secret)
		if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare(sum[:], secretHash) != 1 || e.UsedAt != nil || !s.now().Before(e.ExpiresAt) {
			return Worker{}, Certificate{}, ErrEnrollmentRefused
		}
		pemBytes, crt, err := s.signer.SignCSR(in.CSRPEM, e.OrgID, e.ZoneID, e.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return Worker{}, Certificate{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrBadCSR, err)
		}
		certPEM = pemBytes
		id, err := uuid.NewV7FromReader(s.rand)
		if err != nil {
			return Worker{}, Certificate{}, err
		}
		w := Worker{ID: id, OrgID: e.OrgID, ZoneID: e.ZoneID, Name: e.Name, CertSerial: crt.SerialNumber, Version: in.Version, Status: StatusActive}
		return w, Certificate{Serial: crt.SerialNumber, OrgID: e.OrgID, WorkerID: id, NotBefore: crt.NotBefore, NotAfter: crt.NotAfter}, nil
	})
	if errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
		return EnrollResult{}, ErrEnrollmentRefused
	}
	if err != nil {
		return EnrollResult{}, err
	}
	return EnrollResult{Worker: w, CertPEM: certPEM, CAPEM: s.signer.CAPEM()}, nil
}

// Renew signs a fresh certificate for an authenticated worker; the old one
// stays valid until it expires (open streams are not cut).
func (s *Service) Renew(ctx context.Context, w Worker, csrPEM []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	pemBytes, crt, err := s.signer.SignCSR(csrPEM, w.OrgID, w.ZoneID, w.Name)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrBadCSR, err)
	}
	if err := s.store.AddCertificate(ctx, Certificate{Serial: crt.SerialNumber, OrgID: w.OrgID, WorkerID: w.ID, NotBefore: crt.NotBefore, NotAfter: crt.NotAfter}); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("record renewed certificate: %w", err)
	}
	return pemBytes, nil
}
```

`internal/workers/store_sql.go` (complete file):

```go
package workers

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math/big"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// SQLStore implements Store with sqlc queries on *db.DB. The workers table
// carries no row-level security (migration 00017); its certificates and
// enrollments do, so gRPC-path writes bind the worker's org explicitly.
type SQLStore struct {
	d *db.DB
	q *gen.Queries
}

// NewSQLStore builds the store.
func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore { return &SQLStore{d: d, q: gen.New(d)} }

func (s *SQLStore) Create(ctx context.Context, w Worker, c Certificate) (Worker, error) {
	var out Worker
	err := s.d.WithWriteTx(ctx, w.OrgID, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		row, err := s.q.InsertWorker(ctx, gen.InsertWorkerParams{
			ID: w.ID, OrgID: w.OrgID, ZoneID: w.ZoneID, Name: w.Name,
			CertSerial: w.CertSerial.String(), Version: w.Version,
		})
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := s.insertCert(ctx, c); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		out, err = toWorker(row)
		return err
	})
	if db.IsUniqueViolation(err) {
		return Worker{}, ErrNameTaken
	}
	return out, err
}

func (s *SQLStore) insertCert(ctx context.Context, c Certificate) error {
	return s.q.InsertWorkerCertificate(ctx, gen.InsertWorkerCertificateParams{
		Serial: c.Serial.String(), OrgID: c.OrgID, WorkerID: c.WorkerID, NotBefore: c.NotBefore, NotAfter: c.NotAfter,
	})
}

func (s *SQLStore) List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Worker, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListWorkers(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Worker, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		w, err := toWorker(r)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out[i] = w
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) GetByName(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, name string) (Worker, error) {
	row, err := s.q.GetWorkerByName(ctx, gen.GetWorkerByNameParams{OrgID: orgID, Name: name})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Worker{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, err
	}
	return toWorker(row)
}

// LookupSerial resolves a presented serial without any organization filter:
// the gRPC interceptor has no org context, and the serial is the primary
// key of worker_certificates.
func (s *SQLStore) LookupSerial(ctx context.Context, serial *big.Int) (Worker, Certificate, error) {
	if serial == nil {
		return Worker{}, Certificate{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	row, err := s.q.LookupWorkerSerial(ctx, serial.String())
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Worker{}, Certificate{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, Certificate{}, err
	}
	w, err := toWorker(row.Worker)
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, Certificate{}, err
	}
	cs, ok := new(big.Int).SetString(row.CertSerialPresented, 10)
	if !ok {
		return Worker{}, Certificate{}, fmt.Errorf("certificate serial %q is not decimal", row.CertSerialPresented)
	}
	return w, Certificate{Serial: cs, OrgID: w.OrgID, WorkerID: w.ID, NotBefore: row.CertNotBefore, NotAfter: row.CertNotAfter, RevokedAt: db.TimePtr(row.CertRevokedAt)}, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) Touch(ctx context.Context, workerID uuid.UUID, version string) error {
	return s.q.TouchWorkerByID(ctx, gen.TouchWorkerByIDParams{ID: workerID, Version: version})
}

func (s *SQLStore) AddCertificate(ctx context.Context, c Certificate) error {
	return s.insertCert(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, c.OrgID), c)
}

func (s *SQLStore) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := s.q.DeleteWorker(ctx, gen.DeleteWorkerParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) CreateEnrollment(ctx context.Context, e Enrollment, secretHash []byte) (Enrollment, error) {
	row, err := s.q.CreateWorkerEnrollment(ctx, gen.CreateWorkerEnrollmentParams{
		ID: e.ID, OrgID: e.OrgID, ZoneID: e.ZoneID, Name: e.Name, SecretHash: secretHash, ExpiresAt: e.ExpiresAt, CreatedBy: db.NullUUID(e.CreatedBy),
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Enrollment{}, fmt.Errorf("create enrollment: %w", err)
	}
	return toEnrollment(row), nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) ListEnrollments(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, now time.Time) ([]Enrollment, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListPendingWorkerEnrollments(ctx, gen.ListPendingWorkerEnrollmentsParams{OrgID: orgID, Now: now})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list enrollments: %w", err)
	}
	out := make([]Enrollment, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = toEnrollment(r)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) DeleteEnrollment(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := s.q.DeleteWorkerEnrollment(ctx, gen.DeleteWorkerEnrollmentParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete enrollment: %w", err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

// Enroll locks the enrollment row, runs fn and, when it succeeds, writes
// worker, certificate and the used mark — one transaction. A concurrent
// second redeem blocks on the row lock and then sees used_at set.
func (s *SQLStore) Enroll(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID, fn func(e Enrollment, secretHash []byte) (Worker, Certificate, error)) (Worker, error) {
	orgID, err := s.q.GetWorkerEnrollmentOrg(ctx, id) // unscoped: the id is the lookup key
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Worker{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, fmt.Errorf("find enrollment: %w", err)
	}
	var out Worker
	err = s.d.WithWriteTx(ctx, orgID, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		row, err := s.q.LockWorkerEnrollment(ctx, gen.LockWorkerEnrollmentParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("lock enrollment: %w", err)
		}
		w, c, err := fn(toEnrollment(row), row.SecretHash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := s.q.InsertWorker(ctx, gen.InsertWorkerParams{ID: w.ID, OrgID: w.OrgID, ZoneID: w.ZoneID, Name: w.Name, CertSerial: w.CertSerial.String(), Version: w.Version}); err != nil {
			if db.IsUniqueViolation(err) {
				return ErrNameTaken
			}
			return fmt.Errorf("insert worker: %w", err)
		}
		if err := s.insertCert(ctx, c); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("insert certificate: %w", err)
		}
		if err := s.q.MarkWorkerEnrollmentUsed(ctx, gen.MarkWorkerEnrollmentUsedParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id, WorkerID: db.NullUUID(&w.ID)}); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("mark enrollment used: %w", err)
		}
		out = w
		return nil
	})
	return out, err
}

func toWorker(r gen.Worker) (Worker, error) {
	serial, ok := new(big.Int).SetString(r.CertSerial, 10)
	if !ok {
		return Worker{}, fmt.Errorf("worker %s: parse cert serial %q: not a decimal integer", r.ID, r.CertSerial)
	}
	return Worker{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, ZoneID: r.ZoneID, Name: r.Name, CertSerial: serial,
		LastSeen: db.TimePtr(r.LastSeen), Version: r.Version, Status: Status(r.Status),
		RevokedAt: db.TimePtr(r.RevokedAt), CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt,
	}, nil
}

func toEnrollment(r gen.WorkerEnrollment) Enrollment {
	return Enrollment{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, ZoneID: r.ZoneID, Name: r.Name, ExpiresAt: r.ExpiresAt,
		UsedAt: db.TimePtr(r.UsedAt), CreatedBy: db.UUIDPtr(r.CreatedBy), CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt,
	}
}
```

`internal/workers/store_identity_test.go`:

```go
package workers_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"errors"
	"math/big"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/workers"
)

// TestEnrollIsSingleUseUnderConcurrency: two redeems of one token race;
// the row lock lets exactly one through.
func TestEnrollIsSingleUseUnderConcurrency(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	q := gen.New(d.Pool())
	org := seedOrg(t, q, "enroll-race")
	zone := seedZone(t, q, org, "default")
	store := workers.NewSQLStore(d)
	secret := bytes.Repeat([]byte{3}, 32)
	hash := sha256.Sum256(secret)
	e, err := store.CreateEnrollment(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), workers.Enrollment{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, ZoneID: zone, Name: "fra-1", ExpiresAt: time.Now().Add(time.Hour)}, hash[:])
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var mu sync.Mutex
	wins, refusals := 0, 0
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		serial := big.NewInt(int64(9000 + i))
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			_, err := store.Enroll(ctx, e.ID, func(got workers.Enrollment, h []byte) (workers.Worker, workers.Certificate, error) {
				if got.UsedAt != nil || !bytes.Equal(h, hash[:]) {
					return workers.Worker{}, workers.Certificate{}, workers.ErrEnrollmentRefused
				}
				id := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
				return workers.Worker{ID: id, OrgID: org, ZoneID: zone, Name: got.Name, CertSerial: serial, Status: workers.StatusActive},
					workers.Certificate{Serial: serial, OrgID: org, WorkerID: id, NotBefore: time.Now(), NotAfter: time.Now().Add(time.Hour)}, nil
			})
			mu.Lock()
			defer mu.Unlock()
			switch {
			case err == nil:
				wins++
			case errors.Is(err, workers.ErrEnrollmentRefused):
				refusals++
			default:
				t.Error(err)
			}
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	if wins != 1 || refusals != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("wins %d, refusals %d; want exactly one redeem", wins, refusals)
	}
	if pending, _ := store.ListEnrollments(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), org, time.Now()); len(pending) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("a used enrollment is not pending: %v", pending)
	}
	if err := store.DeleteEnrollment(db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org), org, e.ID); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("a used enrollment cannot be withdrawn: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := store.Enroll(ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), nil); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown enrollment: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestLookupSerialSeesRevocationAndRenewals(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	q := gen.New(d.Pool())
	org := seedOrg(t, q, "lookup-serial")
	zone := seedZone(t, q, org, "default")
	store := workers.NewSQLStore(d)
	svc := workers.NewService(store)
	w, err := svc.Register(ctx, org, workers.RegisterInput{ZoneID: zone, Name: "fra-1", CertSerial: big.NewInt(1001)})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := store.AddCertificate(ctx, workers.Certificate{Serial: big.NewInt(1002), OrgID: org, WorkerID: w.ID, NotBefore: time.Now(), NotAfter: time.Now().Add(time.Hour)}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, err := svc.GetBySerial(ctx, big.NewInt(1002))
	if err != nil || got.ID != w.ID || got.CertSerial.Int64() != 1002 {
		t.Fatalf("renewed serial = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `UPDATE worker_certificates SET revoked_at = now() WHERE serial = '1001'`); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.GetBySerial(ctx, big.NewInt(1001)); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrRevoked) {
		t.Fatalf("revoked certificate: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.GetBySerial(ctx, big.NewInt(1002)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the other certificate is unaffected: %v", err)
	}
	byName, err := store.GetByName(ctx, org, "fra-1")
	if err != nil || byName.ID != w.ID || byName.Status != workers.StatusActive {
		t.Fatalf("GetByName = %+v, %v", byName, err)
	}
	if _, err := store.GetByName(ctx, org, "nope"); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("GetByName unknown: %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/db/worker_backfill_test.go` proves the migration keeps existing workers authenticating:

```go
package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
	"github.com/pressly/goose/v3"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
)

// TestWorkerCertificateBackfill: a worker registered before 00027 keeps
// authenticating — its serial is copied into worker_certificates.
func TestWorkerCertificateBackfill(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	adminURL := testdb.URL(t)
	admin, err := db.Open(ctx, adminURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer admin.Close()
	name := "backfill_" + uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()).String()[:8]
	if _, err := admin.Pool().Exec(ctx, "CREATE DATABASE "+name); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _, _ = admin.Pool().Exec(context.Background(), "DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS "+name+" WITH (FORCE)") })
	u, err := url.Parse(adminURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	u.Path = "/" + name
	fresh, err := db.Open(ctx, u.String())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer fresh.Close()
	sqlDB := stdlib.OpenDBFromPool(fresh.Pool())
	defer sqlDB.Close() //nolint:errcheck
	provider, err := goose.NewProvider(goose.DialectPostgres, sqlDB, os.DirFS("migrations"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := provider.UpTo(ctx, 26); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	org, zone, worker := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	for _, stmt := range []struct {
		sql  string
		args []any
	}{
		{`INSERT INTO organizations (id, slug, name) VALUES ($1, 'bf', 'bf')`, []any{org}},
		{`INSERT INTO zones (id, org_id, slug, name) VALUES ($1, $2, 'default', 'Default')`, []any{zone, org}},
		{`INSERT INTO workers (id, org_id, zone_id, name, cert_serial) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'old', '4242')`, []any{worker, org, zone}},
	} {
		if _, err := fresh.Pool().Exec(ctx, stmt.sql, stmt.args...); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := provider.Up(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var got uuid.UUID
	var status string
	if err := fresh.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT c.worker_id, w.status FROM worker_certificates c JOIN workers w ON w.id = c.worker_id WHERE c.serial = '4242'`).Scan(&got, &status); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got != worker || status != "active" {
		t.Fatalf("backfill = %v %q", got, status)
	}
}
```

`internal/grpcsrv/testfakes.go`: replace `fakeWorkerStore` with the version below and initialise `revoked: map[string]bool{}` in `newHarness`. The existing tests keep using `h.store.GetBySerial` and `h.store.touched[serial]`, which are test helpers now.

```go
// fakeWorkerStore is the in-memory workers.Store the harness runs the
// registry service on. bySerial maps every certificate serial a worker
// holds to its row; touched maps a serial to the last touched version.
type fakeWorkerStore struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	bySerial map[string]workers.Worker
	revoked  map[string]bool // serial → certificate revoked
	touched  map[string]string
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) Create(_ context.Context, w workers.Worker, c workers.Certificate) (workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.bySerial[c.Serial.String()] = w
	return w, nil
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) List(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []workers.Worker
	for _, w := range f.bySerial {
		if w.OrgID == orgID {
			out = append(out, w)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) GetByName(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, name string) (workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for _, w := range f.bySerial {
		if w.OrgID == orgID && w.Name == name {
			return w, nil
		}
	}
	return workers.Worker{}, workers.ErrNotFound
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) LookupSerial(_ context.Context, serial *big.Int) (workers.Worker, workers.Certificate, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	if serial == nil {
		return workers.Worker{}, workers.Certificate{}, workers.ErrNotFound
	}
	w, ok := f.bySerial[serial.String()]
	if !ok {
		return workers.Worker{}, workers.Certificate{}, workers.ErrNotFound
	}
	c := workers.Certificate{Serial: serial, OrgID: w.OrgID, WorkerID: w.ID}
	if f.revoked[serial.String()] {
		now := time.Now()
		c.RevokedAt = &now
	}
	return w, c, nil
}

// GetBySerial is a test helper: the row a serial resolves to.
func (f *fakeWorkerStore) GetBySerial(ctx context.Context, serial *big.Int) (workers.Worker, error) {
	w, _, err := f.LookupSerial(ctx, serial)
	return w, err
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) Touch(_ context.Context, workerID uuid.UUID, version string) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for s, w := range f.bySerial {
		if w.ID == workerID {
			f.touched[s] = version
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) AddCertificate(_ context.Context, c workers.Certificate) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for _, w := range f.bySerial {
		if w.ID == c.WorkerID {
			f.bySerial[c.Serial.String()] = w
			return nil
		}
	}
	return workers.ErrNotFound
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) Delete(_ context.Context, _, _ uuid.UUID) error { return nil }

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) CreateEnrollment(_ context.Context, e workers.Enrollment, _ []byte) (workers.Enrollment, error) {
	return e, nil
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) ListEnrollments(context.Context, uuid.UUID, time.Time) ([]workers.Enrollment, error) {
	return nil, nil
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) DeleteEnrollment(context.Context, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID) error {
	return workers.ErrNotFound
}

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) Enroll(context.Context, uuid.UUID, func(workers.Enrollment, []byte) (workers.Worker, workers.Certificate, error)) (workers.Worker, error) {
	return workers.Worker{}, workers.ErrNotFound
}
```

### Task 13: gRPC server: optional client certificates, Enroll and RenewCertificate, revocation-aware authentication

This task covers spec §4 on the manager's worker endpoint:
- The TLS layer switches to `VerifyClientCertIfGiven`.
- The interceptor requires a certificate for every method except `Enroll`, and refuses unknown or revoked serials (and zone mismatches) with `Unauthenticated`.
- `Enroll` is rate limited per client address.
- The server certificate carries the CA and renews itself.
- A `StreamRegistry` lets a revocation end the worker's open event streams (used by Task 15).
- The manager always builds the CA and one shared `workers.Service`.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/grpcsrv/servercert.go`, `streams.go`, `enroll.go` (Enroll + RenewCertificate; the per-address limit is Task 1's `clientip.Limiter`)
- Modify: `internal/grpcsrv/server.go` (Deps `Streams`, `ClientIP`; TLS config; Server fields), `auth.go` (method allowlist, Unauthenticated, zone check), `events.go` (`RunEvents` ends on revocation; the receive loop moves into `receiveEvents`), `testfakes.go` (functional enrollments in `fakeWorkerStore`; the harness service gets `WithSigner(h.ca)`)
- Modify: `internal/app/app.go`. The CA, `workersSvc` (`WithSigner(caInst)`, `WithZones(hostsSvc)`) and `grpcsrv.NewStreamRegistry()` are built before `api.New`, independent of `GRPCListen`, and `grpcsrv.New` receives `Workers: workersSvc, Streams: streams, ClientIP: clientip.New(cfg.TrustedProxies)`.
- Modify: `internal/worker/client_test.go` (`TestDialUnregisteredWorkerRejected` expects `codes.Unauthenticated`)
- Test: `internal/grpcsrv/enroll_test.go`, `streams_test.go`, `servercert_test.go`, `auth_test.go` (the revoked-worker test becomes the two tests below)

**Interfaces:**
- `grpcsrv.Deps` gains `Streams *StreamRegistry` (nil: private) and `ClientIP *clientip.Resolver` (nil: trust no proxy).
- `func NewStreamRegistry() *StreamRegistry`, `func (r *StreamRegistry) CloseWorker(workerID uuid.UUID) int` (Task 15's revocation port).
- `Enroll` answers `Unauthenticated "enrollment refused"` for every token problem, `InvalidArgument` for a bad CSR, `AlreadyExists` for a name clash and `ResourceExhausted` over 10 calls/min per address. `RenewCertificate` requires authentication. Both record system audit events (`worker.enroll`, `worker.renew`).

```go
// TestAuthUnknownOrRevokedCertificate: the certificate chains to the
// instance CA, but its serial is unknown, or revoked — both Unauthenticated
// (spec 2026-09-18 §4), the same answer so neither can be told apart.
func TestAuthUnknownOrRevokedCertificate(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	unknownCert, unknownKey, _ := h.issue("unregistered", orgID, zoneID, false) // never registered
	revokedCert, revokedKey, revokedSerial := h.issue("revoked", orgID, zoneID, true)

	h.store.revoked[revokedSerial.String()] = true
```

```go
package grpcsrv

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/ca"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/enrolltoken"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/hosts"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/pb/workerpb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/workers"
)

type testZones struct{}

func (testZones) ListZones(_ context.Context, org uuid.UUID) ([]hosts.Zone, error) {
	return []hosts.Zone{{ID: zoneID, OrgID: org, Slug: "default"}}, nil
}

// newKeyAndCSR is what a worker does before Enroll or RenewCertificate.
func newKeyAndCSR(t *testing.T) (*ecdsa.PrivateKey, []byte) {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return key, pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: der})
}

func keyPEM(t *testing.T, key *ecdsa.PrivateKey) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	der, err := x509.MarshalECPrivateKey(key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "EC PRIVATE KEY", Bytes: der})
}

func (h *harness) enrollmentToken(name string) enrolltoken.Token {
	h.t.Helper()
	svc := workers.NewService(h.store, workers.WithSigner(h.ca), workers.WithZones(testZones{}))
	_, token, err := svc.CreateEnrollment(context.Background(), orgID, nil, zoneID, name)
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tok, err := enrolltoken.Parse(token)
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return tok
}

// TestEnrollWithoutAClientCertificate: a certificate-less connection may
// call Enroll (and only Enroll); the returned certificate then
// authenticates every other method.
func TestEnrollWithoutAClientCertificate(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	tok := h.enrollmentToken("fra-9")
	anonymous := h.client(nil, nil)
	if _, err := anonymous.ClaimJob(t.Context(), claimRequest(zoneID)); status.Code(err) != codes.Unauthenticated {
		t.Fatalf("ClaimJob without a certificate: %v", err)
	}
	key, csr := newKeyAndCSR(t)
	resp, err := anonymous.Enroll(t.Context(), &workerpb.EnrollRequest{TokenId: tok.ID.String(), Secret: tok.Secret, Csr: csr, Version: "v0.2c"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	block, _ := pem.Decode(resp.GetCaChain())
	if block == nil || enrolltoken.Pin(block.Bytes) != tok.CAPin {
		t.Fatal("the returned CA must be the pinned one")
	}
	if _, err := h.client(resp.GetCertificate(), keyPEM(t, key)).ClaimJob(t.Context(), claimRequest(zoneID)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the enrolled certificate must authenticate: %v", err)
	}
	if got := h.audit.Events(); len(got) == 0 || got[0].Operation != "worker.enroll" {
		t.Fatalf("audit = %+v", got)
	}
	// Single use, and every refusal looks the same.
	for name, req := range map[string]*workerpb.EnrollRequest{
		"reused token":  {TokenId: tok.ID.String(), Secret: tok.Secret, Csr: csr},
		"unknown token": {TokenId: uuid.NewString(), Secret: tok.Secret, Csr: csr},
		"garbage id":    {TokenId: "x", Secret: tok.Secret, Csr: csr},
	} {
		if _, err := anonymous.Enroll(t.Context(), req); status.Code(err) != codes.Unauthenticated {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v", name, err)
		}
	}
	bad := h.enrollmentToken("fra-10")
	if _, err := anonymous.Enroll(t.Context(), &workerpb.EnrollRequest{TokenId: bad.ID.String(), Secret: bad.Secret, Csr: []byte("x")}); status.Code(err) != codes.InvalidArgument {
		t.Fatalf("bad csr: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestEnrollIsRateLimitedPerAddress(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	anonymous := h.client(nil, nil)
	for i := 0; i < enrollLimit; i++ {
		if _, err := anonymous.Enroll(t.Context(), &workerpb.EnrollRequest{TokenId: uuid.NewString()}); status.Code(err) != codes.Unauthenticated {
			t.Fatalf("call %d: %v", i, err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := anonymous.Enroll(t.Context(), &workerpb.EnrollRequest{TokenId: uuid.NewString()}); status.Code(err) != codes.ResourceExhausted {
		t.Fatalf("over the limit: %v", err)
	}
	h.advance(enrollWindow)
	if _, err := anonymous.Enroll(t.Context(), &workerpb.EnrollRequest{TokenId: uuid.NewString()}); status.Code(err) != codes.Unauthenticated {
		t.Fatalf("after the window: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRenewCertificate(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	certPEM, keyPEMBytes := h.worker()
	client := h.client(certPEM, keyPEMBytes)
	key, csr := newKeyAndCSR(t)
	resp, err := client.RenewCertificate(t.Context(), &workerpb.RenewCertificateRequest{Csr: csr})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	leaf, err := ca.ParseLeaf(resp.GetCertificate())
	if err != nil || leaf.Subject.CommonName != "pulsix-worker fra-1-a" {
		t.Fatalf("renewed = %v, %v", leaf, err)
	}
	if _, err := h.client(resp.GetCertificate(), keyPEM(t, key)).ClaimJob(t.Context(), claimRequest(zoneID)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the renewed certificate must authenticate: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := client.ClaimJob(t.Context(), claimRequest(zoneID)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the old certificate stays valid: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := client.RenewCertificate(t.Context(), &workerpb.RenewCertificateRequest{Csr: []byte("x")}); status.Code(err) != codes.InvalidArgument {
		t.Fatalf("bad csr: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := h.client(nil, nil).RenewCertificate(t.Context(), &workerpb.RenewCertificateRequest{Csr: csr}); status.Code(err) != codes.Unauthenticated {
		t.Fatalf("renew without a certificate: %v", err)
	}
}
```

```go
package grpcsrv

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"
)

func TestStreamRegistry(t *testing.T) {
	r := NewStreamRegistry()
	w := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	a, releaseA := r.track(w)
	_, releaseB := r.track(w)
	releaseB()
	if n := r.CloseWorker(w); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("CloseWorker = %d, want the one open stream", n)
	}
	select {
	case <-a:
	default:
		t.Fatal("the open stream was not signalled")
	}
	releaseA() // after close: a no-op
	if n := r.CloseWorker(w); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("second CloseWorker = %d", n)
	}
}

// TestRevocationEndsOpenStreams: CloseWorker ends the worker's RunEvents
// stream with Unauthenticated.
func TestRevocationEndsOpenStreams(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	jobID := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	h.claimAndBind(jobID, orgID, zoneID)
	stream := h.openStream(jobID)
	if err := sendEvent(stream, jobID, 1, "web1", "ok"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	certPEM, _ := h.worker()
	leaf, _ := parseCert(certPEM)
	w, err := h.store.GetBySerial(t.Context(), leaf.SerialNumber)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
	for h.srv.streams.CloseWorker(w.ID) == 0 {
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			t.Fatal("the stream never registered")
		}
		time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	if code := status.Code(expectEndRecv(stream)); code != codes.Unauthenticated {
		t.Fatalf("stream ended with %s, want Unauthenticated", code)
	}
}
```

```go
package grpcsrv

import (
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/ca"
)

func parseCert(certPEM []byte) (*x509.Certificate, error) {
	block, _ := pem.Decode(certPEM)
	return x509.ParseCertificate(block.Bytes)
}

func TestServerCertificateCarriesTheCAAndRenews(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	now := time.Now()
	src, err := newServerCertSource(h.ca, func() time.Time { return now })
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	first, _ := src.get(nil)
	_, root := h.ca.Pool()
	if len(first.Certificate) != 2 || string(first.Certificate[1]) != string(root.Raw) {
		t.Fatal("the served chain must be [leaf, CA] so a worker can pin the CA")
	}
	if same, _ := src.get(nil); same != first {
		t.Fatal("a fresh certificate must not be re-issued")
	}
	now = now.Add(ca.LeafValidity - ca.LeafValidity/4) // less than a third left
	renewed, _ := src.get(nil)
	if renewed == first {
		t.Fatal("the server certificate was not re-issued")
	}
}
```

```go
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.enrollments[e.ID], f.hashes[e.ID] = e, hash

func (f *fakeWorkerStore) Enroll(_ context.Context, id uuid.UUID, fn func(workers.Enrollment, []byte) (workers.Worker, workers.Certificate, error)) (workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	e, ok := f.enrollments[id]
	if !ok {
		return workers.Worker{}, workers.ErrNotFound
	}
	w, c, err := fn(e, f.hashes[id])
	if err != nil {
		return workers.Worker{}, err
	}
	f.bySerial[c.Serial.String()] = w
	now := time.Now()
	e.UsedAt = &now
	f.enrollments[id] = e
	return w, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the server certificate source and stream registry**

`internal/grpcsrv/servercert.go`:

```go
// The gRPC listener's own certificate. It is chained with the CA so a
// token-enrolling worker can pin the CA by the fingerprint in its token,
// and it is re-issued in memory once less than a third of its validity is
// left — leaves live 90 days, and a manager may run longer than that.
package grpcsrv

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/ca"
)

type serverCertSource struct {
	ca   *ca.CA
	now  func() time.Time
	mu   sync.Mutex
	cert *tls.Certificate
	leaf *x509.Certificate
}

func newServerCertSource(authority *ca.CA, now func() time.Time) (*serverCertSource, error) {
	s := &serverCertSource{ca: authority, now: now}
	if err := s.issue(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return s, nil
}

// issue replaces the served certificate; callers hold mu (or own s).
func (s *serverCertSource) issue() error {
	certPEM, keyPEM, _, err := s.ca.Issue(context.Background(), nil, uuid.Nil, "")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("issue gRPC server certificate: %w", err)
	}
	pair, err := tls.X509KeyPair(certPEM, keyPEM)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("load gRPC server certificate: %w", err)
	}
	_, root := s.ca.Pool()
	pair.Certificate = append(pair.Certificate, root.Raw)
	leaf, err := x509.ParseCertificate(pair.Certificate[0])
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parse gRPC server certificate: %w", err)
	}
	s.cert, s.leaf = &pair, leaf
	return nil
}

// get implements tls.Config.GetCertificate.
func (s *serverCertSource) get(*tls.ClientHelloInfo) (*tls.Certificate, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	if s.leaf.NotAfter.Sub(s.now()) < s.leaf.NotAfter.Sub(s.leaf.NotBefore)/3 {
		if err := s.issue(); err != nil {
			slog.Error("re-issue gRPC server certificate; serving the current one", "err", err)
		}
	}
	return s.cert, nil
}
```

`internal/grpcsrv/streams.go`:

```go
package grpcsrv

import (
	"sync"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// StreamRegistry tracks the open RunEvents streams per worker, so a
// revocation can end them (spec §4 "Revocation"). It lives in process: with
// several managers each ends only its own streams, while the interceptor
// refuses the revoked worker's next call on every manager.
type StreamRegistry struct {
	mu       sync.Mutex
	next     uint64
	byWorker map[uuid.UUID]map[uint64]chan struct{}
}

// NewStreamRegistry builds an empty registry.
func NewStreamRegistry() *StreamRegistry {
	return &StreamRegistry{byWorker: map[uuid.UUID]map[uint64]chan struct{}{}}
}

// track registers one stream of workerID; the channel closes when the
// worker is revoked, release unregisters it.
func (r *StreamRegistry) track(workerID uuid.UUID) (<-chan struct{}, func()) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	r.next++
	id, ch := r.next, make(chan struct{})
	if r.byWorker[workerID] == nil {
		r.byWorker[workerID] = map[uint64]chan struct{}{}
	}
	r.byWorker[workerID][id] = ch
	return ch, func() {
		r.mu.Lock()
		defer r.mu.Unlock()
		if streams, ok := r.byWorker[workerID]; ok {
			delete(streams, id)
			if len(streams) == 0 {
				delete(r.byWorker, workerID)
			}
		}
	}
}

// CloseWorker ends every open stream of workerID and reports how many.
func (r *StreamRegistry) CloseWorker(workerID uuid.UUID) int {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	streams := r.byWorker[workerID]
	for _, ch := range streams {
		close(ch)
	}
	delete(r.byWorker, workerID)
	return len(streams)
}
```

`auth.go`:

```go
// unauthenticatedMethods may be called without a client certificate:
// token enrollment is how a worker gets its first one.
var unauthenticatedMethods = map[string]bool{workerpb.WorkerService_Enroll_FullMethodName: true}

// authUnaryInterceptor authenticates a unary call before the handler runs.
func (s *Server) authUnaryInterceptor(ctx context.Context, req any, info *grpc.UnaryServerInfo, handler grpc.UnaryHandler) (any, error) {
	if unauthenticatedMethods[info.FullMethod] {
		return handler(ctx, req)
	}
	ctx, err := s.authenticate(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return handler(ctx, req)
}
```

`events.go`: replace `RunEvents` with the version below; its former loop becomes `receiveEvents`.

```go
func (s *Server) RunEvents(stream workerpb.WorkerService_RunEventsServer) error {
	ctx := stream.Context()
	jobID, err := s.bindStream(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, w, err := authenticated(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	// A revocation (StreamRegistry.CloseWorker) ends the stream: the handler
	// returns while the receive loop still blocks in Recv, which the
	// finished RPC then unblocks.
	revoked, release := s.streams.track(w.ID)
	defer release()

	// Recv and Send are each single-goroutine, but a Send goroutine may run
	// next to the Recv loop — so the cancel poll sends directly.
	done := make(chan struct{})
	defer close(done)
	go s.pollCancels(ctx, jobID, stream, done)

	recvErr := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { recvErr <- s.receiveEvents(ctx, jobID, stream) }()
	select {
	case err := <-recvErr:
		return err
	case <-revoked:
		return status.Error(codes.Unauthenticated, "worker revoked")
	}
}

// receiveEvents appends every event of the bound stream until the worker
// closes it. Events after a cancel directive are still recorded: the worker
// needs a little more time to shut its run down, and those events are what
// the run's UI shows.
func (s *Server) receiveEvents(ctx context.Context, jobID uuid.UUID, stream workerpb.WorkerService_RunEventsServer) error {
	for {
		ev, err := stream.Recv()
		if err == io.EOF {
			return nil
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err // transport-level failure: pass the status through
		}
		if id := ev.GetJobId(); id != "" && id != jobID.String() {
			return status.Errorf(codes.InvalidArgument, "event job id %q does not match the bound stream", id)
		}
		if err := s.deps.Events.Append(ctx, Event{
			JobID:   jobID.String(),
			Seq:     ev.GetSeq(),
			Host:    ev.GetHost(),
			Task:    ev.GetTask(),
			State:   ev.GetState(),
			Payload: ev.GetPayload(),
			At:      eventAt(ev, s.deps.Clock),
		}); err != nil {
			return status.Errorf(codes.Internal, "append run event: %v", err)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement Enroll and RenewCertificate**

`internal/grpcsrv/enroll.go`:

```go
// Enroll and RenewCertificate: the worker's certificate lifecycle (spec
// 2026-09-18 §4). Enroll is the one method callable without a client
// certificate; it is rate limited per client address.
package grpcsrv

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/netip"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/metadata"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/peer"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/pb/workerpb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/workers"
)

// Enroll is rate limited per client address (spec §4): at most
// enrollLimit calls per enrollWindow, counted in process.
const (
	enrollLimit  = 10
	enrollWindow = time.Minute
)

// Enroll implements workerpb.WorkerServiceServer.Enroll.
func (s *Server) Enroll(ctx context.Context, req *workerpb.EnrollRequest) (*workerpb.EnrollResponse, error) {
	ip := s.peerAddr(ctx)
	if !s.enrollLimit.Allow(ip) {
		return nil, status.Error(codes.ResourceExhausted, "too many enrollment attempts from this address; retry in a minute")
	}
	id, err := uuid.Parse(req.GetTokenId())
	if err != nil {
		return nil, status.Error(codes.Unauthenticated, "enrollment refused")
	}
	res, err := s.deps.Workers.Enroll(ctx, workers.EnrollInput{TokenID: id, Secret: req.GetSecret(), CSRPEM: req.GetCsr(), Version: req.GetVersion()})
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, workers.ErrEnrollmentRefused):
		return nil, status.Error(codes.Unauthenticated, "enrollment refused")
	case errors.Is(err, workers.ErrBadCSR):
		return nil, status.Errorf(codes.InvalidArgument, "%v", err)
	case errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNameTaken):
		return nil, status.Error(codes.AlreadyExists, "a worker with this name already exists in the organization")
	case err != nil:
		return nil, status.Errorf(codes.Internal, "enroll: %v", err)
	}
	s.audit(ctx, SystemEvent{Operation: "worker.enroll", ObjectType: "worker", ObjectID: res.Worker.ID.String(),
		OrgID: res.Worker.OrgID.String(), Summary: "worker " + res.Worker.Name + " enrolled from " + ip.String()})
	return &workerpb.EnrollResponse{Certificate: res.CertPEM, CaChain: res.CAPEM, WorkerId: res.Worker.ID.String()}, nil
}

// RenewCertificate implements workerpb.WorkerServiceServer.RenewCertificate.
func (s *Server) RenewCertificate(ctx context.Context, req *workerpb.RenewCertificateRequest) (*workerpb.RenewCertificateResponse, error) {
	_, w, err := authenticated(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	certPEM, err := s.deps.Workers.Renew(ctx, w, req.GetCsr())
	if errors.Is(err, workers.ErrBadCSR) {
		return nil, status.Errorf(codes.InvalidArgument, "%v", err)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, status.Errorf(codes.Internal, "renew certificate: %v", err)
	}
	s.audit(ctx, SystemEvent{Operation: "worker.renew", ObjectType: "worker", ObjectID: w.ID.String(),
		OrgID: w.OrgID.String(), Summary: "worker " + w.Name + " renewed its certificate"})
	return &workerpb.RenewCertificateResponse{Certificate: certPEM}, nil
}

// audit records a system event; the certificate already exists, so a
// failure is logged rather than undoing it.
func (s *Server) audit(ctx context.Context, ev SystemEvent) {
	if err := s.deps.Audit.Record(ctx, ev); err != nil {
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "audit worker certificate event", "operation", ev.Operation, "err", err)
	}
}

// peerAddr is the caller's address through the trusted-proxy rules.
func (s *Server) peerAddr(ctx context.Context) netip.Addr {
	remote := ""
	if p, ok := peer.FromContext(ctx); ok && p.Addr != nil {
		remote = p.Addr.String()
	}
	var xff []string
	if md, ok := metadata.FromIncomingContext(ctx); ok {
		xff = md.Get("x-forwarded-for")
	}
	return s.deps.ClientIP.Resolve(remote, xff)
}
```

### Task 14: The worker: token enrollment, identity directory, hot-swapped certificate and renewal

This task covers spec §4 on the worker side:
- `PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN` enrollment over a connection pinned to the token's CA fingerprint. The key never leaves the host, and the bundle is written into the identity directory.
- A valid bundle wins on start.
- The certificate is served to every new connection through `GetClientCertificate`.
- The worker renews at start and every 6 h once a third of the validity is left, replacing `cert.pem` atomically.
- The compose stack lets the bundled worker persist renewals.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/worker/identity.go`, `pin.go`, `enroll.go`, `renew.go`
- Modify: `internal/worker/config.go` (`PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN`, `PULSIX_WORKER_IDENTITY_DIR`, bundle paths all-or-none), `client.go` (`Dial` → `DialIdentity` with `GetClientCertificate`; `serverNameOf`), `loop.go` (an `Unauthenticated` claim error logs the re-enroll hint)
- Modify: `cmd/worker/main.go` (EnsureIdentity → LoadIdentity → DialIdentity → Renewer goroutine), `internal/app/embedded_worker.go` (DialIdentity + Renewer)
- Modify: `internal/worker/harness_test.go` (functional enrollments in its `fakeWorkerStore`, `WithSigner(caInst)`, `bufconnDialer()`)
- Modify: `deploy/compose/docker-compose.yml` (worker bundle volume writable), `deploy/compose/.env.example`, `deploy/compose/README.md` ("Workers on other machines" becomes the token flow), `deploy/worker/Dockerfile` (comment: identity dir under the data volume), `scripts/smoke.sh` (certificate registry and 90-day check)
- Test: `internal/worker/identity_test.go`, `pin_test.go`, `enroll_test.go`, `config_test.go` (new case)
- Docs (noix-docs): `admin/configuration/workers-and-zones.md` (token enrollment, renewal), `admin/configuration/environment-variables.md` (`PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN`, `PULSIX_WORKER_IDENTITY_DIR`, `PULSIX_MANAGER_SERVER_NAME`)

**Interfaces:**

```go

func LoadIdentity(certPath, keyPath string) (*Identity, error)
func (i *Identity) Certificate() *tls.Certificate
func (i *Identity) Leaf() *x509.Certificate
func (i *Identity) Signer() crypto.Signer
func (i *Identity) NeedsRenewal(now time.Time) bool // less than a third left
func (i *Identity) Replace(certPEM []byte) error    // same key only; atomic write, then swap

func EnsureIdentity(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, log *slog.Logger) error
func DialIdentity(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, id *Identity) (*grpc.ClientConn, error)
func Dial(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*grpc.ClientConn, error) // LoadIdentity + DialIdentity

const RenewCheckInterval = 6 * time.Hour
func NewRenewer(client workerpb.WorkerServiceClient, id *Identity, now func() time.Time, sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error, log *slog.Logger) *Renewer
```

```go
// bufconnDialer routes a worker's dials to the harness listener.
func (h *harness) bufconnDialer() grpc.DialOption {
	return grpc.WithContextDialer(func(ctx context.Context, _ string) (net.Conn, error) {
		return h.lis.DialContext(ctx)
	})
}
```

`internal/worker/identity_test.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/ca"
)

func TestIdentityRenewalWindowAndReplace(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	cfg := h.issue("fra-1", true)
	id, err := LoadIdentity(cfg.CertPath, cfg.KeyPath)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	leaf := id.Leaf()
	if id.NeedsRenewal(leaf.NotBefore.Add(time.Hour)) {
		t.Fatal("a fresh certificate needs no renewal")
	}
	if !id.NeedsRenewal(leaf.NotAfter.Add(-ca.LeafValidity / 4)) {
		t.Fatal("less than a third left must renew")
	}
	// A renewed certificate for the same key replaces the file atomically.
	csr, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, id.Signer())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	renewed, _, err := h.ca.SignCSR(pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: csr}), orgID, zoneID, "fra-1")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := id.Replace(renewed); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	onDisk, _ := os.ReadFile(cfg.CertPath)
	if string(onDisk) != string(renewed) || id.Leaf().SerialNumber.Cmp(leaf.SerialNumber) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("the renewed certificate must be on disk and in use")
	}
	entries, _ := os.ReadDir(filepath.Dir(cfg.CertPath))
	for _, e := range entries {
		if e.Name()[0] == '.' {
			t.Fatalf("temp file %s left behind", e.Name())
		}
	}
	// A certificate for another key is refused and changes nothing.
	other, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	otherCSR, _ := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, other)
	foreign, _, _ := h.ca.SignCSR(pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: otherCSR}), orgID, zoneID, "fra-1")
	if err := id.Replace(foreign); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a certificate for another key was accepted")
	}
	if err := id.Replace([]byte("garbage")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("garbage was accepted")
	}
}
```

`internal/worker/pin_test.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/enrolltoken"
)

func TestVerifyPinned(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	_, root := h.ca.Pool()
	certPEM, _, _, err := h.ca.Issue(context.Background(), nil, uuid.Nil, "")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	leaf, err := parseCertPEM(certPEM)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	pin := enrolltoken.Pin(root.Raw)
	if err := verifyPinned([][]byte{leaf.Raw, root.Raw}, pin, "manager"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the pinned chain must verify: %v", err)
	}
	for name, c := range map[string]struct {
		raw        [][]byte
		pin        [32]byte
		serverName string
	}{
		"no CA sent":        {[][]byte{leaf.Raw}, pin, "manager"},
		"other fingerprint": {[][]byte{leaf.Raw, root.Raw}, enrolltoken.Pin([]byte("x")), "manager"},
		"wrong host":        {[][]byte{leaf.Raw, root.Raw}, pin, "evil.example"},
		"garbage leaf":      {[][]byte{[]byte("x"), root.Raw}, pin, "manager"},
	} {
		if err := verifyPinned(c.raw, c.pin, c.serverName); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: accepted", name)
		}
	}
}

func parseCertPEM(b []byte) (*x509.Certificate, error) {
	block, _ := pem.Decode(b)
	return x509.ParseCertificate(block.Bytes)
}
```

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"io"

	"os"

	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/workers"
)

type oneZone struct{}

func (oneZone) ListZones(_ context.Context, org uuid.UUID) ([]hosts.Zone, error) {
	return []hosts.Zone{{ID: zoneID, OrgID: org, Slug: "default"}}, nil
}

func quiet() *slog.Logger { return slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)) }

func (h *harness) token(t *testing.T, name string) string {
	t.Helper()
	svc := workers.NewService(h.store, workers.WithSigner(h.ca), workers.WithZones(oneZone{}))
	_, token, err := svc.CreateEnrollment(context.Background(), orgID, nil, zoneID, name)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return token
}

func tokenConfig(t *testing.T, token string) Config {
	t.Helper()
	cfg, err := Load([]string{"PULSIX_MANAGER_ADDR=manager:8444", "PULSIX_WORKER_DATA_DIR=" + t.TempDir(), "PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN=" + token})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return cfg
}

func TestEnrollWritesTheBundleAndClaims(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	cfg := tokenConfig(t, h.token(t, "fra-7"))
	if err := ensureIdentity(t.Context(), cfg, quiet(), time.Now, []grpc.DialOption{h.bufconnDialer()}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for path, mode := range map[string]os.FileMode{cfg.KeyPath: 0o600, cfg.CertPath: 0o644, cfg.CAPath: 0o644} {
		st, err := os.Stat(path)
		if err != nil || st.Mode().Perm() != mode {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v %v, want mode %v", path, st, err, mode)
		}
	}
	if filepath.Dir(cfg.CertPath) != filepath.Join(cfg.DataDir, "identity") {
		t.Fatalf("identity dir = %s", filepath.Dir(cfg.CertPath))
	}
	conn, err := h.dialWith(t, cfg)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = conn.Close() })
	if _, err := workerpb.NewWorkerServiceClient(conn).ClaimJob(t.Context(), &workerpb.ClaimJobRequest{Version: "dev"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the enrolled worker must authenticate: %v", err)
	}
	// Restart: the valid bundle wins, the (now used) token is ignored.
	if err := ensureIdentity(t.Context(), cfg, quiet(), time.Now, []grpc.DialOption{h.bufconnDialer()}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("restart with a valid bundle: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestEnrollRefusesAForeignCA(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	tok, _ := enrolltoken.Parse(h.token(t, "fra-8"))
	tok.CAPin[0] ^= 0xff // a token for some other manager
	cfg := tokenConfig(t, tok.String())
	err := ensureIdentity(t.Context(), cfg, quiet(), time.Now, []grpc.DialOption{h.bufconnDialer()})
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "fingerprint") {
		t.Fatalf("a wrong CA pin must fail the handshake: %v", err)
	}
	if _, statErr := os.Stat(cfg.KeyPath); statErr == nil {
		t.Fatal("nothing may be written after a refused handshake")
	}
}

func TestEnsureIdentityWithoutBundleOrToken(t *testing.T) {
	cfg := tokenConfig(t, "")
	if err := ensureIdentity(t.Context(), cfg, quiet(), time.Now, nil); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	cfg.EnrollToken = "pxw1.garbage"
	if err := ensureIdentity(t.Context(), cfg, quiet(), time.Now, nil); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a malformed token must fail")
	}
}

func TestRenewerRenewsWhenDue(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	cfg := h.issue("fra-1", true)
	id, err := LoadIdentity(cfg.CertPath, cfg.KeyPath)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	conn, err := dialIdentity(t.Context(), cfg, id, []grpc.DialOption{h.bufconnDialer()})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = conn.Close() })
	now := time.Now()
	r := NewRenewer(workerpb.NewWorkerServiceClient(conn), id, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, quiet())
	before := id.Leaf().SerialNumber
	if err := r.Check(t.Context()); err != nil || id.Leaf().SerialNumber.Cmp(before) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("a fresh certificate must not renew: %v", err)
	}
	now = id.Leaf().NotAfter.Add(-time.Hour)
	if err := r.Check(t.Context()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if id.Leaf().SerialNumber.Cmp(before) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("the certificate was not renewed")
	}
	// Run stops on cancel.
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	cancel()
	NewRenewer(workerpb.NewWorkerServiceClient(conn), id, func() time.Time { return now }, nil, quiet()).Run(ctx)
}
```
