- [ ] **Step 5: Implement identity, pinning, enrollment and renewal**

`internal/worker/identity.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"crypto"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// Identity is the worker's client certificate and key, shared by every
// connection: tls.Config.GetClientCertificate reads it on each handshake,
// so a renewed certificate is used by new connections while open streams
// keep the one they were established with (spec 2026-09-18 §4).
type Identity struct {
	certPath string
	mu       sync.RWMutex
	cert     *tls.Certificate
}

// LoadIdentity reads the certificate and key files.
func LoadIdentity(certPath, keyPath string) (*Identity, error) {
	pair, err := tls.LoadX509KeyPair(certPath, keyPath)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("load worker certificate (%s, %s): %w", certPath, keyPath, err)
	}
	if pair.Leaf == nil {
		if pair.Leaf, err = x509.ParseCertificate(pair.Certificate[0]); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse worker certificate %s: %w", certPath, err)
		}
	}
	return &Identity{certPath: certPath, cert: &pair}, nil
}

// Certificate is the current client certificate.
func (i *Identity) Certificate() *tls.Certificate {
	i.mu.RLock()
	defer i.mu.RUnlock()
	return i.cert
}

// Leaf is the current certificate, parsed.
func (i *Identity) Leaf() *x509.Certificate { return i.Certificate().Leaf }

// Signer is the worker's private key (renewal CSRs are signed with it).
func (i *Identity) Signer() crypto.Signer {
	s, _ := i.Certificate().PrivateKey.(crypto.Signer)
	return s
}

// NeedsRenewal reports whether less than a third of the validity is left.
func (i *Identity) NeedsRenewal(now time.Time) bool {
	leaf := i.Leaf()
	return leaf.NotAfter.Sub(now) < leaf.NotAfter.Sub(leaf.NotBefore)/3
}

// Replace installs a renewed certificate for the same key: it is checked
// against the key, written atomically over the certificate file (temp file
// + rename in the same directory) and only then swapped in.
func (i *Identity) Replace(certPEM []byte) error {
	block, _ := pem.Decode(certPEM)
	if block == nil || block.Type != "CERTIFICATE" {
		return errors.New("renewed certificate is not a PEM CERTIFICATE")
	}
	leaf, err := x509.ParseCertificate(block.Bytes)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parse renewed certificate: %w", err)
	}
	cur := i.Certificate()
	signer, ok := cur.PrivateKey.(crypto.Signer)
	pub, eq := leaf.PublicKey.(interface{ Equal(crypto.PublicKey) bool })
	if !ok || !eq || !pub.Equal(signer.Public()) {
		return errors.New("renewed certificate does not match the worker's key")
	}
	if err := writeFileAtomic(filepath.Dir(i.certPath), filepath.Base(i.certPath), certPEM, 0o644); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("write renewed certificate: %w", err)
	}
	next := &tls.Certificate{Certificate: [][]byte{block.Bytes}, PrivateKey: cur.PrivateKey, Leaf: leaf}
	i.mu.Lock()
	i.cert = next
	i.mu.Unlock()
	return nil
}

// writeFileAtomic writes content to dir/name through a temp file in the
// same directory and a rename, so a reader never sees a torn file.
func writeFileAtomic(dir, name string, content []byte, mode os.FileMode) error {
	tmp, err := os.CreateTemp(dir, "."+name+".*")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer func() { _ = os.Remove(tmp.Name()) }()
	if _, err := tmp.Write(content); err != nil {
		_ = tmp.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := tmp.Chmod(mode); err != nil {
		_ = tmp.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := tmp.Sync(); err != nil {
		_ = tmp.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := tmp.Close(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return os.Rename(tmp.Name(), filepath.Join(dir, name))
}
```

`internal/worker/pin.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/enrolltoken"
)

// pinnedTLS is the TLS configuration of the enrollment call: no system
// roots and no trust on first use — the manager's chain must lead to the
// CA whose SHA-256 the enrollment token carries (spec §4).
func pinnedTLS(pin [32]byte, serverName string) *tls.Config {
	return &tls.Config{
		MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS13,
		ServerName: serverName,
		// Verification is verifyPinned below; the standard verifier would
		// consult roots the worker must not trust here.
		InsecureSkipVerify: true, //nolint:gosec // replaced by VerifyPeerCertificate against the pinned CA
		VerifyPeerCertificate: func(raw [][]byte, _ [][]*x509.Certificate) error {
			return verifyPinned(raw, pin, serverName)
		},
	}
}

// verifyPinned requires the presented chain to contain the pinned CA and
// the leaf to verify against it for serverName.
func verifyPinned(raw [][]byte, pin [32]byte, serverName string) error {
	if len(raw) < 2 {
		return errors.New("the manager presented no CA certificate to pin against")
	}
	var root *x509.Certificate
	for _, der := range raw[1:] {
		if enrolltoken.Pin(der) == pin {
			c, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
			if err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("parse pinned CA: %w", err)
			}
			root = c
		}
	}
	if root == nil {
		return errors.New("the manager's CA does not match the fingerprint in the enrollment token")
	}
	leaf, err := x509.ParseCertificate(raw[0])
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parse server certificate: %w", err)
	}
	pool := x509.NewCertPool()
	pool.AddCert(root)
	if _, err := leaf.Verify(x509.VerifyOptions{Roots: pool, DNSName: serverName, KeyUsages: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth}}); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("verify server certificate against the pinned CA: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

`internal/worker/enroll.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"time"

	"google.golang.org/grpc"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/enrolltoken"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/pb/workerpb"
)

// EnsureIdentity makes sure cfg's certificate bundle exists before the
// worker dials. A usable bundle on disk wins and PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN is
// ignored (spec 2026-09-18 §4); otherwise the worker enrolls with the
// token: a fresh ECDSA P-256 key that never leaves the host, a CSR, and the
// Enroll call over a connection pinned to the token's CA fingerprint.
func EnsureIdentity(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, log *slog.Logger) error {
	return ensureIdentity(ctx, cfg, log, time.Now, nil)
}

func ensureIdentity(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, log *slog.Logger, now func() time.Time, extra []grpc.DialOption) error {
	if bundleUsable(cfg, now()) {
		if cfg.EnrollToken != "" {
			log.InfoContext(ctx, "a valid certificate bundle exists; "+envEnrollToken+" is ignored", "cert", cfg.CertPath)
		}
		return nil
	}
	if cfg.EnrollToken == "" {
		return fmt.Errorf("no usable certificate bundle at %s and no %s to enroll with", cfg.CertPath, envEnrollToken)
	}
	tok, err := enrolltoken.Parse(cfg.EnrollToken)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", envEnrollToken, err)
	}
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("generate worker key: %w", err)
	}
	csrDER, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, key)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("create certificate request: %w", err)
	}
	serverName, err := serverNameOf(cfg)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	opts := append([]grpc.DialOption{grpc.WithTransportCredentials(credentials.NewTLS(pinnedTLS(tok.CAPin, serverName)))}, extra...)
	conn, err := grpc.NewClient("passthrough:///"+cfg.ManagerAddr, opts...)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("new gRPC client for %s: %w", cfg.ManagerAddr, err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = conn.Close() }()
	resp, err := workerpb.NewWorkerServiceClient(conn).Enroll(ctx, &workerpb.EnrollRequest{
		TokenId: tok.ID.String(), Secret: tok.Secret,
		Csr: pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: csrDER}), Version: cfg.Version,
	})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("enroll: %w", err)
	}
	caBlock, _ := pem.Decode(resp.GetCaChain())
	if caBlock == nil || enrolltoken.Pin(caBlock.Bytes) != tok.CAPin {
		return errors.New("the manager returned a CA that does not match the enrollment token")
	}
	keyDER, err := x509.MarshalECPrivateKey(key)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("encode worker key: %w", err)
	}
	// The key first, the certificate last: a crash in between leaves no
	// usable bundle, and the next start enrolls again (with a new token).
	for _, f := range []struct {
		path    string
		content []byte
		mode    os.FileMode
	}{
		{cfg.KeyPath, pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "EC PRIVATE KEY", Bytes: keyDER}), 0o600},
		{cfg.CAPath, resp.GetCaChain(), 0o644},
		{cfg.CertPath, resp.GetCertificate(), 0o644},
	} {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(f.path), 0o700); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("create identity directory: %w", err)
		}
		if err := writeFileAtomic(filepath.Dir(f.path), filepath.Base(f.path), f.content, f.mode); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("write %s: %w", f.path, err)
		}
	}
	log.InfoContext(ctx, "worker enrolled", "worker_id", resp.GetWorkerId(), "identity", filepath.Dir(cfg.CertPath))
	return nil
}

// bundleUsable reports whether the configured bundle loads, its key
// matches, the certificate has not expired and the CA file parses.
func bundleUsable(cfg Config, now time.Time) bool {
	id, err := LoadIdentity(cfg.CertPath, cfg.KeyPath)
	if err != nil || !now.Before(id.Leaf().NotAfter) {
		return false
	}
	caPEM, err := os.ReadFile(cfg.CAPath)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	return x509.NewCertPool().AppendCertsFromPEM(caPEM)
}
```

`internal/worker/renew.go`:

```go
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"time"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/pb/workerpb"
)

// RenewCheckInterval is how often the worker checks its certificate
// (spec §4: at start and every 6 h).
const RenewCheckInterval = 6 * time.Hour

// Renewer renews the worker's certificate once less than a third of its
// validity is left, reusing the worker's key.
type Renewer struct {
	client workerpb.WorkerServiceClient
	id     *Identity
	now    func() time.Time
	sleep  func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error
	log    *slog.Logger
}

// NewRenewer builds a renewer; nil now/sleep/log use the wall clock and
// slog.Default.
func NewRenewer(client workerpb.WorkerServiceClient, id *Identity, now func() time.Time, sleep func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error, log *slog.Logger) *Renewer {
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	if sleep == nil {
		sleep = func(ctx context.Context, d time.Duration) error {
			select {
			case <-ctx.Done():
				return ctx.Err()
			case <-time.After(d):
				return nil
			}
		}
	}
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Renewer{client: client, id: id, now: now, sleep: sleep, log: log}
}

// Run checks now and then every RenewCheckInterval until ctx ends. A
// failed renewal is logged and retried at the next check; the current
// certificate keeps working until it expires.
func (r *Renewer) Run(ctx context.Context) {
	for {
		if err := r.Check(ctx); err != nil {
			r.log.ErrorContext(ctx, "certificate renewal failed; retrying later", "err", err)
		}
		if err := r.sleep(ctx, RenewCheckInterval); err != nil {
			return
		}
	}
}

// Check renews when due and reports whether it did.
func (r *Renewer) Check(ctx context.Context) error {
	if !r.id.NeedsRenewal(r.now()) {
		return nil
	}
	csrDER, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, r.id.Signer())
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("create renewal request: %w", err)
	}
	resp, err := r.client.RenewCertificate(ctx, &workerpb.RenewCertificateRequest{
		Csr: pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: csrDER}),
	})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("renew certificate: %w", err)
	}
	if err := r.id.Replace(resp.GetCertificate()); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	r.log.InfoContext(ctx, "certificate renewed", "not_after", r.id.Leaf().NotAfter)
	return nil
}
```

In `internal/worker/client.go`, replace everything from `func Dial` to the end of the file (`dial` and `loadTLS`) with:

```go
func Dial(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*grpc.ClientConn, error) {
	id, err := LoadIdentity(cfg.CertPath, cfg.KeyPath)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return dialIdentity(ctx, cfg, id, nil)
}

// DialIdentity is Dial with an Identity the caller keeps (cmd/worker hands
// the same one to the Renewer, whose Replace the next handshake picks up).
func DialIdentity(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, id *Identity) (*grpc.ClientConn, error) {
	return dialIdentity(ctx, cfg, id, nil)
}

// dial keeps the test harness's entry point: it loads the identity itself.
func dial(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, extra []grpc.DialOption) (*grpc.ClientConn, error) {
	id, err := LoadIdentity(cfg.CertPath, cfg.KeyPath)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return dialIdentity(ctx, cfg, id, extra)
}

// serverNameOf is the TLS ServerName: cfg.ServerName when set, otherwise
// the host part of ManagerAddr (see Config.ServerName's doc comment).
func serverNameOf(cfg Config) (string, error) {
	if cfg.ServerName != "" {
		return cfg.ServerName, nil
	}
	host, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(cfg.ManagerAddr)
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("resolve server name from %q: %w", cfg.ManagerAddr, err)
	}
	return host, nil
}

// dialIdentity builds the connection; extra dial options exist for the
// bufconn test harness.
func dialIdentity(_ context.Context, cfg Config, id *Identity, extra []grpc.DialOption) (*grpc.ClientConn, error) {
	caPEM, err := os.ReadFile(cfg.CAPath)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("read CA pool file %s: %w", cfg.CAPath, err)
	}
	pool := x509.NewCertPool()
	if !pool.AppendCertsFromPEM(caPEM) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("CA pool file %s contains no PEM certificate", cfg.CAPath)
	}
	serverName, err := serverNameOf(cfg)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	tlsCfg := &tls.Config{
		MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS13,
		RootCAs:    pool,
		ServerName: serverName,
		// Every handshake presents the identity's current certificate, so a
		// renewal takes effect on the next connection.
		GetClientCertificate: func(*tls.CertificateRequestInfo) (*tls.Certificate, error) {
			return id.Certificate(), nil
		},
	}
	opts := append([]grpc.DialOption{
		grpc.WithTransportCredentials(credentials.NewTLS(tlsCfg)),
		// The same explicit limits the manager's server sets
		// (workerpb/limits.go): a claim embeds the inventory snapshot.
		grpc.WithDefaultCallOptions(
			grpc.MaxCallRecvMsgSize(workerpb.MaxManagerToWorkerBytes),
			grpc.MaxCallSendMsgSize(workerpb.MaxWorkerToManagerBytes),
		),
	}, extra...)
	conn, err := grpc.NewClient("passthrough:///"+cfg.ManagerAddr, opts...)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("new gRPC client for %s: %w", cfg.ManagerAddr, err)
	}
	return conn, nil
}
```

### Task 15: Revocation and the Workers API

This task covers spec §4 "Revocation (2.24)" and the API behind the Workers page (2.23):
- Revoke marks the worker and all its certificates revoked in one transaction, keeping the row, ends its open streams and requeues its jobs.
- Delete requires a revoked worker.
- The list shows online/offline/revoked, the certificate expiry and the current job.
- Enrollment tokens are created, listed and withdrawn.
- `workers.read`/`workers.write` are admin permissions.

- Modify: `internal/db/queries/jobs.sql`, `internal/db/queries/workers.sql`, regenerated gen

- [ ] **Step 1: Queries**

Append to `internal/db/queries/jobs.sql`:

```sql
-- name: RequeueWorkerJobs :many
-- A revoked worker's claimed and running jobs go back to the queue while
-- attempts remain (the claim already counted the attempt); the reason
-- stays in error until the next finish overwrites it.
UPDATE jobs SET status = 'queued', worker_id = NULL, claimed_at = NULL, started_at = NULL, error = sqlc.arg(reason)::text
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND worker_id = sqlc.arg(worker_id)
  AND status IN ('claimed', 'running') AND attempts < sqlc.arg(max_attempts)::integer
RETURNING id;

-- name: ListWorkerJobsToEnd :many
-- The rest of a revoked worker's jobs: exhausted claims end as error,
-- cancelling ones as cancelled.
SELECT id, status FROM jobs
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND worker_id = sqlc.arg(worker_id)
  AND (status = 'cancelling' OR (status IN ('claimed', 'running') AND attempts >= sqlc.arg(max_attempts)::integer));
```

Append to `internal/db/queries/workers.sql`. The expiry and current job come from two small queries rather than a joined view: sqlc 1.31 cannot infer the nullability of lateral or scalar subquery columns, and generates non-null types that fail on NULL. That was verified while writing this plan.

```sql
-- name: GetWorker :one
SELECT * FROM workers WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id);

-- name: RevokeWorker :one
-- Idempotent: a second revoke keeps the first timestamp.
UPDATE workers SET status = 'revoked', revoked_at = COALESCE(revoked_at, now())
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND id = sqlc.arg(id)
RETURNING *;

-- name: RevokeWorkerCertificates :exec
UPDATE worker_certificates SET revoked_at = now()
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND worker_id = sqlc.arg(worker_id) AND revoked_at IS NULL;

-- name: ListWorkerCertExpiries :many
-- The latest expiry of each worker's unrevoked certificates (Workers page).
SELECT worker_id, max(not_after)::timestamptz AS expires_at
FROM worker_certificates
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND revoked_at IS NULL
GROUP BY worker_id;

-- name: ListWorkerCurrentJobs :many
-- The job each worker is running right now (Workers page).
SELECT DISTINCT ON (worker_id) worker_id, id
FROM jobs
WHERE org_id = sqlc.arg(org_id) AND worker_id IS NOT NULL AND status IN ('claimed', 'running', 'cancelling')
ORDER BY worker_id, claimed_at DESC;
```

```go
package jobs_test

import (
	"crypto/rand"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/crypto"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/testdb"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
)

// TestRequeueWorkerJobs: a revoked worker's running job goes back to the
// queue, an exhausted one ends as error, a cancelling one as cancelled,
// and another worker's job is untouched.
func TestRequeueWorkerJobs(t *testing.T) {
	d := testdb.Open(t)
	org := seedOrg(t, gen.New(d.Pool()), "jobs-revoke")
	zone := seedZone(t, d, org)
	store := jobs.NewSQLStore(d)
	q := jobs.NewSQLQueue(d, crypto.NewBox(testBoxKey("revoke")), emptySources{}, emptySources{}, emptySources{}, emptySources{}, time.Now, rand.Reader)
	scoped := db.WithOrgScope(ctx, org)
	revoked, other := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	mk := func(worker uuid.UUID, status string, attempts int) uuid.UUID {
		t.Helper()
		j := newJob(org, zone, jobs.KindAdhoc, jobs.StatusQueued)
		if err := store.CreateJob(scoped, j, nil); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if _, err := d.Pool().Exec(ctx, `UPDATE jobs SET status = $2, worker_id = $3, attempts = $4, claimed_at = now() WHERE id = $1`,
			j.ID, status, worker, attempts); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return j.ID
	}
	running := mk(revoked, "running", 1)
	exhausted := mk(revoked, "running", 3)
	cancelling := mk(revoked, "cancelling", 1)
	untouched := mk(other, "running", 1)

	requeued, ended, err := q.RequeueWorkerJobs(ctx, org, revoked, "worker revoked", 3)
	if err != nil || requeued != 1 || ended != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("RequeueWorkerJobs = %d, %d, %v; want 1 requeued, 2 ended", requeued, ended, err)
	}
	for id, want := range map[uuid.UUID][2]string{
		running:    {"queued", "requeued: worker revoked"},
		exhausted:  {"error", "worker revoked"},
		cancelling: {"cancelled", "worker revoked"},
		untouched:  {"running", ""},
	} {
		var status, errText string
		var worker *uuid.UUID
		if err := d.Pool().QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT status, error, worker_id FROM jobs WHERE id = $1`, id).Scan(&status, &errText, &worker); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if status != want[0] || errText != want[1] {
			t.Errorf("job %s = %s %q, want %s %q", id, status, errText, want[0], want[1])
		}
		if id == running && worker != nil {
			t.Error("a requeued job must drop its worker")
		}
	}
}
```

```go
package workers_test

import (

type fakeStreams struct{ closed []uuid.UUID }

func (f *fakeStreams) CloseWorker(id uuid.UUID) int {
	f.closed = append(f.closed, id)
	return 2
}

type fakeRequeue struct {
	calls []string
	err   error
}

func (f *fakeRequeue) RequeueWorkerJobs(_ context.Context, orgID, workerID uuid.UUID, reason string) (int, int, error) {
	f.calls = append(f.calls, orgID.String()+"/"+workerID.String()+"/"+reason)
	return 3, 1, f.err
}

func TestRevokeCutsCertificatesStreamsAndJobs(t *testing.T) {
	w := newEnrollWorld(t)
	streams, requeue := &fakeStreams{}, &fakeRequeue{}
	w.svc.SetRevokeHooks(streams, requeue)
	got, res, err := w.svc.Revoke(ctx, w.org, w.existing.ID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got.Status != workers.StatusRevoked || res != (workers.RevokeResult{Streams: 2, Requeued: 3, Ended: 1}) {
		t.Fatalf("revoke = %+v, %+v", got, res)
	}
	if len(streams.closed) != 1 || streams.closed[0] != w.existing.ID {
		t.Fatalf("closed = %v", streams.closed)
	}
	if len(requeue.calls) != 1 || requeue.calls[0] != w.org.String()+"/"+w.existing.ID.String()+"/worker revoked" {
		t.Fatalf("requeue = %v", requeue.calls)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, w.existingSerial); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrRevoked) {
		t.Fatalf("a revoked worker's serial must be refused: %v", err)
	}
	// A new worker under the same name cannot exist while the row is kept,
	// and after deleting it the old serial stays unknown.
	if err := w.svc.Delete(ctx, w.org, w.existing.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := w.svc.GetBySerial(ctx, w.existingSerial); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("after delete: %v", err)
	}
	if _, _, err := w.svc.Revoke(ctx, w.org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())); !errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown: %v", err)
	}
	requeue.err = errors.New("db down")
	other, _ := w.svc.Register(ctx, w.org, workers.RegisterInput{ZoneID: w.zone, Name: "fra-2", CertSerial: big.NewInt(31)})
	if _, _, err := w.svc.Revoke(ctx, w.org, other.ID); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a failed requeue must be reported")
	}
}

func TestListView(t *testing.T) {
	w := newEnrollWorld(t)
	seen := w.now.Add(-time.Minute)
	w.store.mu.Lock()
	w.store.rows[0].LastSeen = &seen
	w.store.mu.Unlock()
	if _, err := w.svc.Register(ctx, w.org, workers.RegisterInput{ZoneID: w.zone, Name: "stale", CertSerial: big.NewInt(32)}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	list, err := w.svc.ListView(ctx, w.org)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("ListView = %v, %v", list, err)
	}
	byName := map[string]workers.View{}
	for _, v := range list {
		byName[v.Name] = v
	}
	if !byName["old-1"].Online || byName["stale"].Online || byName["old-1"].ZoneSlug != "default" {
		t.Fatalf("online/zone = %+v", byName)
	}
	if byName["stale"].CertExpiresAt == nil || !byName["stale"].CertExpiresAt.After(w.now) {
		t.Fatalf("expiry = %v", byName["stale"].CertExpiresAt)
	}
}
```

```go
// TestGRPCPublicAddr: the address remote workers dial defaults to the
// public URL's host with the gRPC port, and only exists with a listener.
func TestGRPCPublicAddr(t *testing.T) {
	base := map[string]string{
		"PULSIX_DATABASE_URL": "postgres://u:p@db/pulsix",
		"PULSIX_MASTER_KEY":   strings.Repeat("ab", 32),
		"PULSIX_PUBLIC_URL":   "https://pulsix.example.test",
	}
	cfg, err := config.Load(env(base))
	if err != nil || cfg.GRPCPublicAddr != "" {
		t.Fatalf("disabled endpoint: %q, %v", cfg.GRPCPublicAddr, err)
	}
	base["PULSIX_GRPC_LISTEN"] = ":8444"
	cfg, err = config.Load(env(base))
	if err != nil || cfg.GRPCPublicAddr != "pulsix.example.test:8444" {
		t.Fatalf("default: %q, %v", cfg.GRPCPublicAddr, err)
	}
	base["PULSIX_GRPC_PUBLIC_ADDR"] = "workers.example.test:443"
	if cfg, err = config.Load(env(base)); err != nil || cfg.GRPCPublicAddr != "workers.example.test:443" {
		t.Fatalf("override: %q, %v", cfg.GRPCPublicAddr, err)
	}
	base["PULSIX_GRPC_PUBLIC_ADDR"] = "no-port"
	if _, err := config.Load(env(base)); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an address without a port must be refused")
	}
}
```

```go
package api_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/api"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/api/apitest"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/enrolltoken"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/workers"
)

func workerCSR(t *testing.T) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE REQUEST", Bytes: der})
}

func TestWorkerEnrollmentAndRevocation(t *testing.T) {
	s := apitest.New(t)
	owner := s.SeedUser("workers-owner@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	operator := s.SeedUser("workers-operator@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	org := s.SeedOrg("workers-org", owner)
	s.AddMember(org, operator, tenancy.RoleOperator)
	c, oc := s.LoginAs(owner), s.LoginAs(operator)
	base := s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/workers-org"
	zones, err := s.Deps.Hosts.ListZones(db.WithOrgScope(context.Background(), org), org)
	if err != nil || len(zones) == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("zones: %v", err)
	}

	if resp := requestJSON(t, oc, http.MethodGet, base+"/workers", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("operator list: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp := postJSON(t, c, base+"/worker-enrollments", map[string]any{"zone_id": zones[0].ID.String(), "name": "hel-1"})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	var created struct {
		Enrollment struct {
			ID string `json:"id"`
		} `json:"enrollment"`
		Token          string `json:"token"`
		DockerSnippet  string `json:"docker_snippet"`
		ComposeSnippet string `json:"compose_snippet"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &created)
	if !strings.HasPrefix(created.Token, "pxw1.") || !strings.Contains(created.DockerSnippet, created.Token) ||
		!strings.Contains(created.DockerSnippet, "PULSIX_MANAGER_ADDR=localhost:8444") || !strings.Contains(created.ComposeSnippet, "PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN") {
		t.Fatalf("created = %+v", created)
	}
	if body := mustReadBody(t, requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, base+"/worker-enrollments", nil)); !strings.Contains(body, created.Enrollment.ID) || strings.Contains(body, created.Token) {
		t.Fatalf("pending list = %s", body)
	}

	// The worker redeems the token (the gRPC Enroll path, called directly).
	tok, err := enrolltoken.Parse(created.Token)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	res, err := s.Deps.Workers.Enroll(context.Background(), workers.EnrollInput{TokenID: tok.ID, Secret: tok.Secret, CSRPEM: workerCSR(t), Version: "v0.2c"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var list struct {
		Workers []struct {
			ID            string  `json:"id"`
			Status        string  `json:"status"`
			ZoneSlug      string  `json:"zone_slug"`
			CertExpiresAt *string `json:"cert_expires_at"`
		} `json:"workers"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, base+"/workers", nil), &list)
	if len(list.Workers) != 1 || list.Workers[0].Status != "offline" || list.Workers[0].ZoneSlug != "default" || list.Workers[0].CertExpiresAt == nil {
		t.Fatalf("workers = %+v", list)
	}
	id := res.Worker.ID.String()
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodDelete, base+"/workers/"+id, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("delete an active worker: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp = postJSON(t, c, base+"/workers/"+id+"/revoke", nil)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	leaf, _ := pem.Decode(res.CertPEM)
	crt, _ := x509.ParseCertificate(leaf.Bytes)
	if _, err := s.Deps.Workers.GetBySerial(context.Background(), crt.SerialNumber); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a revoked worker's certificate must be refused")
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodDelete, base+"/workers/"+id, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	// Withdraw a pending enrollment.
	var second struct {
		Enrollment struct {
			ID string `json:"id"`
		} `json:"enrollment"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, postJSON(t, c, base+"/worker-enrollments", map[string]any{"zone_id": zones[0].ID.String(), "name": "hel-2"}), &second)
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodDelete, base+"/worker-enrollments/"+second.Enrollment.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodDelete, base+"/worker-enrollments/"+uuid.NewString(), nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("withdraw unknown: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	body := mustReadBody(t, requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, base+"/activity?limit=100", nil))
	for _, action := range []string{"POST createWorkerEnrollment", "POST revokeWorker", "DELETE deleteWorker", "DELETE withdrawWorkerEnrollment"} {
		if !strings.Contains(body, `"`+action+`"`) {
			t.Fatalf("activity lacks %q", action)
		}
	}
}

func TestWorkerEnrollmentNeedsTheWorkerEndpoint(t *testing.T) {
	s := apitest.New(t, func(s *apitest.Server) { s.Deps.WorkerEndpoint = api.WorkerEndpoint{} })
	owner := s.SeedUser("workers-off@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	org := s.SeedOrg("workers-off", owner)

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict || !strings.Contains(mustReadBody(t, resp), "PULSIX_GRPC_LISTEN") {
		t.Fatalf("disabled endpoint: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the job requeue**

`internal/jobs/revoke_requeue.go`:

```go
package jobs

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// RequeueWorkerJobs hands a revoked worker's jobs back (spec 2026-09-18 §4
// "Revocation"): claimed and running jobs below maxAttempts return to the
// queue with the reason recorded in error — the claim already counted the
// attempt — exhausted ones end as error with the reason, and cancelling
// ones as cancelled. Their in-process stream records are dropped, so the
// reaper does not count the cut streams as alive.
func (q *SQLQueue) RequeueWorkerJobs(ctx context.Context, orgID, workerID uuid.UUID, reason string, maxAttempts int) (requeued, ended int, err error) {
	ctx = db.WithOrgScope(ctx, orgID)
	g := gen.New(q.db)
	worker := pgtype.UUID{Bytes: workerID, Valid: true}
	ids, err := g.RequeueWorkerJobs(ctx, gen.RequeueWorkerJobsParams{
		Reason: "requeued: " + reason, OrgID: orgID, WorkerID: worker, MaxAttempts: int32(maxAttempts), //nolint:gosec // a small configured count
	})
	if err != nil {
		return 0, 0, fmt.Errorf("requeue worker jobs: %w", err)
	}
	for _, id := range ids {
		q.streams.remove(id)
	}
	rest, err := g.ListWorkerJobsToEnd(ctx, gen.ListWorkerJobsToEndParams{OrgID: orgID, WorkerID: worker, MaxAttempts: int32(maxAttempts)}) //nolint:gosec // as above
	if err != nil {
		return len(ids), 0, fmt.Errorf("list worker jobs to end: %w", err)
	}
	for _, r := range rest {
		t := terminalTransition{jobID: r.ID, status: StatusError, errText: reason, from: []Status{StatusClaimed, StatusRunning}}
		if Status(r.Status) == StatusCancelling {
			t.status, t.from = StatusCancelled, []Status{StatusCancelling}
		}
		applied, err := q.terminate(ctx, t)
		if err != nil {
			return len(ids), ended, fmt.Errorf("end job %s: %w", r.ID, err)
		}
		if applied {
			ended++
		}
	}
	return len(ids), ended, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement revocation and the list in `workers`**

`internal/workers/revoke.go`:

```go
package workers

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// ErrNotRevoked refuses deleting a worker that was not revoked first.
var ErrNotRevoked = errors.New("worker is not revoked")

// OnlineWindow: a worker seen within it counts as online (spec §4).
const OnlineWindow = 2 * time.Minute

// StreamCloser ends a worker's open event streams (grpcsrv.StreamRegistry).
type StreamCloser interface {
	CloseWorker(workerID uuid.UUID) int
}

// JobRequeuer hands a worker's jobs back to the queue (the job queue with
// the configured attempt limit, adapted in internal/app).
type JobRequeuer interface {
	RequeueWorkerJobs(ctx context.Context, orgID, workerID uuid.UUID, reason string) (requeued, ended int, err error)
}

// RevokeResult reports what a revocation cut.
type RevokeResult struct {
	Streams  int
	Requeued int
	Ended    int
}

// View is one row of the Workers page.
type View struct {
	Worker
	ZoneSlug      string
	Online        bool
	CertExpiresAt *time.Time
	CurrentJobID  *uuid.UUID
}

// ViewDetails are the per-worker facts the list joins in.
type ViewDetails struct {
	Expiry     map[uuid.UUID]time.Time
	CurrentJob map[uuid.UUID]uuid.UUID
}

// SetRevokeHooks wires the stream registry and the job requeue a
// revocation triggers (post-construction, like the job queue's sinks).
func (s *Service) SetRevokeHooks(streams StreamCloser, jobs JobRequeuer) {
	s.streams, s.jobs = streams, jobs
}

// Revoke refuses every certificate of the worker from now on and marks it
// revoked (the row stays for audit), then ends its open event streams and
// requeues its jobs (spec §4 "Revocation"). Revoking twice is harmless.
func (s *Service) Revoke(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Worker, RevokeResult, error) {
	w, err := s.store.Revoke(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, RevokeResult{}, err
	}
	var res RevokeResult
	if s.streams != nil {
		res.Streams = s.streams.CloseWorker(id)
	}
	if s.jobs != nil {
		if res.Requeued, res.Ended, err = s.jobs.RequeueWorkerJobs(ctx, orgID, id, "worker revoked"); err != nil {
			return w, res, fmt.Errorf("requeue the revoked worker's jobs: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return w, res, nil
}

// Delete removes a revoked worker's row and certificates; an active worker
// must be revoked first.
func (s *Service) Delete(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	w, err := s.store.Get(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if w.Status != StatusRevoked {
		return ErrNotRevoked
	}
	return s.store.Delete(ctx, orgID, id)
}

// ListView is the Workers page: every worker with its zone, online state
// (seen within OnlineWindow), certificate expiry and current job.
func (s *Service) ListView(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]View, error) {
	list, err := s.store.List(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	details, err := s.store.ViewDetails(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	slugs := map[uuid.UUID]string{}
	if s.zones != nil {
		zones, err := s.zones.ListZones(ctx, orgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("list zones: %w", err)
		}
		for _, z := range zones {
			slugs[z.ID] = z.Slug
		}
	}
	now := s.now()
	out := make([]View, len(list))
	for i, w := range list {
		v := View{Worker: w, ZoneSlug: slugs[w.ZoneID]}
		v.Online = w.Status == StatusActive && w.LastSeen != nil && now.Sub(*w.LastSeen) <= OnlineWindow
		if exp, ok := details.Expiry[w.ID]; ok {
			v.CertExpiresAt = &exp
		}
		if job, ok := details.CurrentJob[w.ID]; ok {
			v.CurrentJobID = &job
		}
		out[i] = v
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

Append to `store_sql.go`:

```go
func (s *SQLStore) Get(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Worker, error) {
	row, err := s.q.GetWorker(ctx, gen.GetWorkerParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Worker{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Worker{}, err
	}
	return toWorker(row)
}

// Revoke marks the worker and every certificate it holds revoked, in one
// transaction bound to the worker's organization.
func (s *SQLStore) Revoke(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Worker, error) {
	var out Worker
	err := s.d.WithWriteTx(ctx, orgID, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		row, err := s.q.RevokeWorker(ctx, gen.RevokeWorkerParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
			return ErrNotFound
		}
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("revoke worker: %w", err)
		}
		if err := s.q.RevokeWorkerCertificates(ctx, gen.RevokeWorkerCertificatesParams{OrgID: orgID, WorkerID: id}); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("revoke worker certificates: %w", err)
		}
		out, err = toWorker(row)
		return err
	})
	return out, err
}

func (s *SQLStore) ViewDetails(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (ViewDetails, error) {
	d := ViewDetails{Expiry: map[uuid.UUID]time.Time{}, CurrentJob: map[uuid.UUID]uuid.UUID{}}
	exp, err := s.q.ListWorkerCertExpiries(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return d, fmt.Errorf("list certificate expiries: %w", err)
	}
	for _, e := range exp {
		d.Expiry[e.WorkerID] = e.ExpiresAt
	}
	cur, err := s.q.ListWorkerCurrentJobs(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return d, fmt.Errorf("list current jobs: %w", err)
	}
	for _, c := range cur {
		if w := db.UUIDPtr(c.WorkerID); w != nil {
			d.CurrentJob[*w] = c.ID
		}
	}
	return d, nil
}
```

```go
func (f *fakeStore) Get(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	for _, r := range f.rows {
		if r.OrgID == orgID && r.ID == id {
			return r, nil
		}
	}
	return workers.Worker{}, workers.ErrNotFound
}

func (f *fakeStore) Revoke(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (workers.Worker, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.init()
	for i, r := range f.rows {
		if r.OrgID == orgID && r.ID == id {
			now := time.Now()
			f.rows[i].Status, f.rows[i].RevokedAt = workers.StatusRevoked, &now
			for s, c := range f.certs {
				if c.WorkerID == id && c.RevokedAt == nil {
					c.RevokedAt = &now
					f.certs[s] = c
				}
			}
			return f.rows[i], nil
		}
	}
	return workers.Worker{}, workers.ErrNotFound
}

func (f *fakeStore) ViewDetails(context.Context, uuid.UUID) (workers.ViewDetails, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	d := workers.ViewDetails{Expiry: map[uuid.UUID]time.Time{}, CurrentJob: map[uuid.UUID]uuid.UUID{}}
	for _, c := range f.certs {
		if c.RevokedAt == nil && c.NotAfter.After(d.Expiry[c.WorkerID]) {
			d.Expiry[c.WorkerID] = c.NotAfter
		}
	}
	return d, nil
}
```

```go
// defaultGRPCPublicAddr joins the public URL's host with the gRPC
// listener's port.
func defaultGRPCPublicAddr(publicURL, listen string) string {
	u, err := url.Parse(publicURL)
	_, port, perr := net.SplitHostPort(listen)
	if err != nil || perr != nil || u.Hostname() == "" {
		return ""
	}
	return net.JoinHostPort(u.Hostname(), port)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The API**

`internal/api/worker_snippets.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
)

// WorkerEndpoint is where remote workers reach the manager: Addr is
// PULSIX_GRPC_PUBLIC_ADDR (empty: the worker endpoint is disabled) and
// ServerName the name the manager's certificate carries.
type WorkerEndpoint struct {
	Addr       string
	ServerName string
}

// workerSnippets renders the docker run and compose snippets the Add
// worker dialog shows next to the token.
func workerSnippets(ep WorkerEndpoint, name, token, version string) (docker, compose string) {
	if version == "" || version == "dev" {
		version = "latest"
	}
	docker = strings.Join([]string{
		fmt.Sprintf("docker run -d --name pulsix-worker-%s --restart unless-stopped \\", name),
		fmt.Sprintf("  -e PULSIX_MANAGER_ADDR=%s \\", ep.Addr),
		fmt.Sprintf("  -e PULSIX_MANAGER_SERVER_NAME=%s \\", ep.ServerName),
		fmt.Sprintf("  -e PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN=%s \\", token),
		fmt.Sprintf("  -v pulsix-worker-%s:/var/lib/pulsix-worker \\", name),
		fmt.Sprintf("  noixdev/pulsix-worker:%s", version),
	}, "\n")
	compose = strings.Join([]string{
		"services:",
		fmt.Sprintf("  pulsix-worker-%s:", name),
		fmt.Sprintf("    image: noixdev/pulsix-worker:%s", version),
		"    restart: unless-stopped",
		"    environment:",
		fmt.Sprintf("      PULSIX_MANAGER_ADDR: %q", ep.Addr),
		fmt.Sprintf("      PULSIX_MANAGER_SERVER_NAME: %q", ep.ServerName),
		fmt.Sprintf("      PULSIX_ENROLL_TOKEN: %q", token),
		"    volumes:",
		fmt.Sprintf("      - pulsix-worker-%s:/var/lib/pulsix-worker", name),
		"volumes:",
		fmt.Sprintf("  pulsix-worker-%s: {}", name),
	}, "\n")
	return docker, compose
}
```

`internal/api/worker_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/workers"
)

type workerDTO struct {
	ID            string     `json:"id"`
	Name          string     `json:"name"`
	ZoneID        string     `json:"zone_id"`
	ZoneSlug      string     `json:"zone_slug"`
	Status        string     `json:"status" enum:"online,offline,revoked" doc:"online = seen within 2 minutes"`
	LastSeen      *time.Time `json:"last_seen,omitempty"`
	Version       string     `json:"version,omitempty"`
	CertExpiresAt *time.Time `json:"cert_expires_at,omitempty"`
	CurrentJobID  string     `json:"current_job_id,omitempty"`
	RevokedAt     *time.Time `json:"revoked_at,omitempty"`
	CreatedAt     time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
}

type workerEnrollmentDTO struct {
	ID        string    `json:"id"`
	Name      string    `json:"name"`
	ZoneID    string    `json:"zone_id"`
	ExpiresAt time.Time `json:"expires_at"`
	CreatedAt time.Time `json:"created_at"`
}

type workerListInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
}

type workerListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Workers []workerDTO `json:"workers"`
	}
}

type workerIDInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type workerRevokeOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Worker        workerDTO `json:"worker"`
		StreamsClosed int       `json:"streams_closed"`
		JobsRequeued  int       `json:"jobs_requeued"`
		JobsEnded     int       `json:"jobs_ended"`
	}
}

type enrollmentListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Enrollments []workerEnrollmentDTO `json:"enrollments"`
	}
}

type enrollmentCreateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body struct {
		ZoneID string `json:"zone_id" format:"uuid"`
		Name   string `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"63"`
	}
}

type enrollmentCreateOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Enrollment     workerEnrollmentDTO `json:"enrollment"`
		Token          string              `json:"token" doc:"Shown once; valid for one hour and one enrollment"`
		DockerSnippet  string              `json:"docker_snippet"`
		ComposeSnippet string              `json:"compose_snippet"`
	}
}

func toWorkerDTO(v workers.View) workerDTO {
	dto := workerDTO{
		ID: v.ID.String(), Name: v.Name, ZoneID: v.ZoneID.String(), ZoneSlug: v.ZoneSlug, LastSeen: v.LastSeen,
		Version: v.Version, CertExpiresAt: v.CertExpiresAt, RevokedAt: v.RevokedAt, CreatedAt: v.CreatedAt,
	}
	switch {
	case v.Status == workers.StatusRevoked:
		dto.Status = "revoked"
	case v.Online:
		dto.Status = "online"
	default:
		dto.Status = "offline"
	}
	if v.CurrentJobID != nil {
		dto.CurrentJobID = v.CurrentJobID.String()
	}
	return dto
}

func toEnrollmentDTO(e workers.Enrollment) workerEnrollmentDTO {
	return workerEnrollmentDTO{ID: e.ID.String(), Name: e.Name, ZoneID: e.ZoneID.String(), ExpiresAt: e.ExpiresAt, CreatedAt: e.CreatedAt}
}

// registerWorkerRoutes wires the Workers page (spec 2026-09-18 §4):
// workers.read (admin) to see, workers.write (admin) to add, revoke and
// delete. Tokens are shown once, in the create response only.
func registerWorkerRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/workers", func(ctx context.Context, in *workerListInput) (*workerListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermWorkersRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Workers.ListView(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapWorkersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &workerListOutput{}
		out.Body.Workers = make([]workerDTO, len(list))
		for i, v := range list {
			out.Body.Workers[i] = toWorkerDTO(v)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/workers/{id}/revoke", func(ctx context.Context, in *workerIDInput) (*workerRevokeOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermWorkersWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "worker id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		w, res, err := deps.Workers.Revoke(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapWorkersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), fmt.Sprintf("revoked worker %s (%d stream(s) closed, %d job(s) requeued, %d ended)", w.Name, res.Streams, res.Requeued, res.Ended))
		out := &workerRevokeOutput{}
		out.Body.Worker = toWorkerDTO(workers.View{Worker: w})
		out.Body.StreamsClosed, out.Body.JobsRequeued, out.Body.JobsEnded = res.Streams, res.Requeued, res.Ended
		return out, nil
	}, withAudit("revokeWorker", "worker"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/workers/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *workerIDInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermWorkersWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "worker id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Workers.Delete(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapWorkersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "deleted revoked worker")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("deleteWorker", "worker"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/worker-enrollments", func(ctx context.Context, in *workerListInput) (*enrollmentListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermWorkersRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Workers.ListEnrollments(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapWorkersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &enrollmentListOutput{}
		out.Body.Enrollments = make([]workerEnrollmentDTO, len(list))
		for i, e := range list {
			out.Body.Enrollments[i] = toEnrollmentDTO(e)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/worker-enrollments", func(ctx context.Context, in *enrollmentCreateInput) (*enrollmentCreateOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermWorkersWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if deps.WorkerEndpoint.Addr == "" {
			return nil, huma.Error409Conflict("the worker endpoint is disabled (PULSIX_GRPC_LISTEN)")
		}
		zoneID, err := parseUUID(in.Body.ZoneID, "zone id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		actor := scope.UserID
		e, token, err := deps.Workers.CreateEnrollment(ctx, scope.OrgID, &actor, zoneID, in.Body.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapWorkersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, e.ID.String(), "created an enrollment token for worker "+e.Name)
		out := &enrollmentCreateOutput{}
		out.Body.Enrollment, out.Body.Token = toEnrollmentDTO(e), token
		out.Body.DockerSnippet, out.Body.ComposeSnippet = workerSnippets(deps.WorkerEndpoint, e.Name, token, deps.Version)
		return out, nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusCreated }, withAudit("createWorkerEnrollment", "worker_enrollment"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/worker-enrollments/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *workerIDInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermWorkersWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "enrollment id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Workers.WithdrawEnrollment(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapWorkersErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "withdrew a worker enrollment token")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("withdrawWorkerEnrollment", "worker_enrollment"))
}

func mapWorkersErr(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, workers.ErrInvalidInput):
		return huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotFound):
		return huma.Error404NotFound("not found")
	case errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNameTaken):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("a worker with this name already exists")
	case errors.Is(err, workers.ErrNotRevoked):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("revoke the worker before deleting it")
	}
	slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "unmapped workers error", "err", err)
	return huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
}
```

```go
	WorkerEndpoint WorkerEndpoint
```

```go
package app

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/jobs"
)

// workerJobRequeuer adapts the job queue to workers.JobRequeuer with the
// configured attempt limit (PULSIX_JOB_MAX_ATTEMPTS).
type workerJobRequeuer struct {
	q           *jobs.SQLQueue
	maxAttempts int
}

func (a workerJobRequeuer) RequeueWorkerJobs(ctx context.Context, orgID, workerID uuid.UUID, reason string) (int, int, error) {
	return a.q.RequeueWorkerJobs(ctx, orgID, workerID, reason, a.maxAttempts)
}
```

```go
	// A revocation ends the worker's open streams and requeues its jobs.
	workersSvc.SetRevokeHooks(streams, workerJobRequeuer{q: jobsQueue, maxAttempts: cfg.JobMaxAttempts})
```

and `api.New` gets `Workers: workersSvc, WorkerEndpoint: api.WorkerEndpoint{Addr: cfg.GRPCPublicAddr, ServerName: cfg.CAListenHost()}`.

### Task 16: The Workers page

This task covers spec §4 "UI": the mockup's Workers screen. It lists status (online means seen within 2 minutes), zone, last seen, version, certificate expiry (highlighted under 14 days) and the current job. It also shows pending enrollment tokens. "Add worker" shows the one-time token with the docker run and compose snippets, and revoke and delete sit behind a confirmation. Owners and admins see the page. Everyone else gets a short note.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/api/hooks/useWorkers.ts`, `web/src/features/workers/{WorkersPage.tsx,AddWorkerDialog.tsx,expiry.ts}`
- Modify: `web/src/api/types.ts` (`Worker`, `WorkerEnrollment`), `web/src/lib/roles.ts` (`workers.read`, `workers.write` → `admin`), `web/src/router.tsx` (`workersRoute`), `web/src/ui/shell.tsx` (sidebar entry after Runs, icon `Cpu`), `web/src/lib/breadcrumbs.ts` (`workers: 'Workers'`)
- Test: `web/src/features/workers/WorkersPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**

Also `GET /api/v1/orgs/{org}/worker-enrollments` → `{enrollments: WorkerEnrollmentDTO[]}` (`{id, name, zone_id, expires_at, created_at}`), `POST …/worker-enrollments` `{zone_id, name}` → `{enrollment, token, docker_snippet, compose_snippet}`, `DELETE …/worker-enrollments/{id}`, `POST …/workers/{id}/revoke` → `{worker, streams_closed, jobs_requeued, jobs_ended}`, and `DELETE …/workers/{id}`. `useZones(org)` comes from `web/src/api/hooks/useHosts.ts`.

Also `EXPIRY_WARN_DAYS = 14`, `daysLeft(at, now)`, `expiryLabel(at, now)`, `seenLabel(at, now)` and the route `/o/$org/workers`.

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';

import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { WorkersPage } from './WorkersPage.tsx';
import { expiryLabel, seenLabel } from './expiry.ts';

const org = (role: string) => ({ org: { id: 'o', name: 'Acme', slug: 'acme', role, created_at: '' } });
const inDays = (d: number) => new Date(Date.now() + d * 86_400_000).toISOString();
const worker = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  id: 'w1', name: 'hel-1', zone_id: 'z1', zone_slug: 'hel', status: 'online', last_seen: new Date().toISOString(),
  version: 'v0.2c', cert_expires_at: inDays(41), created_at: '', ...over,
});

function renderPage() {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  const rootRoute = createRootRoute();
  const orgRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '/o/$org' });
  const listRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/workers', component: WorkersPage });
  const runRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/runs/$id', component: () => <div>RUN VIEW</div> });
  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([orgRoute.addChildren([listRoute, runRoute])]),
    history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: ['/o/acme/workers'] }),
  });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

    expect(seenLabel('2026-09-15T10:00:00Z', now)).toBe('3 d ago');
    expect(seenLabel(undefined, now)).toBe('never');

});
```

`web/src/api/hooks/useWorkers.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../client.ts';

function useInvalidate(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['workers', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['worker-enrollments', org] });
  };
}

/** useWorkers lists the org's workers; refreshed every 30 s so online and
 * offline follow the heartbeat without a reload. */
export function useWorkers(org: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['workers', org],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/workers', { params: { path: { org } } })),
    enabled: !!org && enabled,
    refetchInterval: 30_000,
  });
}

export function useWorkerEnrollments(org: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['worker-enrollments', org],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/worker-enrollments', { params: { path: { org } } })),
    enabled: !!org && enabled,
  });
}

/** useCreateWorkerEnrollment returns the token once; gcTime 0 keeps it
 * out of the query cache like every other secret-bearing mutation. */
export function useCreateWorkerEnrollment(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: { zone_id: string; name: string }) =>
      unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/worker-enrollments', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
    gcTime: 0,
  });
}

export function useWithdrawWorkerEnrollment(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/worker-enrollments/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useRevokeWorker(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/workers/{id}/revoke', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useDeleteWorker(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/workers/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Expiry and last-seen labels**

`web/src/features/workers/expiry.ts`:

```ts
/** EXPIRY_WARN_DAYS: certificates closer to expiry are highlighted (spec §4). */
export const EXPIRY_WARN_DAYS = 14;

/** daysLeft is the whole days until at, negative once expired. */
export function daysLeft(at: string, now: Date): number {
  return Math.floor((new Date(at).getTime() - now.getTime()) / 86_400_000);
}

/** expiryLabel renders a certificate expiry ("in 41 d", "expired"). */
export function expiryLabel(at: string | undefined | null, now: Date): string {
  if (!at) return '—';
  const d = daysLeft(at, now);
  if (d < 0) return 'expired';
  if (d === 0) return 'today';
  return `in ${d} d`;
}

/** seenLabel renders a heartbeat age ("just now", "5 min ago", "3 h ago", "2 d ago"). */
export function seenLabel(at: string | undefined | null, now: Date): string {
  if (!at) return 'never';
  const mins = Math.floor((now.getTime() - new Date(at).getTime()) / 60_000);
  if (mins < 1) return 'just now';
  if (mins < 60) return `${mins} min ago`;
  const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60);
  if (hours < 48) return `${hours} h ago`;
  return `${Math.floor(hours / 24)} d ago`;
}
```

`web/src/features/workers/WorkersPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Cpu } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, GridHeader, GridRow } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill, type Tone } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useOrg } from '../../api/hooks/useOrgs.ts';
import { useDeleteWorker, useRevokeWorker, useWithdrawWorkerEnrollment, useWorkerEnrollments, useWorkers } from '../../api/hooks/useWorkers.ts';
import { may } from '../../lib/roles.ts';
import type { Worker } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { AddWorkerDialog } from './AddWorkerDialog.tsx';
import { EXPIRY_WARN_DAYS, daysLeft, expiryLabel, seenLabel } from './expiry.ts';

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0, 0.9fr) minmax(0, 0.6fr) 100px 110px minmax(0, 0.6fr) 110px minmax(0, 0.7fr) 150px';
const STATUS_TONE: Record<string, Tone> = { online: 'ok', offline: 'warn', revoked: 'unknown' };

/** WorkersPage lists the organization's zone workers (spec 2026-09-18 §4):
 * status (online = seen within 2 minutes), zone, last seen, version,
 * certificate expiry (highlighted under 14 days) and the current job, plus
 * the pending enrollment tokens. Owners and admins add, revoke and delete. */
export function WorkersPage() {
  const { org } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { org: string };
  const toast = useToast();
  const orgInfo = useOrg(org);
  const role = orgInfo.data?.org.role;
  const canRead = may(role, 'workers.read');
  const canWrite = may(role, 'workers.write');
  const list = useWorkers(org, !!orgInfo.data && canRead);
  const pending = useWorkerEnrollments(org, !!orgInfo.data && canRead);
  const revoke = useRevokeWorker(org);
  const remove = useDeleteWorker(org);
  const withdraw = useWithdrawWorkerEnrollment(org);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);

  const orgGuard = useQueryGuard(orgInfo);
  if (orgGuard) return orgGuard;
  if (!orgInfo.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  if (!canRead) {
    return <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Workers are managed by the organization's owners and admins.</p>;
  }
  if (!list.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const workers = list.data.workers ?? [];
  const enrollments = pending.data?.enrollments ?? [];
  const now = new Date();

  async function onRevoke(w: Worker) {
    if (!confirm(`Revoke worker ${w.name}? It is cut off at once; its running jobs go back to the queue.`)) return;
    try {
      await revoke.mutateAsync(w.id);
      toast.show(`Worker ${w.name} revoked`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Revoking failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  async function onDelete(w: Worker) {
    if (!confirm(`Delete worker ${w.name}? Its history stays in the activity log.`)) return;
    try {
      await remove.mutateAsync(w.id);
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Deleting failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Workers</h1>
          <span className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
            {workers.filter((w) => w.status === 'online').length} of {workers.length} online · workers run the jobs of their zone
          </span>
        </div>
        {canWrite && (
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>
            <Cpu size={15} /> Add worker
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
      <Card>
        <GridHeader columns={COLUMNS}>
          <span>Worker</span>
          <span>Zone</span>
          <span>Status</span>
          <span>Last seen</span>
          <span>Version</span>
          <span>Certificate</span>
          <span>Current job</span>
          {canWrite && <span>Actions</span>}
        </GridHeader>
        {workers.map((w) => {
          const expiring = !!w.cert_expires_at && daysLeft(w.cert_expires_at, now) < EXPIRY_WARN_DAYS;
          return (
            <GridRow key={w.id} columns={COLUMNS}>
              <span className="truncate font-mono text-[13px]" data-row>
                {w.name}
              </span>
              <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs">{w.zone_slug}</span>
              <span>
                <Pill tone={STATUS_TONE[w.status] ?? 'unknown'}>{w.status}</Pill>
              </span>
              <span className={`text-xs ${w.status === 'offline' ? 'text-[var(--px-warn)]' : 'text-muted-foreground'}`}>{seenLabel(w.last_seen, now)}</span>
              <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs">{w.version || '—'}</span>
              <span className={`text-xs ${expiring ? 'font-semibold text-[var(--px-warn)]' : 'text-muted-foreground'}`} data-expiring={expiring || undefined}>
                {w.status === 'revoked' ? '—' : expiryLabel(w.cert_expires_at, now)}
              </span>
              <span className="truncate text-xs">
                {w.current_job_id ? (
                  <Link to="/o/$org/runs/$id" params={{ org, id: w.current_job_id }} className="font-mono hover:text-primary">
                    {w.current_job_id.slice(0, 8)}
                  </Link>
                ) : (
                  '—'
                )}
              </span>
              {canWrite && (
                <span className="flex gap-1">
                  {w.status !== 'revoked' ? (
                    <Button type="button" variant="destructive" aria-label={`Revoke ${w.name}`} onClick={() => void onRevoke(w)}>
                      Revoke
                    </Button>
                  ) : (
                    <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Delete ${w.name}`} onClick={() => void onDelete(w)}>
                      Delete
                    </Button>
                  )}
                </span>
              )}
            </GridRow>
          );
        })}
        {workers.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No workers yet.</p>}
      </Card>
      {enrollments.length > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <GridHeader columns="minmax(0, 1fr) 160px 110px">
            <span>Pending enrollment</span>
            <span>Expires</span>
            <span />
          </GridHeader>
          {enrollments.map((e) => (
            <GridRow key={e.id} columns="minmax(0, 1fr) 160px 110px">
              <span className="font-mono text-[13px]">{e.name}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date(e.expires_at).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
              {canWrite ? (
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Withdraw ${e.name}`} onClick={() => void withdraw.mutateAsync(e.id)}>
                  Withdraw
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <span />
              )}
            </GridRow>
          ))}
        </Card>
      )}
      {adding && <AddWorkerDialog org={org} onClose={() => setAdding(false)} />}
    </>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/workers/AddWorkerDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Copy } from 'lucide-react';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useZones } from '../../api/hooks/useHosts.ts';
import { useCreateWorkerEnrollment } from '../../api/hooks/useWorkers.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';
const NAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,62}$/;

interface Created {
  token: string;
  docker: string;
  compose: string;
}

/** AddWorkerDialog creates a one-time enrollment token for a new worker
 * (zone + name) and shows it once, with the docker run and compose
 * snippets that start a worker enrolling with it. */
export function AddWorkerDialog({ org, onClose }: { org: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const zones = useZones(org);
  const create = useCreateWorkerEnrollment(org);
  const [zoneId, setZoneId] = useState('');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [created, setCreated] = useState<Created | null>(null);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'docker' | 'compose'>('docker');
  const zoneList = zones.data?.zones ?? [];
  const zone = zoneId || zoneList[0]?.id || '';

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    if (!NAME_RE.test(name)) {
      setError('Use lowercase letters, digits and hyphens, starting with a letter or digit.');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const res = await create.mutateAsync({ zone_id: zone, name });
      setCreated({ token: res.token, docker: res.docker_snippet, compose: res.compose_snippet });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Creating the token failed');
    }
  }

  if (created) {
    const snippet = tab === 'docker' ? created.docker : created.compose;
    return (
      <Dialog open onClose={onClose} title={`Add worker ${name}`} size="wide">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            Token valid for one hour, single use. The worker connects outbound, receives its certificate and appears here. The token is shown only now.
          </p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <code className="flex-1 truncate rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs" aria-label="Enrollment token">
              {created.token}
            </code>
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label="Copy token" onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(created.token)}>
              <Copy size={13} />
            </Button>
          </div>
          <div role="tablist" className="flex gap-4 border-b border-border">
            {(['docker', 'compose'] as const).map((k) => (
              <button
                key={k}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                aria-selected={tab === k}
                className={`border-b-2 py-1.5 text-xs ${tab === k ? 'border-primary font-semibold' : 'border-transparent text-muted-foreground'}`}
                onClick={() => setTab(k)}
              >
                {k === 'docker' ? 'Docker' : 'Compose service'}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <pre className="overflow-x-auto rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-3 font-mono text-[11px]" aria-label="Worker snippet">
            {snippet}
          </pre>
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label="Copy snippet" onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(snippet)}>
              <Copy size={13} /> Copy
            </Button>
            <Button type="button" variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
              Done
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Dialog>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onClose={onClose} title="Add worker">
      <form className="flex flex-col gap-3" onSubmit={(e) => void submit(e)}>
        <Label htmlFor="worker-zone">Zone</Label>
        <select id="worker-zone" className={SELECT} value={zone} onChange={(e) => setZoneId(e.target.value)}>
          {zoneList.map((z) => (
            <option key={z.id} value={z.id}>
              {z.name} ({z.slug})
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <Label htmlFor="worker-name">Name</Label>
        <Input id="worker-name" className="font-mono" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="hel-1" />
        {error && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--px-crit)]">{error}</p>}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button type="button" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={create.isPending || !zone}>
            Create token
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```
